[1]
śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ
kṛtyakalpataruḥ
gārhasthyakāṇḍam
maṅgalācaraṇam
oṃ namo vighnarājāya nirvighnaphaladāyine |
pratijñā (viṣayanirdeśaḥ)
svādhyāyādhigamo ‘rthatattvagataye satkarmaniṣpattaye
sā niḥśeṣamalakṣayāya sa punar mokṣāya yasyābhavat |
dattānekapuradvijottamavadhūsaṅgītanādotsavo
gārhasthyaṃ vadati dvitīyam adhunā kāṇḍaṃ sa lakṣmīdharaḥ ||
sapallavo vivāho ‘tra tadbhedāś cādhivedanam |
parivedanam ādhānam āvasthyaṃ ca kathyate ||
agnyādhānavidhiḥ paścāt tato yajanayājane |
viprāṇāṃ vṛttikarmāṇi viprāpadvṛttayaḥ kṛṣiḥ ||
vāṇijyaṃ ca kusīdaṃ ca tathāpadvṛttayaḥ parāḥ |
kṣatraviṭvṛttikarmāṇi śuklakṛṣṇadhanāny atha ||
vṛttidharmau ca śūdrasya vratāni snātakasya ca |
[2]        yamādayaḥ kulānāṃ ca nāśanāni kulasthitiḥ ||
vācyāvācye tathā samyag vidhir yānapraveśayoḥ |
anākramyāny adhiṣṭheyāny anālocyāni sarvaśaḥ ||
atha śūdrāpradeyāni krodhādiparivarjanam |
ṛṇāpākaraṇaṃ brūte dhīro lakṣmīdharaḥ kramāt ||
itthaṃ gṛhasthakāṇḍe ‘smin parvaṇāṃ saptaviṃśatiḥ ||
[3]
1
atha gārhasthyam
tatra brahmacāridharmam abhidhāya saṃvartaḥ |
eṣa dharmaḥ samākhyātaḥ prathamāśramiṇi dvije |
ataḥ paraṃ samāvṛttaḥ kuryād dāraparigraham ||
“samāvṛttaḥ” gurukulād āgatya kṛtasnānākhyasaṃskāraḥ |
manuḥ |
vedān adhītya vedau vā vedaṃ vāpi yathākramam |
aviluptabrahmacaryo gṛhasthāśramam āviśet ||
vedān vedāv ityādikaḥ puruṣaśaktyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ | “yathākramam” yathavihitādhyayanetikartavyatayā |
tathā ca yamaḥ |
vedaṃ vedau ca vedān vā tato ‘dhītya yathāvidhi |
aviluptabrahmacaryo dārān kurvīta dharmavit ||
[4] evaṃ viśīrṇabrahmacaryasyākṛtaprāyaścittasyottarāśrameṣv anadhikāraḥ sūcito bhavati |
tathā |
guruṇānumataḥ snātvā samāvṛtto yathāvidhi |
udvaheta dvijo bhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām ||
“lakṣaṇānvitāṃ” śubhasūcakacihnayuktām |
yamaḥ |
guruṃ vā samanujñāya pradāya gurudakṣiṇām |
sadṛśīm āhared bhāryāṃ mātāpitṛmate sthitaḥ ||
vāśabdo naiṣṭhikāpekṣayā | “samanujñaya” iti samanujñāpya ityarthe antarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ |
nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ (?) kṣatriyaviṣāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca parigrahe |
sajātiḥ śreyasī bhāryā sajātiś ca patiḥ striyāḥ ||
“sajātiḥ” samānajātiḥ | “śreyasī” śreṣṭhā |
[5]        vasiṣṭhaḥ |
gṛhastho vinītakrodhaharṣo guruṇānujñātaḥ snātvāsmānārṣeyīm aspṛṣṭamaithunām avaravayasīṃ sadṛśīṃ bhāyāṃ vindeta |
gṛhastha iti bhāvini bhūtopacāraḥ taddharmaprāptyarthaḥ | tena akṛtadāro ‘pi gārhasthyasaṃkalpavān āśramāntarāt nivṛtto gṛhasthadharmeṣv adhikriyata iti bhartṛjñaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
samānām udvahet patnīṃ kulaśīlavayoguṇaiḥ |
tayā saha cared dharmaṃ śrautaṃ smārtaṃ samāhitaḥ ||
“samānām” anurūpāṃ vayasā cānurūpām | tac ca,
varṣair ekaguṇāṃ bhāryām udvahet triguṇaḥ svayam |
ityādinoktam |
taṃ pratītaṃ svadharmeṇa brahmadāyaharaṃ pituḥ |
[sragviṇaṃ talpa] āsīnam arhayet prathamaṃ gavā || [Manu 3.3]
“taṃ” brahmacāriṇam | “pratītam” pratinivṛttaṃ gṛhasthāśramonmukham | “svadharmeṇa” dākṣiṇyādinā | “brahmadāyaharaṃ pituḥ” [6] iti | brahmaiva dāyo brahmadāyaḥ | pituḥ sakāśād adhigatavedam iti mukhyaprāptyarthānuvādaḥ | “arhayet” pūjayet pitrādir iti śeṣaḥ | “gavā” iti madhuparkopalakṣaṇam |
hārītaḥ |
guruṇānujñāto ‘laṅkārādīn gṛhṇīyāt | yathārthaṃ caitān bibhṛyād āmantrya vidhivad dārān āhṛtyāgnīn ādhāyoñchasilābhyām ayācitopapannaṃ sādhubhyo yācamānebhyo (?) pāyajanād eva pitṛmanuṣyārthe vṛttyādānaṃ kurvīta, nātmārthe |
“alaṅkārādīn” svasvagṛhyoktakuṇḍalaprabhṛtīn | ādiśabdena sraggandhādīn gṛhyādeśitān, teṣāṃ samāvartanakāle grahaṇaṃ nityam | uttarakāle yathārthaṃ dhāraṇam | “yathārtham” yathāprayojanaṃ samāvartanād ūrdhvaṃ dhārayet | samāvartanakāle tu niyamaḥ |
yamaḥ |
ekaṃ dvau trīn vā vedān adhītya guruṃ paritoṣyānujñāto muñjāpanayanaṃ kṛtvā savarṇāṃ pariṇīya brāhmādinā vivāhena ṛtukālābhigāmī putrānutpādyāvasathyādhānaṃ kṛtvātithīn pūjayet |
[7]        kaśyapaḥ |
dārādhīnāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
dārān sarvaprayatnena viśuddhān udvahet tataḥ ||
devatānāṃ namaskāraḥ ptiṝṇāṃ codakakriyā |
dārataḥ saṃpravartante dārāḥ svargasya saṃkramaḥ ||
iti etat kaśyapamataṃ brahmaṇaś cānuśāsanam ||
“viśuddhān” kulataḥ svarūpataś ca |
atha vivāhyāvivāhyakanyakānirūpaṇam
tatra manu-śātātapau |
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ |
sā praśastā dvijātīnāṃ dārakarmaṇi maithune ||
“mātur asapiṇḍā” mātuladuhitrādibhyo ‘nyā |
atra kecid āhuḥ — “dārakarmaṇi” dāratvajanake vivāhe | [8] “maithune” mithunaśabdavācya-strīpuṃsasādhyādhānādikarmaṇi, na kevalaṃ strīsādhyapākādikarmaṇi | api tūbhayasādhye ‘pi sā praśastā syād ity arthaḥ | atha vā “maithune” mithunasādhye dharmyaputrotpattyādāv iti |
gautamaḥ |
asamānapravarair vivāhaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyaḥ bījinaś ca mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt |
samānapravaratvaṃ ca saṃkhyāsaṃjñayoḥ sāmye bhavati, tathaiva prakaraṇāt | “pitṛbāndhavāḥ” pitṛmātulapitṛmātṛsvasṛputrādayaḥ | “bījinaś ca” ity atra “ūrdhvaṃ saptamād” ity anuvartate | “bījī” yaḥ parakṣetre jātimātrotpannena (?) śāstreṇa prāpto bījam prakṣipati | “mātṛbāndhavāḥ” mātṛmātulo (?) mātṛsvasṛmātṛṣvasā vā | eṣāṃ yaḥ pañcamaḥ tata ūrdhvaṃ vivāhaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
asamānārṣeyīṃ kanyāṃ varayet pañca mātṛtaḥ pariharet sapta pitṛtaḥ | trīn mātṛtaḥ pañca pitṛta iti vā |
atra ca samānajātīye pañca, asamānajātīye trīn iti vyavasthito vikalpaḥ |
[9]        sumantuḥ |
pitṛṣvasṛsutāṃ mātulasutāṃ mātuḥ sagotrāṃ samānārṣeyīṃ vivāhya cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret, parityajya caināṃ bibhṛyāt |
ayaṃ ca mātṛsagotrāniṣedho mātṛsaṃbandhitvena janmaparaḥ janmanāmnoḥ pratyabhijñāne sati |
tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
sagotrāṃ mātur apy eke necchanty udvāhakarmaṇi |
janmanāmnor avijñāne udvahed aviśaṅkitaḥ || iti |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kulotpannāṃ yuktācārāṃ varayet | pitṛmātṛsvasṛduhitaro mātulasutāś ca dharmatas tāḥ bhaginyo varjayet | [iti vijñāyate] |
ayaṃ ca pitṛmātṛbandhutvena niṣedhe siddhe ‘pi punar niṣedho doṣādhikyapratipādanārthaḥ |
[10]        manuḥ |
pitṛṣvasreyīṃ bhaginīṃ svarīyāṃ mātur eva ca |
mātuś ca bhrātur āptasya gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
etās tisras tu bhāryārthe nopayaccheta buddhimān |
jñātitvenānupeyās tāḥ patati hy upayann imāḥ ||
ity etāsām upayamane manunā prātityakīrtanāt | “mātuś ca bhrātur āptasya” sodarasya duhitaram iti śeṣaḥ | mātulasutāniṣedhasya smṛtyantareṣūkteḥ |
sumantuḥ |
mātṛpitṛsaṃbandhā ā saptamād avivāhyāḥ kanyā bhavanti | ā pañcamād anyeṣām | pitṛpatnyaḥ sarvā mātaraḥ | tadbhrātaro mātulās tadduhitaraś ca bhaginyas tadapatyāni bhāgineyāni | anyathā saṅkarakāriṇyaḥ | svasyādhyāpayitur etad eva |
“ā sapatamād” iti mātṛsapiṇḍaviṣayam | “\
mātuḥ sapiṇḍā yatnena varjanīyā dvijātibhiḥ |
iti vyāsavacanāt | [11] “anyathā” iti | eṣām anantaroktānāṃ kanyāḥ vivāhitāḥ saṅkaradharmā [karma?] rūpādharmakāriṇyo bhavantīty arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
avyaṅgāṅgīṃ saumyanāmnīṃ haṃsavāraṇagāminīm |
tanulomakeśadaśanāṃ mṛdvaṅgīm udvahet striyam ||
“saumyanāmnīṃ” anudvegakaranāmnīm | “tanulokakeśadaśanāṃ” tanu sūkṣmaṃ, lomādi yasyāḥ sā tathā |
śātātapaḥ |
haṃsasvanāṃ meghavarṇāṃ madhupiṅgalalocanām |
tādṛśīṃ varayan kanyāṃ gṛhasthaḥ sukham edhate ||
“meghavarṇāṃ” meghavat snigdhaśyāmavarṇām | “madhupiṅgalalocanāṃ” madhuvad īṣatpiṅgalalocanām | “sukham edhate” sukhaṃ yathā bhavati tathā vardhate |
āśvalāyanaḥ |
buddhirūpaśīlalakṣaṇasampannām arogām upayaccheta |
“lakṣaṇāni” śubhasūcakacihnāni | tāni ca bhaviṣyapurāṇdyuktāni |
[12]        pratiṣṭhitatalāḥ samyak raktāmbhojasamatviṣaḥ ||
pratiṣṭhitatalāḥ samyak” saṃlagnabhūbhāgapādādhara[gra?]talāḥ |
tathā |        karālair atha nirmāṃsai rūkṣair atha śirātataiḥ |
dāridryaṃ durbhagatvaṃ ca prāpnuvanti na saṃśayaḥ ||
karālo bhayānakaḥ |
tathā |
aṅgulyaḥ saṃhatā vṛttā ṛjvyaḥ ślakṣṇanakhās tathā |
kurvanty atyantam aiśvaryaṃ rājabhogaṃ ca yoṣitām ||
“saṃhatāḥ” aviralāḥ | “rājabhogaṃ” rājabhogopasaṃbhogam |
hrasvāsu jīvitaṃ hrasvaṃ viralā vittahānaye |
dāridryaṃ mūlabhugrāsu preṣyatvaṃ pṛthulāsu ca ||
“mūlabhugrāḥ” prathamaparvavakrāḥ | “pṛthulāḥ” sthūlāḥ |
tathā |
parasparasamārūḍhais tanubhir vṛddhaparvabhiḥ |
bahūn api patīn hatvā dāsī bhavati vai dvijāḥ ||
[13]        aṅgulyonnataparvāṇas tuṅgāyāḥ komalānvitāḥ |
ratnakāñcanalābhāya viparītā vipattaye ||
subhagatvaṃ nakhaiḥ snigdhair ātāmraiś ca dhanāḍhyatā |
putrāḥ syur unnatair ebhiḥ susūkṣamaiś cāpi rājatā ||
pāṇḍuraiḥ sphuṭitai rūkṣair nīlair dhūmrais tathā kharaiḥ |
niḥsvatā bhavati strīṇāṃ pītaiś cābhakṣyabhakṣaṇam ||
śuklāḥ snigdhāś ca vṛttāś ca samārūḍhaśirās tathā |
yadi syun pupuraṃ (?) dadhyur bāndhavādyaiḥ samāpnuyuḥ ||
aśirāḥ śarakāṇḍābhāḥ suvṛttālpatanūruhāḥ |
jaṅghāḥ kurvanti saubhāgyaṃ yānaṃ ca gajavājibhiḥ ||
kliśyate romajaṅghā strī bhramaty unnatapiṇḍikā |
kākajaṅghā patiṃ hanti vācāṭā kapilā ca yā ||
jānubhiś caiva mārjārasiṃhajānvanukāribhiḥ |
[14]        śriyam āpya subhāgyatvaṃ prāpnuvanti sutāṃs tathā ||
ghaṭābhair adhvagā nāryo nirmāsaiḥ kulaṭāḥ striyaḥ |
śirālair api hiṃsrāḥ syur viśliṣṭair dhanavarjitāḥ ||
atyantakuñcitai rūkṣaiḥ sphuṭitāgrair guḍaprabhaiḥ |
anekajais tathā romaiḥ keśaiś cāpi tathāvidhaiḥ ||
atyantapiṅgalā nārī piṣakanyeti niścitā |
saptāhābhyantare jāyā patiṃ hanyān na saṃśayaḥ ||
hastihastanibhair vṛttai rambhābhaiḥ karabopamaiḥ |
prāpnuvanty ūrubhiḥ śaśvat striyaḥ sukham anaṅgajam ||
daurbhāgyaṃ ca nirmāsābhir bandhanaṃ romaśorubhiḥ |
tanubhir vadhm ity āhuḥ madhyacchidrais tv anīśatā ||
madhyāvartaṃ samaṃ cāru sūkṣmaromācitaṃ pṛthu |
jaghanaṃ śasyate strīṇāṃ ratisaukhyakaraṃ dvijāḥ ||
ūrdhvaroma bhage yasyāḥ samaṃ suśliṣṭasaṃsthitam |
[15]        api nīcakulotpannā rājapatnī bhavaty asau ||
aśvatthapatrasadṛśaḥ kūrmapṛṣṭho hy abhūtikaḥ (?) |
śaśibimbanibhaś cāpi tathā ca śakaṭākṛtiḥ ||
bhagaḥ śastatamaḥ strīṇāṃ ratisaubhāgyavardhanaḥ |
tilapuṣpanibho yaś ca yasyāśvakhurasaṃnibhaḥ ||
dvāv apy etau parapreṣyāṃ kurvāte ca daridratām |
ulūkhalanibaiḥ śokaṃ maraṇaṃ vivṛtānanaiḥ ||
nirūpair atinirmāṃsair gajāśvasamaromabhiḥ |
dauḥśīlyaṃ durbhagatvaṃ ca dāridryam abhigacchati ||
kapitthaphalasaṃkāśaḥ pīno balivivarjitaḥ |
sphik praśasto hi nārīṇāṃ nindito hy anyathā dvijāḥ ||
payodharabharān namraḥ prabālītrivalīguruḥ (?) |
madhyaḥ śubhāvahaḥ strīṇāṃ romarājīvibhūṣitaḥ ||
paṇavābhair mṛdaṅgābais tathā madhye yavopamaiḥ |
prāpnuvanti bhayāyāsaṃ dauḥśīlyam api cedṛśaiḥ ||
[16]        suvakram ulbaṇaṃ pṛṣṭham aromaṃ susamāhitam |
nānāprastaraparyaṅkaṃ ratisaukhyakaraṃ param ||
kubjam adroṇikaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ romaśaṃ yadi yoṣitaḥ |
svapnānte ‘pi sukhaṃ tasyā nāsti hanyāt patiṃ ca sā ||
vipulaiḥ sukumāraiś ca kukṣibhiḥ subahuprajāḥ |
maṇḍūkakukṣir yā nārī rājānaṃ sā prasūyate ||
unnataiḥ valibhir vandhyāḥ saṃvṛttaiḥ kulaṭāstriyaḥ |
jārakarmaratā sā syān na prabhutvam avāpnuyāt ||
unnatāvanataiḥ kṣudrāḥ viṣamair viṣamāśayāḥ |
āyuraiśvaryasampannā vanitā hṛdayaiḥ samaiḥ ||
suvṛttam unnataṃ pīnam adūronnatam āyatam |
stanayugmaṃ sadā śastam ato ‘nyad asukhāvaham ||
unnatiḥ prathame garbhe dvayor ekasya bhūyasī |
vāme tu jāyate kanya dakṣiṇe tu bhavet sutaḥ ||
dīrghe tu cūcuke yasyāḥ sā strī dhūrtā ratipriyā |
[17]        āviṣṭe tu punar yasyā dveṣṭi sā puruṣaṃ sadā ||
stanaiḥ sarpaphaṇākāraiḥ śvajihvākṛtibhis tathā |
dāridryam adhigacchanti striyaḥ puruṣaceṣṭitāḥ ||
ārakūṭaghaṭais tuyair bhavantīha tathā dvijāḥ |
samañcamāṃsalaṃ cāru śirāromavivarjitam ||
vakṣo yasyā bhaven nāryā bhogān bhuṅkte tathepsitān |
hiṃsrā bhavati vakreṇa dauḥśīlyaṃ romaśena tu ||
nirmāṃsena tu vaidhavyaṃ vistīrṇe kalahapriyā |
catasro raktagambhīrā rekhāḥ snigdhāḥ kare striyāḥ ||
yadi syuḥ sukham āpnoti vicchinnābhir anīśatā |
rekhā kaniṣṭhikārabhya mūlā yasyāḥ pradeśinīm ||
śatam āyur bhavet tasyā ūnāyā nyūnatā kramāt |
saṃvṛttāḥ samaparvāṇas tīkṣṇāgrāḥ komalatvacaḥ ||
samā hy aṅgulayo yasyāḥ sā nārī bhogavardhinī |
bandhujīvāruṇaiḥ tuṅgair nakhair aiśvaryam āpnuyāt ||
nakhair dhūmravarṇābhaiḥ śvetapītair anīśatā |
raktair mṛdubhir aisvaryaṃ nicchidrāṅgulibhir dvijāḥ ||
[18]        sphuṭair aviralai rūkṣaiḥ kleśaṃ pāṇitalaiḥ kharaiḥ |
sapta rekhā yavā yāsām aṅguṣṭhāṅguliparvasu ||
tāsāṃ hi vipulaṃ saukhyaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ tathākṣayam |
maṇibandho ‘vyvacchinno rekhātrayavibhūṣitaḥ ||
dadhāti ca cirād eva maṇikāñcanamaṇḍitam |
śrīvatsapadmaśaṅkhāṅkagajavājiniveśanaiḥ ||
cakrasvastikavajrāsipūrṇakumbair ivāṅkuśaiḥ |
prāsādacchatramukuṭair hārakeyūrakuṇḍalaiḥ ||
śaṅkhatoraṇaniryūhair hastanyastair nṛpastriyaḥ |
yasyāḥ pāṇitale rekhā rakto srakkuṇḍakuṇḍikāḥ (?) ||
dṛśyante caraṇe yasyā rājapatnī bhavaty asau |
vīthyāpaṇatulāmānaiḥ tathā mudrādihiḥ striyaḥ ||
bhavanti vaṇijāṃ patnyo ratnakāñcanaśālinām |
dātrayoktrayugābandhaphālolūkhalalāṅgalaiḥ ||
[19]        bhavanti dhanadhānyāḍhyāḥ kṛṣīvalajanāṅganāḥ |
anuddhataśirāsandhipīnaṃ romavivarjitam ||
gopucchākṛti nārīṇāṃ bhujayor yugalaṃ śubham |
nigūḍhagranthayo yāsāṃ kūrparā romavarjitāḥ ||
bahavo lilitā yāsāṃ praśastā vṛttakomalāḥ |
unnatāvanataś caiva nātisthūlo na romaśaḥ |
sa skandhas tu sadā strīṇāṃ saubhāgyārogyavardhanaḥ ||
sthūle skandhe vahed bhāraṃ romaśe vyādhitā bhavet |
vakre skandhe bhaved vandhyā kulaṭā connatānate ||
sphuṭaṃ rekhādvayaṃ yasyā grīvāyāṃ caturaṅgulam |
maṇikāñcanamuktāḍhyaṃ sa dadhāti vibhūṣaṇam ||
adhanā strī kṛśagrīvā dīrghagrīvā ca bandhakī |
hrasvagrīvā sthirāpatyā sthūlagrīvā ca duḥkhitā ||
anunnatā samāṃsā ca samā yasyāḥ kṛkāṭikā |
sudīrgham āyus tasyās tu ciraṃ bhartā ca jīvati ||
nirmāṃsā bahumāṃsā ca śirālā romaśā tathā |
kuṭilā vikaṭā caiva vistīrṇā ca na śasyate ||
[20]        na sthūlo na kṛśo ‘tyarthaṃ na vakro na ca lomaśaḥ |
hanur evaṃvidhaḥ śreyān ato ‘nyo na praśasyate ||
caturasramukhī dhūrtā maṇḍalāsyā samaṃ bhavet |
aprajā vājivaktrā strī mahāvakrā ca durbhagā ||
śvavarāhavṛkolūkamarkaṭāsyāś ca yāḥ striyaḥ |
krūrās tāḥ pāpakarmiṇyaḥ prajābandhuvivarjitāḥ ||
mālatīvakulāmbhojanīlotpalasugandhi yat |
vadanaṃ mucyate naiṣāṃ na ca tāmbūlabhojanaiḥ ||
tāmrābhaḥ kiṃcid ānamraḥ sthaulyakārśyavivarjitaḥ |
adharo yadi tuṅgaś ca nārīṇāṃ bhogadaṃ sadā ||
sthūle kalahaśīlā syād vivarṇe cātiduḥkhitā |
uttaroṣṭhena tīkṣṇena vanitātyantakopanā ||
jihvā tanutarāvakrā tāmrā dīrghā ca śasyate |
sthūlā hrasvā vivarṇāntā vakrā bhinnā ca ninditā ||
śaṅkhakundendudhavlaiḥ snigdhais tuṅgair asandhibhiḥ |
miṣṭānnapānam āpnoti dantair ebhir anunnataiḥ ||
[21]        sūkṣmair atikṛśair hrasvaiḥ sphuṭitair viralais tathā |
rūkṣaiś ca duḥkhitā nityaṃ vikaṭair vimatir bhavet ||
sumṛṣṭadarpaṇāmbhojapūrṇabimbendusaprabham |
vadanaṃ varanārīṇām adṛṣṭaphaladaṃ śubham ||
sthūlā kṛśā nātivakrā nātidīrghā samunnatā |
tādṛśī nāsikā yasyāḥ sā dhanyā tu śubhaṃkarī ||
unnatā mṛdutomāṇo rekhāḥ śuddhā na saṃgatāḥ |
krikārdhatulyāḥ sūkṣmāś ca yoṣitāṃ tu sukhāvahāḥ ||
dhanustulyābhiḥ saubhāgyaṃ vandhyā syād dīrgharomabhiḥ |
piṅgalā saṃgatā hrasvā dāridryāya na saṃśayaḥ ||
nīlotpaladalaprakhyair tāmraiś cārupakṣmabhiḥ |
vanitā nayanair ebhir bhogasaubhāgyabhāginīṃ ||
śaśakākṣī mṛgākṣī ca varāhākṣī varāṅganā |
yatra tatra samutpannā mahāntaṃ bhogam āpnuyāt ||
agambhīrair asaṃkṣiptair bahurekhāsamanvitaiḥ |
[22]        rājapatnyo bhavantīha nayanair madhupiṅgalaiḥ ||
vāyasākṛtinetrāṇi dīrghāpāṅgani yoṣitām |
anāvilāni cārūṇi bhavanti na [hi?] vibhūtaye ||
gambhīraiḥ piṅgalaiś caiva duḥkhitāḥ syuś cirāyuṣaḥ |
vayomadhye tyajet prāṇān unnatākṣī ca yāṅganā ||
raktākṣī viṣamākṣī ca dhūmrākṣī pretalocanā |
varjanīyā sadā nārī śvanetrā caiva dūrataḥ ||
udbhrāntakairavaiś citrair nayanair aṅganās tv iha |
madyamāṃsapriyā nityaṃ capalāś caiva sarvataḥ ||
lakārākṛtayaḥ karṇā nataskandhāgrasaṃsthitāḥ |
vahanti vikasatkānti hemaratnavibhūṣaṇam ||
kharoṣṭranakulolūkakapotaśravaṇāḥ striyaḥ |
prāpnuvanti mahāduḥkhaṃ prāyaśaḥ pravrajanti vai ||
īṣadāpāṇḍugaṇḍā yāḥ suvṛttāḥ pārvaṇatviṣaḥ |
praśastā ninditās tv alpā romakūpakabhūṣiāḥ ||
ardhrenduprathanābhogaṃ romaśaṃ susamāhitam |
[23]        bhogasaukhyakaraṃ śreṣṭhaṃ lalāṭaṃ varayoṣitām ||
dviguṇaṃ pariṇāhena lalāṭaṃ triguṇaṃ ca yat |
śiraḥ praśastaṃ nārīṇām avandhyā hastimastakāḥ ||
sūkṣmāḥ kṛṣṇā mṛdusnigdhāḥ kuñcitāgrāḥ śiroruhāḥ |
bhavanti śreyase strīṇām anye syuḥ kleśaśokadāḥ ||
haṃsakokilavīṇāliśikhiveṇusvarāḥ striyaḥ |
prāpnuvanti bahūn bhogān bhṛtyān ājñāpayanti ca ||
bhinnakāṃsyasvanā nārī kharakākasvarā ca yā |
rogavyādhibhayaṃ śokaṃ dāridryaṃ cādhigacchati ||
haṃsagovṛṣacakrāhvamattamātaṅgagāminī |
svargalabhyā bhaven nārī mahiṣī pārthivasya ca ||
śvaśṛgālagatir nindyā yā ca vāyasavad vrajet |
dāsī mṛgagatir nārī durbhagā syāc ca bandhakī ||
phalinī rocanā hemakuṅkumatviṣa eva ca |
varṇāḥ śubhakarāḥ strīṇāṃ yaś ca dūrvāṅkuropamaḥ ||
mṛdūni mṛduromāṇi nātyantaṃ svedajāni ca |
surabhīṇi ca gātrāṇi yāsāṃ tāḥ pūjitāḥ striyaḥ ||
[24]        āśvalāyanaḥ |
durvijñeyāni lakṣaṇāny aṣṭau piṇḍān kṛtvā “ṛtam agnau prathamaṃ yajna ṛte satyaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ yad iyaṃ kumāry abhijātā tad idam iha pratipadyatām | yat satyaṃ tad dṛśyatām” iti piṇḍān abhimantrya kumārīṃ brūyād eṣām ekaṃ gṛhāṇeti |
kṣetrāc ced ubhayataḥ sasyād gṛhṇīyād annavaty asyāḥ prajā bhaviṣyatīti vidyāt | goṣṭhāt paśumatī, vedipurīṣād brahmavādinī | avināśino hradāt sarvasaṃpannā, devanāt kitavī, catuṣpathād vipravrājinī, īriṇād anapatyā, śmaśānāt patighnī |
aṣṭapiṇḍakaraṇaṃ ca vakṣyamāṇa kṣetrādimṛttikābhiḥ | “goṣṭhaṃ” gavām avasthānadeśaḥ | “vedapurīṣaṃ” vedimṛttikā | “devanaṃ” dīvyate ‘sminn iti dyūtasthānam | “vipravrājinī” bhartāraṃ vihāya parivrajanaśīlā | "īriṇaṃ" ūṣaram |
[25]        manuḥ |
manānty api samṛddhāni go’jāvidhanadhānyataḥ |
strīsaṃbandhe daśaitāni kulāni parivarjayet ||
hīnakriyaṃ niṣpuruṣaṃ niśchando lomaśārśasam |
kṣayyāmayāvyapasmāriśvitrikuṣṭhikulāni ca ||
“mahānti” bahujanyāni | “smṛddhāni” saṃpannāni vakṣyamāṇair gavādibhiḥ | “strīsaṃbandhe” kartavya iti śeṣaḥ | “hīnakriyaṃ” yathāvihitagarbhādhānādikriyārahitam | “niṣpuruṣaṃ” kanyājanmabahulam | “niśchando” vedābhyāsarahitam | “lomaśaṃ” atyulbaṇalomayuktam | “arśasaṃ” arśo vyādhiyuktam | “śvitri” śvetakuṣṭhayuktam |
hārītaḥ |
śvitrikuṣṭyudariyakṣmāmayāvyapasmāry anārṣeyam abrahmasamānārṣeyaṃ cety etāni apatitāny api kulāni varjanīyāni bhavanti | kulānurūpāḥ prajāḥ saṃbhavantīti | āditaḥ ṣaṭ ayajñiyatvād anārṣeyāṇi | adaivatvād abrāhma[26]ṇakulatvāt samānārṣeyam iti | tasmāt sapta pitṛtaḥ parīkṣya pañca mātṛto nagnikāṃ śreṣṭhāṃ bhrātṛmatīṃ bhāryāṃ vindet |
āmayāvy atra uktetarasaṃcārirogavān | atra śvitryādikulāni ṣaṭ varjanīyānīty atra hetuḥ “kulānurūpāḥ prajā bhavantīti” | itikaraṇaṃ hetau | “anārṣeyaṃ” asmaryamāṇagotrapravaram | tasya varjyatvaṃ “ayajñiyatvāt” | ayajñiyatvaṃ ca yajñeṣu cāsmaryamāṇapravarasyānadhikārāt | “abrahma” avidyamānavedādhyayanam | tadvarjyatve hetuḥ “adaivatvād” iti | daivakarmaśūnyatvāt | “nagnikā” daśavarṣā | “daśavarṣā tu nagnikā” iti bhaviṣyapurāṇavacanāt |
punar hārītaḥ |
[satyaś ca matraivaṃ] tasmāt kulanakṣatravijñānopapannāṃ varayet | pañcamīṃ mātṛbandhubhyaḥ saptamīṃ pitṛbandhubhyaḥ |
yamaḥ |
caturdaśa kulānīmāny avivāhyāni nirdiśet |
anārṣeyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ṛtvijāṃ ca vivarjayet ||
atyuccamatihrasvaṃ ca ativarṇaṃ ca varjayet |
hīnāṅgam atiriktāṅgam āmayāvikulāni ca ||
[27]        śvitrikuṣṭikulādīnāṃ kuryād dhi parivarjanam |
sadā kāmakulaṃ varjyaṃ lomaśānāṃ ca yat kulam ||
apasmārikulaṃ yac ca yac ca pāṇḍukulaṃ bhavet |
“ṛtvijo” atra nindyayājakāḥ | “kāmakulaṃ” kāmapradhānaṃ kulam | “ativarṇam” atikṛṣṇam atigauravaṃ vā |
manuḥ |
nodvahet kapilāṃ kanyāṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ na rogiṇīm |
nālomikāṃ nātilomāṃ na vācālāṃ na piṅgalām ||
narkṣavṛkṣanadīnāmnīṃ nāntyaparvatanāmikām |
na pakṣyahipreṣyanāmnīṃ na ca bhīṣaṇanāmikām ||
yasyās tun a bhaved bhrātā na vijñāyeta vā pitā |
nopayaccheta tāṃ prājñaḥ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā ||
yasyā bhrātā na bhavet tāṃ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā nopayacched ity arthaḥ | putrikādharmaś cāyaṃ yat tat putreṇa kanyāpitā putravān janakaḥ |
[28]  gautamaḥ |
abhisandhimātrāt putrikety ekeṣāṃ tacchaṅkayā nopayacchetābhrātṛkām |
“abhisandhimātrāt” asmād ayam etadīyam apatyam ity abhiprāyāt | mātraśabdena vahniṃ prajāpatiṃ cetyādi gautamoktaputrikāvidhivyavacchedaḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
lokāpavādaduṣṭā yā duṣṭā sā tu prakīrtitā |
anyatrāsaktavittā yā yā syāt saṃspṛṣṭamaithunā ||
“saṃspṛṣṭamaithunā” kṣatayoniḥ |
nāradaḥ |
dīrghakutsitarogārtā vyaṅgā saṃspṛṣṭamaithunā |
dhṛṣṭānyagatabhāvā ca kanyādoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
“dīrghaḥ” cāturthikajvarādiḥ | “kutsitarogo” apasmārādiḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
nātikeśām akeśāṃ vā nātikṛṣṇām apiṅgalām |
nisargato ‘dhikāṅgīṃ ca hīnāṅgīṃ cāpi nodvahet ||
nāviśuddhāṃ sarogāṃ vā kuṭilāṃ vātirogiṇīm |
na duṣṭāṃ duṣṭabhāvāṃ vā vyaṅginīṃ pitṛmātṛtaḥ ||
na śmaśruvyañjanavatīṃ na caiva puruṣākṛtim |
na ghargharasvarāṃ kṣāmāṃ tathā kākasvarāṃ na ca ||
nātibaddhekṣaṇāṃ tadvad vṛttākṣīṃ nodvahet striyam |
yasyāś ca romaśe jaṅghe gulphau yasyās tathonnatau ||
gaṇḍayoḥ kūpakau yasyā hasantyāś caiva jāyate |
nodvahet tādṛśīṃ kanyāṃ prājñaḥ kāryavicakṣaṇaḥ ||
nātirūkṣacchaviṃ pāṇḍukarajām aruṇekṣaṇām |
āpītahastapādāṃ ca na kanyām udvahed budhaḥ ||
[30]        na vāmanāṃ nātidīrghāṃ nodvahet saṃnatabhruvam |
na cāticchidradaśanāṃ na karālamukhīṃ tathā ||
“atikeśāṃ” bahukeśām | “nisargataḥ” svabhāvato ‘dhikāṅgīm | “vyaṅginīṃ pitṛmātṛtaḥ” yasyāḥ pitarau vyaṅgau bhavataḥ | “śmaśrumatī” śmaśrusthāne dīrgharomā | “vyañjanavatī” kucādiyuktā | “kṣāmā” kṛśā | “atibaddhekṣaṇāṃ” nimīlitaprāyanetrām | “vṛttākṣīṃ” vartulākṣīm | “karajaṃ” nakham | “āpīnaṃ” īṣatpītam | “saṃnatabhruvaṃ” saṃlagnabhruvam |
karālamukhīṃ nodvahed ity anuvṛttau brahmapurāṇe |
alomām atilomāṃ ca vācāṭāṃ kekarekṣaṇām |
“kekarekṣaṇāṃ” cakraikanayanām |
kaśyapaḥ |
sapta paunarbhavāḥ kanyā varjanīyāḥ kulādhamāḥ ||
vācā dattā manodattā kṛtakautukamaṅgalā |
udakasparśitā yā ca yā ca pāṇigṛhītikā ||
agniṃ parigatā yā ca punarbhūprasavā ca yā |
ity etāḥ kaśyapenoktā dahanti kulam agnivat ||
“kṛtakautakamaṅgalā” kṛtakaṅkaṇabandhanā | “udakasparśitā” udakapūrvaṃ dattā |
[31]        smṛtiḥ |
vāgdattā manodattā, agniṃ parigatā, saptamaṃ padam ānītā, bhuktā, gṛhītagarbhā, prasūtā, saptavidhā unarbhūs tāṃ gṛhītvā vṛthākārīṃ na prjāṃ na dharmaṃ vindet |
“prajāṃ” svajātidharmām iti śeṣaḥ | dharmaṃ patnīsādhyam agnihotrādi | atra ca prakaraṇe yeṣāṃ vidhiniṣedhānāṃ dṛṣṭaguṇadoṣānuṣaṅgo nāsti, yathā – “asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur” ity evamādīnāṃ tadatikrame bhāryātvam eva na bhavati | bhāryāśabdasya tu āhavanīyādiśabdavat saṃskāraśabdatvāt | yeṣāṃ tu dṛṣṭaguṇadoṣadarśanena vidhineṣedhau, yathā –
sphītād api na saṃcārirogadoṣasamanvitāt |
ityādi na tadatikrame bharyātvābhāvaḥ, kevalaṃ niyamātikramāt prāyaścittamātraṃ bhavati | ṛkṣavṛkṣanāmnyādiṣu tu dṛṣṭaguṇadoṣānuvidhāyividhiniṣedhaprakaraṇamadhyapātāt | manunā sapiṇḍādi prati niṣedhasya ca pṛthagvidhānād adṛṣṭa eva doṣaḥ kalpyaḥ | parityāgaś ca yathā sagotrāyāḥ smaryate – “sagotrāṃ ced amatyopyacchet, mātṛvad enāṃ bibhṛtyād” iti baudhāyanavacanāt | na tathā kapilānadīnāmnyādeḥ | evaṃ vare ‘pi parivettṛnapuṃsakādāv ayam eva nyāyaḥ samānaḥ |
[32]
atha varanirūpaṇam
nāradaḥ |
unmattaḥ patitaḥ klībo durbhagas tyaktabāndhavaḥ |
kanyādoṣau ca yau pūrvāv eṣa doṣaguṇo vare ||
“durbhagaḥ” strīdveṣyaḥ | “tyaktabāndhavaḥ” aduṣṭān bandhūn vihāya svatantravyavahārī | “kanyādoṣau ca yau pūrvau” iti,
dīrghakutsitarogārtā vyaṅgā saṃspṛṣṭamaithunā |
dhṛṣṭānyagatabhāvā ca kanyādoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
it yatra śloke uktau “pūrvau” prathamau dīrghakutsitarogatvavyaṅgatvarūpau nāradoktāv eva |
kātyāyanaḥ |
niṣkrāntas tena vidviṣṭas tathā tyaktaḥ svabandhubhiḥ |
anyajātiḥ kṣayī dāso liṅgasthaś codarī yadi ||
unmattaḥ patitaḥ kuṣṭhī tathā ṣaṇḍhaḥ sagotrajaḥ |
cakṣuś śrotravihīnaś ca tathāpasmāradūṣitaḥ ||
[33]        varadoṣāḥ smṛtā hy ete kanyādoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
prāk paścād vā dānaṃ yatra vivarjayet ||
“niṣkānto” deśāntaragamanaśīlaḥ | “dāsaḥ” preṣyaḥ | “liṅgasthaś codarī yadi” udaraṃ bharaṇārthaṃ yady āśramaliṅgadhārī | “prāg” vivāhopakramāt | “paścāt” vāgdānānantaram |
manuḥ |
kāmam ā maraṇāt tiṣṭhed gṛhe kanyartumaty api |
na tv evaināṃ prayaccheta guṇahīnāya karhicit ||
guṇahīnāya na deyety atra tātparyam, rajasvalā tu na dhāryā |
yamaḥ |
dadyād guṇavate kanyāṃ nagnikām eva śaktitaḥ |
na tv eva guṇahīnāya noparundhyād rajasvalām ||
“śaktitaḥ” sāmarthye sati | “nagnikām eva” daśavarṣāṃ dadyāt |
[34]        manuḥ |
utkṛṣṭāyābhirūpāya varāya sadṛśāya ca |
aprāptām api tāṃ tasmai kanyāṃ dadyād yathāvidhi ||
“utkṛṣṭāya” kulaśīlādibhiḥ | “aprāptāṃ” yāvad vayaskā deyatvenoktā tāvad vayo ‘prāptavatīm api |
āśvalāyanaḥ |
kulam agre parīkṣeta [ye] “mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaś ca” iti yathoktaṃ purastād buddhimate kanyāṃ prayacchet |
śātātapaḥ |
varo varayitavyo ‘rthī kulaśīlasamanvitaḥ |
rūpavān paṇḍitaḥ prājño yuvā doṣavivarjitaḥ ||
kanyāguṇādhikāre yājñavalkyaḥ |
etair eva guṇair yuktaḥ savarṇaḥ śrotriyo varaḥ |
yatnāt parīkṣitaḥ puṃstve yuvā dhīmān janapriyaḥ ||
[35]        nāradaḥ |
parīkṣyaḥ puruṣaḥ puṃstve nijair evāṅgalakṣaṇaiḥ |
pumāṃś ced avikalpena sa kanyāṃ labdhum arhati ||
subaddhajaru jānvasthiḥ subaddhāṃsaśirodharaḥ |
sthūlagāḍhas tanūjatvag avilagnagatiś caraḥ ||
reto ‘sya plavate cāpsu chadimūtraṃ ca phenilam |
pumān syāl lakṣaṇair ebhir viparītais tu ṣaṇḍakaḥ ||
caturdaśavidhaḥ śāstre ṣaṇḍo dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
cikitsyaś cācikitsyaś ca teṣām ukto vidhiḥ kramāt ||
nisargaṣaṇḍo baddhaś ca pakṣaṣaṇḍas tathaiva ca |
agniśāpād guroḥ krodhād devakrodhāt tathaiva ca ||
[36]        īrṣyāṣaṇḍaś ca sevyaś ca vātaretā mukhebhagaḥ |
ākṣiptamoghabījau ca śālīno ‘nyāpatis tathā ||
tatrādvāv apratīkārau pakṣākhyo māsam ācaret |
anukramāt trayasyāsya kālaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
īrṣyāṣaṇḍādayo ye ‘nye catvāraḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
tyaktavyās te patitavat kṣatayonyo ‘pi ca striyaḥ ||
ākṣiptamoghabījābhyāṃ kṛte ‘pi patikarmaṇi |
patir anyaḥ smṛto nāryā vatsarārdhaṃ pratīkṣate ||
śālīnasyāpi dhṛṣṭastrīsaṃyogād bhraśyate dhvajaḥ |
taṃ hīnavegavantaṃ strī lālanābhir upācaret ||
anyasyāṃ yo manuṣyaḥ syād amanuṣyaḥ svayoṣiti |
labheta sānyaṃ bhartāram etat kāryaṃ prajāpateḥ ||
[37]        apatyārthaṃ striyaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ strī kṣetraṃ bījino narāḥ |
kṣetraṃ bījavate deyaṃ nābījī kṣetram arhati ||
“subaddhaṃ” suśliṣṭam | “jatruḥ” grīvāskandhayoḥ saṃdhiḥ | “ghāṭā” grīvāyāḥ paścād bhāgaḥ | “nisargaṣaṇḍhaḥ” liṅgavṛṣaṇahīn evotpannaḥ | “baddhaḥ” gālitāṇḍaḥ | “pakṣaṣaṇḍho” yasa pakṣāntaritā ratiśaktiḥ | “guror abhiśāpāt” ekaḥ, “devakrodhāt” devanindājānitāt krodhāt | “īrṣyāṣaṇḍhaḥ” svastrīviṣaye īrṣyayā stambhitaretāḥ | “sevyaḥ” striyā bahuprakāraṃ sevyamāno ‘pi gatariraṃso ‘jātariraṃsaḥ | “vātareto” yasya ratāne liṅgena vātaniḥsaraṇamātram | “mukhebhagaḥ” yaḥ paramukha eva bhagakāryaṃ karoti | “ākṣiptaretāḥ” visargasamaye yasya retaḥ pratyak pravartate | “moghabījaḥ” yasya garbhādhānasamarthaṃ reto na bhavati | “śālīnaḥ” yasya dhṛṣṭastrīsaṃnidhānāt liṅgapātaḥ | “anyāpatiḥ” yasya svabhāryāvyatiriktāyām eva yoṣiti puṃsthvam | “māsam ācaret” masaṃ yāvat pratīkṣeta | etebhyaḥ pūrvajñātebhyo na kanya deyā | yadi vājñānād dadyāt tadā cikitsyeṣu cikitsāṃ yāvat pratīkṣeta, acikisyeṣu tu [tadā dattāpy ācchidya] anyasmai deyeti |
[38]
atha dārānukalpaḥ
tatra manuḥ |
savarāṇgre dvijātīnāṃ praśastā dārakarmaṇi |
kāmatas tu pravṛttānām imāḥ syuḥ kramaśo varāḥ ||
śūdrasya bhāryā śūdraiva sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛtaḥ |
te ca svā caiva rājñas tu tāś ca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ — sajātīyakanyāprāptau satyāṃ saiva praśastā prathamakalpe | tadalābhe ca sakāmasya krameṇa imāḥ kṣatraviṭśūdrakanyā vivāhyāḥ | tatrāpi pūrvapūrvābhāve uttarottarā vivāhyāḥ | niṣkāmas tu yady āśrame ‘samarthaḥ snātakavratair eva varteta |
tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
alābhe kanyāyāḥ snātakavrataṃ caret | api vā kṣatriyāyāṃ putram utpādayet | vaiśyāyāṃ śūdrāyāṃ vety anya iti |
yamaḥ |
bhāryāḥ svānyāś ca sarveṣāṃ dharmaḥ prāthamakalpikaḥ |
[39]        ānupūrvyeṇa bhāryāḥ syuś catasro brāhmaṇasya tu ||
kṣatriyasyānupūrvyeṇa tisro jñeyā varāḥ striyaḥ |
vaiśyasya cāvare dharmye dve bhārye cābravīn manuḥ ||
śūdraḥ svajātiṃ vindeta tāsv apatyaṃ samaṃ bhavet |
samatvaṃ ca putrotpattinimittapaitṛkaṛṇāpākaraṇābhiprāyam |
atra sājātyānantaraṃ nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇasyānulomyena striyo ‘nyās tisra eva tu |
śūdrāyāḥ prātilomyena tathānye patayas trayaḥ ||
dve bhārye kṣatriyasyānye vaiśyasyaikā prakīrtitā |
vaiśyāyā dvau patī jñeyāv eko ‘nyaḥ kṣatriyaḥ patiḥ ||
“anyā” anyajātīyā vaiśyasya ekāneti śeṣaḥ | vaiśyāyā dvau patī jñeyāv anyāv iti śeṣaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
tisro brāhmaṇasya bhāryā varṇānupūrveṇa, dve rājanyasya, [40] ekaikā vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ | śūdrāyām apy eke mantravarjam, tatra na tathā kuryād ato hi dhruvaḥ kulāpakarṣaḥ pretya cāsvargaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
yad ucyate dvijātīnāṃ śūdrād dāropasaṃgrahaḥ |
naitan mama mataṃ yasmāt tatrāsmāj jāyate svayam ||
viṣṇuḥ |
dvijasya bhāryā śūdrā tu dharmārthaṃ na bhavat kvacit |
ratyartham eva sā tasya rāgāndhasya prakīrtitā ||
“ratyartham eva” iti | na tu sahadharmacāriṇī bhavatīty arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor āpady api hi tiṣṭhatoḥ |
kasmiṃścid api vṛttānte śūdrā bhāryopapadyate ||
śrāddhādidharmakāryārtham iti śeṣaḥ |
[41]  manu-viṣṇū |
hīnajātistriyaṃ mohād udvahanto dvijātayaḥ |
kulāny eva vahanty āśu sasantānāni śūdratām ||
manuḥ |
śūdrāvedī pataty atreḥ utathyatanayasya ca |
gautamasya sutotpattyā tadapatyatayā bhṛgoḥ ||
śūdrāṃ śayanam āropya brāhmaṇo yāty adhogatim |
janayitvā sutaṃ tasyāṃ brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate ||
“utathyatanayasya” gautamasya matam ity adhyāhāraḥ sarvatra |
śaṅkhaḥ |
āpady api na kartavyā śūdrā bhāryā dvijena tu |
[42]        yasmāt tasyāṃ prasūtasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate ||
tapasvī yajñaśīlaś ca sarvadharmabhṛtāṃ varaḥ |
dhruvaṃ śūdratvam āpnoti śūdraśrāddhe trayodaśe ||
nīyate tu sapiṇḍatvaṃ yeṣāṃ śūdraḥ kulodbhavaḥ |
sarve śūdratvam āyānti yadi svargajitas tu te ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ kulajasya tathā dhruvam |
śrāddhadvādaśakaṃ kṛtvā śrāddhe prāpte trayodaśe ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇānarho na ca śūdras tathā druvaḥ |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena śūdrāṃ bhāryāṃ vivarjayet ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇo vṛṣalīṃ gatvā tryahaṃ bhavati sūtakī |
athāsyāṃ garbham ādhatte brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate ||
na brahmahā brahmahā vai brahmahā vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
yas tatra jāyate garbhaḥ sa tena brahmahā bhavet ||
[43]        hārītaḥ |
brāhmaṇo vṛṣalīṃ gatvā sadyo gacched adhogatim |
athāsyāṃ garbham ādhatte pataty āśu na saṃśayaḥ ||
brahmahā na bhavaty anyo brahmahā vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
yas tasyām āhito garbhaḥ sa tena brāhmaṇo hataḥ ||
uśanāḥ |
niṣkṛtiḥ syāt surāpasya hatvā vā brahmavādinam |
vṛṣalyabhiprajātasya niṣkṛtir nopapadyate ||
manu-yama-hārītāḥ |
vṛṣalīpītaphenasya niśvāsopahatasya ca |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasy niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate ||
“pītaphenasya” pītamukhāsavasya |
uśanāḥ patati vṛṣalīpatir ity ācakṣate | na patatīty eke | brāhmaṇakalpavihitāś catasra ānupūrvyeṇa bhāryā bhavantīti vasiṣṭha āha | patati na patatīti saṃśayaḥ | vṛṣalyā patatīti hārītaḥ | sutajananāt patatīti śaunakaḥ | [44] tadapatyaiḥ patatīti gautamaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
adho ‘varṇeṣu prajāyamānaḥ patati na patatīti saṃśayaḥ | na kṣatriyaviśor yajñasaṃyogād vṛṣalyāṃ yaḥ prajāyate sa patati gamanād adhastarāṃ varṇakṣaye sarvān eva janayan na duṣyati |
atrāyaṃ prakaraṇārthaḥ — dvijātīnāṃ sajātīyakanyāyā alābhe uttarottaravijātīyā api krameṇa vivāhyāḥ | tathā adhivedananimitte sati pūrvabhāryāmaraṇe ca sat savarṇāyā alābhe sati uttarottarā vivāhyāḥ |
tad uktaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
kṣatraviṛśūdrakanyās tun a vivāhyā dvijātibhiḥ |
vivāhyā brāhmaṇī paścād vivāhyāḥ kvacid eva tu ||
“kvacid eva” iti kvacid adhivedananimitte dāramaraṇe vā sati | śūdrāyāṃ tv ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ — yadītarāsu prajāyā asaṃbhave prajotpattimātrārthaṃ sāpi vivāhyā | varṇakṣaye vā [45] sarvān eva janayan na duṣyatīti hārītavacanam | sahadharmacāriṇī tu tadāpi sā na bhavatīti, “dharmārthaṃ na bhaved” iti viṣṇuvacanāt | śūdrāvivāhaniṣedhas tu itarāsāṃ lābhe | yas tu śūdrāvivāhe putrapautrajanane ca nindārthavādaḥ so ‘py evaṃvidhavivāhaviṣayaḥ | nindāviśeṣaśravaṇaṃ tu doṣabhūystvapratipādanārtham |
iti dārānukalpaparva
atha kanyāvarayoḥ kramanirṇayaḥ
tatra viṣṇupurāṇe |
varṣair ekaguṇāṃ bhāryām udvahet triguṇaḥ svayam |
manu-yamau |
triṃśadvarṣo vahet kanyāṃ hṛdyāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm |
tryaṣṭavarṣo ‘ṣṭavarṣāṃ vā dharma sīdati satvaraḥ ||
mahābhārate |
triṃśadvarṣaḥ ṣoḍaśavarṣāṃ bhāryāṃ vindeta nagnikām |
ekaviṃśativarṣo vā saptavarṣām avāpnuyāt ||
[46] tad ayam eṣāṃ vacanānām arthaḥ — aṣṭavarṣaparyantā kanya triguṇavayasaiva vivāhitā | upari tu dvādaśavarṣaparyantā nyūnatriguṇavayasā vā | dvādaśavarṣopari ṣoḍaśavarṣaparyantā sārdhadviguṇatriguṇakiṃcinnyūnatriguṇavayasā vivāhitā praśastatarā bhavati | alpavayaskamātreṇa vivāhitāpi patnī bhavati | na tu samyak prajārthaṃ bhavati | dharma ‘pi sīdati sati satvaro ‘pi yavīyasītvamātrādareṇa vivāhaṃ kuryād iti |
atha kanyādātṛnirūpaṇam
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo jananī tathā |
kanyāpradaḥ pūrvanāśe prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo mātāmaho mātā ceti kanyāpradāḥ | pūrvapūrvābhāve prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ | iti |
[47] “prakṛtisthaḥ” pātityonmādādidoṣarahitaḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
svayam evaurasīṃ dadyāt pitābhāve svabāndhavāḥ |
mātāmahas tato ‘nyāṃ hi mātā vā dharmajāṃ sutām ||
“bāndhavāḥ” atra smṛtyantaroktāḥ kanyāpradānādhikāriṇaḥ pitāmahādayo vivakṣitāḥ | “anyāṃ” anaurasīṃ | “dharmajāṃ” niyogotpāditām |
tathā |
dīrghapravāsamukteṣu pogaṇḍeṣu ca bandhuṣu |
mātā tu samaye dadyād aurasīm api kanyakām ||
“pogaṇḍo” vyavahārāsamarthaḥ | “samaye” kanyādānocite kale |
atha kanyāsvayaṃvaraḥ
tatra nāradaḥ |
yadā tu naiva kaścit syāt kanya rājānam āvrajet |
anujñayā tasya varaṃ parīkṣya varayet svayam ||
[48]        savarṇam anurūpaṃ ca kularūpavayaḥśrutaiḥ |
saha dharmaṃ caret tena putrāṃś cotpādayet tataḥ ||
“tataḥ” tasmāt varāt |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
gamyaṃ tv abhāve dātṝṇāṃ kanya kuryāt svayaṃvaram |
“gamyaṃ” kulaśīlādibhir gamanārham |
manuḥ |
trīṇi varṣāṇy upāsīta kumāry ṛtumatī satī |
ūrdhvaṃ tu kālād etasmād vindeta sadṛśaṃ patim ||
adīyamānā bhartāram adhigacchet svayaṃ tathā |
nainaḥ kiṃcid avāpnoti na ca yaṃ sādhigacchati ||
alaṃkāraṃ nādadīta pitṛdattaṃ svayaṃvarā |
mātṛto bhrātṛdattaṃ vā steyaṃ syād yadi taṃ haret ||
pitre na dadyāc chulkaṃ tu kanyām ṛtumatīṃ haran |
sa hi svāmyād atikrāmed ṛtūnāṃ pratirodhanāt ||
[49] “enaḥ” bandhakītvanibandhanam |
baudhāyanaḥ |
trīṇi varṣāṇy ṛtumatī kāṅkṣeta pitṛśāsanam |
tataś caturthe varṣe tu vindeta sadṛśaṃ patim ||
avidyamāne sadṛśe guṇahīnam api śrayet |
viṣṇuḥ |
ṛtutrayam upāsyaiva kanya kuryāt svayaṃvaram |
ṛtutraye vyatīte tu prabhavaty ātmanaḥ sadā ||
piṭdeśmani yā kanya rajaḥ paśyaty asṃskṛtā |
sā kanya vṛṣalī jñeyā haraṃs tāṃ na ca duṣyati ||
gautamaḥ |
trīn kumāry ṛtūn atītya svayaṃ yujyetāninditenotsṛjya pitryān alaṃkārān | pradānaṃ prāg ṛtoḥ aprayacchan doṣī | prāk vāsasaḥ pratipatter ity eke |
[50]        yamaḥ |
kanya dvādaśa varṣāṇi yāpradattā vased gṛhe |
bhrūṇahatyā pitus tasyāḥ sā kanyā varayet svayam ||
evaṃ copanatāṃ patnīṃ nāvamanyet kadācana |
tāṃ kanyāṃ bandhakīṃ vidyān manuḥ svāyambhuvo ‘bravīt ||
idaṃ ca varṣatrayapratīkṣaṇaṃ pitrādīnāṃ kenacit kāraṇena yatnavatām api dānāsampattau, ṛtutrayapratīkṣaṇaṃ yathoktavarasaṃpattāv api pitrādinām upekṣāyām, “tulyaiḥ sakāmām api yācyamānām” ityādivasiṣṭhavacanāt |
tathā |
kanya nartum upekṣeta bāndhavebhyo nivedayet |
na te dadyuś ca tāṃ sarve te syur bhrūṇahabhiḥ samāḥ ||
“bhrūṇo” ‘tra garbhaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
pituḥ pramādāt tu yadīha kanya
[51]                vayaḥpramāṇaṃ samatītya tiṣṭhet |
sā hanti dātāram adīyamānā
kālātiriktā gurudakṣiṇeva ||
“kālātiriktā” atikrāntadānakālā yathā guror dakṣiṇā adīyamānā dātāraṃ hanti pāpotpattinimittaṃ bhavati tathety arthaḥ |
tathā |
prayacchen nagnikāṃ kanyāṃ ṛtukālabhayāt pitā |
ṛtumatyāṃ hi tiṣṭhatyāṃ doṣaḥ pitaram ṛcchati ||
yāvat tu kanyām ṛtavaḥ spṛśanti
tulyaiḥ sakāmām api yācyamānām |
bhrūṇāni tāvanti hatāni tāsāṃ
mātāpitṛbhyām iti dharmavādaḥ ||
“tulyaiḥ” kulādinā |
baudhāyanaḥ |
dadyā guṇavate kanyāṃ nagnikāṃ dharmacāriṇīm |
[52]        api vā guṇahīnāya noparundhyād rajasvalām ||
“guṇahīnāya” kulīnaśīlarūpavattvādibhir guṇair rahitāyāpi tathāpi (?) |
tathā |
trīṇi varṣāṇy ṛtumatīṃ yaḥ kanyāṃ na prayacchati |
sa tulyaṃ brahmahatyāyā doṣam ṛcchaty asaṃśayam ||
“ṛcchati” prāpnoti |
na yācate ced evaṃ syād yācate cet pṛthak pṛthak |
ekaikasmin ṛtau doṣaṃ pātakaṃ manur abravīt ||
ayam arthaḥ — yadi kaścid api kanyāṃ na yācate, tadā varṣatrayeṇa bhrūṇahatyā | atha tulyarūpāya yācamānāya na dadāti, tadā pratyṛtu brahmahatyā | pātakaṃ cātra bhrūṇahatyādoṣa eveti |
atri-kaśyapau |
pitur gehe tu yā kanya rajaḥ paśyed asaṃskṛtā |
bhrūṇahatyā pitus tasyāḥ sā kanya vṛṣalī smṛtā ||
yas tu tāṃ varayet kanyāṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
aśraddheyam apāṅkteyaṃ taṃ vidyād vṛṣalīpatim ||
[53]        paiṭhīnasiḥ |
yāvan nodbhidyataḥ stanau tāvad eva deyā | atha ṛtumatī bhavati dātā pratigrahītā ca narakaṃ prāpnoti | pitā pitāmahaś ceti viṣṭhāyāṃ jayate kṛmiḥ | tasmān nagnikā dātavyā |
aṅgirāḥ |
anavāptarajā gaurī prāpte rajasi rohiṇī |
avyañjanakucā śyāmā kucahīnā tu nagnikā ||
“avyañanakucā” iti | avidyamānau vyañjanakucau yasyāḥ sā tathā | anudbhinnaromastanety arthaḥ |
tathā |
vyañjaneṣūpayukteṣu somo bhuṅkte tu kanyakām |
payodhare tu gandharvo vahnir bhuṅkte rajasvalām ||
tasmād avyañjanopetām arajaskapayodharām |
anupabhuktāṃ somādyair dadyād duhitaraṃ pitā ||
“vyañjanāni” udbhūtavyañjanāni lomāni |
[54]        saṃvartaḥ |
romakāle tu saṃprāpte somo bhuñjīta kanyakām |
rajaḥkāle tu gandharvo vahnir bhuṅkte rajasvalām ||
tasmād udvāhayet kanyāṃ yāvan nartumatī bhavet |
vivāhas tv aṣṭavarṣāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ śasyate budhaiḥ ||
kaśyapaḥ |
saptavarṣā bhaved gaurī daśavarṣā tu kanyakā |
saṃprāpte dvādaśe varṣe kumārīty abhidhīyate ||
bhuñjīta vyañjane somo gandharvās tu payodhare |
valimārgāgame vāyū rajasy agniḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ ||
tasmād avyañjanopetām arajaskapayodharām |
nopabhuktāṃ tu somādyair dadyād duhitaraṃ pitā ||
vyajñaneṣu kulaṃ hanyāt pitṝn hanyat payodhare |
hanyād iṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ca prāpte rajasi kanyakā ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
aprāptarajasā gaurī prāpte rajasi rohiṇī |
avyañjanakucā kanya kucahīnā tu nagnikā ||
[55]        saptavarṣā bhaved gaurī daśavarṣā tu nagnikā |
dvādaśe tu bhavet kanya ata ūrdhvaṃ rajasvalā ||
saptavarṣaiva gaurī ca mukhyā | “anavāptarajā gaurī” ity etad apy etatparam |
vivāhas tv aṣṭavarṣāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ śasyate budhaiḥ |
iti saṃvartavacanam api saptamasyaiva garbhāṣṭamatvāt tatparam eva | “nagnikā tu mukhyā, prāg vāsasaḥ pratipatter” iti gautamavacanāt paridhānānabhijñā | daśavarṣā kucahīnā ca sā vivāhe doṣābhāvapratipādanārthaṃ paribhāṣītā | praśasyate tu pūrvoktaiva |
ato ‘pravṛtte rajasi kanyāṃ dadyāt pitā sakṛt |
mahad enaḥ spṛśed enam anyathaiṣa vidhiḥ satām ||
rajaḥpravṛtteḥ pūrvaṃ deyeti “satāṃ vidhiḥ” dharmaḥ | “anyathā” rajaḥpravṛttau dānād enaṃ dātāraṃ mahat pāpaṃ spṛśed ity arthaḥ |
[56]        manu-kātyāyana-bṛhaspatayaḥ |
sakṛd aṃśo nipatati sakṛt kanya pradīyate |
sakṛd āha dadāmīti trīṇy etāni sakṛt sakṛt ||
yathā “aṃśo” dāyabhāgaḥ “sakṛn nipatati” kriyate, yathā ca gavādidravyam prayacchan dadānīti sakṛd āha, evaṃ kanyādāne ‘pi |
nāradaḥ |
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu pañcasv eṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
guṇāpekṣaṃ bhaved dānam āsurādiṣu ca triṣu ||
ayam arthaḥ — “brāhmādiṣu” sagandharveṣu “eṣa” sakṛddānarupaprakāraḥ | āsurarākṣasapaiśāceṣu tu vivāhitāpy ācchidyānyasmai deyety arthaḥ |
[57]        tathā |
kanyāyāṃ prāptaśulkāyāṃ śreyāṃś ced cara āvrajet |
dharmārthakāmasaṃyuktaṃ vākyaṃ tatrānṛtaṃ bhavet ||
“śulkam” mūlyam, āsuravivāhaviṣayam etat, vivāhatrayopalakṣaṇaṃ caitat śulkasya sarvatrāsaṃbhavāt | “dharmārthakāmasaṃyuktaṃ vākyaṃ” iyaṃ kanya tubhyaṃ mayā dātavyeti | pratiśrava iti pāṭhāntaram | “dharma cārthe ca kāme ca nāticaritavyam” iti evam etat |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
sakṛt pradīyate kanya haraṃs tāṃ cauradaṇḍabhāk |
dattām api haret pūrvaṃ śreyāṃś ced vara āvrajet ||
tathā |
dattāṃ kanyāṃ haran daṇḍyaḥ vyayaṃ dadyāc ca sodayam |
“śreyān” vakṣyamāṇavaradoṣarahitaḥ | “sodayaṃ” salābham |
manuḥ |
na datvā kasyacit kanyāṃ punar dadyād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
[58]        datvā punaḥ prayacchaṃs tu prāpnoti puruṣānṛtam ||
evaṃ ca na pare cakrur nāpare jātu sādhavaḥ |
yad anyasyābhyanujñāya punar anyasya dīyate ||
“puruṣānṛtam” ca puruṣaviṣaye anṛtavacanena yat pāpam |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte |
ity atroktam | “abhyanujñāya” dātuṃ pratijñāya |
kaśyapaḥ |
pitā vā yadi vā mātā bhrātā vā yadi devaraḥ |
kanyānṛtakarā mūḍhā yāṃ gatiṃ yānti tāṃ śṛṇu ||
anivartī yathā śyeno gacchaty eva yathānilaḥ |
evaṃ narakam āyānti kanyānṛtakarā narāḥ ||
“itaro” ‘tra dānādhikārī | “kanyānṛtakarāḥ” kanyādānaviṣaye asatyavādinaḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
anekebhyo ‘pi dattāyām anūḍhāyāṃ tu yatra vai |
varāgamaś ca sarveṣāṃ labhetādivaras tu tām ||
paścād vareṇa yad dattaṃ tasyāḥ pratilabheta yaḥ |
[59]        atha gacched anūḍhāyā dattaṃ pūrvaṃ varo haret ||
anekebhyo dhanagrahaṇapūrvakaṃ dātuṃ pratijñāya tasyām anūḍhāyāṃ yatra bahūnāṃ varāṇām āgamaḥ tatra sarveṣāṃ ādyo yaḥ prathamo varaḥ sa tāṃ kanyāṃ labheta | anye tu varaṃ dattaṃ malayaṃ (?) labherann ity arthaḥ |
nāradaḥ |
pratigṛhya ca yaḥ kanyāṃ naro deśāntaraṃ vrajet |
trīn ṛūn samatikramya sā cānyaṃ varayed varam ||
“pratigṛhya” vāgdattāṃ svīkṛtya |
kātyāyanaḥ |
pradāya śulkaṃ gacched yaḥ kanyāyāḥ strīdhanaṃ tathā |
dhāryā sā varṣam ekaṃ tu deyānyasmai vidhānataḥ ||
atha pravṛttir āgacchet pratīccheta samātrayam |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pradātavyā kanyānyasmai yathecchataḥ ||
pūrvaṃ dāttā tu yā kanya anyenoḍhā yadā bhavet |
[60]        saṃskṛtāpi predeyā syād yasmai pūrvaṃ pratiśrutā ||
anyadattā tu yā kanya vareṇoḍhā yadā bhavet |
kālena vidhinā tāṃ tu saṃskṛtāṃ labhate bhṛguḥ ||
“strīdhanaṃ” mudrikādi | “pravṛttiḥ” kṣemavārttā | idaṃ ca vivāhitāyā api pūrvavarāya punar dānam anantaroktapratīkṣaṇakālād arvāg boddhavyam |
yamaḥ |
vācā dattā tu yā kanya yadi tasyā varo mṛtaḥ |
na ca mantropapannā yā kanyakā pitur eva sā ||
atra mantropapattiḥ pāṇigrahaṇam eva saṃskāraḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
adbhir vācā ca dattāyāṃ mriyetordhvaṃ varo yadi |
[61]        na ca mantropanītā syāt kumārī pitur eva sā ||
manuḥ |
kanyāyāṃ dattaśuklāyāṃ mriyate yadi śukladaḥ |
devarāya pradātavyā yadi kanya tu manyate ||
niyogavidhyanantaraṃ manuḥ |
yasyā mriyeta kanyāyā vācā satye kṛte patiḥ |
tām anena vidhānena nijo vindeta devaraḥ ||
yathāvidhy adhigamyaināṃ śuklavastrāṃ śucivratām |
mitho bhajetāprasavāt sakṛt sakṛd ṛtāv ṛtau ||
“yathāvidhi” yathāgṛhyaśāstram | “śucivratatvaṃ” vāgādiniyamaḥ |
atha nāradaḥ |
pratigṛhya tu yaḥ kanyām aduṣṭām utsṛjen naraḥ |
vineyaḥ so ‘py akāmo ‘pi kanyāṃ tām eva codvahet ||
kanyādoṣe ‘phalaṃ dānaṃ dātuḥ ṣaṇṇavatir damaḥ |
[62]        śulkapradānasya ca tathā kanyāyāś ca nivartanam ||
gūhayitvātmano doṣān arthato dviguṇo ‘tyayaḥ |
varasya dattanāśaś ca bhavet strī ca nivartate ||
“atyayaḥ” daṇḍaḥ | “dattanāśo” dattaśulkasya nāśaḥ |
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
varo doṣam anākhyāya pāṇiṃ gṛhṇāti yo naraḥ |
yācanaṃ vā prakurvīta tad dattaṃ nāpnuyāt sutaḥ ||
kanyādoṣe ‘py ayaṃ dharmo dātā daṇḍyo varas tathā |
dadyād gṛhītaṃ dātā ca na tv evābhiprajātayoḥ ||
“dātā daṇḍyo varas tathā” iti kanyādoṣe ‘nākhyāte dātā daṇḍyaḥ, varadoṣe varo daṇḍya ity arthaḥ | “abhiprajātayoḥ” prastutayoḥ |
sumantuḥ |
kanyā kutsitānyajātikarmaśīlavyādhyupahatā pari[63]ṇītāpi akṣatayoniś cet parityaktavyā | kṣatayonis tu bhartavyā | varo ‘py ebhir eva doṣair yuktaḥ parityaktavyo bhavati |
“kutsitā” lakṣaṇādihīnā | “anyajātiḥ” varāpekṣayā | “karmopahatā” pātakaduṣṭā | “śīlopahatā” duḥśīlā | “vyādhupahatā” kuṣṭādiyuktā |
nārada-kātyāyanau |
nāduṣṭāṃ dūṣayet kanyāṃ nāduṣṭaṃ dūṣayed varam |
doṣe sati na doṣaḥ syād anyonyaṃ tyajatos tayoḥ ||
“doṣe” kuṣṭhādau | “doṣaḥ” pāpam |
manuḥ |
vidhivat pratigṛhyāpi tyajet kanyāṃ vigarhitām |
vyādhitāṃ vipraduṣṭāṃ vā chadmanā copapāditām ||
“vidhivat” udakādipūrvikām | “vigarhitāṃ” durlakṣaṇām | “vipraduṣṭāṃ” anyagatabhāvām | “chadmanā” anyakanyopadarśanapūrvakānyakanyādānādiprakāreṇa |
[64]        tathā |
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ kanyāsv eva pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
nākanyāsu kvacin nṝṇāṃ luptamantrakriyā hi tāḥ ||
unmattā yā na kuṣṭhinyā na ca yā spṛṣṭamaithunā |
pūrvadoṣān avikhyāpya pradātā daṇḍam arhati ||
“pratiṣṭhitāḥ” viniyuktāḥ saṃskārādyaiḥ | “akanyāsu” doṣavatīṣu |
atha strīpuruṣasaṃbandhaḥ
tatra nāradaḥ |
strīpuṃsayos tu saṃbandhād varaṇaṃ prāg vidhīyate |
varaṇād grahaṇaṃ pāṇeḥ saṃbandho hi dvilakṣaṇaḥ ||
“saṃbandhāt” pāṇigrahaṇalakṣaṇāt | “varaṇaṃ” –
suhṛdaḥ samavetān mantravato varān prahiṇuyāt,
ity āpastambagṛhyoktam | “dvilakṣaṇaḥ” dviprakāraḥ |
tathā |
tayor aniyataṃ proktaṃ varaṇaṃ doṣadarśanāt |
[65]        pāṇigrahaṇikā mantra niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam ||
“aniyataṃ” tayoḥ bhāryātvaniyamāpādakam | “doṣadarśanāt” klībādidoṣāṇāṃ kanyāpratyāvṛtteḥ śāstre darśanāt | “pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ” vivāhaprayogāṅgabhūtāḥ “gṛhṇāmi te saubhagatvāya hastam” ityādayaḥ |
manuḥ |
maṅgalārthaṃ svastyayanaṃ yajñaś cāsāṃ prajāpateḥ |
prapūjyante vivāheṣu pradānaṃ svāmyakāraṇāt ||
“svastyayanaṃ” – “svasti bhavanto bruvantu” ityādi, “daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi patim ekādaśaṃ kṛdhi” ityādi ca | “prajāpater yajñaḥ” prajāpatidevatākaṃ karma | “vivāheṣu” vivāhe yat kriyate maṅgalārtham aśubhalakṣaṇadoṣanirāsārtham | “pradānaṃ svāmyakārakam” iti | pradānenaiva kanyāvarayoḥ svasvāmisambandho jāyate | pituś ca svāmyaṃ nivartate |
[66] atra manu-kātyāyanau |
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantra niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam |
teṣāṃ niṣṭhā tu vijñeyā vidvadbhiḥ saptame pade ||
“niṣṭhā” parisamāptiḥ | samāptiś cātra bhāryātvaniṣpādakatvarūpā | “saptame pade” saptamaṃ padaṃ prāptavatyāṃ kanyāyām ity arthaḥ |
atra yamaḥ |
nodakena nav ā vācā kanyāyāḥ patir ucyate |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārāt patitvaṃ saptame pade ||
“udakena” udakapūrvadānena | “vācā” vāgdānena |
hārītaḥ |
āsanābhivādanotthānārghyātithyāni dharmasādhanāni sādhūnām eteṣāṃ pradānaṃ kanyāyā atikramo na vidyate, anyatra yāthātathyāt | klībapatitaproṣitapravrajita-pramīteṣu mīmāṃsante nodakena na vāceti prajāpatiḥ | snātvā cet pratigṛhṇīyāt tasya dadyād ity eke manyante, tatrākṛtsnavidhānāt saṃkhyāyogāt | pāṇigrahaṇamantrair apare | tad apy ādyānām adhovarṇeṣu triṣu śarapratodayaṣṭiśāntagrahaṇāt [67] siddham | tatrāpi na pāṇigrahaṇena jāyātvaṃ, kṛtsnaṃ hi jānīyāt saptame pade jāyāpatitvam |
“āsana”ityasyāyam arthaḥ — kanyāvaraṇārtham āgatānām arghyādipradāne | “kanyāyā atikramaḥ” kanyātvasya nivṛttiḥ, varāntaradānānarhatvaṃ na bhavati | “anyatra yāthātathyād” iti | yāthātathyāt kanyādānaviṣayasatyavacanād anyatra | anyatra klībādiṣu satyavacane ‘pi kṛte atikramo nāstīty uktam “klība”ityādinā | eteṣu ca udakapūrvadāne, vāgdāne ca kanyātvanivṛttiḥ | yat tu “adbhir eva dvijāgrāṇām” ityādi, tad dānopakramamātram iti prajāpater matam | anye tu vidhipūrvakapratigraheṇa dānaniṣpattir iti manyata ityāha “snātvā”ityādi | tad api dūṣayat – “tatra” ityādi | “akṛtsnavidhānāt” vidher asaṃpūrṇatvāt | tatra hetuḥ – “saṃkhyāyogād” iti | sa cokto nāradena [12.2]:
varaṇād grahaṇaṃ pāṇeḥ sa vivāho dvilakṣaṇaḥ | iti |
evam apy akṛtsna ity āha “pāṇigrahaṇa”ityādi |
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantra niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam |
iti manau [8.227] niyataśabdaprayogāt pāṇigrahaṇānantaraṃ vivāhasaṃpattir ity apare | “tad apy ādyānām” ityādi | ādyānāṃ brāhmaṇānām, “adhovarṇeṣu” kṣatriyādiṣu yadi pratodaḥ | eteṣu pāṇigrahanābhāve śaṅkā na kāryā | pāṇigrahaṇaṃ kāryam, śarādigrahaṇasya vidhānāt siddhaṃ pāṇigrahaṇam | evam api jāyāpatitvaṃ na saṃpūrṇam, yathā saṃpūrṇaṃ bhavati tad idānīm āha – “kṛtsnaṃ hi” ityādi | etena vareṇa kanyāyāḥ saptamapadaparyantanayanaṃ mantravat pāṇyādigrahaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ dvijāīnāṃ [68] pradhānaṃ vivāhakarma | eta deva ca jāyātvāpādakam | śūdrasya etad evāmantrakam |
manuḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāraḥ savarṇāsūpadiśyate |
asavarṇāsv ayaṃ jñeyo vidhir udvāhakarmaṇi ||
śaraḥ kṣatriyayā grāhyaḥ pratodo vaiśyakanyayā |
vasanasya daśā grāhyā śūdrayotkṛṣṭavedane ||
yamaḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārah savarṇāsu vidhīyate |
vivarṇāsu yathā dṛṣṭaṃ tathā vakṣyāmy anukramam ||
vaiśyā pratodaṃ gṛhṇāti gṛhṇāti kṣatriyā śaram |
daśāṃ gṛhṇāti vai śūdrā karān vai varavarṇinī ||
[“daśāṃ” vastraprāntaḥ | “karārthe” pāṇigrahaṇakārye |]
agniparikramo brāhmaṇasyoktaḥ | rājanyavaiśyayor ācāryaparikrama iti | “parikarmaḥ” pradakṣiṇīkaraṇam |
[manuḥ] |
adbhir eva dvijāgryāṇāṃ kanyādānaṃ praśasyate |
[69]        itareṣāṃ tu varṇānām itaretarakāmyayā ||
“itaretarakāmyayā” kanyāvarayoḥ parasparānurāgeṇa |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
sāṅguṣṭhaṃ brāhmaṇyāḥ pāṇiṃ gṛhṇīyāt | kṣatriyāyāḥ saśaram | sapratodaṃ vaiśyāyāḥ | śūdrāyā [vastradaśām] |
bahvṛcagṛhyapariśiṣṭam |
kanyāṃ varayamāṇānām eṣa dharmo vidhīyate |
pratyaṅmukhā varayanti pratigṛhṇanti prāṅmukhāḥ ||
ubhayasyāpi pakṣasya pitāputrīm anukramet |
kanyāṃ dadati ye tatra pitā putrī tathaiva ca ||
“ubhayasyāpi” pakṣasya mātṛpakṣasya pitṛpakṣasya ca | “anukramet” tṛtīyapuruṣād ārabhya krameṇa kīrtayet | “kanyāṃ dadati” iti | na kevalaṃ yaḥ kanyāṃ prārthayitā sa ubhayapakṣasya pitāputrīm anukramet, kiṃ tu ye ‘pi kanyāṃ dadati kanyādātāras te ‘pi  tathaiva ubhayapakṣasya pitāputrīm anukramerann ity arthaḥ |
[70]
2
atha vivāhabhedāḥ
tatra manuḥ |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ pretya ceha hitāhitān |
aṣṭāv imān samāsena strīvivāhān nibodhata ||
“strīvivāhān” iti striyāḥ saṃskāryatvābhidhānām |
nāradaḥ |
aṣṭau vivāhā varṇānāṃ saṃskārārthaṃ prakīrtitāḥ |
brāhmas tu prathamas teṣāṃ prājāpatyas tathaiva ca ||
ārṣaś caivātha daivaś ca gāndharvaś cāsuras tathā |
rākṣaso ‘nantaras tasmāt paiśācaś cāṣṭamo ‘dhamaḥ ||
eṣāṃ tu dharmyāś catvāro brāhmādyāḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
sādhāraṇaḥ syād gāndharvas trayo dharmyās tathāpare ||
[71] “sādhāraṇaḥ” notkṛṣṭo nāpakṛṣṭaḥ |
nāradakrameṇa vivāhānantaraṃ gautamaḥ |
catvāro dharmyāḥ prathamāḥ, ṣaḍ ity eke |
hārītaḥ |
athāṣṭau vivāhāḥ sambhavanti | brāhmo daivo gāndharva āsuro rākṣasaḥ paiśāco mānuṣaḥ kṣātraś ceti | teṣāṃ pūrve saptobhayataḥ | teṣāṃ vindetālābhe mānuṣam |
“teṣāṃ pūrve” iti | teṣāṃ madhye pūrve ādyās trayo vivāhāḥ | “ubhayato” mātṛpitṛvaṃśajān sapt sapta tārayanti iti śeṣaḥ | “teṣāṃ” trayāṇām | “alābhe mānuṣaṃ” labheta |
punar hārītaḥ |
eteṣām ekatamenoḍhāṃ dharmapatnīṃ prāhuḥ |
atra ca yady api sarvavarṇānāṃ sarvavivāhānarhatā, tathāpi yasya yo viśeṣeṇa vihitas tena tasya dharmapatnī bhavati |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ṣaḍ vivāhāḥ – brahmo daiva ārṣo gāndharvaḥ kṣātro mānuṣaś ceti |
brāhmārṣadaivagāndhavāsurarākṣasavivāhān uktvā āpastambaḥ |
[72]        teṣāṃ trayaḥ ādyāḥ praśastāḥ pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān |
manur yamaś ca ādye |
brāhmo daivas tathaivārṣaḥ prājāpatyas tathāsuraḥ |
gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva paiśācaś cāṣṭamo ‘dhamaḥ ||
yo yasya dharma varṇasya guṇadoṣau ca yasya yau |
tad vaḥ sarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi prasave ca guṇāguṇān ||
ṣaḍ ānupūrvyā viprasya kṣatasya caturo ‘varān |
viṭśūdrayos tu tān eva vidyād dharmyān (?) arākṣasān ||
caturo brāhmaṇasyādyān praśastān kavayo viduḥ |
rākṣasaṃ kṣatriyasyaikam āsuraṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ ||
pañcānāṃ tu trayo dharmyā dvāv adharmye (?) smṛtāv iha |
paiśācaś cāsuraś caiva na kartavyau kadācana ||
pṛthak pṛthag vā miśrau vā vivāhau pūrvacoditau |
gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva dharmyau kṣatrasya tau smṛtau ||
[73] “prasave” apatye | “guṇāguṇān” guṇadoṣān | “ānupūrvyā” uddiṣṭakrameṇa | “praśastān” viśiṣṭādṛṣṭajanakān | “avarān” āsuragāndharvapaiśācān adhastanān | “pṛthak pṛthak” ekaikaśaḥ | “miśrau” ubhayalakṣaṇasaṃkīrṇau | yathā kanyāvarayor anyonyānurāge ca saty eva pitrā kanyāyām adīyamānāyāṃ prasahya haraṇena vivāho gāndharvarākṣasayoḥ saṃkīrṇatvam |
patiṭhīnasiḥ |
brāhmaprājāpatyārṣadaivā brahmaṇasya, gāndharva āsuro rājanyasya, rākṣaso vaiśyasya, paiśācaḥ śūdrasya, sarveṣām ārṣe pramāṇam |
“ārṣaṃ” devavākyam |
nāradakrameṇa vivāhābhidhānānantaraṃ devalaḥ |
etān saśulkān sāmānyān vivāhāṃś caturo viduḥ |
kevalaṃ kṣatriyasyaiva praśastatvāc ca rākṣasaḥ ||
[74] tathaiva baudhāyanaḥ |
teṣāṃ catvāraḥ pūrve brāhmaṇasya teṣv api pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān | itareṣām uttarottaraḥ pāpīyān | tatrāpi ṣaṣṭhasaptamau kṣatradharmānugatau tatpūrvakatvāt kṣatrasya | pañcamāṣṭamau vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ | ayantritakalatrā hi vaiśyaśūdrā bhavanti | karṣaṇaśuśrūṣādhikṛtatvāt | gāndharvam apy eke praśaṃsanti sarveṣāṃ snehānubhavatvāt | yathāyukto vivāhas tathāyukā prajā bhavatīti vijñāyate |
“catvāraḥ” praśastā iti śeṣaḥ | pūrvapūrvas” tv atiśayena praśasta ity arthaḥ | “itareṣāṃ” gāndharvādīnāṃ uttaro ‘praśastaḥ | “ṣaṣṭhasaptamau” āsurarākṣasau, “kṣatradharmānugatau” kṣatriyadharmānusāriṇau, taddharmaś ca dharmapradānabalasaṃpattirūpaḥ | sa ca pūrvabhūto ‘rathaḥ kṣatriyasyāstīti vivāhadvayasaṃbhavas tasya | “pañcamāṣṭamau” gāndharvapaiśācau vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ | “ayantritakalatrāḥ” asaṅkocitastrīkāḥ | kalatram atra strīmātrāṇām | etena tayoḥ kanya gāndharvapaiśācavivāhayogyā bhavanti | asaṅkocitakalatratvaṃ ca tayoḥ karṣaṇaśuśrūṣādhikṛtatvāt | snehānugatatvāt | sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ “yathāyuktaḥ” yena guṇena doṣeṇa vā saṃbandhaḥ | “tathāyuktā” tadguṇadoṣavatī |
[75]        manuḥ |
aninditaiḥ strīvivāhair anindyā bhavati prajā |
ninditair ninditā nṝṇāṃ tasmān nindyān vivarjayet ||
atreyaṃ vyavasthā nāradoktā — prājāpatyārṣadaivabrāhmāḥ brāhmaṇasya praśastāḥ | gāndharvas tu tasyaiva dharmāvirodhī | āsuras tu yadyapi tasyaiva manunā dharma uktaḥ, tathāpi gāndharvān nyūnaḥ atyantāpadi kāryaḥ,
paiśācaś cāsuraś (?) caiva na kartavyau kathaṃcana |
iti manunaivokteḥ | rākṣasapaiśācau tu brāhmaṇena kadācid api na kartavyau, “ṣaḍ ānypūrvyā viprasya” ity abhidhānāt | yat tu baudhāyanenārṣasya daivāt śreṣṭhatvam uktam, tat sahadharmacāritavmātreṇa prajāyām, tatra manunā daivasya śraiṣṭhyābhidhnāt | hārītoktaṃ tu gāndharvaśreṣṭhatvaṃ sarvān prati svayaṃvaragāndharvābhiprāyam | “svayaṃ kanya varayet sa gāndharvaḥ” iti gāndharvābhidhnāt | yas tatra paiṭhīnasi-hārīta-mahābhāreteṣu rākṣasāsurakṣātrapaiśācaśabdair balād āhṛtakanyāharaṇād vivāhaḥ praśasta [76] ity uktam, sa kṣatriyasyaiva | brāhmadaivau tu brāhmaṇasyaiva netareṣām, mahābhārate brāhmavivāham abhidhāya
brāhmaṇānāṃ satām eṣa prokto dharmo yudhiṣṭhira |
ity abhidhānāt, daivasya ṛtviksaṃpradānakatvāt, kṣatriyādīnāṃ ārtvijyāsaṃbhavāt | paiśācas tu vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ | yat tu vaiśyasya rākṣasaśraiṣṭhyābhidhānaṃ tad āsurābhiprāyam, “śuklena pāṇiṃ tv ādadato rākṣasaḥ” iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt | ārṣapājāpatyagāndharvāṇāṃ tu sarvasādhāraṇyaṃ, aninditatvāt yogyatvāt | saṃkarajātānāṃ tu brāhmadaivavyatiriktās tattadvarṇasaṃbandhenetare vivāhāḥ iti |
atha brāhmalakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
ācchādya cārhyitvā ca śrutaśīlavate svayam |
[77]        āhūya dānaṃ kanyāyā brāhmo dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
“ācchādya” vastrayugaṃ paridhāpya |
devalaḥ |
tato vinītāṃ kalyāṇīṃ kanyāṃ kulavayonvitām |
alaṃkṛtyārhate dadyād vivāho brahma ucyate ||
“kalyāṇīm” uktakanyādoṣarahitām | “arhate” uktavaralakṣaṇayuktāya |
vyāsaḥ |
ācchādyālaṃkṛtāṃ kṛtvā triḥ parikramya pāvakam |
nāmagotre samuddiśya dadyād brāhmo vidhis tv ayam ||
saṃvartaḥ |
alaṃkṛtya tv alaṃkārair varāya sadṛśāya ca |
brāhmeṇa tu vivāhena kanyāṃ dadyāt supūjitām ||
“supūjitāṃ” puṣpacandanādisaṃmānitām |
yamaḥ |
adbhir yā dīyate kanya brahmadeyeti tāṃ viduḥ |
[78] “brahmadeyā” iti saṃjñā śrāddhe | tato jātasya paṅktipāvanatvavyavahārārtham |
hārītaḥ |
anindann anyair avitarkayan vidhivad vastrayugaṃ datvā saha dharmas caryatām iti brāhmaḥ |
“anindan” doṣābhidhānam akurvan | “anyair avitarkayan” anyaiḥ saha doṣam anāśaṅkamānaḥ | “vidhivat” madhuparkādipūrvakam |
āpastambaḥ |
brāhme vivāhe bandhuśīlaśrutārogyāṇi buddhvā prajāṃ sahatvakarmabhyaḥ pratipādayec chaktiviṣayeṇālaṃkṛtya |
“sahatvakarmabhyaḥ” ityādi | sahatvena sahabhāvitveneṣṭā dhānādikarmasv adhikriyate, tādarthyena itthaṃbhūtaṃ pratipādanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
śrutaśīle vijñāya brahmacāriṇe ‘rthine deyeti sa brāhmaḥ |
[79] “brahmacāriṇe” asaṃjātastrīsaṃparkāya | “arthine” icchate |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tatra savarṇaḥ savarṇāya vidito viditāya nagnikāṃ dadyāt sa brāhmaḥ |
“viditaḥ” kulādibhiḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
tasmān nagnikāṃ kanyāṃ sahiraṇyāṃ dadyāt ayaṃ brāhmo vivāha iti |
śreṣṭhavarānuvṛttau brahmapurāṇe |
sadṛśāya kadā kanyāṃ dadadhvaṃ śraddhayānivitāḥ |
alaṃkṛtya yathāśaktyā pūrayitvā dhanena ca ||
“alaṃkṛtya” varam alaṃkṛtya | “dhanena” gohiraṇyādinā | “pūrayitvā yathāśaktyā” ity ubhayatra yojyam |
brāhmānuvṛttau saṃvartaḥ |
sa kanyāyāḥ pradānena śreyo vindati puṣkalam |
[80]        sadbhir ity ucyate sādhuḥ kīrtiṃ cāpnoti puṣkalām ||
“iti”karaṇaḥ sādhuśabdāt paro draṣṭavyaḥ |
tathā |
jyotiṣṭomātirātrāṇāṃ śataṃ śataguṇīkṛtam |
prāpnoti puruṣīṃ datvā homamantrais tu saṃskṛtām ||
“puruṣīṃ” kanyām | atra gṛhyeṣu dānānantaraṃ homavidhānād arthād datvā homamantraiḥ saṃskṛtām ity avadhātavyam |
kaśyapaḥ |
anaḍuhāṃ sahasrāṇāṃ daśānāṃ dhuryavāhinām |
supātre vidhivad dānaṃ kanyādānaṃ ca tatsamam ||
yas tu satyena dharmeṇa pitā sparśayate sutām |
sa pretya labhate sthānaṃ yathā dakṣaḥ prajāpatiḥ ||
“satyena” chadmādirahitena | “sparśayate vareṇa saṃyojayati |
[81]        ādityapurāṇe |
śrutvā kanyāpradānaṃ tu pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ |
muktā vai sarvapāpebhyo brahmalokaṃ vrajanti te ||
devalaḥ |
tisraḥ kanyā vidhivad datvā
[yathānyāyaṃ pālayitvā nivedya ca |
na pitā narakaṃ yāti nārī vā strīprasūtinī ||
“strīprasūtinī” kanyāprasūtinī |
smṛtiḥ |
nāgnihotrādibhis tat syāt brahmato brāhmaṇasya vā |
yat kanyāṃ vidhivad datvā] phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
brāhmānuvṛttau saṃvartaḥ |
tāṃ ca datvā pitā kanyāṃ bhūṣaṇācchādanāsanaiḥ |
pūjayan svargam āpnoti nityam utsavavṛddhiṣu ||
iti brāhmalakṣaṇam
[82]
atha daivalakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
yajñe tu vitate samyak ṛtvije karma kurvate |
alaṃkṛtya sutādānaṃ daivaṃ dharma pracakṣate ||
“samyak” yathārtham askhalitam iti yāvat | karma kurvate ṛtvije |
devalaḥ |
antarvedi samānīya kanyāṃ kanakamaṇḍitām |
ṛtvije tu dadāty eṣa vivāho daivasaṃjñakaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
sa ṛtvijam alaṃkṛtya vivāho daiva ucyate |
hārītaḥ |
vedyante mithune datvā sa daivaḥ |
“vedyante” vedyā ekadeśe gomithunaṃ varāya deyam, svīyam eva varāya datvā na tu varād upāttam ārṣatvaprasaṅgāt |
[83]        baudhāyanaḥ |
dakṣiṇāsu nīyamānāsv antarvedi ṛtvije sa daivaḥ |
dānārthaṃ dakṣiṇāsu nīyamānāsu ṛtvije yad dāna mity arthaḥ |
ādityapurāṇe |
daivena tu vivāhena yas tu kanyāṃ prayacchati |
kṛtvā dvāraṃ tu vipulaṃ bhānusopānam uttamam ||
ādityalokād ūrdhvaṃ tu gamanaṃ pratipadyate ||
athārṣalakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
ekaṃ gomithunaṃ dve vā vadād ādāya dharmataḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ vidhivad ārṣo dharmo ‘yam ucyate ||
“dharmataḥ” ity ānṛśaṃsyārthaṃ na tu śulkabuddhyā | gomithunagrahaṇaṃ ca kanyādātuḥ svayam adhikadānāsamarthasya kanyopakārādhikyam icchato veditavyam |
devalaḥ |
pradānaṃ yat tu kanyāyāḥ saha gomithunena ca |
[84]        śastāya vāsagotrāya tam ārṣam ṛṣayo viduḥ ||
“saha gomithunena” iti | varād upādattena saha gomithunena sahitam ity arthaḥ | “śastāya” yathoktalakṣaṇopetāya |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
gomithunena vastryugalena cārṣaḥ, sarveṣv alaṃkāraḥ strīdhanaṃ ca deyam iti |
“strīdhanam” iti alaṃkārādhikam anyad api kiṃcit |
baudhāyanaḥ |
pūrve lājāhutīr datvā gobhyāṃ ārṣaḥ |
pūrve catasṛṇāṃ madhye dve ādye | “gobhyām” iti | gomithunena saha deyety anuṣaṅgaḥ |
atha prājāpatyalakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
sahobhau caratāṃ dharmam iti vācānubhāṣya ca |
[85]        kanyāpradānam abhyarcya prājāpatyo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
kanyādāne saha anyonyāvyabhicāreṇa ubhau caratāṃ dhamma ity anena vākyena samayaṃ kṛtvā yaḥ kanyādānaṃ sa prājāpatyaḥ |
devalaḥ |
sahadharmakriyāhetor dānaṃ samayabandhanāt |
alaṃkṛtyaiva kanyāyā vivāhaḥ sa prajāpateḥ ||
“samayabandhanaṃ” sahobhau caratām iti saṅketakaraṇam |
gautamaḥ |
saṃyogamantraḥ prājāpatye “sahadharmaś caryatām” iti |
viṣṇuḥ |
prārthitapradānaṃ prājāpatyaḥ |
prārthitāya pradānaṃ prārthitapradānam |
[86]
athāsuralakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ datvā kanyayai caiva śaktitaḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ svācchandyād āsuro dharma ucyate ||
“āpradānam” ādānaṃ grahaṇam iti yāvat | “svācchandyāt” svecchayā, na tu kanyāpituḥ pāratantryeṇa |
devalaḥ |
śulkaṃ pradāya kanyāyāḥ supradānaṃ vidhānataḥ |
vittahetur vivāho ‘yam āsuraḥ ṣaṣṭha ucyate ||
vittaṃ hetur yasya sa tathā|
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
śulkena paṇitvā dadata āsuraḥ |
“paṇitvā” vikriña |
hārītaḥ |
dambhachadmabhyāṃ parais tarkitāya yad dīyate pratipadya vā sa āsuraḥ |
[87] “paraiḥ” anyaiḥ | “dambhadhadmabhyāṃ” dāmbikatvena chādmikatvena vānyaiḥ jñātāya varāya svayam evādāmbhikatvaṃ chādmikatvaṃ ca pratipādya jñātvā yatra dānaṃ sa āsuraḥ |
punar hārītaḥ |
śulkena mānuṣaḥ |
idaṃ cāsurasyaiva saṃjñāntaram |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
paṇitvā dhanakrītī sa mānuṣaḥ tasmād duhitṛmate ‘dhirathaṃ śataṃ deyam iti ha krayo vijñāyate |
“dhanakrītī” it yatra bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | “atirathaṃ” rathādhikaṃ gośataṃ deyam ity arthaḥ | “iti ha” śabdena yā parāya krītā saty etā anyaiś caratīti ha cāturmāsyeṣv iha krayapradhānārthaṃ śrutyupanyāsaḥ |
kaśyapaḥ |
krītā dravyeṇa yā nārī na sā patnī vidhīyate |
[88]        na sā daiva na sā pitrye dāsīṃ tāṃ kaśyapo ‘bravīt ||
āpastambaḥ |
dānaṃ vikrayadharmaś cāpatyasya na vidyate |
manuḥ |
na kanyāyāḥ pitā vidvān gṛhṇīyāc chuklam aṇv api |
gṛhṇan hi śulkaṃ lobhena syān naro ‘patyavikrayī ||
ādadīta na śūdro ‘pi śulkaṃ duhitaraṃ dadan |
śulkaṃ hi gṛhṇan kurute channaṃ duhitṛvikrayam ||
yamaḥ |
nānuśuśruma jātv etat pūrveṣv api hi janmasu |
śulkasaṃjñena mūlyena pāpaṃ duhitṛvikrayam ||
[89]        ārṣe gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ kecid āhur mṛṣaiva tat |
svalpo ‘py evaṃvidho vāpi tāvān eva sa vikrayaḥ ||
strīdhanāni tu ye mohād upajīvanti bāndhavāḥ |
nārīyānāni vastraṃ vā te pāpā yānty adhogatim ||
ārṣāsuryor dhanagrahaṇam uktam | tatra cāyaṃ niṣedhaḥ śrūyate — na ca dhanagrahaṇaṃ vinā ārṣāsurayos saṃbhavaḥ | tatreyaṃ vyavasthā — ātmārthaṃ dhanagrahaṇe doṣaḥ | kanyārthaṃ tu dhanagrahaṇe vikrayadoṣaḥ | “strīdhanāni” it prasaṅgād abhidhānam | “upajīvanti” upabhuñjate | etena kanyārhaṇārthadattadhanopajīvinām api tadbāndhavānāṃ doṣabhāgitā, tasmād gṛhītaṃ svayaṃ nopajīvyam |
kaśyapaḥ |
śulkena ye prayacchanti svasutāṃ lobhamohitāḥ |
ātmavikrayiṇaḥ pāpā mahākilibiṣakārakāḥ ||
patanti narake ghore ghnanti cāsaptamaṃ ulam |
[90]        gamanāgamane caiva sarvaśulko vidhīyate |
“gamanāgamane” kanyāprārthanārthaṃ kanyāpitur gamanāgamane |
manuḥ |
yāsāṃ nādadate śulkaṃ jñātayo na sa vikrayaḥ |
arhaṇaṃ tat kumārīṇām ānṛśaṃsyaṃ ca kevalam ||
“ānṛśaṃsyam” anukampā |
āpastambaḥ |
vivāhe duhitṛmate dānaṃ kāmyaṃ dharmārthaṃ śrūyate | tasmād duhitṛmate adhirathaṃ śataṃ deyaṃ tasmin mithunī kuryād iti | tasya krayaśabdaḥ saṃstutimātraṃ dharmād dhi pañcadhā |
ayam arthaḥ — dānaṃ krayaś cāpatyasya na vidyata ity uktam | tat katham ārṣavivāhe gomithunādigrahaṇam ity āśaṅkyāha “vivāhe” ityādi | vivāhe yat kanyāpitre dānaṃ tat kāmyam | nātra ṛṣitulyaputrotpattiviṣayatā, “yathāyukto vivāhas tathāyuktā prajā bhavati” iti vacanāt | “tan mithunīkuryāt” iti | tad dattaṃ rathādikaṃ gośataṃ mithunīkuryād iti | [91] jāyāpatitvam āpādayed ity arthaḥ | mithunaśabdān mitho ‘rthāntarasyāvibhakteḥ chāndaso ‘yādeśaḥ | yas tu tasyām ārṣavivāhoḍhāyāṃ smṛtyantare krayaśabdaḥ, sa śabdamātram, na vāstavas tatra krayaḥ | atra hetuḥ “dharmād dhi saṃbandhaḥ” iti | dharmanimitto hy aśaulka iti |
atha gārdharvalakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
icchayānyonyasaṃyogaḥ kanyāyāś ca varasya ca |
gāndharvaḥ sa tu vijñeyo maithunyaḥ kāmasaṃbhavaḥ ||
“anyonyasaṃyogaḥ” kanyāvarayoḥ samayabandhaḥ | “maithunyaḥ” mithunasya karma maithunyaṃ tasmai hitaḥ |
devalaḥ |
vivikte svayam anyonyaṃ strīpuṃsor yaḥ samāgamaḥ |
prītihetuḥ sa gāndharvo vivāhaḥ pañcamo mataḥ ||
gautamaḥ |
alaṃkṛtya iccantyā svayaṃ saṃyogo gāndharvaḥ |
[92] “alaṃkrtya” ātmānam iti śeṣaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ |
dvayoḥ sakāmayor mātṛpitṛdānarahito gāndharvaḥ |
mātṛpitṛnairapekṣyeṇa ātmānaṃ alaṃkṛtyeti śeṣaḥ | “mātṛpitṛdānarahitaḥ” iti svayaṃvaragādharaviṣayam |
hārītaḥ |
svayaṃ kanyā varayet sa gāndharvaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sakāmāṃ kāmayamānaḥ sadṛśīṃ yo nirundhyāt sa gāndharvaḥ |
āśvalāyanaḥ |
mithaḥ samayaḥ kṛtvopayacchet sa gāndharvaḥ |
atha rākṣasalakṣaṇam
tatra manuḥ |
hatvā chittvā ca bhittvā ca krośantīṃ rudatīṃ gṛhāt |
[93]        prasahya kanyāṃ harato rākṣaso vidhir ucyate ||
“hatvā” tāḍayitvā | kanyāsaṃbandhina iti śeṣaḥ | “krośantīṃ” bhayāt svajanam āhvayantīm |
devalaḥ |
vikrameṇa prasahya syāt kumārīharaṇaṃ punaḥ |
vīryahetur vivāhaḥ sa rākṣasaḥ saptamo mataḥ ||
“prasahya” abhibhūya |
āśvalāyanaḥ |
hatvā bhittvā ca śīrṣāṇi rudantīṃ rudadbhyo haret sa rākṣasaḥ |
“rudadbhyaḥ” kanyāsaṃbandhibhya iti śeṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
rājāśrayeṇa vadhadaṇḍābhighātabhayaviśeṣād rākṣasaḥ |
rājānam āśritya yad vadhadaṇḍādibhayam utpādya kanyāharaṇaṃ sa rākṣasa ity arthaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yasmin balena haranti sa kṣātraḥ |
[94] hārītaḥ |
alaṃkṛtām abhijayan kṣātraḥ |
vivāhārtham alaṃkṛtāṃ kanyām uddiśya yuddhe vijayamānasya kanyāprāptiḥ sa kṣātra ity arthaḥ |
atha paiśācalakṣaṇam
tatra manu |
suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā raho yatropagacchati |
sa pāpiṣṭho vivāhānāṃ paiśācaḥ prathito ‘ṣṭamaḥ ||
“rahaḥ” ekānte | “upagacchati” āliṅganādi karoti na tu upabhuṅkta eva | kṣatayonitve pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārābhāvaprasaṅgāt |
devalaḥ |
suptān mattāc ca kanyāyāḥ harati vyasanād api |
pramādahetuḥ paiśācaḥ vivāho ‘ṣṭama ucyate ||
[95] “suptāt” svapnāt | “mattāt” madyapānena mattatvāt | “vyasanāt” rājādyupadravāt | [kanyāvatām iti śeṣaḥ | “suptānāṃ pramattānāṃ paiśāca” ity āśvalāyanavacanāt] |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
paiśācaḥ kanyakācchalāt |
“kanyakācchalāt” kanyāpratāraṇāt |
hārītaḥ |
strīpānamadyabogasaṃprayogāt paiśācaḥ |
kanyāpitrādīnāṃ striyāṃ pāne madye bhoge ca gītanṛtyādau saṃprayogād āsakteḥ |
atha vasiṣṭha-baudhāyanau |
balāc cet prahṛtā kanyā mantrair yadi na saṃskṛtā |
anyasmai vidhivad deyā yathā kanyā tathaiva sā ||
etac ca mantrasaṃskārāt pūrvaṃ kanyāpratyāharaṇam, kulaśīlādivarāntaraprāptau boddhavyam |
[96]        yamaḥ |
varaś cet kulaśīlābhyāṃ na yujyeta kathaṃcana |
na mantrāḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na ca kanyānṛtaṃ bhavet ||
samākṣipya hṛtāṃ kanyāṃ balād akṛtayonikām |
punar guṇavate dadyād iti śātātapo ‘bravīt ||
“knayānṛtaṃ” kanyādānānyathākaraṇapāpam | “samākṣipya” ācchidya |
śātātapaḥ |
hīnasya kulaśīlābhyāṃ haran kanyāṃ na doṣabhāk |
na mantrāḥ kāraṇaṃ tasya na ca kanyānṛtaṃ bhavet ||
samākṣipya balāt kanyāṃ pāpād akṣatayonikām |
kulaśīlavate dadyād iti śātātapo ’bravīt ||
udvāhitā tu yā kanyā saṃprāptā na ca maithunam |
bhartāraṃ punar abhyeti yathā kanyā tathaiva sā ||
“pāpāt” puruṣāt arūpavataḥ |
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
sa vā yady anyajātīyaḥ patitaḥ klība eva vā |
[97]        vikarmasthaḥ sagotro vā dāso dīrghāmayo ‘pi vā ||
klībo vā yadi vā bhartā visṛṣṭaḥ puṃstvakāraṇaiḥ |
ūḍhāpi deyā sānyasmai saprāvaraṇabhūṣaṇā ||
gandharvādivivāhacatuṣṭayānantaraṃ devalaḥ |
prāpteṣūrdhvaṃ vivāheṣu vidhir vaivāhikaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kartavyaś ca tribhir varṇaiḥ samayenāgnisākṣikaḥ ||
“vaivāhiko vidhiḥ” vivāhāṅgabhūto jālahomādiḥ |
bahvṛcapariśiṣṭam |
brāhmeṣv ārṣeṣu daiveṣu prājāpatyeṣu yājñikaiḥ |
pūrvaṃ homavidhiḥ proktaḥ paścāt pariṇayaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
gāndharvāsurapaiśācā vivāho rākṣasaś ca yaḥ |
pūrvaṃ pariṇayas teṣāṃ paścād dhomo vidhīyate ||
atha vivāhaguṇāguṇau
tatra manuḥ |
ye yasyaiṣāṃ vivāhānāṃ manunā parikīrtitā guṇāḥ |
sarvaṃ śṛṇuta taṃ viprāḥ samyak kīrtayato mama ||
[98]        daśa pūrvān parān vaṃśyān ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakam |
brāhmīputraḥ sukṛtakṛn mocayaty enasaḥ pitṝn ||
daivoḍhajāsutaś caiva sapta sapta parāvarān |
ārṣoḍhajāsutas trīṃs trīn ṣaṭ ṣaṭ kāyoḍhajāsutaḥ ||
daśaśabdaḥ pūrvāparaśabdābhyāṃ pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate, “ekaviṃśakam” ity abhidhānāt | “brāhmī” brāhmavivāhoḍhā | “enasaḥ” pāpāt | “pitṝn” iti putrādayo ‘py ajahatsvārthalakṣaṇayā vṛtyā pitṛśabdenopādīyante | “daivoḍhajāsutaḥ” daivena vivāhenoḍhā daivoḍhā, tasyāṃ jātā daivoḍhajā tasyāḥ sutaś ca | “ṅyāpoḥ saṃjñācchandasor bahulam” iti hrasvatvam | kvacitpāṭhaḥ “daivoḍhāyāḥ sutaḥ” iti |
prājāpatyavivāham uktvā yājñavalkyaḥ |
sa kāyaḥ pāvayet tajjaḥ ṣaṭ ṣaḍ vaṃśyān sahātmanā |
gautamaḥ |
punanti sādhavaḥ putrās tripuruṣam ārṣād daśa daivād daśaiva prājāpatyāt |
[99]        āśvalāyanaḥ |
dvādaśāvarān dvādaśa parān punāty ubhayataḥ | daśāvarān daśa parān putāty ubhayataḥ | aṣṭāvarān aṣṭa parān punāty ubhayataḥ | saptāvarāgn sapta parān punāty ubhayataḥ |
“avarān” adhastanān svavaṃśyān | etac ca vākyacatuṣṭayaṃ brāhmadaivaprājāpatyārṣaviṣayaṃ yathākramam, tadanantaram abhidhānāt |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
saptādyantayor ātmānaṃ ca prājāpatyaḥ | pañcārṣaḥ | trīn daivo, nottareṣu kriyāprādhānyam |
pnātīty anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ |
prājāpatyaputraś caturaḥ |
brahmādivivāhacatuṣṭayābhidhānānantaraṃ devalaḥ |
caturdhaiteṣu dattāyām utpannās tanayāḥ striyām |
dātuḥ pratigrahītuś ca punanty āsaptamaṃkulam ||
[100]        manu-yamau |
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣv evānupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmavarcasvinaḥ putrā jāyante śiṣṭasaṃmatāḥ ||
rūpavanto guṇopetā dhanavanto yaśasvinaḥ |
paryāptabhogā dharmiṣṭhā jīvanti ca śataṃ samāḥ ||
itareṣv anuviṣṭeṣu nṛśaṃsānṛtavādinaḥ |
jāyante durvivāheṣu brahmadharmadviṣaḥ sutāḥ ||
vivāhānuvṛttau āśvalāyanaḥ |
ata ūrdhvam akṣāralavaṇāśinau brahmacāriṇāv analaṃkurvāṇau adhaḥśāyinau syātām, trirātraṃ dvādaśarātraṃ saṃvatsaraṃ vā |
brahmapurāṇe |
kṛte vivāhe varṣaṃ tu vastavyaṃ brahmacāriṇā |
yady aṣṭavarṣā kanyā syāt tathātra triguṇaḥ pumān ||
“triguṇaḥ” caturdaśavarṣaḥ |
[101]         tathā |
atha vā dvādaśāhāni triṃśadvarṣeṇa sarvadā |
yadi dvādaśavarṣā syāt kanyā trūpaguṇānvitā ||
dvātriṃśadvarṣapūrṇena yadi ṣoḍaśavārṣikī |
labdhā tadā tu ṣaḍrātraṃ vastavyaṃ saṃyatena tu ||
viṃśatyabdā yadā kanyā vastavyaṃ tatra vai tryaham |
ata ūrdhvam ahorātraṃ vastavyam api saṃyataiḥ ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsandhvigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
gṛhasthakāṇḍe vivāhabhedāḥ ||
[102]
3
athādhivedanam
tatra manuḥ |
madyapāsatyavṛttā ca pratikūlā ca yā bhavet |
vyādhitāpy adhivettavyā hiṃsrārthaghnī ca sarvadā ||
“madyapā” atra dvijātistrī boddhavyā | “asatyavṛttā” asādhvācārā | “pratikūlā” bhartur aniṣṭakāriṇī | “vyādhitā” gṛhakṛtyāsāmarthyāpādakavyādhiyuktā | “hiṃsrā” putradāsyāditāḍanaśīlā | “arthaghnī” upekṣādinā arthavināśakāriṇī | “sarvadā”ity asyāsatyavṛttādibhir anvayaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
surāpī vyādhitā dhūrtā vandhyārthaghny apriyaṃvadā |
strīprasūś cādhivettavyā puruṣadveṣiṇī tathā ||
adhivinnā tu bhartavyā mahad eno ‘nyathā bhavet |
[103] “dhūrtā” vaṇcanapradhānā | “strīprasūḥ” strīmātrajananī |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
apriyaśīlāṃ puruṣadveṣiṇīm anukūlāṃ vādhivindet |
“apriyaśīlā” bhartur aprītikaraśīlavatī | “puruṣadveṣiṇī” patiṃ yā dveṣṭi |
manuḥ |
vandhyāṣṭame ‘dhivedyābde daśame tu mṛtaprajā |
ekādaśe strījananī sadyas tv apriyavādinī ||
atra adhivettavyeti śeṣaḥ |
devalaḥ |
ākāṅkṣetāṣṭa varṣāṇi bhartātiprasavāṃ striyam |
daśa vandyāṃ ca vindhyāṃ ca dvādaśa strīprasūtinīm ||
tato vindeta vidhinā putrārthīṃ dharmataḥ striyam |
putralābhāt paraṃ loke nāsty atiprasavāhi tāḥ ||
[104] ekāsudhru (?) puruṣabhāṣaṇaśīlā |
hārītaḥ |
aprajā navame varṣe daśame tu mṛtaprajā |
ekādaśe strījananī sadyas tv apriyavādinī ||
ekām udvāhya kāmārtham anyāṃ labdhuṃ ya icchati |
samarthas toṣayitvārthaiḥ pūrvoḍhām aparāṃ vahet ||
ekā śūdrasya vaiśyasya dve tisraḥ kṣatriyasya ca |
catasro brāhmaṇasya syur bhāryā rājño yathecchataḥ ||
“atiprasavā” prasavaḥ puṣpaṃ rajas tadyogyakāle ‘py atikrāntaṃ yathā | “vindhyā” chidrādiyuktā | “prasavā” apatyāni | “samarthaḥ” tattoṣaṇārthārthadāna iti śeṣaḥ | aṣṭavarṣādikālapratīkṣāvikalpo vayaḥśaktyapekṣayā vyavasthito boddhavyaḥ | yeṣu tu adhivedananimitteṣu na kālaviśeṣaḥ śrūyate teṣu yāvatā kālena taddoṣaviścayo bhavati, tāvantaṃ kālaṃ pratīkṣeta | yathā dīrgharoge ‘dhivindeta | pūrvastrīvivāhād upari stryantaravivāhaṃ kuryāt |
manuḥ |
yā rogiṇī syād duhitā saṃpannā caiva śīlataḥ |
[105]        sānujñāpyādhivettavyā nāvamānyā ca karhicit ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
adhivinnastriyai dadyād ādhivedanikaṃ samam |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yasyai datte tv ardhaṃ prakīrtitam ||
‘ādhivedanikam’ adhivedanaprayojanam alaṃkaraṇādi yāvad ekasyā dīyate, tāvad evādhivinnāyā api dātavyam ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
adhivinnā tu yā nārī nirgacched ruṣitā gṛhāt |
sā sadyaḥ saṃniroddhavyā tyājyā vā kulasaṃnidhau ||
nāradaḥ |
anukūlām avāgduṣṭāṃ rakṣāṃ sādhvīṃ prajāvatīm |
tyajan bhāryām avasthāpyo rājñā daṇḍena bhūyasā ||
ity adhivedanaparva ||
[106]
4
atha parivedanavidhiḥ
tatra manu-śātātapau yama-baudhāyanau ca dvitīye |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo ‘graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ ||
parivittiḥ parivettā yayā ca parividyate |
sarve te narakaṃ yānti dātṛyājakapañcamāḥ ||
yājakaś cātra vivāhāṅgabhūte home ādhāneṣu ca ṛtvik |
atra [hārītaḥ] |
jyeṣṭhe anirviṣṭe kanīyān nirviśan parivettā bhavati parivinno jyeṣṭhaḥ | parivedinī kanyā paridāyinī parikartā yājakaḥ sarve te patitāḥ |
‘anirviṣṭe’ akṛtavivāhe | niṣṭhānirdeśād aniṣpanne jyeṣṭhavivāhe vartamāne ‘pi kariṣṭhasya vivāha nādhikāraḥ | [107] tasmāt sahavivāho ‘pi na kāryaḥ | bhātṛpadena sodarya evocyate | sāpatnyādau ekakāraṇatvaguṇayogād api bhrātṛśabdaprayogopapatter nābhidhāśaktikalpanā | etannyāyārthavādinī ca “pitṛvyaputrān” ityādi śātātapasmṛtiḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
dārādhigamanādhāne yaḥ kuryād agrajāgrataḥ |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ ||
parivittiparivettārau narakaṃ gacchato dhruvam |
ācīrṇaprāyaścittau tau pādonaphalabhāginau ||
pātakaphalasya narakasya pādonaphalabhāgināv ity arthaḥ |
tathā |
dhanavārdhuṣikaṃ rājasevakaṃ karṣakaṃ tathā |
proṣitaṃ ca parīkṣeta varṣatrayam api tvaran ||
proṣitaṃ yad yaś ca evāstv abdād iti samācaret (?) |
āgate tu tatas tasmin pādaṃ vā śuddhaye caret ||
[108] “api tvaran” iti | tvarann apīti yojyam | yadi dhanavārdhuṣikādideśāntare vartamānaṃ śṛṇuyāt tadā vivāhe “tvaran” tvaramāṇo ‘pi varṣatrayaṃ pratīkṣeta | yadi tu taṃ proṣitādṛṣṭa evābdaṃ pratīkṣya vivāham ācarati, tadā āgate tasmin parivedanaprāyaścittapādaṃ kuryāt |
gautamaḥ |
naṣṭe bhrātari jyāyasi ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ kṣapaṇam, śrūyamāṇe ‘bhigamanam, pravrajite tu nivṛttiḥ, prasaṅgāt taddvādaśavarṣāṇi brāhmaṇasya vidyāsaṃbandhe bhrātari caivaṃ jyāyasi yavīyān kanyāgnyupayameṣu ṣaḍ ity eke |
jyeṣṭhe sodare ‘kṛtavivāhe anāhitāgnau ca proṣite | kanīyān evaṃ dvādaśāṣṭavarṣakam | ṣaḍvarṣapratīkṣaṇaṃ kuryād aśrūyamāṇe jyeṣṭhe vivāhāgnyādhāne kārayitum abhisusagamanam | pravrajite ca tasmin gamananivṛttiḥ | brāhmaṇasya vidyārjanārthaṃ [109] gatasya dvādaśavarṣapratīkṣaṇam | ṣaḍvarṣapratīkṣaṇaṃ vā ekeṣāṃ matam | varṣasya sarvasaṃkhyāyāḥ pratīkṣaṇe vayaḥśaktyapekṣayā vikalpaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
aṣṭau daśa dvādaśavarṣāṇi jyeṣṭhe bhrātaram aviniṣṭam apratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittī bhavati |
tatra ślokau bhavataḥ |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayogataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ ||
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klībaḥ eva vā |
rājayakṣmāmayāvī ca na nyāyyaḥ syāt pratīkṣitum ||
“āmayāvī” rogātiśayavān | “sarvatrāmayasyopasaṅkhyānam” iti dīrghaḥ | atirogiṇaṃ prati chandogapariśiṣṭavacanāt | matvarthīyasya ca itipratyayasyātiśāyane ‘pi smaraṇāt |
śātātapaḥ |
klībe deśāntaragate patite bhikṣuke ‘pi ca |
yogaśāstrābhiyukte ca na doṣaḥ parivindataḥ ||
[110]        khañjavāmanakubjeṣu gadgadeṣu jaḍeṣu ca |
jātyandhabadhire mūke na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
pitṛvyaputrān sāpatnyān paranārīsutāṃs tathā |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
“gadgado” aspaṣṭavāk | “jaḍaḥ” punaḥ punar adhyāpyamāno ‘pi grahaṇāsamarthaḥ | paranārīṣu (?) dattakṛttimādayo bhrātaraḥ | yogaśāstrābhiyogena viraktaṃ lakṣyate |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
deśāntarsthaklībaikavṛṣaṇān asahodarān |
vaiśyābhiśastapatitaśūdratulyātirogiṇaḥ ||
jaḍamūkāndhabadhirakubjavāmanakuṇṭhakān |
ativṛddhān kudaryāṃś ca kṛṣiśāktān nṛpasya ca ||
dhanavṛddhiśastāṃś ca kāmataḥ kāriṇas tathā |
kulaṭonmattacaurāṃś ca parivindan na duṣyati ||
“ekavṛṣaṇaḥ” ekāṇḍaḥ ṣaṇḍaviśeṣaḥ | “śūdratulyāḥ” gorakṣaṇādikarmaparāḥ,
[111]        gorakṣakān vāṇijakān tathā kārukuśīlavān |
preṣyān vārdhuṣikāṃś caiva viprān śūdravad ācaret ||
iti manuvacanāt | “kuṇṭhakaḥ” pāṇirahitaḥ | “ācāryān nṛpasya” iti nṛpasya ācaraṇīyān ity arthaḥ |
atreyaṃ vyavasthā — dvādaśavarṣaparīkṣaṇavacanaṃ kaniṣṭhasya dharmaḥ | dhanavidyārthe proṣite punaḥ punaḥ śrūyamāṇe jyeṣthe daśāṣṭavarṣapratīkṣaṇaṃ tu vayaḥsāmarthyāpekṣayā deśāntarasthavārtāśravaṇāttayā vā | ṣaḍvarṣapratīkṣaṇaṃ tu gautamīyaṃ dharmadhanavidyārthaproṣite sarvathā aśrūyamāṇe | varṣatrayapratīkṣaṇaṃ tu dharmadhanayor uktatvāt pāriśeṣyāt kāmāsakte proṣite rājasevake, kṛṣyāsakte ca jyeṣṭhe atitvaramāṇasya kaniṣṭhasya |
atra cāgnihotrādhānavad āvasathyādhāne ‘pi jyeṣṭhenākṛte kaniṣṭhena kriyamāṇe parivettṛtvaṃ bhavaty eva, kātyāyanenādhānaśabdenādhānamātropādānāt | “kānyāgnyupayameṣv” iti gautamenāgnimātrasvīkāropādānāt samācārāc ca |
hārītaḥ |
sodaryāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ parivettā kathaṃ bhavet |
dārais tu parivindyante nāgnihotreṇa cejyayā ||
ādhānāpekṣayā dāraparivedane doṣādhikyapratipādanaparam etat, na tu paryādhāne doṣābhāvārtham,
[112]        dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo ‘graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ ||
iti manunā tatrāpi doṣapratipādanāt, paryādhāne ca pṛthak prāyaścittābhidhānāt | etenaiva saptāgnayaḥ parivindanti ity api vyākhyātam |
tathā ca āpastambena
abhinirmuktābhyuditakunakhīśyāvadantāgrāvidhiśrudidhiṣupatiparyāhita-parīṣṭaparivittiparivedaneṣu cottarottarasminn aśucikaranirvedo garīyān |
iti vadatā paryādhānāpekṣayā dāraparivedanasyādhikadoṣatvam uktam | evam evāvasathyādhāne ‘pi gautamena “yavīyān kanyābhyupagameṣu” iti aviśeṣeṇāgnimātropādānāt |
śātātapaḥ |
nāgnayaḥ parivindanti na vedā na tapāṃsi ca |
na ca śrāddhaṃ kaniṣthasya viphalā yā ca kanyakā ||
iti hṛdayadharātmajabhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharviracite
kṛtyakalpatarau gṛhakalpakāṇḍe praivedanaparva ||
[113]
5
athāvasathyādhānam
atra chandogapariśiṣṭhe katyāyanaḥ |
yasya dattā bhavet kanyā vācā sapadi kenacit |
so ‘ntyāṃ samidham ādhāsyan nādadhītaiva nānyathā ||
“sapadi” tatkāle | “antyāṃ samidham” samāvartanakālīnām | etad uktam bhavati — yatkṛte brahmacaryāvasthāyām eva kanyāpitrā kanyā dātuṃ pratijñātā bhavati, sa tasminn eva kāle ‘gnim ādhāya tasminn agnau samāvartanādyudīcyam agnisādhyaṃ karma kuryāt | sa ādhādyann antyāṃ samidham ādadhītaivety avyavadhānena vākyaṃ yojanīyam |
tathā ca gobhilagṛhyam |
yadaivānyāṃ samidham abhyādadhāti jāyāyāḥ pāṇiṃ jighṛkṣan juhoti tam abhisaṃyacchet sa evāsya gṛhyo ‘gnir bhavati |
[114] tathā |
anūḍhaiva tu sā kanyā pañcatvaṃ yadi gacchati |
na tathā tantralopo ‘sya tenaivānyāṃ samudvahet ||
tantraśabdenātra prakramād āvasathyādhānānuṣṭhānam upāttam | “tenaiva” arthād āhitenāgninā |
tathā |
atha cen na labhetānyāṃ yācamāno ‘pi kanyakām |
tam agnim ātmasātkṛtvā kṣipraṃ syād uttarāśramī ||
“ātmasāt kṛtvā” ātmamayaṃ kṛtvā mantreṇātmany āropyeti yāvat |
pāraskaraḥ |
āvasathyādhānaṃ dārakāle kāyādyakāla ekeṣām |
“dāyādyakāle” dāyabhāgādānakāle |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
vaivāhikam agnim indhīta sāyaṃ prati yathākālaṃ samidho ‘bhyādadhyāt | “agnaye prajāpataye ‘numataye” iti sāyam, “sūryāya prajāpataye ‘numataye” iti prātaḥkāle |
[115] hārītaḥ |
audvāhikagrahaṇāt gṛhyavidhānāc ca gṛhasthaḥ | tasmād gṛhoṣita iti | gṛhapatiyajanād gārhasthyadharmāḥ pravartante |
dārān āhṛtyāgnīn ādhāyety uktvā pañca yajñā anye ca dharmāḥ śrautādhānottarakālam anuṣṭheyatvenoktāḥ | tāni śrautādhānāt prāg api bhavantīty anenocyate | atas te gṛhasthasyānuṣṭheyāḥ | sa ca “audvāhikagrahaṇāt gṛhyavidhānāc ca gṛhasthaḥ” | “audvāhiko” vaivāhiko ‘gniḥ | gṛhyavidhānaṃ gṛhyoktakarmakartṛkatvena vidhānam, tasmāt tadanantaroktakāraṇadvayāt “gṛhoṣita iti” gṛhasthaḥ san tataḥ prabhṛti pākayajñair yajate | tata evārabhya gārhasthyadharmāḥ pravartante |
manu-viśvāmitrau |
vaivāhike ‘gnau kurvīta gṛhyakarma yathāvidhi |
[116]        pañcayajñavidhānaṃ ca paktir anvāhikīṃ gṛhī ||
“vaivāhike” vivāhakālaparigṛhīte | “gṛhyakarma” pākayajñādi | “paktiḥ” pākaḥ | “anvāhikī” pratyahaṃ yā kriyate |
baudhāyanaḥ |
bhāryodvāhādir agnis tasmin karmakaraṇaṃ prāg agnyādheyād iti |
āpastambādyanusāreṇa sarvādhānapakṣe laukike gṛhyakarmaprāptyartham |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
karma smārtaṃ vivāhāgnau kurvīta pratyahaṃ gṛhī |
dāyakālāhṛte cāpi smārtaṃ vaitānikāgniṣu ||
vaitānikaḥ vitāno yajñas tatprayojanaḥ |
[117]
6
athāgnihotrādhānādi
tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
avaśyaṃ brāhmaṇo ‘gnīn ādadhīta darśapūrṇamāsāgrahaneṣṭicāturmāsya-paśusokaiś ca yajeta | naiyamikaṃ hy etat ṛṇasaṃstutaṃ ca |
“naiyamikam” niyame bhavaṃ nityam ity arthaḥ | “ṛṇasaṃstutam” ṛṇāpākaraṇarūpeṇa kīrtitaṃ “jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇaḥ” ityādinā |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
dārān āhṛtyāgnyādheyapunarādheyāgnihotradarśapaurṇamāsadākṣāyaṇa-cāturmāsyapaśasautrāmaṇyantaiḥ haviryajñaiḥ saptabhir yajet |
“punarādheyaḥ” ādhānaprakāraviśeṣaḥ | “dākṣāyaṇaḥ” pañcadaśavarṣāvidhitayā darśapūrṇamāsayor eva pratiparvāvṛtyānuṣṭhānena prakāraviśeṣo yājñikaprasiddhaḥ |
[118] paiṭhīnasiḥ |
agnir vai devatā manuṣyāṇāṃ tasmād agnim ādadhīta agnimukhāḥ hi devatāḥ | havyaṃ vahati devānāṃ kavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ sāyaṃ prātar baliharaṇam | darśapūrṇamāsau naveṣṭiḥ aṣṭakā paśubandhaḥ pitṛyajña iti pākayajñāḥ |
“havyaṃ vahati devānām” ity atrāgnir iti śeṣaḥ | “kavyam” ity atra vahatīti śeṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
pākayajñān yajen nityaṃ haviryajñāṃś ca nityaśaḥ |
saumyāṃś ca vidhipūrveṇa ya icched dharmam avyayam ||
saumyaḥ aṣṭamyām anuṣṭhānam |
pakṣādikaraṇam amāvāsyādi śrāddham | śravaṇakarma āgrayaṇī kriyā | āśvayujīkarma caitrīvidhānam agnyādhānam agnihotram, darśapūrṇamāsaś cāturmāsyakarma paśubandhaḥ sautrāmaṇīkaraṇam agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo ‘tirātram aptoryāma iti somayāgān anutiṣṭhet |
[119] amāvāsyādītyādiśabdenāṣṭakādiśrāddhopasaṃgrahaḥ |
devalaḥ |
hutaḥ prahuta ahutaḥ śūlagavo baliharaṇaṃ pratyavarohaṇam aṣṭakāhoma iti pākayajñasaṃsthāḥ sapta | agnihotram agnyādheyaṃ darśapūrṇamāsau āgrayaṇam ayanayajñaś cāturmāsyāni sautrāmaṇīti imā haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ sapta | agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī | vājapeyo ‘tirātro aptoryāmaḥ itīmāḥ somasaṃsthāḥ sapta | ekaviṃśatividho yajñaś cādhvaravedoktaḥ | vājapeyāśvamedharājasūya-pauṇḍarīkagosavādayo mahāyajñāḥ kratavaḥ |
