etāvad iti kālam uddiśya yajño ‘yaṃ vratadānādyanuṣṭhānaṃ sattraṃ (?) prakrāntena pañcapadāyajñāndāyajñapravṛttir iti yajñaḥ “kṛtaḥ” (hutaḥ?) | devoddeśena homaḥ “prahutaḥ” | brāhmaṇāya pākāgnidānam “ahutaḥ” | vāstupālabhūtebhyo baliharaṇam | “śūlagavaḥ” rudradaivataḥ puṅgavayāgaḥ sthālīpākāṅgakaḥ | “baliharaṇaṃ” sarpaḥ (?) | “pratyavarohaṇam” mārgaśīrṣyāṃ pratyavarohaṇam ityādyuktāstamite sthālīpākasya juhuyur ityādyāśvalāyanoktam | “ayanayajñaḥ” nirūḍhapaśuyāgaḥ | etāvad iti [120] kālam uddiśyeti | dvādaśarātrādisahasradinaparyantaṃ sattrāṅgaṃ kālam uddiśyety arthaḥ “pañcapadā” pañcasthālīpākayajñasaṃsthāḥ haviḥsaṃsthāḥ somasaṃsthāḥ, mahāyajñāḥ sattrarūpāḥ karotīti yāvat |
baudhāyanaḥ |
agnyādheyaprabhṛty athemāny ajasrāṇi bhavanti | yathaitad agnyādheyam agnihotraṃ darśapūrṇamāsāgrayaṇam udagayanadakṣiṇāyanayoḥ paśuś cāturmāsyāny ṛtumukhe ṣaḍḍhotā vasante jyotiṣṭomena ity evaṃ kṣemaprāpaṇam |
“ajasrāṇi” svakāla āvaśyakānīty arthaḥ | “ṣaḍḍhotā” “vāgdhotā” ityādimantreṇa ṣaḍṛtumukheṣv āhutiḥ | kṣemaprāpaṇam akaraṇanimittapratyavāyaparihāraḥ |
manuḥ |
agnihotraṃ ca juhuyād ādyante dyuniśoḥ sadā |
darśeṇa cārdhamāsānte pūraṇamāsena caiva hi ||
sasyānte navasasyeṣṭyā tathartvante dvijo ‘dhvaraiḥ |
paśunā tv ayanasyānte samānte saumikair makhaiḥ ||
nāniṣṭvā navasasyeṣṭyā paśunā cāgnimān dvijaḥ |
navānnam adyān māsaṃ vā dīrgham āyur jijīviṣuḥ ||
[121]        navenānnena cāniṣṭvā paśuhavyena cāgnayaḥ |
prāṇān evāttum icchanti navānnāmiṣagarhitāḥ ||
“ādyante dyuniśoḥ” iti | sāyaṃprātaḥ saṃdhyayor ity arthaḥ | “ṛtvante” ṛtukṣaye | caturṣu māseṣu adhvaraiś cāturmāsyayāgaiḥ | “paśuhavyena” paśuhaviṣā |
hārītaḥ |
agnihotre ca yajñābhijitam aśnīyān nānyābhijitam enastvagdārubhir indhano vānaspatyam abhijayaty adbhir āpaḥ payaādibhiḥ | paya ājyādi (?) | vrīhiyavābhyām oṣadhayaḥ, paśūnāṃ paśūn |
“yajñābhijitaṃ” yajñena kṛtvā puruṣeṇābhijitam abhijitād abhyasya etasvāpāt (?) | nanv evaṃ vṛkṣaphalādīnāṃ mahiṣādipayasaḥ savikārasya, mṛgāṇāṃ cāyajñiyatvāt bhakṣaṇe [122] katham adoṣaḥ | tatrāha — dārubhirityādi | “vānaspatyaṃ” vanaspatibhavaṃ phalādi | “adbhir” iti | pākādikāle pratiṣekopayuktābhiḥ yajñopayuktair gavyaiḥ | ādiśabdena dadhyādīnāṃ grahaṇam | “paśunā” chāgādinā yajñopayuktena | na grāmyān āraṇyān abhijayatīti saṃbandhaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratisaṃvatsare somaḥ paśuḥ pratyayanaṃ tathā |
kartavyā āgrayaṇeṣṭiḥ cāturmāsyāni caiva hi ||
eṣām asaṃbhave kuryād iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ dvijaḥ |
hīnakalpaṃ na kurvīta sati dravye ‘phalapradam ||
“vaiśvānarī” cānukalpaḥ | “sati dravye” iti | vidyamāne prathamakalpasaṃpādake dhane, “hīnakalpaṃ na kurvīta” ity atra hetuḥ — “aphalapradam” iti yata iti śeṣaḥ |
yajeta ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ |
śaradgrīṣmayoś cāgrayaṇena | vrīhiyavayoḥ pāke ca |
[123] jābālaḥ |
sāyaṃ prātaḥ sadābhyastaṃ vaitānaṃ juhuyād dvijaḥ |
darśena cārdhamāsānte pūrṇamāsena caiva hi ||
sasyapaktau naveṣṭyā tu cāturmāsyair ṛtor mukhe |
ayanādau nirūḍhena paśunā vatsare ‘pi vā ||
samānte somayāgena tv iṣṭvā mukto na saṃśayaḥ |
yamaḥ |
yajed apy agnihotreṇa gṛhamedhī divāniśam |
darśena pūrṇamāsena yajeta pitṛdevatāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
nāgnihotrāt paro dharmo nāgnihotrāt paraṃ tapaḥ |
nāgnihotrāt paraṃ dānaṃ nāgnihotrāt paro damaḥ ||
nāgnihotrāt paraṃ śreyo nāgnihotrāt paraṃ yaśaḥ |
[124]        nāgnihotrāt parā siddhir nāgnihotrāt parā gatiḥ ||
nāgnihotrāt paraṃ sthānaṃ nāgnihotrāt paraṃ vratam |
nirdvandvā niryaśaskārā yam upāsan manīṣiṇaḥ ||
so ‘yaṃ sūkṣmo ‘gnihotreṣu gūḍho ‘gnir iva dāruṣu |
śukriyeṣu rahasyeṣu niṣatsūpaniṣatsu ca ||
adhīyate mahātmānaḥ so ‘gnihotre prakāśate ||
“śukriyeṣu” pravargyakāṇḍeṣu | “rahasyeṣu” aghamarṣaṇādisūkteṣu | [sūkṣmaṃ paramātmānaṃ śukriyādiṣu paṭhanti | so ‘gnihotre prakāśata ity anvayaḥ |]
tathā |
agnihotrādibhiḥ pūto niyato vijitendriyaḥ |
sūkṣmāt sūkṣmam avāpnoti gagane suṣiraṃ yathā ||
suhutāny agnihotrāṇi nayanti paramāṃ gatim |
atra svargaś ca mokṣaś ca yathā yo gantum icchati ||
“sūkṣmāt sūkṣmaṃ” paraṃ brahma | “gagane suṣiraṃ yathā” iti | mahākāśe suṣirād ānīyate (?) yathety arthaḥ |
[125] [jābālaḥ] |
santoṣāmṛtam āsvādya tanmanā dharmavin naraḥ |
kāmena cāgnihotrasya hotā svajyotir āpnuyāt ||
atra paiṭhīnasiḥ |
agnim evopāsīta nānyadaivatam, agnir bhūyām iti vijñāyate | na pravaset, yadi pravased uktam upasthānaṃ pravasitasya prāśitam agnyādheyaṃ juhuyāt |
naveṣṭyām evam aupāsanasyoktaṃ kalpasūtroktam upasthānam | kartavyam iti śeṣaḥ | “prāśitam” yajamānaprāśanīyo bhāgaḥ | evam āvasathyasaṃbandhinyāṃ naveṣṭyāṃ proṣitasya yajamānabhāgam agnau prakṣipet |
aṅgirāḥ |
laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vāpi yaj juhoti yathāvidhi |
vaidikāt svargam āpnoti laukikaṃ svargasādhanam ||
“laukikam” smārtam | “vaidikaṃ” śrautam | “svargam āpnoti” [mokṣam āpnoti] |
[126] devalaḥ |
nāgnihotraṃ samādhāya jīvitārtham api tyajet |
sarve vedās tadarthā hi loke brāhmaṇajanma ca ||
manuḥ |
na ca kanyā na yuvatir nālpavidyo na bāliśaḥ |
hotā syād agnihotrasya nārto nāsaṃskṛtas tathā ||
narakaṃ hi patanty ete juhvataḥ sa ca yasya yat |
tasmād vaitānakuśalo hotā syād vedapāragaḥ ||
“alpavidyaḥ” pratinidhiprāyaścittādyanabhijñaḥ | “bāliśaḥ” ṣoḍaśavarṣād arvācīnaḥ | “ārto” rogādipīḍitaḥ | “asaṃskṛtaḥ” garbhādhānādisaṃskārarahitaḥ | “yasya” agnihotriṇaḥ | tadagnihotram iti śeṣaḥ | “vaitānakuśalaḥ” agnitrayasādhyakarmakaraṇakuśalaḥ | kanyādīnāṃ cātrāgnihotre ṛtvikpratinidhitvena prāptānāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
asamakṣaṃ tu dampatyor hotavyaṃ nartvigādinā |
[127]        dvayor apy asamakṣe hi bhaved ddhutam anarthakam ||
vihāyāgniṃ sabhāryaś cet sīmām ullaṅghya gacchati |
homakālātyaye tasya punar ādhānam iṣyate ||
araṇyātyayanāśāgnidāhe hy agniṃ samāhitaḥ |
pālayed upaśānte ‘smin punar ādhānam iṣyate ||
“sīmā” agnyādhānādisīmā | patnīyajamānayoḥ sīmāntaragatayor homakālātikrame punar ādhānam ity arthaḥ | “nāśaḥ” adarśanam | “samāhitaḥ” samyagagnirakṣaṇayatnavān |
atra śaṅkhalikhitau |
avāpya vṛttiṃ dharmeṇa nityasnāyī priyātithiḥ |
agnihotraparaḥ samyak gṛhastho vāvasīdati ||
[hārītaḥ] |
askannapākayajñaḥ sarvasūnākṛtebhyaḥ pāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati | yathā prajānām aśanam evam agner agnihotram iti śrutiḥ | yathā anaśnantyaḥ prajāḥ pāpīyastvam upayānti, pramīyante vā, evaṃ vicchinnāgnihotrāgnayaḥ kratunopapannenāpy avasīyastvam upayānti pramīyanta iti | tasmād [128] bhṛtyai [dhṛtyai?] vāgner aprajāḥ | pāpīyastvam upayānti pramāpayantīti cāgnihotram iti śrutiḥ |
āyuṣkṛd yajamānaḥ āyur eva prāṇāgnihotraṃ yathā tṛptāḥ samarthā bhavanti, evam avicchinnāgnihotrād agnayaḥ tṛptāḥ prītāḥ kratuṣu saṃbhavanti | jarāmaryāyaiva sattrāya dīkṣito yo darśapūrṇamāsārambhaṇīyaṃ karoti, sa paurṇamāsena haviṣauṣadhisānnāyyapaśuṣu saṃbhavitvāt somam āmāvāsyena darśena yatrāpyāyayati sa somayājī, tasmād darśasaṃsthau darśapūrṇamāsabudhatya (?) prathamam eva pūto ‘mṛtatvaṃ gacchanty atirekād anyaiḥ | sa dvividho dravyejyāna ātmejyānaś ca | yo ‘stv evam eva dravyam iti phalam abhisandhāya sopadhaṃ vā yajeta sā dravyejyā |
atha yaḥ śrutismṛtyātmajñānavān amṛtavad dhavir bhūtādhi[129]daivatādhyātmabhūtaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ yajati sa ātmejyā | tad apy etad ṛcoktam – “vaiśvānaram atithim ādadānam antarvidhau parame vyomany ātmany ātmānam abhisaṃvidānaḥ | pratisāyam aratir yāti vidvān samyag vīram atithiṃ rocayanti imāṃl lokān amṛtāḥ sañcarema” iti |
“askannapākayajñaḥ” askhalitapākayajñaḥ | askannapākayajña iti pūrvoktānuvādo ‘yaṃ phalapradarśanārthaḥ | “yathā prajānām aśanam” ityādinā “śrutir⌈ ityantena laukikāśanadṛṣṭāntenāgnihotrasyāvaśyakartavyatām āha | “anaśnantyaḥ” bhojanam akurvatyaḥ | “pāpīyastvaṃ” kārśyam | “kratunopapannena” darśapūrṇamāsādinā prasiddhena | “avasīyastvam” aśreṣṭhatvam | “dhṛtyai” svarūpāvasthānāya | “guptyai” rakṣaṇāya | “āyuṣkṛd yajamānaḥ” ityādinā “saṃbhavanti” ityantena (?) prāṇāgnihotradṛṣṭāntenāgnihomatṛptānām uttarakratuṣu sāmarthyaṃ pradarśyate | “āyuṣkṛt” prāṇāgnihotrakṛt | ayuḥśabdena āyurvardhanahetutvāt prāṇādidevatākam agnihotraṃ lakṣyate | “tṛptāḥ” samarthāḥ | “jarāmaryāya” jarāmaraṇāvadhitayā | “jarāmaryāyaivam” ityādinā “atrekād anyair” ityantena “ārambhaṇīyety” upakrameṇa prathamam eva darśapūrṇamāsau prāpya pūto bhavati | anyais tu dvitīyādiprayogair jarāmaraṇaparyantair [130] atirekād anuṣṭhānabhūyastvād amṛtatvam upagacchatīty uktam | auṣadhyādiṣu somasya saṃbhavitvāt | somayāgena somam āpyāyayati | “sa dvividhaḥ” ityādinā “sañcarema” ityantena yāga evādhikāriviśeṣeṇa phalaviśeṣa uktaḥ | “sopadhaṃ” naimittikādyupādhisahitam | “śrutyādīty” atra jñānaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ śrutyādibhiḥ saṃbadhyate | “amṛtaṃ” brahma | “havirbhūtaṃ” havirātmakam | adhidaivatātmakam | “adhyātmabhūtaṃ” yajamānabhūtam | “vaiśvānaraṃ” vaiśvānaratapanahetum | evaṃ bhāvāpattau yad yajanaṃ sātmejyā | “vaiśvānaram atithim” ityādi vaiśvānaram atithirūpam | “ādadānam” ādareṇopacarantam | “antarvidhau” antaryajane | “ātmani” paramātmani | “ātmānaṃ” jīvātmānam | “abhisaṃvidānaḥ” dhyānābhyāsenaikatvenopalabhamānaḥ | “aratiḥ” apagatabāhyaviṣayaratiḥ | “parame vyomani” paramākāśe paramārthe viyati, paramātmanīti yāvat | tadekatvaṃ prāpnoti brahmībhāvam anubhavatīty arthaḥ | “pratisāyaṃ” pratisaṃdhyāyāṃ homakāla ity arthaḥ | idaṃ nityakarma kālopakaraṇārtham |
punar hārītaḥ |
akṣarād brahma bhavati tataḥ karma tataḥ ṛtuḥ tataḥ parjanyas tato ‘nnaṃ tato bhūtāni jajñire |
[131]        yajñāt prajā viśvataḥ saṃbhavanti yajño dhārayati prajāḥ |
yajñād bhūtam idaṃ sarvaṃ yajño medha imās tanūḥ ||
yajamānasya ye nindāṃ pravadanty abudhā janāḥ |
yajñāpoḍhaṃ tu naitasya pāpaṃ gṛhṇanty a[ceta]saḥ ||
nāsty ayajñasya loko vai nāyajño vindate sukham |
aniṣṭayajño ‘pūtātmā bhraśyate śuṣkaparṇavat ||
yajñena lokā vimalā vibhānti
yajñena devā amṛtatvam āpnuvan |
yajñena pāpair bahubhir vimuktaiḥ
prāpnoti lokān paramasya viṣṇoḥ ||
“akṣarāt” paramātmanaḥ | brahmavido bhavati prathamata upalabhyate, “niḥśvasitam idaṃ yad ṛgveda” ityādiśrutidarśanāt | “tato” vedāt | “karma” kriyā | “kratuḥ” yāgaḥ | “viśvataḥ” sarvataḥ | “tanūḥ” śarīrāṇi | “yajñāpoḍhaṃ” yajñenāpahṛtam |
vāyupurāṇe |
darśaṃ a paurṇamāsaṃ ca ye yajanti dvijātayaḥ |
[132]        na teṣāṃ punar āvṛttiḥ brahmalokāt kadācana ||
punar hārītaḥ |
divyāṃ gatim avāpnoti |
[tathā]
ahar ahar agnihotradarśapūrṇamāsāgrayaṇacāturmāsyeṣṭipaśubandhaprabhṛtibhiḥ vidhivat vṛtyatirekād agniṣṭomādyaiḥ pravṛttiḥ |
“pravṛttiḥ” anuṣṭhānam ity arthaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śraddhāśīlo ‘spṛhayāluḥ alaso ‘gnyādheyāya, nānāhitāgniḥ syāt | alaṃ ca somāya nāsomayājī |
“aspṛhayāluḥ” paravittānupalolupaḥ | “alaṃ” samarthaḥ | sāmarthyaṃ ca dhanavidyādisaṃpattiḥ |
manuḥ |
yasya traivārṣikaṃ bhaktaṃ paryāptaṃ bhṛtyavṛttaye |
[133]        adhikaṃ vāpi vindeta sa somaṃ pātum arhati ||
atas tv alpīyasi dravye yaḥ somaṃ pibati dvijaḥ |
sa pītasomapūrvo ‘pi na tasyāpnoti tat phalam ||
“traivārṣikaṃ” varṣatrayaṃ yena vartanaṃ bhaviṣyatīti | “bhṛtyāḥ” avaśyapoṣyāḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
traivārṣikādhikānno yaḥ sa somaṃ pibati dvijaḥ |
prāksaumikīḥ kriyāḥ kuryād yasyānnaṃ vārṣikaṃ bhavet ||
“prāksaumikīḥ” somāt prāk tatra bhavāḥ cāturmāsyādikriyā | etac ca nityaviṣayam api, “somaṃ pibati” ityaviśeṣopādānāt | traivārṣikādhikān pratyaścānarī na (?) pratisaṃvatsaraṃ somam ity evam uktā, tattadabhāve vaiśvānarī yājñavalkyenāpy uktā yataḥ |
manuḥ |
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ nityaṃ nirvaped abdaparyayte |
[134]        kḷptānāṃ paśusomānāṃ niṣkṛtyartham asaṃbhave ||
“kḷptānāṃ” āvaśyakatvena bodhitānām | “niṣkṛtiḥ” tadakaraṇapratyavāyanirgamanam | “asaṃbhave” pūrvoktadhanapaśvādyasaṃbhave |
tathā |
puṇyāny anyāni kurvīta śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ |
na tv alpadakṣiṇair yajñair yajeteha kathaṃcana ||
indriyāṇi yaśaḥ svargam āyuḥ kīrtiṃ prajāḥ paśūn |
hanty alpadakṣiṇo yajñas tasmān nālpadhano yajet ||
prājāpatyam adatvāśvam agnyādheyasya dakṣiṇām |
anāhitāgnir bhavati brāhmaṇo vibhave sati ||
“brāhmaṇo vibhave sati anyāni puṇyāni” atyantadhanasādhyāni dānādīni | “alpadhanair” ity alpatvaṃ vihitadvādaśaśatādisaṃkhyāpekṣayā | “aśvaḥ prājāpatyaḥ” svabhāvād evāśvasya prajāpatidevatākatvāt | “anāhitāgnir bhavati” ādhānaphalaṃ na prāpnotīty arthaḥ |
[135] rājadharmeṣu śaṅkhalikhitau |
nālpasaṃbhāro yajeta nāstīti saṃvatsaravṛttir aniṣṭaṃ hi rāṣṭre nipataty aniṣṭaṃ yajñasavanaṃ yajamānaṃ chādayati |
“aniṣṭaṃ” anarthasyāyajanam | “yajñasavanaṃ” yajñaḥ sa hi taṃ “chādayati” pāpena saṃbadhnāti |
punaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
ṛtvik mantrasamāyuktaḥ yas tv auṣadhiguṇānvitaḥ |
yajñena devān āpnoti yāvan nāpnoti dakṣiṇām ||
pratyāśramaṃ sthitā varṇāḥ karmabhir vyaktatāṃ gatāḥ |
niyamācāradharmāṇāṃ brāhmaṇas tatra deśakaḥ ||
saṃpūjyaḥ so ‘rcanīyaś ca guruḥ saṃskārakarmakṛt |
agnayas tanmukhāḥ sarve devāś cāgnimukhāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni atharvāṅgirasāni ca |
tāni vipreṣv adhītāni te yajñā āptadakṣiṇāḥ ||
samyagvittādyāptā dakṣiṇā yais te āptadakṣiṇāḥ | anena ca brāhmaṇadvāreṇa saṃpūrṇadakṣiṇādānaṃ pratipādyate |
[136] chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
naikayāpi vinā kāryam ādhānaṃ bhāryayā dvijaḥ |
akṛtaṃ tad vijānīyāt sarvā anvārabhanti yat ||
varṇajyeṣṭhena bahvībhiḥ savarṇābhiś ca yatnataḥ |
kāryam agnicyuter ābhiḥ sādhvībhir manthanaṃ pṛthak ||
na tu śūdrāṃ niyuñjīta na drohadveṣyakāriṇīm |
nāśāsanasthām anyena puṃsā ca saha saṃgatām ||
tataḥ śaktatarā paścād āsām anyatamaiva yā |
upetānāṃ cānyatamā manthed agniṃ nikāmataḥ ||
arthā bhavanti sahādhikāraṃ pratipadyante | etenaitad uktam — bahupatnīkenaikasyā api patnyā asannidhāne ādhānaṃ na kartavyam iti | bahvīṣu anekavarṇāsu sarvajyeṣṭhaivāgnimanthanaṃ kuryāt | sarvābhir api savarṇābhiḥ sādhvībhiḥ pṛthaṅmanthanaṃ kāryam | “agnucyuteḥ” yāvad araṇībhyām agniniḥsaraṇam | “aśāsanasthāṃ” bhartur ājñollaṅghanakarīm | “tato” manthanāt | “āsāṃ” bharyāṇāṃ madhye “yā śaktatarā” sā agneḥ paricaraṇaṃ kuryāt | “śaktatarā” agniśuśrūṣādikaraṇakṣamā | “upetānāṃ” samīpasthitānāṃ madhye ekā kācid yathecchāto ‘gniṃ paricaret |
[137] yājñavalkyaḥ |
dāhayitvāgnihotreṇa striyaṃ vṛttavatīṃ patiḥ |
āhared vidhivad dārān agniṃ caivāvilaṅghayan ||
“vṛttavatīṃ” yathoktasvadharmācāravatīm | “vidhivat” yathoktavivāhaprakāreṇa |
strīdharmānantaraṃ manuḥ |
evaṃ vṛttāṃ savarṇāṃ strīṃ dvijātiḥ pūrvamāriṇīm |
dāhayed agnihotreṇa yajñapātraiś ca dharmavit ||
bhāryāyai pūrvamāriṇyai datvāgnīn antyakarmaṇi |
punar dārakriyāṃ kuryāt punar ādānam eva ca ||
“antyakarmaṇi” dāhakarmaṇi |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
jyeṣṭhā ced bahubhāryasya abhicāreṇa gacchati |
punar ādhānam atraika icchanti na tu gautamaḥ ||
[138]        dāhayitvāgnibhir bhāryāṃ sadṛśīṃ pūrvasaṃsthitām |
pātraiś cāthāgnim ādadhyāt kṛtadāro ‘vilambitaḥ ||
“abhicāraḥ” vyabhicāraḥ | “pūrvasaṃsthitāṃ” pūrvamṛtām | etac cāgnihotragrahaṇaṃ smārtāgner apy upalakṣaṇārtham | ayam ācāraḥ savarṇajyeṣṭhapatnīmātramaraṇaviṣayaḥ | bhāryādiśabdair aviśeṣeṇa bharyāmātropādānāt | na cāgnaye vāpy ete patnyāṃ mṛtāyām [ādri]yanta ity agnikratau aviśeṣeṇa jyeṣṭhapatnīmātramaraṇe agnīnāśāvāgnitvenattya (?) jya[tva]pratipādanāt (?) |
yāni tu,
mṛtāyām api bhāryāyāṃ vaidikāgniṃ na hi tyajet |
upādhinā hi tat karma yāvajjīvaṃ samāpayet ||
yo dahed agnihotreṇa svena bhāryāṃ kathaṃcana |
sā strī saṃpadyate na strī dāhī tu na pumān bhavet ||
dvitīyāyāṃ ca bhāryāyāṃ dahed vaitānikāgnibhiḥ |
[139]        jīvantyāṃ pūrvabhāryāyāṃ surāpānasamaṃ hi tat ||
mṛtāyāṃ tu dvitīyāyāṃ yo ‘gnihotraṃ samutsṛjet |
brahmojjhaṃ taṃ vijānīyāt yaś ca kāmān samutsṛjet ||
barhistaraṇamantreṇa paścād agner anekasyeti śrutāni vacanāni barhistaraṇam ity ato ‘nyāni (?) tāvat saṃdigdhamūlāni asavarṇadvitīyapatnīmaraṇa[mātra]viṣayāṇi |
[140]
7
atha yajanayājane
tatra baudhāyanaḥ |
athāgnyādheye “yad devā devaheḍanaṃ yad adīvyann ṛṇam ahaṃ babhūva āyuṣṭe viśvato dadhat” iti pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvāgnihotram āripsamāno daśahotāraṃ kṛtvā, darśapūrṇamāsāv ārapsyamānaś caturhotāram, cāturmāsyam ārapsyamānaḥ pañcahotāraṃ kṛtvā, paśubandhe ṣaḍḍhotāraṃ some saptahotāram iti vijñāyate | karmādiṣv etair juhuyāt pūto devalokaṃ samaśnuta iti hi brāhmaṇam |
uktāgnyādheyādikarmārambheṣu daśahotrādayaḥ “citiḥ sruk” ityādayo mantraviśeṣāḥ |
atra āpastambaḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇād adhi gṛhamedhinoḥ vṛttaṃ kālayor bhojanam atṛptiś cānnasya, parvasu cobhayor upavāsaḥ aupavastam eva kālāntare bhojane tṛptiś cānnasya yac caitayoḥ priyaṃ syāt tad etasminn [141] ahani bhuñjīyātām adhaś ca śayiyātāṃ maithunavarjam | śvobhūte sthālīpākas tasyopacāraḥ pārvaṇena vyākhyātaḥ | nityaṃ loka upadiśati | yatra kvacanāgnim upasamādhāsyan syāt tatra prācīr udīcīś ca tisras tisro lekhā likhitvādbhir avokṣyāgnim upasamindhyād utsicyodakam uttareṇa pūrveṇa vānyad upadadhyād adbhir aśūnyāni syuḥ |
“pāṇigrahaṇād adhi” pāṇigrahaṇopari | “gṛhamedhinoḥ” dampatyoḥ | “kālayoḥ” sāyaṃ prātaś ca | “atṛptiḥ” atyaśananivṛttiḥ | “annasya” iti suhitārthayoge karaṇe ṣaṣṭhī | “parvasu” paurṇamāsyamāvāsyayoḥ | “ubhayoḥ” jāyāpatyoḥ | upavāso ‘trānaśanaṃ kṛtvopavastavyam eva | (?) auparabhañcayagrāmyāraṇyaṃ dhruvanty aktvā gārhapatyāhavanīyavasanaṃ tad eva ca bhojanaṃ kālāntare, bhojanaṃ tv agnyādhānānantaram eva sāyaṃ prātaḥ kālayor vā | kiṃ tu “aparāhṇe vratopāyanīyam aśnīta” ityādi kātyāyanavacanāt “tṛptiś cānnasya” iti āpastambaśākhinām amāvāsyāviṣayam | paurṇamāsyām evopavatsyantau nātisuhitāv iti kalpasūtravacanāt |
[142]        yac caitayoḥ priyaṃ syāt vihitānām ājyadadhyādīnām anyatamam | etasminn ahani na tu pūrvadine | “adhaḥ” na paryaṅkādau | “śvo bhūte” pratipadi | “upacāraḥ” phalīkaraṇādiḥ | “pārvaṇena” parvabhavena sthālīpākena | “upasamindhyāt” dīpayet | “utsicyodakaṃ” yasyāṃ kasyāṃ diśi | “anyat” udakāntaram | upadhānāni ca samādīni | |”nityaṃ” yāvat prayogakālam |
hārītaḥ |
devapitrarthe śvobhūte apy aupavastaṃ naivātikāmam aśnīyāt |
“śvo bhūte” darśapūrṇamāsābhyām anyatrāpi devapitṛkārye śvobhāvini sati | “aupavastam” bhojananiyamaviśeṣāḥ, “amāṣam amāṃsaṃ bahusarpiṣkaṃ vrataṃ vratayati” ity etadvacanavihitam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
aupavasathye ‘hani bhaktaṃ haviṣyam ity uktam, na kruddho nānṛtaṃ vadet | na vṛthā janaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta | śucir dānto ‘pramatto dakṣo jitendriyo yajet | madhyāhnasyopariṣṭād [143] dhaviṣyaṃ bhuktvā barhir idhmaṃ sannabhyāgniparigrahaṃ kṛtvā na pravased adhaḥ śayīta kāmaṃ tu vā yajet | paurṇamāsyāṃ kṛttaśmaśrur alaṃkṛto ‘bhyakto gandhapuṣpādi seveta | pitaro ha vai śucikāmā śrāddhakalpena brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ vyākhyātam iti |
“aupavasathye ‘hani” yāgadināt pūrvadine | “bhaktaṃ” bhakṣyam | “agniparigrahaḥ” atrāgnyādidevatānāṃ yaṣṭavyatvena saṃkalpaḥ | “abhyakto” ghṛtādinā | “brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ” paurṇamāsyamāvāsyayoḥ | “śrāddhakalpena” śrāddhaprakāreṇa bahūn bhojayed ity arthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
ahatavāsasāṃ śucir iti tasmād yat kiṃcejyādisaṃyuktaṃ [144] syāt sarvaṃ tad ahatair vāsobhiḥ kuryāt prakṣālitopavātāny akliṣṭāni vāsāṃsi patnīyajamānāv ṛtvijaś ca paridadhīrann evaṃ prakramād ūrdhvaṃ dīrghasomeṣu | sarvatraiva ṛtvijo yathā samāmnātaṃ syāt | yathaitad abhicaraṇīyeṣṭipaśumedheṣu lohitoṣṇīṣāḥ lohitavāsasaś cartvijaḥ pracareyuś citravāsasaś citrasaṃkhyā vṛṣākapāv iti |
tathā |
kṣaumāṇi vāsāṃsi teṣām alābhe kārpāsikāny aurṇikāni bhavanti |
vāsasāṃ madhye ahataṃ vāsaḥ śreṣṭham, tad dadhatā kriyamāṇo yajñaḥ śuciḥ | “upavātāni” śuṣkāṇi | “akliṣṭāni” acchidrāṇi | “dīrghasomeṣu” dīrghakālavyāpiṣu someṣu | ṛtvijas tu sarvāṇi karmāṇi yathāvihitam | yādṛśaṃ vihitaṃ tādṛśaṃ vāsaḥ | tad eva darśayati — “tathaitad” ityādinā | “abhicaraṇīyam” abhicārasādhanaṃ śyenādi | “citravāsasaḥ” vicitravastrāḥ | “vṛṣākapau” prakramyamāne | “kṣaumāṇīty”ādinā patnīyajamānayor eva prakṛtatvāt | ādhane vasontaravidhānam etat |
[145] yamaḥ |
kṛṣṇājinaṃ brāhmaṇasya vaiyāghraṃ kṣatriyasya tu |
bastājinaṃ tu vaiśyasya śāṇaṃ kṣaumaṃ tu sarvataḥ ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
uttarata upacāro vihāraḥ | tathāpavargaḥ | viparītaṃ pitryeṣu | padopahataṃ prakṣālayet | aṅgam upaspṛśya sicaṃ vā apaḥ spṛśet | evaṃ chedanabhedana-khananadahananirasanapitryarākṣasanairṛtaraudrābhicārikeṣv apa upaspṛśed iti vijñāyate |
na mantravatā yajñāṅgenātmānam abhipariharet | abhyantarāṇi yajñāṅgāni | bāhyā ṛtvijaḥ | patnīyajamānau ṛtvigbhyo ‘byantaratarau | yajñāṅgebhya ājyam ājyād dhavīṃṣi, havirbhyaḥ paśuḥ, paśoḥ somaḥ, somād agnayaḥ |
yatha karmartvijo na vihārād abhiparyāvarteran | prāṅmukho dakṣiṇam aṃsam abhiparyāvartate | pratyaṅmukhaḥ savyam | antareṇa cātvālotkarau yajñasya tīrtham | acātvāla āhavanīyo[146]tkarau | tataḥ kartāro yajamānaḥ patnī ca prapadyeran | visaṃsthite | saṃsthite ca saṃcaro ‘nūtkaradeśāt | nāprokṣitam aprapannaṃ klinnaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ samidhaṃ vāgnāv ādadhyāt | agreṇāhavanīyaṃ brahmayajamānau pradyete | jaghanenāhavanīyam ity eke | dakṣiṇenāhavanīyaṃ brahmāyatanaṃ, apareṇa yajamānasya | uttarāṃ śroṇim uttareṇa hotuḥ | utkara āgnīdhrasya | jaghanena gārhapatyaṃ patnyāḥ | teṣu kāle kāla eva darbhasaṃstaraṇam | ekaikasya codakakamaṇḍalur upāttaḥ syād ācamanārthe | vratopreto dīkṣitaḥ syāt | na parāpavādaṃ vaden na krudhyen na rodet mūtrapurīṣe nābhivīkṣeta | amedhyaṃ dṛṣṭvā japati — “abaddhaṃ mano daridraṃ cakṣuḥ sūrto jyotiṣāṃ śreṣṭho dīkṣe mā mā hāsīr” iti |
vihriyata iti vihāraḥ vihṛto ‘gniḥ | sa “uparata upacāraḥ” uttarasmin bhāge upacāraḥ praveśopakramo yasya sa tathoktaḥ | “apavargaḥ” karmasamāptau nirgamanam | so ‘pi tathā uttarataḥ | “pitryeṣu” piṇḍapitṛyajñadiṣu | etad dvayam api viparītaṃ virahitam etasmin vihārasya dakṣiṇabhāga ity arthaḥ | “padopahatam” pādena spṛṣṭam | yajñāṅgaṃ sruvāti “aṅgaṃ upaspṛśya [147] svāṅgam aṅgam atra nābher ūrdhvam, “ūrdhvaṃ vai puruṣasya nābher medhyam avācīnam amedhyam” iti taittirīyaśruteḥ | “sik” vastraprāntaḥ | udakasparśanaṃ kāryam | “mantravatā” mantravad yat karma yajñe kriyate ‘tra yat sādhanaṃ sruvādi, tenātmānam abhipariharet | nātmaśarīrād anyatre tasmin kāle tad dhārayet |
atraiva hetuḥ — “ābhyantarāṇi yajñāṅgāni bāhyāḥ” anabhyantaraṅgāḥ | “yajñāṅgebhyaḥ” sruvādibhyaḥ | (?) “ājyaṃ havīṃṣi” puroḍāśarūpāṇi | “yathākarma” iti | yatra yatra karmaṇi ya ṛtvik sa tat karma parisamāptikālaṃ yasmāt tasmād vihāradeśāt svecchayā āvaśyakakāryārthaṃ vinānyatra na gacched ity arthaḥ | “prāṅmukhaḥ” yadā paryāvarteta tadā | “dakṣiṇaṃ” aṅgam abhidakṣiṇam ity arthaḥ | pratyaṅmukhaḥ san yajamāno nābhiparyāvarteta | tadā savyam aṅgam api pradakṣiṇam ity arthaḥ |
“antareṇa” iti catvālotkaramadhye | “yajñatīrtham” yajñārtham ṛtvigādīnāṃ praveśaṃ āhuḥ | “acātvāle” cātvālarahite darśapūrṇamāsādau | “āhavanīyotkarau” antareṇa yajñatīrtham ity anuṣaṅgaḥ |
“tataḥ” tena mārgena | “kartāraḥ” ṛtvijaḥ | “pratipadyeran” praviśeyuḥ | “saṃsthite” samāpte yajñe | “saṃcāraḥ” saṃcaraṇaṃ notkaradeśād bahir iti śeṣaḥ | “aprapannam” aprayatnaprāptam | “jaghanena” paścāt | “tam apareṇa” tasmād brahmaṇaḥ paścimena “uttarāṃ śroṇiṃ” veder iti śeṣaḥ | “utkaraḥ” āgnidhriyasya āyatanam [148] ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “jaghanena gārhapatyaṃ patnyāḥ” gārhapatyāt paścime pradeśe | atrāpy āyatanam ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “teṣu” āyataneṣu kāleṣu “dīkṣitaḥ” dīkṣāvān “vratopeto” bhavet | tāny evāha – “parāpavādaṃ” viruddhaṃ parasya kṛte na brūyād ity arthaḥ | “amedhyaṃ” mūtrapurīṣe | “abaddham” ityādi “mā mā hāsīḥ” ityanto mantraḥ |
hārītaḥ |
devatottāraṇārthaṃ saṃskāreṇa saṃskṛte devānāṃ samānatāṃ samānalokatāṃ sāyujyatāṃ gacchati | tena devasaṃmitam evāhitāgniṃ prāhuḥ | sa ṛgyujuḥsāmasu lokeṣu jyotiḥṣu deveṣu brahmaṇi vā hito bhavati | sa hy etāṃ sāyaṃprātar ahar ahaḥ prīṇāti | taṃ hy ete sāyaṃprātar ahar ahaḥ pāvayanti | tasyāhar ahar agnimukhāḥ sāyaṃprātar havyāyāvatiṣṭhante | tasmād dhavirvidām apy anāhitāgnīnām āhitāgniḥ puṇyatamo bhavati | tasmān nopaviṣṭāgnihotrī syāt |
yathā vai brāhmaṇān āhūya niyujya tasyānnaṃ nāśnīyād evaṃ [149] yad ahute ‘śnāti tasmād abhijitam aśnīyāt | nānṛtaṃ brūyān nāślīlaṃ nāniyato nāśuciḥ syāt nānyadārān gacchet na tais tiṣṭhen nāviṣṭo ‘paḥ piben nāśuciḥ klinnapūtibhir vā dārubhir agnim indhīta nāgnivelayoḥ pravaset | parvasu cāpatsu proṣito velāyāṃ vāgyataḥ | pratidinam agnihotraṃ manasā dhyātvāvasthāya mantrato hutaṃ jñātvā vrataṃ ye dampatyopasthāyām abhibhāṣeta (?) nimrukte vācaṃ yacched odayād ā homād ubhayataḥ | svayam agnihotraṃ juhuyād anyatropāsīta | sāyam apy upasthāya parītya namaskṛtya prasaret tathā | prātar nādarbho ‘gniṃ paridhvareta nāyajñiyair juhuyād dhutaśeṣaṃ brāhmaṇāya dadyāt |
rūḍho yuvā na na bāliśo vā pradoṣe ‘gniṃ juhuyāt | “devasaṃmitaṃ” devatulyam | upaviṣṭāgnihotrī upaviṣṭaṃ homavadhaśūnyaṃ (?) agnihotraṃ yasya sa tathā | “āhūya” niyujya | śrāddhabhojanārtham upaviśya tasya saṃbandhi yat tad ātmanāśnīyāt | [150] evam evāsau yo hutaṃ aśnāti | “abhijitaṃ” hutapūrvakaṃ, “aślīlaṃ” asabhyaṃ na brūyād ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “aniyataḥ” asaṃyatendriyaḥ | nāviṣṭaḥ ekādhāraḥ | “agnivelā” homadānakālaḥ | parvasu ca na praviśed ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | yadi ca pravaset vidhānam akṛtvā āpatkāle kāryatāratamyena pravaset | tadā pratidinam agnīn manasā dhyātvā upasthānaṃ kṛtvā mantrato hutaṃ jñātvā “vratayet” bhuñjīta | “etya” pravāsād āgatya upasthāya abhibhāṣeta | agnyupasthānam akṛtvā na kiṃcid brūyād ity arthaḥ | “nimrukte” astamite | “ā udayāt” āsūryodayāt | “ā homāt” sāyaṃhomasamāptiparyantam | ayaṃ codayaparyantaṃ vāṅniyamaḥ asamaśiṣṭatvāt phalaviśeṣārthino boddhavyaḥ | “ubhayataḥ” sāyaṃ prātaś ca | “svayaṃ” ātmanaiva | “anyatra” iti | anyasmin agnihotrahomakāriṇi | “svayam agnim upāsīta” dhyāyan samīpe tiṣṭhed ity arthaḥ | nityaṃ kartavyāntram āha — apahṛtya pradakṣiṇam akṛtvā prātar apy upasthānāditrayaṃ kuryāt | “adarbhaṃ” darbhasya dakṣiṇahastasaṃbandhaṃ vinā | “ayajñiyaiḥ” māṣādibhiḥ |
punar hārītaḥ |
māṣamasūramadhumāṃsaparānnamaithunāni vratye ‘hani varjayet | [151] vrtā(?)yenākāle vācaṃ visṛjet | navasasyam aśnīyād dīrghasatre ṛtau jāyām upeyāt | na madhv aśnīyāt paya ādihaviḥśeṣaṃ vā dīkṣito dīkṣitaniyamān vā yajamānaniyamān iṣṭvā veṣṭiniyamān |
dīkṣitaḥ kṣaumavāsā jaṭī mekhalī yajñopavītī uṣṇīṣī kṛṣṇājina āsīta | vāgvisargāśruvidalanāvagāhanamehanavivṛtaśāyanadarśanavismitadantadhāvana-niṣkramaṇāni ca dīkṣito vivarjayet | vijṛmbhamāṇo hasitvā pāṇinā pidadhyāt | mūtrapurīṣaniṣṭhīvanaṃ cātape na kuryāt iti dīkṣitaniyamāḥ |
krodhāhaṃkārabhayaviṣādakārpaṇyalobhān yajamāno varjayet | āhitubhir devān yajet | atrādyena dakṣiṇābhiś ca brāhmaṇān iti yajamānaniyamāḥ |
sa eṣa yoninānāsaṅgaviṣṭhā(vi)varjayet (?) | jalāgnivāyusomasūryāṇām aśucibhir arthair nopacared itīṣṭiniyamāḥ |
na paśuṃ saṃjñapyamānam īkṣeta, na spṛśed yūpaśūlān iti [152] niyataṃ saṃkīrṇapratito yajñaḥ | sravaty anuyajñaṃ yajamāna ārtīr upaiti sarvayoniṣv āgacchati āyuṣo vāvarudhyate | tasmāt kulaśrutaśīlavṛttasaṃpannān ṛtvijo vṛṇuyāt | tasya sarvakāmaduhaṃ yajñaṃ dakṣiṇābhiḥ kurvanti | anuyajñaṃ yajamānaḥ anuyajamānaṃ prajāḥ paśūnāṃ dadyād oṣadhipaśusomair hutaṃ yajñaṃ dakṣiṇābhiḥ saṃpādayanti | dakṣiṇāhata eva hataḥ | tasya sarvakāmaduhaṃ yajñaṃ dakṣiṇābhiḥ saṃpādayantīti dakṣiṇāṃ dadāti candamasas tena lokaṃ yajati | yad dhotre ‘gnilokaṃ yad udgātre parjanyalokaṃ tena yad dhotrāsaṃsibhyaḥ diśas tena yad vasu anugrahakartṛbhya idaṃ tena lokaṃ yac chuśrūṣave anūcānāya svargaṃ tena lokaṃ yāvat dadāti kṣetram ārttīś ca tena yajati | yat pāpīyasa dadāti, tad anayā dakṣiṇayā niṣkrīṇāmīti |
candramā vai brahmā tad yāvac candramā na kṣīyate tāvad asyeṣṭaṃ dattam akṣayaṃ bhavati | hotar vācaṃ te dadāmi tam anayā dakṣiṇayā niṣkrīṇāmi cety agnir hotā tad yāvad agnir na kṣīyate, tāvad asyeṣṭaṃ dattam akṣayaṃ bhavati | udgātaḥ śrotraṃ te dadāmi tad anayā dakṣiṇayā niṣkrīṇāmi parjanyo vai śrotraṃ parjanya udgātā tad yāvat parjanyo na kṣīyate, tāvad asyeṣṭaṃ dattam akṣayaṃ bhavati | adhvaryo cakṣus te dadāmi tad anayā dakṣiṇayā [153] niṣkrīṇāmi ity agnir asau vā ādityaś cakṣur asāv ādityo ‘dhvaryus tad yāvad asāv adityo na kṣīyate, tāvad asyeṣṭaṃ dattam akṣayaṃ bhavati | hotrāśaṃsinaḥ aṅgāni vo dadāmy anayā dakṣiṇayā niṣkrīṇāmiti diśo vai hotrāśaṃsino diśo ‘ṅgāni tad yāvad diśo na kṣīyate tāvad asyeṣṭaṃ dattaṃ akṣayaṃ bhavati | athāpy atrodāharanti —
brahmayajño brāhmaṇeṣv anvatiṣṭhat
tasmāt tasya brāhmaṇāḥ pāvitāraḥ |
tasmāt teṣāṃ yajñabhāgārhakāṇāṃ
yajñe viprāṇāṃ dakṣiṇā sādhu deyā ||
trayo yajñas tṛtīyas tasya prāṇāḥ
prāṇair yajñas tv āyate (?) karmayogāt |
tasmād ṛtvigbhyo bhūyasīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ vai
dadyāt prājño yajñabhūyastvam icchan ||
yajur yajñaṃ vyabhajad bhāgaśo vai
madhyadyattaṃ (?) na tu te karmayogāt |
[154]                yajuṣv ete mahimānaṃ viditvā
yajurvidbhyaḥ kartṛdhanaṃ ca deyam ||
varṇair mātrābhir akṣaraiś ca svaraiś ca
pādaiḥ pādair ardharcair virāmaiḥ |
hotraiḥ stotraiḥ kālakarmaprayogaiḥ
ye voḍhāro bhāgyadānaṃ tu teṣām ||
dharmo yajño dharmato dharmavidbhiḥ
dharmo bhūtaḥ kriyate karmahetoḥ |
dharmo lokāṃs trāyate tatra viprāḥ
tasmāt tadvidbhyo dharmadhanaṃ pradeyam ||
sūryaḥ svarbhānus tamasā nāśayed yat
tato lokā nāvir āsan na yajñaḥ |
na satriratvāya śānnamaḥ
tasmād yajñaś catridhanaṃ pracakṣuḥ (?) ||
suhṛn mitraṃ bāndhavāḥ kāriṇaś ca
nānāyogaiḥ saṃbhavante makhaṃ vai |
[155]                tato yajñas trāyate nāsahāyas
tasmād vidvān pūjayen nānyathāvat ||
nyāyād dravyād ṛtvigbhiś caiva śuddhai
ratnatyāgād dakṣiṇābhiś ca yajñaḥ |
iṣṭān lokān sarvakāmopapannān
nayed virājaṃ brahmalokaṃ pratiṣṭhām ||
viṣṇuṃ yajñaṃ pravadantīha tajjñā
yajñasyāgnim atithiṃ caiva vadanti |
tasmād yajñe vividhair dravyayogais
trayo bhūtvā tarpayed vai hutāśam ||
agniṃ rudram uta kālaṃ vadanti
yasyeme mohānubhūtāḥ prajā vai |
tarpayitvaivainam amṛtā bhavanti
nainaṃ ye vidus te ‘nnam ivāsya kḷptāḥ ||
agniḥ pitā hy uta mātātra na bhrātā
agnir goptā rakṣitā duṣkṛtāc ca |
[156]                agnir dātā putrapatnīśriyāṃ hi
sarvasmād durgāt tāraṇaṃ cāgnim āhuḥ ||
devā devatvam agninaivāptavantaḥ
sarvān lokān amṛtatvaṃ tathaiva |
tapas taptvaivāgninaivāgnilokān
jitvā ṛṣayo brahmabhūyāya gacchan ||
tasmād vīraḥ śuśruvān nyāyavṛttiḥ
cinvāno ‘gnīn vidhim āsthāya bhaktyā |
kṣānto dānto nirmalo vītaśoko
gacchel lokān ṛṣidevānuyātān ||
sukhasaumyān sarvakālopapannān
svastho gacchet khecarair vandyamānaḥ |
ṛddhaḥ śrīmān susugandhir ghṛtātmā
mṛtyor mukto brahmalokaṃ prayāti || iti |
“vratye” paurṇamāsyādau | “vratopetaḥ” kṛtavāṅniyamaḥ | “akāle” haviḥkṛtāt kālāder arvāk | “agnim upetya” agni[157]samīpaṃ gatvā | “na paryāvarteta” parvartanam agniparāṅmukhatvam |
“bibhradvratakāmavadbhyām” ityādi | yadā vāso bibhṛyāt tadā “tvam agne vratapā asi,” “tubhyantā aṅgirastama” ity etābhyāṃ vratakāmaśabdayuktābhyām upasthāyānyatra vaset |
punar ityāder ayam arthaḥ — bhayenānyatra vaset | “mano jyotir juṣatām” iti, “tvam agne vratapā asi” iti dvābhyāṃ mantrābhyām astamayāt prāk prathamodite ca sūrye upasthānaṃ kṛtvā darśādiyāgaṃ kuryāt |
iṣṭvā navasasyenaiva navasasyam aśnīyān navānnam aśnīyād ity arthaḥ | “dīrghasatreṣu” śatarātrādiṣu | “paya ādihaviḥśevaṃ vā” iti | payodadhyādiyāgaśeṣabhūtaṃ vāśnīyād ity anuṣaṅgaḥ |
yajamāno dīkṣitaś cādīkṣitaś ca | vāgvisargaḥ “dīkṣitavācaṃ visṛjasva” iti praśnottarakālam | “uditeṣu” nakṣatreṣu | “vivṛtaśayanaṃ” patnīśālāto bahiḥśayanam | “vismitaṃ” vismayaḥ | “pāṇinā pidadhyāt” āsyam iti śeṣaḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
nikṣipyāgniṃ svadāreṣu parikalpyartvijaṃ tathā |
pravasan kāryavān vipro mṛṣaiva na ciraṃ kvacit ||
[158]        manasā naityakaṃ karma pravasann apy atandritaḥ |
upaviśya śuciḥ sarvaṃ yathākālam upācaret ||
“nikṣipya” iti paricaraṇārthaṃ samarpya | [“vikalayya”] vyavasthāpya | “parikalpyartvijam” agnihotrādikāriṇaṃ kṣīrahomādikāryārtham avasyabhartavyabharaṇayogyadhanārthaṃ kṣemārthaṃ ca | “mṛṣā” vinā kāryam | kāryārtham api “na ciraṃ” tat tat kalpasūtroktapravāsakālam ity arthaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
nāgniṃ citvā kṛṣṇavarṇāṃ rāmām upeyāt | kṛṣṇavarṇā yā rāmā ramaṇāyaiva na dharmāyeti |
“agniṃ citvā” agnicayanavantaṃ yāgaṃ kṛtvā | “kṛṣṇavarṇīyāṃ” śūdrajātīyāṃ svavivāhitām api na gacched ity arthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
tāmasena tu dravyeṇa ṛtvigbhis tāmasais tathā |
tāmasaṃ bhāvam āsthāya tāmaso yajña ucyate ||
tāmasena tu yajñena dānena tapasā tathā |
[159]        niraye janma ced evaṃ ṛddhiṃ vidyāt tu tāmasīm ||
rājasena tu dravyeṇa ṛtvigbhī rājasais tathā |
rājasaṃ bhāvam āsthāya rājaso yajña ucyate ||
rājasena tu yajñena dānena tapasā tathā |
nirayasvargayor janma krūraṃ rājyaṃ śriyā yutam ||
sātvikena tu dravyeṇa ṛtvigbhiḥ sātvikais tathā |
sātvikaṃ bhāvam āsthāya sātviko yajña ucyate ||
sātvikena tu dānena yajñena tapasā tathā |
devaloke dhruvaṃ vāso devasāyujyam eva ca ||
“tāmasaṃ” kṛṣṇam |
pārśvikadyūtacauryādipratirūpakasāhasaiḥ |
vyājenopārjitaṃ yac ca tat kṛṣṇaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
iti nāradoktam | ṛtvijāṃ ca tāmasabhāvadoṣeṇa yajñe pracaraṇād dānatapasor api tāmasatvam anantaroktaprakāram eva boddhavyam | “tāmasī ṛddhiḥ” mlecchādhipatyādirūpā | “rājasaṃ dravyam” śabalam | tac ca,
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijyaśulkaśilpānuvṛttibhiḥ |
kṛtopakārād āptaṃ ca rājasaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
[160] iti nāradoktam | rājyasya krūratvam ahiṃsyahiṃsādinā | “sātvikaṃ dravyaṃ” śuddham | tac ca,
śrutaśauryatapaḥkanyāśiṣyayājyānupāgatam |
dhanaṃ saptavidhaṃ śuddham jñeyam ityādi sātvikam ||
iti nāradoktam |
manuḥ |
na yajñārthaṃ dhanaṃ śūdrād dvijo bhikṣeta dharmavit |
yajanārtho hi bhikṣitvā cāṇḍālaḥ pretya jāyate ||
yajñārtham arthaṃ bhikṣitvā yo na sarvaṃ prayacchati |
sa yāti bhāsatāṃ vipraḥ kākatāṃ vā samāpnuyāt ||
yajñakaraṇārthaṃ dravyaṃ śūdrāt svīkṛtya na tena yajñaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | brāhmaṇādibhyo ‘pi yajñārtham ārjitaṃ dhanaṃ sarvam eva deyam | adāne tu bhāsaḥ kāko vā bhavet | “bhāsaḥ” goṣṭhakukkuṭaḥ | “samāḥ” varṣāṇi |
[161] hārītaḥ |
asaddravyapraṇīto yajñaḥ prasravati anṛtvikpraṇītaḥ cyavate ‘vidvatpraṇīto ‘bhāvukaḥ | saṃkīrṇapraṇīto ‘siddhārtho ‘kṛtsno ‘jyotir vidhihīno yātayāmaḥ | chadmo ‘dhruvaḥ sopadho ‘lpaphalo (na ca ?) upadiśati adakṣiṇo ‘nṛta ity ete yajñopasargāḥ, yair upasṛṣṭo yajño ‘bhāvuko bhavati |
“asaddravyam” asadvṛttyārjitam | “sravati” niṣphalatāṃ gacchati | “anṛtvijaḥ” anūcānatvādilakṣaṇarahitāḥ | “avidvān” yajñakarmānabhijñaḥ yajamāno ‘pi | “abhāvukaḥ” arakṣakaḥ | “saṃkīrṇaḥ” saṃkarākhyapāpakārī | “asiddhārthaḥ” asiddhaprayojanaḥ | “akṛtsnaḥ” asaṃpūrṇaḥ aṅgayāgahīnaḥ | “ajyotiḥ” atejaskaḥ, prabhāvahīna iti yāvat | “vidhihīnaḥ” aṅgapradhānetikartavyatārahitaḥ | “chadma” dambhaḥ | “adhruvaḥ” alpakālaphaladāyī | “sopadhaḥ” iṣṭakīrtyādyartham anuṣṭhitaḥ | “upadiśati” upakṣīṇo bhavati | “upasṛṣṭaḥ” upasargākhyadoṣasaṃbaddhaḥ |
[162] manuḥ |
ye śūdrād adhigamyārtham agnihotram upāsate |
ṛtvijas te ‘pi śūdrāṇāṃ brahmavādiṣu garhitāḥ ||
teṣāṃ satatayajñānāṃ vṛṣalāgnyupasevinām |
padā mastakam ākramya dātā durgāṇi saṃtaret ||
“brahmavādino” vedavādinaḥ | “vṛṣalaḥ” śūdraḥ |
atra chāgaleyaḥ |
yaḥ śūdrād adhigamyārtham agnihotram upāvaset |
dātā na phalam āpnoti kartā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
manuḥ |
yajñaś cet pratiruddhaḥ syād ekenāṅgena yajvanaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya viśeṣeṇa dhārmike sati rājani ||
yo vaiśyaḥ syād bahupaśur hīnakratur asomapaḥ |
[163]        kuṭumbārtasya (?) tad dravyam āhared yajñasiddhaye ||
āharet trīṇi vā dve vā kāmaṃ śūdrasya veśmanaḥ |
na hi śūdrasya yajñeṣu kaścid asti parigrahaḥ ||
yo ‘nāhitāgniḥ śatagur ayajvā ca sahasraguḥ |
tayor api kuṭumbābhyām āhared avicārayan ||
ādānanityāc cādātur āhared aprayacchataḥ |
yathā yajño ‘sya prathate dharmaś caiva vivardhate ||
“pratiruddhaḥ” ārabdhaḥ na vilambitaḥ | “ekāṅgena” ekenāṅgena dakṣiṇādinā | “āharet” tenādīyamānaṃ tadaṅgabhūtaṃ dravyaṃ tasmād gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | vaiśyāsaṃbhave tu trīṇy aṅgāni dve vā aṅge śūdrād api grāhyāṇi “śūdrasya veśvamaḥ” iti vābhidhānāt | balāt śūdragṛhād ācchedanīyam, “na hi śūdrasya” ity anena prakāreṇa śūdradhanasya yajñānupayuktatvam āha | “ayajvā” somayāgarahitaḥ | “ādānanityaḥ” ninditāninditaprakāreṇārthārjanatatparaḥ | “adātā” adānaśīlaḥ | adātur aprayacchata ity ubhayopādānāt | traivarṇikaḥ prathamaṃ yācanīyaḥ | yācitaś cen na dadāti tadā āhared avicārayan |
gautamaḥ |
dravyādānaṃ vivāhasiddhyarthaṃ dharmatantraprasaṅge ca śūdrād [164] anyatrāpi śūdrād bahupaśor ahīnakarmaṇaḥ | śatagor anāhitāgneḥ | sahasragor asomāt | saptamīṃ cābhuktvānicayāyāpy ahīnakarmaṇā ācakṣīta rājñā pṛṣṭas tena bhartavyaḥ | śrutaśīlasaṃpannaś ced dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ tasyākaraṇe doṣaḥ |
dravyasa parakīyasya ananujñātasyāpi svīkaraṇaṃ yāvatā vinā vivāho na niṣpadyate tāvataḥ | idaṃ ca vivāhakālātikramasaṃbhāvanāyāṃ kanyādātuḥ | dharmatantrasya agnihotrādeḥ pravṛttasya prasaṅgo vicchedaḥ | cakāraḥ siddhyartham ity asyānukarṣaṇārthaḥ | atrāpi yāvatā dhanena tat sidhyati tāvad evāpahṛtya svīkartavyam, adhikasvīkaraṇe steyāpatteḥ |
kutas tat svīkartavyam ata āha — “śūdrāt” | śūdrād alābhe śūdrād anyato ‘pi vakṣyamāṇāt traivarṇaikāt | “ahīnakarmā” dhanasādhyādṛṣṭārthakarmaśūnyaḥ | gograhaṇaṃ tanmūlyasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham | paradravyādāne aparaṃ nimittāntaram āha — “saptamīṃ cābhuktvā” iti | sāyaṃ prātar bhojanasaṃkhyayā [yadbhojanādikrame saptamabhojanavelāyām anicayāya] yāvad bhojanamātrārthaṃ na tu saṃcayāya | cauryanimittaṃ ca yadi rājñā gṛhyate, tadā “ācakṣīta” kathayen na tu bhayān na bravīta (?) | “dharmatantrapīḍāyāṃ” [165] tasya dharmatantrapīḍāvataḥ | anantaroktanimittatrayavataḥ pradhānakāraṇe |
aṅgirāḥ |
khalakṣetragataṃ dhānyaṃ vāpīkūpagataṃ jalam |
abhojyād api tad grāhyaṃ yac ca goṣṭhagataṃ payaḥ ||
vicchinneṣu tu pakṣeṣu haviṣāṃ śrapaṇeṣu ca |
iṣṭiṃ tv avikṛtāṃ kuryāt sukṛtaṃ tu na lopayet ||
“abhojyāt” śūdrādeḥ | etac ca dhānyādi yajñavicchedaśaṅkāyāṃ tatparimāṇapūraṇāyaiva grāhyam | “avikṛtām” iti | bahvannatvādivikārarahitām |
atra baudhāyanaḥ |
amedhyādyādhāne samāropya nirmathitvā pavamāneṣṭiḥ | śaucadeśamantrāc ca dravyasaṃskārakālabhedeṣu pūrvaprādhānyam agniṣu |
amedhyaprakṣepe tam agnim araṇyoḥ samāropya nirmathanaṃ kṛtvā pavamāneṣṭiḥ kāryety arthaḥ |
atra paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kāṇḍamūlaparṇapuṣpaphalapraroharasagandhādīnāṃ sādṛśyena [166] pratinidhiṃ kuryāt, sarvālābhe yavaḥ pratinidhir bhavati |
āpastambaḥ |
nānūcānam ṛtvijaṃ vṛṇīte na paṇamānam | ayājyo ‘nadhīyānaḥ |
amukena dakṣiṇākalpenaiva tvāṃ yājayāmīti yaḥ paṇate, sa paṇamānaḥ | “anadhīyāno” yady api yajñavidbhyo dhanavān |
atra śaṅkhaḥ |
vṛttaṃ tu na tyajed vidvān pūrvam eva sadarjitam |
karmaṇā janmanā śuddham ijyayādhyayanena ca ||
etair eva guṇair yuktaṃ dharmārjitadhanaṃ yadā |
yājayeta tadā vipro grāhyas tasmāt pratigrahaḥ ||
pūrvam ādhānakāla eva yathoktadharmādisaṃpannam ṛtvijaṃ parityajyodīcyadarśādiyāgādau ṛtvigantaraṃ ca na kuryād ity arthaḥ |
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsandhvigrahika-
śrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
gṛhasthakāṇḍe gārhasthyaparvapallavaḥ ||
[167]
8
atha brāhmaṇakarmadharmavṛttayaḥ
tatra manuḥ |
etad vo vihitaṃ kṛtsnaṃ vidhānaṃ pāñcayajñikam |
dvijātimukhyavṛttīnāṃ vidhānaṃ śrūyatām iha ||
hārītaḥ |
adhyāpayed adhīyīta yājayet yajeta ca |
pratigṛhṇīta dadyāc ca ṣaṭkarmā brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
yājanādhyāpanādibhyas tathā dānapratigrahaiḥ |
virājaḥ svargam āyānti vimānaiḥ saṃyutā dvijāḥ ||
“virājaḥ” śobhamānāḥ | “yājanādhyayanādibhyaḥ” iti hetau pañcamī | atra ādiśabdena yajanādhyayanayor grahaṇam |
manuḥ |
adhyāpanam adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ yājanaṃ tathā |
dānaṃ praigrahaś caiva ṣaṭkarmāṇy agrajanmanaḥ ||
[168]        ṣaṇṇāṃ tu karmaṇām asya trīṇi karmāṇi jīvikā |
yājanādhyāpane caiva viśuddhaś ca pratigrahaḥ ||
“viśuddho” yadīyapratigrahe viśeṣato na prāyaścittaṃ samāmnātam |
vṛttyadhikāre āpastambaḥ |
dāyādyaṃ śiloñchau anyāc cāparigṛhītam |
“dāyādyaṃ” dāyam ādatta iti dāyādaḥ putrādiḥ, tasya karmaṇā yad dhanagrahaṇam | “aparigṛhītam” asvāmikaṃ nīvārādi |
hārītaḥ |
dvividham eva gṛhasthaṃ prāhuḥ – śālīnaṃ yāyāvaraṃ ca | śālīnāt puṇyataro yāyāvaraḥ śreyān |
sarvārambheṣu bahvyo ‘sya śālā it śālīnaḥ | [169] śālāvān ātmavṛttibhiḥ līnaḥ iti vā śālīnaḥ | sa dvividhaḥ – gṛhyaśālīnas tretāśālīnaś ca | gṛhyaśālīno jātaputrāyādhānaṃ saṃvatsarād vā śuśrūṣāvān yaḥ, sa eva dvividhaḥ – saṃpannavṛttir asaṃpannavṛttiś ca | tayor asaṃpannavṛttiḥ śreyān, sa vyākhyātaḥ | saṃpannā yājanavṛttiḥ kṛṣivṛttiś ca |
śālā gṛhāṇi | śālāvān śayyāṃ sukhāsikām | “gṛhyaśālīnaḥ” gṛhyaśālāvān | “tretāśālīnaḥ” gārhapatyāgnitrayaśālāvān | “jātaputrāyādhānaṃ” kuryur iti śeṣaḥ | jātaputrāyādhānam ity atra jātaputraśabdaḥ prathamābahuvacanāntaḥ, śākalyamatāśrayeṇa yākārapāṭhaḥ | saṃvatsarād vā śuśrūṣāvān yaḥ | yaḥ śuśrūṣāvān gṛhyāgniparicaraṇavān bhavati | saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam ādhānaṃ kuryāḍ ity arthaḥ | sakṛdādhāno ‘saṃpannaḥ śiloñchādivṛttiḥ | “sa vyākhyātaḥ” so ‘pi saṃpannavṛttir dārān āhṛtyāgnīn ādhāyetyādinā kathitaḥ | “saṃpannaḥ” saṃpannavṛttiḥ |
punar hārītaḥ |
ṛtāmṛtopahitam adhyāpanāt sannavṛttyāpadvṛttayo bhavanti |
“ṛtam” uñchaśilaṃ bhaikṣam | “amṛtam” ayācitopapannaṃ sadbhyaḥ | adhyāpanāc chiṣyopahitavṛttiḥ | arthavṛttiḥ sanna[170]vṛttiḥ | āsāṃ pūrvāḥ śreyasyo bhavanti | “adhyāpanād” iti ca yathāvihitavṛttyupalakṣaṇam |
manuḥ |
adroheṇaiva bhūtānām alpadroheṇa vā punaḥ |
yā vṛttis tāṃ samāsthāya vipro jīved anāpadi ||
yātrāmātraprasiddhyarthaṃ svaiḥ karmabhir agarhitaiḥ |
akleśena śarīrasya kurvīta dhanasaṃcayam ||
ṛtāmṛtābhyāṃ jīvet tu mṛtena pramṛtena tu |
satyānṛtābhyām api vā na śvavṛttyā kathaṃcana ||
ṛtam uñchaśilaṃ jñeyam amṛtaṃ syād ayācitam |
mṛtaṃ tu yācitaṃ bhaikṣaṃ pramṛtaṃ karṣaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
satyānṛtaṃ tu vāṇijyaṃ tena caivāpi jīvyate |
sevā śvavṛttir vyākhyātā tasmāt tāṃ parivarjayet ||
“drohaḥ” parapīḍanam, na hiṃsā, atiniṣiddhatvāt | “yātrā” prāṇavṛttiḥ | mātraśabdena bhogāyarthaṃ dhanādānaṃ vyavasthite (?) |
ṛtād ity anuvṛttau yamaḥ |
ādyāś catasraḥ śreṣṭhāḥ syuḥ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca vṛttayaḥ |
brāhmaṇasyety anuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
[171]        kṛṣivāṇijye vā svayaṃkṛte kusīdaṃ ca |
“kusīdaṃ ca” vṛddhyarthaṃ dhanaprayogaḥ | pṛthakgrahaṇaṃ cāsya svayaṃkṛtasyāpy anujñānārthe “kusīdavṛttiḥ dharmyā” iti pradeśāntare ‘bhidhānāt |
hārītaḥ |
agnīn ādhāyoñchaśilābhyām ayācitopapannaṃ sādhubhyo yājanāt sadbhyo vā yācanād devapitṛmanuṣyārthe vṛttyādānaṃ kurvīta |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
atha gṛhasthadharmāṃś cariṣyann alopena dharmasya vṛttim upapādadīta |
manuḥ |
kusūladhānyako vā syāt kumbhīdhānyaka eva vā |
tryahaihiko vāpi bhaved aśvastanika eva ca ||
caturṇām api caiteṣāṃ dvijānāṃ gṛhamedhinām |
jyāyān paraḥ paro jñeyo dharmato lokavittamaḥ ||
ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavaty eṣāṃ tribhir anyaḥ pravartate |
[172]        dvābhyām ekaś caturthas tu brahmasatreṇa jīvati ||
“kusūladhānyakaḥ” koṣṭikāparimitadhānyavān | “kumbhī” mṛdbhāṇḍaviśeṣaḥ | “tryahaihikaḥ” īhā tasyāṃ bhavam aihikaṃ dhānyam, tryāhm aihikaṃ yasya sa tathā | śvobhavaṃ svastanaṃ dhānyaṃ, “śvasastuṭ ca” ity tuṭ tad asyāstīti śvastanikaḥ | na śvastaniko ‘aśvastanikaḥ, sadyaḥ prakṣālaka ity arthaḥ |
kusūlakumbhīparimāṇaṃ tu – ekadine svakuṭumbavartanocitadhānyasvīkartuḥ śvastanāpekṣayā tryahaihikadinatraiguṇyadarśanāt tadanusāreṇaiva | kusūladhānyakakumbhīdhānyakayor apy uttarottaraṃ dinatraiguṇyena boddhavyam | ayaṃ ca vṛttiviśeṣaparigraho dharmātiśayārthaṃ gṛhasthamātraviṣayaḥ, na yāyāvaraviṣayaḥ | anantaram eva caturṇām eva teṣām uttarottaraśreṣṭhatvam abhidhāya “ṣaṭkarmaiko bhavaty eṣām” ity abhidhānāt | ṣaḍvṛttayo ‘tra vāṇijyāntarbhāvena, pūrvoktānām uñchāvṛttīnāṃ parityāgakāraṇābhāvāt |
na ca vāṇijyaṃ yāyāvarasya vihitam, ṣaṭkarmāṇi uñchaśilāyācitakṛṣivāṇijyarūpāṇi | “tribhiḥ” uñchaśilāyācitaiḥ | “dvābhyām” uñchaśilābhyām | “brahmasatreṇa” uñchena | brahmasatraśabdaḥ brāhmaṇānāṃ satataṃ trāyamāṇatvenoñchaparaḥ |
[173] yājñavalkya–viśvāmitrau |
kusūlakumbhīdhānyo vā tryaihiko ‘śvastano ‘pi vā |
jīved vāpi śiloñchena śreyān eṣāṃ paraḥ paraḥ ||
[hārītaḥ] |
śīlenātmavṛttiyāpanād vara iti yāyāvaraḥ |
daśa daśa vā rātrīḥ vasan prayātīti yāyāvaro svarāṣṭrasvavṛttiṣu varayā varayātmānaṃ yāpayatīti yāyāvaraḥ | sa dvividhaḥ – kṛtaprasthāno ‘kṛtaprasthānaś ca |
yasya vidhivad vapanaṃ kārayitvā āgneyyāṃ prātar udite vaiśvānaryeṣṭyā mantrato digdravyādīn pratiṣṭhāpayanti sa kṛtaprasthānaḥ | atha yasya vidhivad vapanaṃ kṛtvā prātar udite vaiśvadevyeṣṭyā mantrato digdravyādīn pravartayanti so ‘kṛtaprasthānaḥ |
brahmavad asya ṛṣitvaṃ saṃpadyate sa prathamayā enaso [174] – vimucyate | dvitīyayā devebhyaḥ prakāśaḥ samānalokaś ca bhavati | tṛtīyayā brahmarṣitvaṃ saṃpadyate | pare ca brahmaṇi sthito bhavati | tasmād brahmaṇi sthito nāmantrataḥ śayanāsanāhāravihārotthānadānopapradānaḥ syāt |
tasya parvasu cayanaṃ cāgniparicaraṇaṃ svayaṃ homo yathāsthānaṃ vopahṛtya gārhapatyāya nivedanam | yathoktānāṃ balihomānāṃ kāraṇaṃ svāśrayaviśeṣāṇām akhyāpanaṃ dharmoparodhinām arthānāṃ varjanam |
athāṣṭau vṛttayo bhavanti | uñchaśilāyācitopapannasandarśanababhru-skannakuddālakāpotīty etāsām ekatamayā vṛtyā varayāvarayā yatra yayā vārtayā yajet | ayonikaṇḍanasthānagosthānadhānyakaraṇalavaṇam iti pañca uñchasthānāni | lūnaṃ śīrṇam anujñātaṃ śilam ity āhuḥ | brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviḍbhyo ‘yācitopapannāṃ śūdrebhyo ‘py eke | āpadi sandarśayann ātmārthe gṛhṇīyāt sandarśī bhikṣetety evam udāharanti |
parṇaśākhayā pañca sīrā vāhayet | babhruskandha ākā[175]lāt | kuddālī rājño bhāgaṃ dadyād anyatra vārthaviśeṣo ‘syopavṛttyupapattau someneṣṭvā agniṃ citvā sarvasvaṃ datvā aṅguṣṭhānāmike saṃyamya grīvāyām āsajya kapotavad ekaikaṃ gṛhṇīyād bhinnād bhinnavṛttir ity eke | ity etābhir vṛttibhir āhṛtya pūrvavat kuryāt |
ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchraṃ pratyāhṛtātmā yamaniyamam iti “yāyāvaraḥ” | paraṃ brahmāmṛtam āpnoti yad gatvā neha jāyate | “yāpanāt” saṅkocanāt |
“aṣṭa” vakṣyamāṇāḥ uñchādyā vṛttayaḥ | vāpanam āgneyī ceṣṭiḥ pūrvedyuḥ karyā | prātar udite tu vaiśvānaryeṣṭyā imāṃ diśaṃ vrahed eva dravyaṃ vrīhīn yavān āharateti |
yasya digdravyādīn pratiṣṭhāpayanti vyavasthāpayanti ṛtvijaḥ sa kṛtaprasthānaḥ |
akṛtaprasthānas tu yasya pūrvavad vāpanādi karayitvā prātar udite vaiśvadeveṣṭyā asyāṃ diśi tiṣṭhatedaṃ vrīhyādidravyam āhāryaṃ tvayeti digdravyādīn pravartayanti ādiśanti ṛtvijaḥ |
sa tathā pramamāgneyī, dvitīyā vaiśvānarī, tṛtīyā vaiśva[176]devī, devebhyaḥ prakāśo dyotamāno bhavatīty arthaḥ |
amantrata iti tṛtīyārthe tasiḥ | vinā mantreṇa na śayanādi kuryāt | “upādānaṃ” pratigrahaḥ | tasya parvasv iti āhitāgnitvena prāptānāṃ vapanādīnāṃ punarvacanaṃ niyamārtham |
gārhapatye nivedanaṃ ca prāptam evāsya vidhīyate | athāśramaviśeṣāḥ yāyāvaratvādayaḥ | “athāṣṭau vṛttayaḥ” iti “ekatamayā” iti vṛttisaṃkaraniṣedhārtham | “yāpayet” gamayet kālān iti śeṣaḥ | gosthānaṃ yatra gobhyo yavādikaṃ dīyate | dhānyakaraṇaṃ khalam, lavanaṃ kṣetraṃ lūyate ‘sminn iti vyutpatyā | anujñātam atra svāmyupekṣitam | “saṃdarśayann” iti saṃnihite svāmini tad upekṣitam api dhānyādi saṃdarśayann eva gṛhṇīyāt | “ātmārtham” iti | vṛttimātropayogino bhogārthaṃ saṃdarśī bhikṣetetyādidravyasvāmy agre ātmānaṃ darśayan bhikṣeta | na tu śabdena yācata ity arthaḥ | babhruskandhaṃ vāhayet | “parṇaśākhayā” ityādi | parṇaḥ palāśaḥ, tasya śākhayā yāvān babhruvarṇavṛṣaskandho āyastā vavṛtaḥ (?) | pañcāṅgulamātraṃ vahayet | arthaprāptāṃ bhuvam | kecit tu caturviṃśatisītā iti paṭhanti | “etac ca vāhanam” ākālād bījavapanakālaṃ yāvat | anyatra vā kuddālād anyatra babhruskandhe bhāgadāne [177] vikalpaḥ | kāpotīm āha — “annaviśeṣebhyaḥ” ityādi | ayācitādilābhenānnaviśeṣātireke sati somayāgādi kṛtvā uktaprakārāṃ kāpotīm anutiṣṭhet | grīvāyām āsajya praveśikādi pātram iti śeṣaḥ |
“bhinnād (?) bhinnavṛttir ity eke” sati (?) sā kāpoty eva bhinnād vidīrṇād goṇyāder yad ekaikabījādānaṃ sā bhinnābhinnavṛttiḥ ity eke manyante | tenātrāpi sarvasvādānādītikartavyatā tulyaiva | kāpotīviśeṣatvād eva cāsya tadanantaram abhidhānam | pūrvavat gārhapatyādinivedanādi ṣaṇmāsān kṛcchrān kuryād ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | kṛcchraḥ viśeṣānupādānāt prājāpatyāḥ | pratyāhṛtātmā yamaniyamān | atrāpi kuryād ity aniṣaṅgaḥ | prtyāhṛto viṣayebhyo nivartita ātmā yena sa pratyāhṛtātmā |
baudhāyanaḥ |
atha śālīnacakracarayāyāvaradharmākāṅkṣiṇāṃ navibhiḥ vṛttibhir vartamānānāṃ teṣāṃ tadvartanāt vṛttir ity ucyate |
śālāśrayatvāc chālīnatvam | anukramacaraṇāc cakracaratvam | vṛtyā varayā yātīti yāyāvaratvam | tā anuvyā[178]khyāsyāmaḥ | ṣaṇṇivartanī kauddālī dhruvā saṃprakṣālinī samūlā pālinī śiloñchā kāpotī siddheccheti navaitāḥ | tāsām evānyāpi daśamī vṛttir bhavaty ā navamavṛtteḥ | tāsām eva cānyāpi daśamī vṛttir bhavaty ā navamavṛtteḥ |
keśaśmaśrulomanakhāni vāpayitvopakalpayet – kṛṣṇājinaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ yaṣṭiṃ vīrudhaṃ kuthahārim iti | traidhātavīyena iṣṭvā prasthāsyati, vaiśvānaryā vā | prātar udite āditye yathāsūtram agniṃ prajvālya gārhapatya ājyaṃ vilāpyotpūya srukdruvaṃ niṣṭapya sruci caturgṛhītaṃ gṛhītvāhavanīye vāstoṣpatyaṃ juhoti – “vāstoṣpate pratijānīhy asmān” ity anudrutya “vāstoṣpate śagmayā saṃsadā ta” iti juhoti | sarva evāhitāgnir ity eke | tad bhūyaḥ sruksruvaṃ niṣṭapya sruci ca kṛtya [179] grāmānte grāmasīmānte vā tiṣṭhati | tatra kuṭīṃ maṭhaṃ vā karoti kṛtaṃ vā praviśati | kṛṣṇājinādīnām upakḷptānāṃ yasminn arthe yena yat prayojanaṃ tena tat kuryāt |
prasiddham agnīnāṃ paricaraṇam, prasiddhaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ yajanam, prasiddham āgrayaṇam, prasiddhaṃ pañcānāṃ mahayajñānām anuprayoga utpannānām oṣadhīnāṃ nirvāpaṇaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ bhavati | “viśvebhyo devebhyo juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmi” iti tūṣṇīṃ vā | athaitā adbhiḥ prokṣati “viśvebhyo devebhyo juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi” iti vā tūṣṇīṃ vā | tāḥ saṃskṛtya sādhayanti | tasyādhyāpanayājanapratigrahā nivartante anye ca yajñakratava iti | haviṣyaṃ ca vratopāyanīyam | tad yathā — sarpirmiśraṃ dadhimiśram apiśitam akṣāralavaṇam paryuṣitam | brahmacaryam ṛtau vā gacchati | parvaṇi parvaṇi keśaśmaśrulomanakhavāpanaṃ śaucavidhiś ca |
“dharmakāṅkṣiṇām” iti | atra dharmakāṅkṣī cakracaro ‘bhimataḥ | yāyāvarād api tasya dharmātiśayakāṅkṣitvā | anukrameṇa vṛttīnāṃ svīkaraṇāt | “upakalpayate” utpādayati | “vīvadho” bhārayaṣṭiḥ | “kuśahāraḥ” svadhitikhanitrādiḥ | “prasthāsyati” iti | yadā prasthāsyati tadā vāstoṣpatīyam uktetikartavyatākaṃ kuryāt | “anudrutya” anūdya | “dṛṣṭaṃ bhavati” yathā darśapūrṇamāsayor dṛṣṭaṃ tathā bhavatīty arthaḥ | “vratopāyanīyam” vartārtham upaharaṇīyam | bahvatīti śeṣaḥ | “apiśitam” māṃsavarjam | śaucavidhiḥ bāhyo mṛjjalādinā | “āntarāḥ” satyadamādinā |
[180]
punar baudhāyanaḥ |
yathaitat ṣaṇnivartanīti, ṣaḍ eva nivartanāni nirupahatāni karoti | bhūmisvāmine bhāgam utsṛjaty anujñātaṃ vā gṛhṇāti | prāk prātarāśāt karṣī syād asyūnanāsikābhyāṃ samuṣkābhyām atudann ārayā muhur muhur abhyucchandayan | yatraitena vidhinā ṣaṇṇivartanāni karotīti ṣaṇṇivartanī |
kauddālīti jalābhyāse kuddālena phālena vā tīkṣṇakāṣṭhena vā khanati bījāny āvapati, kandamūlaphalaśākauṣadhīr niṣpādayati | kuddālena karotīti kauddhālī |
dhruvāyāṃ vartamānaḥ śuklena vāsasā śiro veṣṭayati | “bhūtyai tvā śiro veṣṭayāmi” iti | “brahmavarcasam asi brahmavarcasāya tvā” iti kṛṣṇājinam ādatte | abliṅgābhiḥ pavitram | “balam asi balāya tvā” iti kamaṇḍalum | “dhānyam asi puṣṭyai tvā” iti vīvadham | “sakhā mā gopāya” iti daṇḍam |
athopaniṣkramya | vyāhṛtīr japitvā diśām anumantraṇam japati —
pṛthivī cāntarikṣaṃ ca dhauś ca nakṣatrāṇi ca yā diśaḥ |
agnir vāyuś ca sūryaś ca pāntu māṃ pati devatāḥ || iti |
mānastokīṃ ca japitvā grāmaṃ praviśya gṛhadvāre gṛhadvāra ātmānaṃ vīvadhena saha darśanāt sandarśanīty ācakṣate |
[181]                        vṛtter vṛtter avārtāyām avārtāyāṃ tathaiva ca |
tasyāṃ dhruvaṃ vartamānā dhruveti parikīrtitā ||
saṃprakṣālanīti | utpannānām oṣadhīnāṃ prakṣepaṇaṃ nikṣepaṇam nāsti nicayo vā bhājanāni saṃprakṣālya nyubjatīti saṃprakṣālanī |
samūheti | avāritasthāneṣu pathi vā kṣetreṣu vāpratihatāvakāśeṣu vā yatra yatrauṣadhayo vidyante tatra tatra samūhanyā samūhya tābhir vartayatīti samūhā |
pālanīti | ahaṃsikety evedam uktaṃ bhavati | tuṣavihītāṃs taṇḍulān icchati sajjanebhyo bījāni vā pālayatīti pālanī |
śiloñcheti | avāritasthaleṣu vā pathi vā kṣetreṣu vāpratihatāvakāśeṣu vā yatra yatrauṣadhayo vidyante tatra tatraikaikaṃ kaṇiśam uñchayitvā kāle kāle śilair vartayatīti śiloñchā |
kāpoteti | avāritasthāneṣu pathi vā kṣetreṣu vāpratihatāvakāśeṣu vā yatra yatrauṣadhayo vidyante tatra tatrāṅgulibhyāṃ [182] ekaikām oṣadhim uñchayitvā sandarśanāt kapotavad iti kāpotī |
siddheccheti | vṛttibhiḥ śrānto vṛddhatvād dhātukṣayād vā sajjanebhyo siddham annam icchatīti siddhecchā |
tasyātmasamāropaṇaṃ vidyate saṃnyāsivad upacāraḥ pavitrakāṣāyavāsovarjam |
vānyāpi vṛkṣagulmalatāvallyoṣadhīnāṃ ca tṛṣṇauṣadhīnāṃ ca śyāmākanīvārajarttilādīnāṃ vānyābhir vartayatīti vānyā | yady etāsu vṛttiṣv āsthāpanaṃ tābhyo ‘vasīdatīti | athāpy udāharanti —
parāṃ vṛttim upāśritya parāṃ paścād upāśritaḥ |
parivrājye sthito mūḍhaḥ sa icched gṛhamedhitām || iti |
“nivartanī” daśahastena daṇḍena triṃśaddaṇḍaparimitā bhūmiḥ nivartanam | tad dhastāt trir upahatāni svakṛṣṭāni | “bhūmisvāmine” bhūmir asyāstīti vrīhyādidarśaṇād iniḥ | “prāk prātarāśeti” avyayībhāvaḥ | tena divābhojanāt pūrvaṃ karṣed ity arthaḥ | astu masti nāma (?) | “samuṣkaḥ" sāṇḍaḥ | “abhyucchandayan” viśrāmayan | “karoti” saṃskaroti | “vṛtte” ādravyatevya (?) atikrānte | “avārtāyām avārāyam” iti vīpsā nairantaryapradarśanārtham | “prakṣepaṇam” kusūlādau dhānyāder nikṣepaṇam | parahaste dhāraṇaṃ sa prakārāntareṇāpi kartavya ity arthaḥ | “apratihatā[183]vakāśeṣu” anivāritaprasareṣu sandarśanenaiva grahaṇāt | tasya samāropaṇaṃ sasyasiddhoñchavṛtter agnīnām ātmani samāropaṇam bhavati |
manuḥ |
vartayaṃś ca śiloñchābhyām agnihotraparāyaṇaḥ |
iṣṭīḥ pārvāyanāntīyāḥ kevalā nirvapet sadā ||
na lokavṛttaṃ varteta vṛttihetoḥ kathaṃcana |
ajihmām aśaṭhāṃ śuddhāṃ jīved brāhmaṇajīvikām ||
santoṣaṃ param āsthāya sukhārthī saṃyato bhavet |
santoṣamūlaṃ hi sukhaṃ duḥkhamūlaṃ viparyayaḥ ||
“pārvāyanāntīyāḥ” parva ca ayanāntaś ca tayor bhāvaḥ | stārkikarmaṇaṃ (?) kṛtvā vṛddhācchaḥ (vṛddhoñcaḥ?) kartavyaḥ | “lokavṛttaṃ” dhanikastutyādi | “ajihmāṃ” kāuṭilyavarjitām | “aśaṭhāṃ” garvādirahitām | “śuddhāṃ vṛttiṃ” saṅkaravarjitām | “jīvet” brāhmaṇajīvikāṃ kuryāt |
[184]
9
athāpadvṛtiḥ
tatra manuḥ |
śiloñcham apy ādadīta vipro jīvan yatas tataḥ |
pratigrahāc chilaḥ śreyān tato ‘py uñchaḥ praśasyate ||
mukhyayā vṛttyā jīvanābhāve yatas tato ‘pakṛṣṭād api śiloñchāv ādadīta, na tu pratigraham | yasmāt pratigrahāpekṣayā śiloñchau praśastau | tayor abhāve pratigrahād apy upādadīta |
manuḥ |
varaṃ svadharmo viguṇo na pārakyaḥ svanuṣṭhitaḥ |
paradharmeṇa jīvan hi sadyaḥ patati jātitaḥ ||
[185] śaṅkhalikhitau |
tasya svadharma eva śreyān svalpo ‘py anuṣṭhito nāvarasevitaḥ | jaghanyasevī ha brāhmaṇo jaghanyeṣūpapadyeta | tasmāt praśastasamayācāraḥ sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt | nātmanas tatas tṛptim icchet | kāmaṃ trayāṇāṃ varṇānāṃ yājanaṃ kurvīta | dharmeṇa tebhyo vṛttim ādāya dharmakṛtyeṣūpaharet |
“tasya” brāhmaṇasya | “svadharmaḥ” svavṛttyupāyaḥ | “svalpo ‘pi” yathākathaṃcid anuṣṭhito ‘pi | “avaraḥ” kṣatriyādiḥ | praśastānāṃ śiṣṭānāṃ samayaḥ siddhāntaḥ tam ācaratīti “praśastasamayācāraḥ” | “sarvataḥ” śūdrāder api gṛhṇīyāt | “tataḥ” śūdrādipratigraheṇa | “tṛptiṃ” bhogākāram avaraṃ (?) | “dharmeṇa” śāstrataḥ |
atrāpatkalpe brāhmaṇasyety anuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāḥ sarveṣām | pūrvaḥ pūrvo guruḥ | tadalābhe kṣatravṛttiḥ, tadalābhe vaiśyavṛttiḥ |
[186] “sarveṣām” apraśastānām api | “guruḥ” doṣāvahaḥ | yac ca manuvacanam “pratigrahaḥ pratyavaraḥ” iti pratigrahanindā, sā praśastayājanāpekṣayā |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kāmaṃ vā svayaṃ kṛṣyotpādya tilān vikrīṇīrann anṛṇatvenāyājyayājanaṃ prasiddham iti graho ‘stīti | athāpy udāharanti |
agnaya iva dīpyante japahomair dvijottamāḥ |
pratigraheṇa śāmyanti udakenāgnayo yathā || |
“pratigraheṇa” niṣiddheneti śeṣaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
āpatkāle brāhmaṇasya mātāpitṛmato bahubhṛtyasyānantarikā vṛttir iti vikalpaḥ |
[187]        yatra brāhmaṇe mātāpitṛṣu caritraguṇasaṃpanneṣu vṛtter daurbalyam ākhyeyam |
yad brūyus te sa dharmaḥ, kṣātre ‘bhiniveśas trayāṇāṃ brāhmaṇakarmaṇāṃ vyāvṛttau yājanādhyāpanapratigrahāṇām | evam apy ajīvan vaiśyavṛttim evopajīvet |
“vikalpo” vimarśaḥ | “kṣātre ‘bhiniveśaḥ” kṣatriyavṛttiniṣṭhatvam |
śiloñcādyanantaraṃ budhaḥ |
tadasaṃbhave kṣatravṛttyā vaiśyavṛttyā vā | āpatkalpe asādhubhyaḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt | vṛttisaṃkaraṃ na kuryāt karma, varṇasaṃkaraṃ kulaśuddhyartham |
“āpatkalpe” vaiśavṛttyasaṃbhave | asādhur atra yadīyapratigrahasya kṛṣyādyapekṣayāpakarṣaḥ | “śreyān svadharmo viguṇaḥ” ity pratigrahādeḥ śreyatvābhidhānaṃ śūdrādipratigrahaviṣayam |
yājanādhyāpanapratigrahānuvṛttau baudhāyanaḥ |
tadaśaktau kṣatradharmeṇa jīvet, pratyanantaratvāt | neti gautamaḥ | hāsyārtham api brāhmaṇaḥ āyudhaṃ nādadīta | atyugro hi kṣatradharmo brāhmaṇasya |
[188]        athāpy udāharanti |
brāhmaṇārthe gavārthe vā varṇānāṃ cāpi saṃkare |
gṛhṇīyātāṃ vipraviśau śastraṃ dharmavyapekṣayā ||
vaiśyavṛttir anuṣṭheyā | kṣatradharmeṇa śastradhāraṇavyatirikeneti boddhavyam, brāhmaṇasya dharmārtham eva śastradhāraṇavidhānāt |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṣātreṇa dharmeṇājīved viśāṃ vāpy āpadi dvijaḥ |
nistīryārthe samutsṛjya pāvyātmānaṃ bhavet pathi ||
“nistīrye” āpadam iti vipariṇatyānuṣaṅgaḥ |
nāradaḥ |
āpady anantarā vṛttir brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
vaiśyavṛttis tataś coktā na jaghanyā kadācana ||
na kathaṃcana kurvīta brāhmaṇaḥ karma vārṣalam |
vṛṣalaḥ karma vā brāhmaṃ patanīye hi te tayoḥ ||
[189]        utkṛṣṭaṃ cāvakṛṣṭaṃ ca tayoḥ karma na vidyate |
madhyame karmaṇī hitvā sarvasādhāraṇe hi te ||
āpadaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ tīrtvā kṣatravṛttyārjite dhane |
utsṛjet kṣatravṛttiṃ tāṃ kṛtvā pāvanam ātmanaḥ ||
tasyām eva tu yo vṛttau brāhmaṇo ramate rasāt |
kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaś cyuto mārgāt so ‘pāṅkteyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
[kāṇḍapṛṣṭho brāhmaṇādhamaḥ]
baudhāyanaḥ |
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijyaṃ prakurvīta svayaṃ kṛtam |
āpatkalpe ‘nyathā kurvann enasā yujyate dvijaḥ ||
labdhakāmaḥ pitṝn devān brāhmaṇāṃś cāpi pūjayet |
te tuṣṭāḥ tasya taṃ doṣaṃ śamayanti na saṃśayaḥ ||
[190]        vaṇik kusīdī dadyāt tu vastragokāñcanādikam |
kṛṣīvalo ‘nnapānāni yānaśayyāsanāni ca ||
paṇebhyo viṃśakaṃ datvā paśusvarṇādikaṃ śatam |
vaṇik kusīdy adoṣaḥ syād brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojane ||
mahābhārate |
sthūlāc chulkāc ca vacanāt mṛdusnigdhāc ca bhojanāt |
aparapreṣyabhāvāc ca bhūya icchan vrajaty adhaḥ ||
atra sarvavākyeṣu brāhmaṇasyāpadi vaiśyavṛttyanuvṛttir draṣṭavyā |
[191]
10
atha kṛṣiḥ
tatra manuḥ |
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvaṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo ‘pi vā |
hiṃsāprāyaṃ parādhīnāṃ kṛṣiṃ yatnena varjayet ||
kṛṣiṃ sādhv iti manyante sā vṛttiḥ sadvigarhitā |
bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃś caiva hanti kāṣṭham ayomukham ||
iyaṃ ca kṛṣir vigarhitā itaravaiśyavṛttyasaṃbhave draṣṭavyā |
baudhāyanaḥ |
kṛṣir vedavināśāya vedaḥ kṛṣivināśakaḥ |
śaktimān ubhayaṃ kuryād aśaktas tu kṛṣiṃ tyajet ||
hārītaḥ |
sahasūnaṃ lāṅgalam | tat brāhmaṇe na vidyate | āpady ācaritatād yadi kuryād yathārthaṃ kuryāt |
“yathārthaṃ” yāvatārthenāpan nistīryate tāvato ‘natikrameṇa |
[192] baudhāyanaḥ |
prāk prātarāśāt karṣī syāt | asyūtanāsikābhyāṃ samuṣkābhyām atudann ārayā muhur muhur abhyucchandayan |
paithīnasiḥ |
kṣetraṃ sāṇḍābhyām anaḍubhyāṃ kṛṣīta nidāghe ca yaḥ | prayacchaṃs tilān vaped ity arivallarī lāṅgalamiti vijñāyate | parīro asyendu | suśevaṃ kalyāṇanāsikaṃ somo bāhyasya prāpnotīti somepitsatu (? somapitsaru) | tad utkṛṣati gāṃ vā [? jāvivāṃśavaja] prāpnoti | tasmāt kṛṣati tilān vaped iti annārthaṃ vacanam | parīraval lāṅgalam ityādikam arthavādaḥ | karṣaṇaḥ saiva ajājyayājanapratiṣiddhapratigrahābhyāṃ kṛṣis tūtpannam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
tasmāt sāṇḍābhyām anaḍudbhyām anasyūtābhyāṃ prāk prātarāśakarṣī syāt | nidāghe ‘paḥ | prayacchel lāṅgalaṃ pavīravaṃ suśevaṃ [193] somapitsaru | tad udvapati gām aviṃ cānān aśvān aśvatarakharoṣṭrāṃś ca praphavya ca pīvarīm | prasthāvad rathavāhanam iti | lāṅgalaṃ pavīrat vīravat sumanuṣyavad anaḍudvat | suśevaṃ kalyāṇanāsikaṃ kalyāṇī hi asya nāsikā nāsikayodvapati dūre ‘pavidhyati | somapitsaru somo hy asya prāpnoti tatsaru | tad udvapati | gāṃ cāvim ajān aśvān aśvatarakharoṣṭrāṃś ca | prapharvyaṃ ca pīvarīṃ darśanīyāṃ kalyāṇīṃ prathamayuvatīm |
parīvat parīraṃ phālaḥ tadval lāṅgalaṃ pariḥ śalyo bhavati, “yad dhi punāti bhūmiṃ tadvat parīravam” iti niruktakāravacanāt | parīravad ity asya pradarśanārtham āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “manuṣyavad anaḍudvad” iti | “suśevaṃ” sumukham | “nāsikā” halāgrabhāgaḥ | “apavidhyati” madam utsādayati | “somapī” iti somam āpnotīti ṇinipratyayāntam | dhātvākārasya chāndaso lopaḥ, somo yavādiḥ, “annaṃ vai somaḥ” iti śatapathaśruteḥ | “tsaruḥ” yasmin sthāne vṛtyā vāhyate | “tad udvapati” utpādayati | “prapharvyāṃ” prakṛṣṭaparvavatīṃ chāndaso varṇavikāraḥ |
hārītaḥ |
āpatsu ca kṛṣiṃ prāhuḥ kṛṣed ya āyojanavad āyojya bālavṛddhakṛśārtādīn varjayann adīrgheṇa pratodena śanair vāhayed [194] yaṣṭimuṣṭiloṣṭāratni-jānuyoktraiḥ drāvaṇākrośanaprabhṛtibhir nābhinighnīta | na parvasu (?) na saṃdhivelayor vāhayet | dvihalakṛte vā pariyogo ‘vamṛjya mokṣaṇam | mṛduvac chāyāyāṃ pānāhārādīn dadyāt | kṛmikīṭakilītakadaṃśamaśakamakṣikānivṛtti-sṛmaraśītavātoṣṇavarṣeṣu uddharaṇakarṇakaṇḍūyanadhūmāgninivāta-pracchannasamupastīrṇādiṣu ca gopāyet | sannabhagnavilagnārttānauṣadhayavasa-lavaṇasnehādiṣu yathārthaṃ pavanalavanamardanapavanātihāreṣu balihomādi kuryāt |
devalaḥ |
snātvānaḍuho ‘laṃkṛtya brāhmaṇān bhojayet saurabheyāṇāṃ puṇyam iti | ṣaḍbhāgaṃ rājñe datvā pañcamād brāhmaṇān datvā toṣayet |
athāpy atrodāharanti |
yūpo ‘yaṃ nihito madhye medhinām iha karṣakaiḥ |
tasmād atandrito dadyād annadhānyārthadakṣiṇāḥ ||
bhūmiṃ bhitvauṣadhīḥ chittvā hatvā kīṭapipīlikān |
punanti khalayajñena karṣakā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
[195] “āyonajavat” āyojanaṃ agnicayane gṛhye coktam tadvat | “aratniḥ” prasṛtāṅgulir hastaḥ | “drāvaṇam” udvāsanam | “dvihalakṛte” punaḥkarṣaṇāya | “pariyogo” hale saṃyojanam | parvādāv api kuryād ity arthaḥ | “avamṛjya” hastādinā dhūlikardamādīn apanīya | “mṛduvat” akrudhyan | “kilītakaḥ” gavādistanādideśe yo bhavati raktapaḥ kīṭaviśeṣaḥ | “nivṛttayaḥ” camarāḥ | “sṛmarāḥ” kṣudramakṣikā gaṇacāriṇyaḥ | kṛmikīṭādiṣv eteṣūpdraveṣu yathāyogaṃ samuddharaṇādinā gopāyet | “sannaḥ” avasannaḥ | “vilagnaḥ” paṅkādau magnaḥ | “yathārthaṃ” etān ārtān auṣadhayavasādiṣu bhojayed ity arthaḥ | “pavanaṃ” śramāpanayanam | “atihāraḥ” khalād gṛhānayanam | “saurabheyāṇāṃ puṇyaṃ” kalyāṇam astv iti brāhmaṇān bhojayed ity arthaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājñe datvā ca ṣaḍbhāgaṃ devatānāṃ ca viṃśakam |
triṃśadbhāgaṃ tu viprāṇāṃ kṛṣiṃ kṛtvā na doṣabhāk ||
hārītaḥ |
aṣṭagavaṃ dharmahalaṃ ṣaḍgavaṃ jīvitārthinām |
[196]        caturgavaṃ nṛśaṃsānāṃ dvigavaṃ brahmaghātinām ||
tathā |
bālānāṃ damanaṃ caiva vāhanaṃ na ca śasyate |
vṛddhānāṃ durbalānāṃ ca prajāpativaco yathā ||
devalaḥ |
puṃstvopaghātaṃ nāsye vā vāhanānāṃ na kārayet |
vṛddhaṃ yugme na yuñjīta jīrṇaṃ vyādhitam eva ca ||
brahmapurāṇe |
brahmā viṣṇuś ca rudraś ca kaśyapaḥ surabhī tathā |
indraḥ pracetāḥ parjanyaḥ śeṣacandrārkavahnayaḥ ||
baladevo halaṃ bhūmiḥ ṛṣabhau rāmalakṣaṇau |
rakṣoghnau jānakī sītā yugaṃ gamanam eva ca ||
ete dvāviṃśatiḥ proktāḥ prajānāṃ patayaḥ śubhāḥ |
gā maṅgaleṣu saṃpūjyāḥ kṛṣyārambhe mahotsave ||
[197]        arghyaiḥ puṣpais tathā dhūpair mālyai ratnaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
sadakṣiṇair vidhānena homair brāhmaṇatarpaṇaiḥ ||
tataḥ śakunibhuktena vādyaśabdena bhūriṇā |
halena vāhayed bhūmiṃ svayaṃ snātaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ ||
pūrvottarāṃ diśaṃ gacchet kramāt paurandarīṃ śubhām |
kṛtvā pradakṣiṇāvartam aiśānyābhimukhas tataḥ ||
vimucya vṛṣabhau bījaṃ puruṣo lakṣaṇānvitaḥ |
hṛṣṭaḥ suvāsāḥ sragvī ca suvarṇajalasecitam ||
suvarṇapātrasaṃyuktaṃ prāṅmukho nirvaped bhuvi |
uptvā bījaṃ ca tatraiva bhoktavyaṃ bandhubhiḥ saha ||
utsavaś caiva kartavyo gītavādyasamākulaḥ ||
tathā |
na ṣaṇḍaṃ vāhayed dhenuṃ na gāṃ bhāreṇa pīḍayet |
na vāhanīyāḥ śiśire vṛṣabhā yatra kutracit ||
devīpurāṇe |
sudṛṣṭaṃ pṛṣṭhadeśe tu skandhadeśe tu yo vṛṣam |
vāhayed gurubhāreṇa sa tu bhāraśataṃ vahet ||
[198] rāmāyaṇe |
yo durbalaṃ pariśrāntaṃ bāliśaṃ pāpanirbhayaḥ |
vāhayiṣyaty anaḍvāhaṃ sa goghnaḥ pāpam āpsyati ||
śataṃ samarthaṃ balinaṃ yo vṛṣaṃ vāhayiṣyati |
grāsopahārasaṃyuktaṃ na sa pāpam avāpsyati ||
tatra sarvavākyeṣv āpadi vaiśyavṛttiṣu brāhmaṇādhikāro draṣṭavyaḥ |
[199]
11
atha vāṇijyam
tatra manuḥ |
idaṃ tu dharmavaikalyāt tyajato dharmanaipuṇam |
viṭpaṇyam uddhṛtoddhāraṃ vikreyaṃ vṛttivardhanam ||
sarvān rasān apoheta kṛtānnaṃ tu tilaiḥ saha |
aśmano lavaṇaṃ caiva paśavo ye ca mānavāḥ ||
sarvaṃ ca tāntavaṃ raktaṃ śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca |
ap cet syur araktāni phalamūle tathauṣadhīḥ ||
apaḥ śastraṃ viṣaṃ māṃsaṃ somaṃ gandhāṃśca sarvaśaḥ |
āraṇyāṃś ca paśūn sarvān daṃṣṭriṇaśca vayāṃsi ca |
[200]        madyaṃ nīlīṃ ca lākṣāṃ ca sarvāṃś caikaśaphāṃs tathā ||
trapu sīsaṃ tathā lauhaṃ taijasāni ca sarvaśaḥ |
bālāṃś carma tathāsthīni vasāḥ snāvāni rocanām ||
“viṭpaṇyaṃ” viśāṃ paṇyam | “uddhṛtoddhāraṃ” uddhriyata iti uddhāro māṃsādi taduddhṛtam | “rasāḥ” atra mādhuryādiṣaḍrasapradhānāni dravyāṇi | “apoheta” tyajet | “kṛtānnaṃ” pakvānnam | “aśmānaḥ” pāṣāṇāḥ padmarāgādyāḥ | rasatvena lavaṇādīnāṃ niṣedhe siddhe punar niṣedho doṣātiśayajñāpanārthaḥ | “snāvāni” snāyūni |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
phalopalakṣaumasomamanuṣyāpūpavīrudhaḥ |
tilaudanarasakṣāradadhikṣīraghṛtaṃ jalam ||
śastrāsavaṃ madhūcchiṣṭaṃ madhulākṣāśmabarhiṣaḥ |
mṛccarmapuṣpakutapakeśatakraviṣakṣitīḥ ||
[201]        kauśeyanīlalavaṇaṃ māṃsaikaśaphasīsakam |
śākāny auṣadhipiṇyākapaśugandhāṃs tathaiva ca ||
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvaṃs tu vikrīṇīta na jātucit |
“vīrudhaḥ” guḍūcyādayaḥ | “kṣārāḥ” yavakṣārādayaḥ |
kusumbhaṃ nālikeraṃ ca vṛntākaṃ pūtikaṃ tathā |
somaḥ śleṣmātako yajñas toyaṃ dharmaś ca sarvaśaḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
evam apy ajīvan vaiśyavṛttim evopajīvet tatra saṃvatsarasatrāṇām ādhāram uddhṛtya śeṣeṇa dravyam āharet | anyatra puṣparasagandhamadyamāṃsaudana-tilaśākadadhipayaḥsomajalamadhunīlakauśeyapānabhakṣyajatulohapāṣāṇa-kutapaśatraviṣamānuṣakṣaumakṛṣṇājinaikaśaphaprabhṛtibhyaḥ |
“saṃvatsarasatrāṇām” iti saṃvatsaraṃ yāvat avaśyakartavyadharmārthaṃ dhanam avasthāpya “śeṣeṇa” vāṇijyena (?) vāṇijyaṃ kuryāt |
[202] brāhmaṇānuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
tasyāpaṇyaṃ gandharasakṛtānnatilamāṃsakṣaumājināni raktāni nirṇikte vāsasī kṣīraṃ savikāraṃ mūlaphalam auṣadhaṃ madhumāṃsatṛṇodakāpathyāni, paśavaś ca hiṃsāsaṃyoge puruṣavaśākumārīvehataś ca nityaṃ bhūmivrīhiyavājāvya-ṛṣabhadhenvanaḍuhaś caike |
“apathyaṃ” viṣādi | “vaśā” vandhyā gauḥ | “kumārī” alpavayaskā gaur eva | “vehat” garbhaghātinī gauḥ |
apaṇyam ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ |
grāmyapaśūnām ekaśaphāḥ | keśinaś ca sarve cāraṇyāḥ paśavo vasāṃsi daṃṣṭriṇaś ca |
[203] āpastambaḥ |
avihitā vāṇijyā brāhmaṇasya | yadi vyavaharet paṇyānām apaṇyāni vyudasyan | manuṣyān rasān nāgān gandhān annaṃ carma gavāṃ vaśāṃ śleṣmodake tokmakiṇve pippalīmarīce dhānyaṃ māṃsam āyudhaṃ sukṛtāśāṃ ca | tilataṇḍulāṃs tv eva dhānyasya viśeṣeṇa na vikrīṇīyāt |
“paṇyānām apaṇyāni vyudasyan” paṇyānāṃ vrīhyādīnāṃ apaṇyāni vyudasyan apanayan | “śeṣma” carmādisandhānahetur vṛkṣaniryāsaḥ | “tokma” īṣadvirūḍhaṃ bījam | “kiṇvaṃ” surākāraṇadravyam | “sukṛtā” sadvihitā | dhānyasya madhye tilataṇḍulā ity arthaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
vidyā pūrtam iṣṭaṃ gāvaḥ śaṅkhaś corṇā cety avikreyāṇi |
avikreyāṇi brāhmaṇena ity upakramya uśanāḥ |
śuktiviṣatuṣakutapasalilasomagandhakṣaumaraktavastrakauśeyacarmakambala-prabhṛtīti |
[204] “kutapo” nepālakambalaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
pitṝn vā eṣa vikrīṇīte yas tilān vikrīṇīte | prāṇān vā eṣa vikrīṇīte yas taṇḍulān vikrīṇīte | sukṛtānaṃśān vā eṣa vikrīṇīte yaḥ paṇamāno duhitaraṃ dadāti |
apaṇyam ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭha-manu-paiṭhīnasi-baudhāyanāś ca śloke dhānyānāṃ tilān
āhuḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
bhojanābhyañjanādānād yad anyat kurute tilaiḥ |
kṛmir bhūtvā sa viṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati ||
yamaḥ |
rasagandhatilānāṃ ca lākṣālavaṇasarpiṣām |
bhūgo’gnikṣīrameṣāṇāṃ tailāśvajalavāsasām ||
māṃsakṛṣṇājinānāṃ ca vikretā sarvavikrayī |
snehadravyarasānāṃ ca satvānāṃ jīvatām api ||
[205]        vikretā dānapuṇyānāṃ procyate bhrūṇahā budhaiḥ |
vaktā ca dānapuṇyānāṃ śapathe so ‘pi vikrayī ||
trapusīsāyasānāṃ ca dhātūnām api sarvaśaḥ |
vajraśuktipravālānāṃ maṇīnāṃ mākṣikasya ca ||
pratirūpakakartā ca bhrūṇahā so ‘pi kathyate |
“snehadravyam” tilādi | “satvānāṃ” prāṇinām | “śapathe” śapathakaraṇe | dānapuṇyānāṃ vaktety anvayaḥ |
mahābhārate |
vikrayaṃ hīha somasya garhayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
ye vainaṃ krīṇate tāta ye ca vikrīṇate janāḥ ||
te vai vaivasvataṃ prāpya rauravaṃ yānti sarvaśaḥ |
chāgaleyaḥ |
na vedavikrayaṃ kuryād agnivikrayam eva ca |
yo dharmavikraye puṃsāṃ narakaḥ sa prajāyate ||
tasmān na vikrayo vede agnihotre tathaiva ca |
prakhyāpanaṃ prādhyayanaṃ praśnapūrvaḥ pratigrahaḥ ||
[206]        yājanādhyāpane vādaḥ ṣaḍvidho vedavikrayaḥ |
anantaraśloke vyāso ‘pi |
“agnihotraśabdaḥ” atra tretāgniparaḥ | “yājanādhyāpane” cātra bhṛtipūrvake | “vādaḥ” atra paṇapūrvakaḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
vedavikrayajaṃ neṣṭaṃ striyā yac cārjitaṃ dhanam |
na deyaṃ pitṛdevebhyo yac ca kṣībāt khagādhipa ||
anuyogena yo dadyād brāhmaṇāya pratigraham |
sa pūrvaṃ narakaṃ yāti brāhmaṇas tadanantaram ||
vedākṣarāṇi yāvanti niyujyante ‘rthakāraṇāt |
tāvatīr bhrūṇahatyās tu vedavikrayy avāpnuyāt ||
yamaḥ |
gavāṃ vikrayakārī tu gavi romāṇi yāni tu |
tāvad varṣasahasrāṇi gavāṃ goṣṭhe kṛmir bhavet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na vikrīṇīyād avikreyāṇi tiladadhikṣaudralavaṇalākṣāmadyamāṃsakṛtānnastrī-puruṣahastyaśvagovṛṣagandharasakṣaumakṛṣṇājinasomodakanīlavikrayāt sadyaḥ patati brāhmaṇaḥ |
[207] “nīlaṃ” nīlavarṇavastram |
hārīto ‘pi | manu-vasiṣṭha-śātātapāś ca śloke |
na tv evānavikreyavikrayī syāt |
evaṃ tv āha |
sadyaḥ patati māṃsena lākṣayā lavaṇena ca |
manuḥ |
itareṣām apaṇyānāṃ vikrayād iha kāmataḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ saptarātreṇa vaiśyabhāvaṃ niyacchati ||
“itareṣāṃ” lākṣālavaṇamāṃsakṣīrebhyo ‘nyeṣām | “niyacchati” prāpnoti |
hārītaḥ |
yas tu saṃbhṛtya saṃbhāraṃ durbhikṣaṃ samudīkṣate |
[208]        hīnārghamānadātā vai prokto durbhikṣakaṇṭakaḥ ||
hīnasaṅkaramānais tu yaś caiva harate malam |
sa malaḥ procyate prājñaiḥ prajānāṃ malahārakaḥ ||
“hīnārghamānadāthā” hīnārghena mānena bahumūlyam ādāya vikretety arthaḥ | “durbhikṣakaṇṭakaḥ” durbhikṣe janānāṃ kaṇṭakaḥ śatruḥ | “hīnasaṅkaramānaiḥ” hīnenālpenādhikaṃ "saṅkareṇa" apadravyamiśrīkaraṇena | "mānena" iti viśeṣaṇena meyadravyālpatvahetunā | etair yo harate malaṃ malinaṃ dravyam, pāpahetutvāt |
atha lāṅgalasya gavādihetupratipādakaṃ mantram abhidhāya, vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kathaṃ hi lāṅgalam udvapet anyatra dānyavikrayāt |
“udvapet” utpādayet gavādīn iti śeṣaḥ |
manuḥ |
kāmam utpādya kṛṣyāṃ tu svayam eva kṛṣīvalaḥ |
vikrīṇīta tilān śuddhān dharmārtham acirasthitān ||
“acirasthitān” arghādhikyārthaṃ ciram anavasthitān | “dharmārthaṃ” dhanasādhyāvaśyakarmasiddhyartham,
aśaktau bheṣajasyārthe yajñahetos tathaiva ca |
[209]        yady avaśyaṃ ca vikreyāḥ tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ ||
iti nāradavacanāt |
tathā |
rasā rasaiḥ nimātavyā na tv evaṃ lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ |
kṛtānnaṃ cākṛtānnena tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ ||
“kṛtānnam” odanaṃ saktvādi | “akṛtānnaṃ” vrīhyādi | “nimātavyāḥ” parivartayitavyāḥ |
gautamaḥ |
niyamanaṃ rasānāṃ rasaiḥ | paśūnāṃ ca | lavaṇakṛtānnayor tilānāṃ ca | samenāmena tu pakvasya saṃpratyarthe |
“paśūnāṃ ca” paśubhir ity arthaḥ | “saṃpratyarthe” āmasya tatkālopayoge; yasyaiva pakvānnavikrayo niṣiddhas taṃ praty eva pratiprasavāt |
na tilās tailatāṃ yānti na gāvo yānti kṣīratām |
rasas teṣām avikreyo yathā gāvas tathā tilāḥ ||
[210] hārītaḥ |
niyamas tu rasādīnāṃ vikrayo naiva duṣyati |
yajñārthaṃ dakṣiṇānāṃ ca prajāpativaco yathā ||
rasādīnāṃ niyamo na duṣyati | yajñārthaṃ dakṣiṇānāṃ vikrayo ‘pi na duṣyatīty arthaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
rasā rasaiḥ samato hīnato vā nimātavyā na tv eva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ | tilataṇḍulapakvānnavidyāmanuṣyāś ca vihitāḥ parivartakena |
“samato” hīnato vā iti tṛtīyāntāt tasiḥ | tena samair nyūnair vā parivartanīyā rasāḥ, na tv adhikaṃ grāhyam ity arthaḥ |
tilataṇḍulānuvṛttau āpastambaḥ |
vihitaś caiṣāṃ mitho niyamo ‘nnena cānnasya manuṣyāṇāṃ ca manuṣyai rasānāṃ ca rasair gandhānāṃ ca gandhair vidyayā ca vidyānām akrītapaṇyair vyavaharet | muñjabalvajais tṛṇakāṣṭhair avikṛtair nātyantam anvavasyet tatra vṛttiṃ prāpya viramet |
“akrītapaṇyaiḥ” krayavyatiriktaprakāropapannaiḥ | muñja[211]balvajayos tṛṇaśabdenopāttayor api vikrayābhyanujñānārtham upādānam | “anvavasyet” āsakto bhavet | “vṛttiṃ” mukhyām iti śeṣaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
tṛṇakāṣṭham avikṛtaṃ vikreyam | athāpy udāharanti |
paśavaś caikatodantā aśmā ca lavanoddhṛtaḥ |
etad brāhmaṇato ‘paṇyaṃ tantuś cārajanīkṛtaḥ || iti |
“aśmā” pāṣāṇāḥ | “lavaṇoddhṛtaḥ” lavaṇād anyaḥ | ayaṃ cāśmano vikrayo ‘tyantāpadi boddhavyaḥ, smṛtyantare vikrayaniṣedhāt | “arajanīkṛtaḥ” kusumbhādibhiḥ na raktīkṛtaḥ |
nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya tu vikreyaṃ sūkṣmaṃ dāru tṛṇāni ca |
[212]        gandhadravyair erakā ca gurutūlakuśād ṛte ||
svayaṃ śīrṇaṃ ca vidalaṃ phalānāṃ badareṅgude |
rajjuḥ kārpāsikaṃ sūtraṃ tac ced avikṛtaṃ bhavet ||
“erakā” tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ | “tūlaṃ” śālmalyādiḥ | “vidalaṃ” valkalam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
suddhakāṣṭhabadareṅgudabilvakārpāsikasūtrair avikṛtair dravyam āharet |
hārītaḥ |
pūrvavṛttiṣu ajīvan śuṣkakāṣṭhatṛṇavikrayaṃ vā kuryāt | gā eva vā rakṣet |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
āpadgato brāhmaṇaḥ kāmaṃ jijīviṣuḥ ekāntaritāṃ vṛttim āsthāya paṇyaṃ paṇyam iti brūyāt | sakṛd evaikaṃ mūlyam icchet |
[213]
atha pāśupālyam
tatra śaṅkhalikhitau |
gāvo rakṣet | tāsu nāpītāsu pibet | na tiṣṭhantīṣūpaviśet | na svayam utthāpayet śanair ārdraśākhayā pṛṣṭhato ‘bhihanyāt | nātīrthena viṣamenālpodake cāvatārayet | bālavṛddharogārtāḥ śāntā upāsīta | śaktitaḥ pratīkāraṃ ca kuryāt | gavām eva dharmo ‘nyathā viplavaḥ |
“upāsīta” upacaret | gavām eva dharmo nājādīnām |
brahmapurāṇe |
paribhūyātha gāṃ mohād avīci narake naraḥ |
gobhir niśvāsanirbhinno dahyate narakāgninā ||
tasmān mārgaṃ nayetāśu kṣīrivṛkṣotthavalkalaiḥ |
he śrīmātas tvam āyāhi vatsāyeti bruvan vacaḥ ||
gobrāhmaṇānāṃ saṃgrāme nihataḥ śāṅkaraṃ padam |
yo ‘pi kaścin naro yāti kiṃ punar brāhmaṇādayaḥ ||
[214]
12
atha kusīdam
atra vākyeṣv āpadgatabrāhmaṇādhikāro draṣṭavyaḥ | tatra nāradaḥ |
āpadaṃ nistared vaiśyaḥ kāmaṃ vārdhuṣikarmaṇā |
āpatsv api hi kaṣṭāsu brāhmaṇānāṃ na vārdhuṣam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ paiṭhīnasiś ca śloke |
brāhmaṇarājanyau vārdhuṣikaṃ na dadyātām | athāpy udāharanti |
samarghaṃ dhanam uddhṛtya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ ||
anvanyayā (?) kalayā uddhṛtya ṛṇaṃ gṛhītvā |
[215] tathā ca baudhāyanaḥ |
samarghaṃ dhanam uddhṛtya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
sa hṛhītavarṇe mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati (?) ||
yamaḥ |
samarghaṃ yaḥ samādāya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma ye tu vṛddhyā prayuñjate ||
“samargham” atrānujñātaṃ yathāpracaran mūlyam | “mahārgham” cātra śāstrānujñātamūlyātiriktaṃ mūlyam | vāṇijyamātrasyāpadi brāhmaṇasyānujñānāt | “vṛddhyā prayuñjate” annam iti śeṣaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhogo yad dviguṇād ūrdhvaṃ cakravṛddhiś ca gṛhyate |
mūlaṃ ca sodayaṃ paścād vārdhuṣaṃ tad vigarhitam ||
“bhogaḥ” dviguṇībhūtadhanād adhikavṛddhigrahaṇam | “cakravṛddhiḥ” kalāyām eva yā kalā | “sodayam” yāvan mūlyaṃ grāhyatvena vyavasthāpitaṃ tāvato ‘dhikam |
[216] nāradaḥ |
vṛddhis tu yoktā dhānyānāṃ vārdhuṣyaṃ tad udāhṛtam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
vārdhuṣyaṃ pariharet | athāpy udāharanti |
vārdhuṣir bhrūṇahā caiva tulāyām avaropitau |
ātiṣṭhad bhrūṇahā koṭyāṃ vārdhuṣiḥ samakalpata || iti |
vārdhuṣikaḥ pūrvam uktaḥ |
bṛhadvṛddhyanantaraṃ hārītaḥ |
brahmaghnaṃ vṛddhijīvaṃ ca tulāyāṃ samadhārayat |
ātiṣṭhed brahmahā koṭyāṃ vṛddhijīvas tv akalpata ||
chāgaleyaḥ |
na kuryād brāhmaṇo vṛddhiṃ bhrūṇaghātād viśiṣyate |
bhrūṇahatyāṃ ca vṛddhiṃ ca tulāyāṃ samatolayat ||
na pāpaṃ bhrūṇahantṝṇāṃ na pāpaṃ vṛxalīpateḥ |
na pāpam agnidātṝṇāṃ na pāpaṃ guruhiṃsake ||
sarvapāpādhikāṃ vṛddhiṃ bhrūṇaghā samatolayat |
manuḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vāpi vṛddhiṃ naiva prayojayet |
[217]        kāmaṃ tu sukhadharmārthaṃ dadyāt pāpīyase ‘lpakam ||
“vṛddhim” ninditām | “dharmārtham” avaśyakṛtyārtham | “pāpīyase” hīnajātīyāya | “alpakam” yāvatyā dhanavṛddhyā āvaśyakaṃ karma siddhyati tāvatyā |
brāhmaṇarājanyānuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kāmaṃ vā pariluptakṛtyāya pāpīyase dadyātām |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kāmaṃ tu pāpīyase dadyād daśa ity eke | na jyāyasīṃ vṛddhim upādadīta | na tv eva kanīyasīm |
daśapaṇasaṃkhyayā ekaikasmai pāpīyase dadyād ity arthaḥ | śāstravihitakalāpekṣayā vṛddher nyūnatvam adhikatvaṃ ca jñeyam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
vasiṣṭhavacanaproktāṃ vṛddhiṃ vārdhuṣike śṛṇu |
pañca māṣās tu viṃśatyā evaṃ dharmo na hīyate ||
viṃśatikārṣāpaṇānāṃ pañcamāṣā vṛddhir grāhyā,
[218]        māṣo viṃśatibhāgaś ca jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya tu |
iti nāradavacanāt | “pañca māṣā” aśītibhāgatulyā bhavanti | iyaṃ ca vṛddhiḥ pratimāsaṃ grāhyā,
aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyān māsād vārdhuṣikaḥ śate |
iti manuvacanāt |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
aśītibhāgo vardheta lābho dviguṇatām iyāt |
prayuktaṃ saptabhir varṣais tribhāgonair na saṃśayaḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
sabandhe bhāga āśītiḥ sāṣṭabhāgaḥ salagnake |
nirādhāne dvikaśataṃ māsalābha udāhṛtaḥ ||
“āśītiḥ” aśītitamaḥ | ayam eva ca salagnake svakīyāṣṭamabhāgasahitaḥ sāṣṭabhāgaḥ | “nirādhāne” bandhakarahite |
manuḥ |
dvikaṃ śataṃ pragṛhṇīyāt satāṃ dharmam anusmaran |
[219]        dvikaṃ śataṃ hi gṛhṇāno na bhavaty arthakilbiṣī ||
yathā [aśītibhāgaṃ gṛhṇāno na bhavaty arthakilbiṣī tathā] dvikam api śataṃ gṛhṇāno na doṣabhāk | atra ca sabandhakaviṣayatayā vyavasthā | “sabandhe bhāga āśītiḥ” ityādivyāsavacanānusārāt |
hārītaḥ |
purāṇapañcaviṃśatyāṃ māse aṣṭapaṇā vṛddhir evaṃ ṣaḍbhir māsaiś caturbhir varṣair dviparyāgataṃ saṃtiṣṭhate | eṣā vṛddhir dharmavṛddhir nānayā dharmāt cyavate |
“purāṇam” ṣoḍaśa paṇāḥ | paṇaḥ kākiṇīcatuṣṭayam, tathaiva prasiddheḥ | “dviparyāgatam” dviguṇībhūtam | “saṃtiṣṭhate” vyavatiṣṭhate na punar vardhata iti yāvat |
manuḥ |
dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ samam |
māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyād varṇānām anupūrvaśaḥ ||
[220] “dvikaṃ” dvivṛddhir dīyate yasmin mūle tat | evaṃ trikādy api | “samaṃ” mātrayāpy anadhikam |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
aśītibhāgo vṛddhiḥ syān māsi māsi sabandhake |
varṇakramāc chataṃ dvitricatuṣpañcakam anyathā ||
“anyathā” abandhake |
atha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
athottamarṇo ‘dhamarṇakād yathā dattam arthaṃ gṛhṇīyād dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ pañcakaṃ śataṃ varṇānukrameṇa pratimāsam |
gautamaḥ |
nātisāṃvatsarīm eke |
manuḥ |
nātisāṃvatsarīṃ vṛddhiṃ na vādṛṣṭāṃ punar haret |
cakravṛddhiḥ kalāvṛddhiḥ kāritā kāyikā ca yā ||
māsād ārabhya saṃvatsaraṃ yāvat vṛddhiḥ parimāṇato ‘pi nirūpayitavyā | māsena ya vardate saṃvatsareṇa tad ity evaṃ [221] prayogaḥ kartavyo na tva adhikasya | “adṛṣṭām” śāstreṇādṛṣṭāṃ pañcakād adhikāṃ vṛddhiṃ gṛhīyād ity arthaḥ | cakravṛddhyādivṛddhayo ‘pi na grāhyāḥ |
tatsvarūpaṃ tu yājñavalkyoktam | tathā ca
vṛddher vṛddhiś cakravṛddhiḥ pratimāsaṃ tu māsikā |
icchākṛtā kāritā syāt kāyikā kāyakarmaṇī ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bahavo vartanopāyā ṛṣibhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
sarveṣām api caiteṣāṃ kusīdam adhikaṃ viduḥ ||
anāvṛṣṭyā rājabhayān mūṣikādyair upadravaiḥ |
kṛṣyādike bhaved dhāniḥ sā kusīde na vidyate ||
śuklapakṣe tathā kṛṣṇe rajanyāṃ divase ‘pi vā |
uṣṇe varṣe niśīthe vā vardhanaṃ na nivartate ||
diśaṃ gatānāṃ yā vṛddhir nānāpaṇyopajīvinām |
kusīdaṃ kurvataḥ samyak sāsmin tasyaiva jāyate ||
[222]
13
anyā āpadvṛttayaḥ
athānyā āpadvṛttayaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
vidyā śilpaṃ bhṛtiḥ sevā gorakṣaṃ vipaṇiḥ kṛṣiḥ |
dhṛtir bhaikṣaṃ kusīdaṃ ca daśa jīvanahetavaḥ ||
“vidyā” vedavidyāvyatiriktā gāruḍādividyā | “śilpam” citrādikarma | “bhṛtiḥ” vetanam | “vipaṇiḥ” vāṇijyam | “dhṛtiḥ” santoṣaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṛṣiḥ śilpaṃ dhṛtir vidyā kusīdaṃ śakaṭaṃ giriḥ |
sevānūpaṃ nṛpo bhaikṣyam āpattau jīvanāni tu ||
[223] bṛhaspatiḥ |
jātyāṇe (?) śakaṭārāgamayābjāvṛddhir vaṇik kṛṣiḥ |
anūpaṃ parvato rājā vipattau jīvanāni tu ||
“vaṇik” vāṇijyam |
chāgaleyaḥ |
śakaṭaṃ śākinī gāvo jālam aspandanaṃ vanam |
anūpaṃ parvato rājā durbhikṣe nava vṛttayaḥ ||
“śakaṭam” dhānyādivahanabhāṭakadvāreṇopajīvyam | “śākinī” śākavāṭikā | “jālam” matsyāharaṇena | “aspandanam” ekatrāvasthānam, vyayādhikyavirahāt tṛṇādilābhāc ca | “vanam” phalamūlādyāharaṇena | “anūpam” bahūdako deśaḥ bisaśālūkādyāharaṇena | “parvataḥ” gaurikādyāharaṇena |
hārītaḥ |
yuktabharam ayogakṣemaṃ pañcasūnam ano manyante daśasūnaṃ rātrau śatasūnaṃ varṣāsu tasmān nānasā rātrau caret na varṣāsu | māsenānovṛttiḥ patatīti dharmavido ‘gnidevatātithigṛhayajñopakaraṇam anena bhavatīty āhuḥ |
[224]  śatamūlam ikṣupatraṃ tad yajñārthaṃ kuryāt | “bharaḥ” atra upari sthāpyamāno vrīhyādisamūhaḥ | “ayogakṣemam” ayogaśākaṭikānvitatvena | “anaḥ” śakaṭam | sayogakṣemam api rātrau daśamūlam | evaṃ śatamūlaṃ varṣāsu | agnyupakaraṇasamitpuṣpayavasa-vrīhyādyāhareṇena | anena niṣpāpaḥ yajñārthe kuryān na vṛttyādyartham |
gautamaḥ |
sarvathā vṛttir aśaktāv aśaudreṇa | tad apy eke prāṇasaṃśaye | tadvad varṇasaṃkarābhakṣyaniyamas tu | prāṇasaṃśaye brāhmaṇo ‘pi śastram ādadīta |
“sarvathā” pratiṣiddhavikrayavṛttisaṃkarādibhiḥ | “aśaktau” nityanaimittikānuṣṭhānāsaṃpattau | “aśaudreṇa” śūdrakarmavyatiriktena ucchiṣṭabhakṣaṇaśilpādinā | “tad api” śaudrakarmāpi | “prāṇasaṃśaye” ātmano ‘vaśyabhartavyānāṃ ceti śeṣaḥ | “tadvad varṇasaṃkaraḥ” sahāsanādiḥ | “abhakṣyam” laśunādi | “niyamaḥ” nivṛttiḥ | “śastram ādadīta” prāṇadhāraṇārtham iti śeṣaḥ |
aṅgirāḥ |
vyādhitasya daridrasya kuṭumbāt pracyutasya ca |
adhvānaṃ vā prapannasya bhikṣācaryā vidhīyate ||
idaṃ vyādhitatvādi sarvaprakārapākāsaṃbhavopalakṣaṇaparam |
[225] hārītaḥ |
cāturvarṇaṃ tu vā bhaikṣyam ekānnaṃ vā dvijādiṣu |
bhaikṣam eva praśaṃsanti na tv ekānnaṃ dvijātiṣu ||
svakarmopārjitaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ vidhinā tv anasūyayā |
vāyutulyaṃ praśaṃsanti ṛṣayo dharmakovidāḥ ||
“svakarmaṇā” yathokādhyayanādiprayojanena | “vidhinā” yāvadartham amāyayetyādinirūpitaprakāreṇa | “vāyutulyam” śuddhatvena |
manuḥ |
tathaiva saptame bhakte bhaktāni ṣaḍ anaśnatā |
aśvastanavidhānena kartavyaṃ hīnakarmaṇaḥ ||
khalāt kṣetrād agārād vā yato vāpy upalabhyate |
ākhyātavyaṃ tu tat tasmai pṛcchate yadi pṛcchati ||
brahmasvaṃ na ca hartavyaṃ kṣatriyeṇa kadācana |
[226]        dasyuniṣkriyayos tu svam ajīvan hartum arhati ||
“aśvastanavidhānena” ekadinopayogena | “dasyuḥ” cauraḥ | “niṣkriyo” nityanaimittikakriyārahitaḥ | tayor brāhmaṇayor api hartavyam ity arthaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
bubhukṣitas tryahaṃ sthitvā dhānyam abrāhmaṇād dharet |
pratigṛhya tad ākhyeyam abhiyuktena dharmataḥ ||
“pratigṛhya” gṛhītvā |
dravyādīnām ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
