saptamīṃ cābhuktāni ca (?) vādyāpy [cābhuktvānicayāya api] ahīnakarmabhya ācakṣīta rājñā pṛṣṭaḥ |
“saptamīṃ” bhojanavelām iti śeṣaḥ |
[227] hārītaḥ |
ārogyavṛttiḥ cikitsitam upapativṛṣavaiśyopajīvanaṃ caitasyā vaṇik kairāṭī vārdhuṣikī anṛtī kitavavṛttiḥ vivādapraiṣyavṛttis tārkikāśvavṛttiḥ rājapauruṣyaṃ dambhajīvanaṃ kalkavṛttiḥ | parasvāpaharaṇaṃ steyavṛttiḥ | vadhabandhopajīvanaprabhṛtayaś caitāḥ kilbiṣavṛttayo bhavanti | kilbiṣavṛttayo nirayānuśiṣṭāḥ pāpayoniṣu jāyante tasmān nāsadvṛttiḥ syāt |
“upapatyupajīvanam” bhāryājāropajīvanam | gavāśvāder vṛṣabhasya mūlyena tatstrīṣūtsarjanam eṣā tasyā nāma vṛṣavṛttiḥ | “vaṇik” paṇyavikrayī | “kirāṭī” ghṛtatailataṇḍulādyavikreyavikrayī | kusīdavṛttyupajīvitvam “vārdhuṣikavṛttiḥ” | eṣānṛtavṛttiḥ | “kitavavṛttitā” dyūtavṛttitvam | “vivādavṛttiḥ” kalahaśauṇḍatayā jīvanam | “praiṣyavṛttiḥ” sevakaḥ | “tārkiko” hetuvādajīvī chalādikuśalaḥ sadvidyopamardanena yo jīvati | eṣā catuḥprakārāśvavṛttiḥ | “rājapauruṣyam” rājabhayopadarśanenotkocagrahaṇam | dambhasya vyaktākalkatācchalena parasvāpaharaṇaṃ steyavṛttiḥ | “nirayānuśiṣṭāḥ” narakayātanāṃ lambhitā ity arthaḥ |
[228] devalaḥ |
cikitsyāṃ jyotiṣaṃ rūpaṃ śakaṭaṃ tilapīḍanam |
surāṃ tulāṃ ca nājīvet putrācāryatvam eva ca ||
lākṣāmāñjiṣṭhamāṃsāni madhulohaviṣāṇi ca |
ajīvan vṛṣalo ‘py āryaḥ karmaṇā tena varjitaḥ ||
“rūpam” veśyādīnām iva jīvanopāyaḥ | “putrācāryatā” ācāryatayā putrād dhanādānam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nāntaritāṃ nāviśiṣṭāṃ nādhikāṃ na samāṃ na hīnāṃ na kutsitāṃ nānarthakarīṃ vṛttiṃ lipset |
“antaritā” vyavahitā | yathā kṣatriyasya śūdravṛttiḥ | “avaśiṣṭā” viśuddhapratigrahasaṃbhave saty aviśuddhapratigrahādirūpā | “adhikā” svotkṛṣṭavarṇasaṃbandhinī | “samā” āvaśyakakarma-bhṛtyabharaṇe ‘py asamarthā | “hīnā” hīnavarṇasaṃbhandhinī | “kutsitā” ninditakarmārjitā | “anarthakarī” ahitā |
manuḥ |
nehetārthaṃ prasaṅgena na niṣiddhena karmaṇā |
[229]        na kalpyamāneṣv artheṣu nārtyām api yatas tataḥ ||
“prasaṅgena” gītavādyādiprasaktyā | “kalpyamāneṣu” pitrādikarmāgateṣu vṛttisamartheṣu dhaneṣu satsu | “yatas tataḥ” abhiśastapatitādeḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
na svādhyāyavirodhyartham īheta na yatas tataḥ |
na viruddhaprasaṅgena santoṣī ca bhavet sadā ||
laghuhārītaḥ |
adhikārī tu yo yasya sa dāsas tasya kīrtitaḥ |
tasmād dvijottamenaiva na kāryaṃ karma karhicit ||
adhikārī tu yaḥ pūrvam iṣṭaṃ vā karma kārayet |
sa gacchen narake ghore yasyārthas tasya tat phalam ||
“yasyārthas tasya tatphalam” iti | vṛttyarthadattadhanavyatiriktena svāmidhanena dharmaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
bṛhaspati-vyāsau |
vṛddhau mātāpitarau sādhvī bhāryā sutaḥ śiśuḥ |
[230]        apy akāryaśataṃ kṛtvā bhartavyā manur abravīt ||
“akāryaśatam” atra ninditā vṛttayaḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
tṛtīyeṣv eva bhāgeṣu poṣyavargārthasādhanam |
mātā pitā gurur bhāryā prajā dīnaḥ samāśritaḥ ||
abhyāgato ‘tithiś cāgniḥ pauṣyavarga udāhṛtaḥ |
jñātir bandhujanaḥ kṣīṇaḥ tathānāthaḥ samāśritaḥ ||
anyo ‘pi dhanayuktasya poṣyavarga udāhṛtaḥ |
jñānavidbhyaś ca dātavyam anyathā narakaṃ vrajet ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena poṣyavargaṃ sadā bharet |
bharaṇaṃ poṣyavargasya praśastaṃ svargasādhanam ||
narakaṃ pīḍane cāsya tasmād yatnena taṃ bharet |
sa jīvati pumān eko bahubhir yo ‘nujīvyate ||
jīvanto ‘pi mṛtās tv anye puruṣāḥ svodarambharāḥ |
bahvarthe jīvyate kaiścit kuṭumbārthe tathāparaiḥ ||
ātmārthe ‘nyaiḥ na śaknoti svodareṇāpi duḥkhitaḥ |
“arthasādhanam” dharmārjanam | “dīnaḥ samāśritaḥ” dīnaḥ san samāśrito na punaḥ sarvaḥ | “abhyāgataḥ” asaṃbandhī [231] grāmāntarād āgataḥ | “jñātiḥ” pitṛbandhuḥ | “bandhuḥ” mātṛbandhuḥ | “anāthaḥ” pitrādipoṣakarahitaḥ | “jñānavit” vedaśāstravit |
atha pratigrahavidhiḥ
tatra vyāsaḥ |
dvijātibhyo dhanaṃ lipset praśastebhyo dvijottama |
api dvijātimātrebhyo na tu śūdrāt kathaṃcana ||
rājato dhanam anvicchet saṃsīdan snātakaḥ kṣudhā |
yājyāntevāsinor vāpi na tv anyata iti sthitiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājāntevāsiyājyebhyaḥ sīdann icched dhanaṃ kṣudhā |
dambhihaitukapāṣaṇḍibakavṛttīṃś ca varjayet ||
“haitukāḥ” hetuvādaratāḥ trayībāhyāḥ |
nāradaḥ |
arthānāṃ bhūribhāvāc ca devatvāc ca mahātmanām |
śreyān pratigraho rājñāṃ nānyeṣāṃ brāhmaṇād ṛte ||
brāhmaṇaś caiva rājā ca dvāv evaitau dhṛtavratau |
[232]        naitayor antaraṃ kiṃcit prajādharmābhirakṣaṇāt ||
dharmajñasya kṛtajñasya rakṣārthaṃ śāsato ‘śucīn |
medhyam eva dhanaṃ prāhus tīkṣṇasyāpi mahīpateḥ ||
śucīnām aśucīnāṃ tu saṃnipāto yathāmbhasām |
samudre samatāṃ yāti tadvad rājño dhanāgamaḥ ||
yathāgnau saṃsthitaṃ dīptaṃ śuddhim āyāti kāñcanam |
evaṃ dhanāgamāḥ sarve śuddhim āyānti rājani ||
svadharme brāhmaṇas tiṣṭhan vṛttim āhārayen nṛpāt |
nāsadbhyaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād brāḥmaṇo vibhave sati ||
aśucir vacanād yasya śucir bhavati pūruṣaḥ |
śuciś caivāśuciḥ sadyaḥ kathaṃ rājā na daivatam ||
vidur ya enaṃ devatvaṃ rājño hy amitatejasaḥ |
tasya te pratigṛhṇanti na lipaynte kathaṃcana ||
“aśucīn” kṛtāparādhān | “devatvam” aṣṭalokapālarūpatvena manvādibhiḥ saṃskṛtatvāt |
śiloñchavṛttyanantaraṃ manuḥ |
sīdadbhiḥ kupyam icchadbhir dhanaṃ vā pṛthivīpatiḥ |
yācyaḥ syāt snātakair viprair aditsaṃs tyāgam arhati ||
[233]        akṛtaṃ ca kṛtāt kṣetrād gaurajāvikam eva ca |
hiraṇyaṃ dhānyam annaṃ ca pūrvaṃ pūrvam adoṣakṛt ||
“kupyaṃ” hemarajatābhyām anyat kāṃśyādidhanam | “snātakaiḥ” vipraiḥ | “anyat” kāṃsyādi | “dhanaṃ” kupyād anyat suvarṇādi | “aditsan” dātum icchārahitaḥ | “tyāgam arhati” tasyā rāṣṭre na vastavyam iti medhātithiḥ | “akṛtam” akṛṣṭam | “annam” pakvam |
baudhāyanaḥ |
tasya vṛttiḥ brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyarathakāreṣu āmaṃ lipset | bhikṣeta vāgyatas tiṣṭhan |
“rathakāro” māhiṣyeṇa karaṇyāṃ jātaḥ | “lipseta” ayācitam iti śeṣaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
bhikṣamāṇo nimittāntaraṃ brūyāt | na raṅgāvatāraṃ kuryān na strīṃ nāprāptavyavahāraṃ nāparyāptasaṃnidhānam anuddiśyānnaṃ bhikṣeta | yadarthaṃ bhikṣeta tam evārthaṃ kuryāt | śeṣam ṛtvigbhyo nivedayet | yo vānyaḥ sādhutamas tasmai dadyāt |
“nimittaṃ” bhikṣāprayojakaṃ vivāhādi | “raṅgo” nṛtyādisthānam, tatrāvatāro bhikṣārthaṃ tatpradeśagamanam | “aprāpta[234]vyavahāraḥ” ūnaṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ | “aparyāptasaṃnidhānaḥ” dānāyogyadeśakālasthitaḥ | “anuddiśya” anirdiśya | “anyam” akṛtād arthāntaram | “bhikṣeta” iti na strīm ityādibhiḥ saṃbadhyate |
manuḥ |
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād arājanyaprasūtitaḥ |
sūnācakradhvajavatāṃ veśenaiva jivatām ||
daśasūnāsamaṃ cakraṃ daśacakrasamo dhvajaḥ |
daśadhvajasamā veśyā daśaveśyāsamo nṛpaḥ ||
daśasūnāsahasrāṇi yo vāhayati saunikaḥ |
tena tulyaḥ smṛto rājā ghoras tasya pratigrahaḥ ||
yo rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇāti lubdhasyocchāstravartinaḥ |
sa paryāyeṇa yātīmān narakān ekaviṃśatim ||
tāmisram andhatāmisraṃ mahārauravarauravau |
narakaṃ kālasūtraṃ ca mahānarakam eva ca ||
saṃjīvanaṃ mahāvīcīṃ tapanaṃ saṃpratāpanam |
saṃghātaṃ ca sakākolaṃ kuḍmalaṃ pūtimṛttikam ||
[235]        lohasaṅkum ṛtīkṣaṃ ca panthānaṃ śālmalīṃ nadīm |
asipatravanaṃ caiva lohāṅgārakam eva ca ||
etad vidanto vidvāṃso brāhmaṇā brahmavādinaḥ |
na rājñaḥ pratigṛhṇanti pretya śreyo ‘bhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ ||
“sūnī” māṃsavikrayī | “cakrī” tailikaḥ | “dhvajvān” śauṇḍikaḥ | “veśyā” gaṇikā |
yamaḥ |
arājanyaprasūtasya rājñaḥ svacchandavartinaḥ |
ghoraḥ pratigrahas tasya madhvāsvādo viṣopamaḥ ||
rājakilbiṣadagdhānāṃ viprāṇām akṛtātmanām |
svinnānām iva bījānāṃ punar janma na vidyate ||
tasmāt pratigrahaṃ rājño varjayhanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
devatānnair dvijānnaiś ca punar āpyāyayanti te ||
tathaiva rājamahiṣī rājāmātyapurohitāḥ |
pāpenārdhena saṃyuktāḥ sarve te rājadharmiṇaḥ ||
“akṛtātmānaḥ” ninditarājapratigrahānuṣṭhānena | “devatānnair [236] dvijānnaiś ca punar āpyāyayanti te” devasvabrahmasvābhyām api varam ātmānam āpyāyayanti, na tu rājapratigrahaṃ kurvantīti tātparyārthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
daśasūnāsahasrāṇi ahnā rājā karoti vai |
tāny eva kurute rātrau ghoras tasya pratigrahaḥ ||
ardhārdhaṃ tu tato ‘mātyāḥ senānīdaṇḍanāyakau |
tato ‘ardhārdham athārdhārdham āśriteṣu ca jāyate ||
aṅgirāḥ |
ṣaṇmāsān yo dvijo bhuṅkte śūdrānnaṃ ca vigarhitam |
jīvet sa tu bhavec chūdro mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate ||
ete doṣā bhavanty atra śūdrād eva pratigrahaiḥ |
gurvartham atithīnāṃ ca bhṛtyānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||
śūdrānnaṃ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu bhuṅkte svayaṃ tataḥ |
[237]        anugrahapravṛttasya na doṣo ‘ṅgiraso matam ||
“anugrahapravṛttasya” vakṣyamāṇagurvādhyanugrahapravṛttasya |
yamaḥ |
anirdaśāhe pretasya pratigṛhṇanti ye dvijāḥ |
samā śatasahasraṃ te narakaṃ paryupāsate ||
“anirdaśāhe pretasya” mṛtakāśaucamadhya ity arthaḥ |
aṅgirāḥ |
saṃskāraiḥ śudhyati hy āmaṃ dhānyaṃ tena śuci smṛtam |
tasmād āmaṃ grahītavyaṃ mṛtasūtāntareṣv api ||
pakvānnaṃ varjayed vipro godhānyaṃ kṣatriye tathā |
vaiśyebhyaḥ sarvadhānyāni śūdrād grāhyāḥ paṇās tathā ||
“saṃskāraiḥ” khaṇḍanadalanādibhiḥ | “paṇaḥ” tāmrakaḥ kārṣikaḥ paṇaḥ | etac ca sarvam āpadgatsays boddhavyam | śūdrasya sarvathā apratigrāhyadhanasya śauce paṇagrahaṇābhyanujñānāt |
[238] apāṅkteyānuvṛttau śūdrayājakānantaraṃ manuḥ |
vedavic cāpi vipro ‘sya lobhāt kṛtvā pratigraham |
vināśaṃ vrajati kṣipram āmapātram ivāmbhasā ||
hārītaḥ |
patitaduṣkṛtebhyaḥ pratigṛhītam asvargyam ayajñiyaṃ na tena (?) puṇyārthān āpnoti devādārthābhyanārthānartho brāhmaṇam abhijitya tāmasam anyasmai dīyamānaṃ pratiṣidhya svayaṃ pratigṛhṇan tasya duṣkṛtam āpnoti | āṛṇyārthe pratigṛhya tebhyo ‘kṛtvopayogānāṃ stainyam asatkāryārthe majjaty ātmārthe nibadhnāti | etaiḥ pratigrahopaśayaiḥ pratigṛhṇan nirayāya kalpate |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
asato ‘pi samādāya sādhubhyaḥ saṃprayacchati |
[239]        dhanasvāminam ātmānaṃ saṃtārayati dustarāt ||
yamaḥ |
yas tu labdhaṃ punar dadyāt pratigṛhya yatas tataḥ |
yaḥ sa pṛcchati rājānaṃ keṣāṃ bhavati dakṣiṇā ||
jānatājānatā vāpi yat kiṃcid duṣkṛtaṃ bhavet |
sarvaṃ tarati dharmātmā pratigṛhya punar dadat ||
saktāḥ pratigrahe ye tu na tu dāne kadācana |
teṣāṃ vaivasvataḥ prāha na tūrdhvaṃ gamanaṃ kvacit ||
“saktāḥ” āsaktāḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
devātithyarcanakṛte gurubhṛtyādivṛttaye |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyād ātmavṛttyartham eva ca ||
“ātmavṛttiḥ” ātmaprāṇayātrārūpā |
manuḥ |
gurūn bhṛtyāṃś cojjihīrṣann arciṣyan devatātithīn |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān na tu tṛpyet svayaṃ tataḥ ||
[240]        guruṣu tv abhyatīteṣu vinā vā tair gṛhe vasan |
ātmano vṛttim anvicchan gṛhṇīyāt sādhutaḥ sadā ||
eteṣv api ca kāryeṣu samarthaḥ san pratigrahe |
nādadyāt kulaṭāṣaṇḍhapatitebhyas tathā dviṣaḥ ||
“guravaḥ” pitrādayaḥ | “ujjihīrṣan” kṣudhādyavasannān uddhartum icchan | “arciṣyan” devatātithīn | āvaśyakakarmasaṃpatyartham ity arthaḥ | “sarvataḥ” sādhvasādhubhyaḥ | “na tu tṛypyet svayaṃ tataḥ” iti | prāṇayātrāmātrātiriktaṃ bhogādi tena na kuryād ity arthaḥ | “vinā vā tair gṛhe vaset” jīvatsv api gurvādiṣu prakārāntareṇa saṃbhavadvṛttiṣu, ātmavṛttyarthaṃ sādhubhyo dhārmikebhya eva pratigṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ |
gautamaḥ |
edhodakamūlaphalamadhvabhayābhyudyataśayyāsanāvasathayānapayodadhi-dhānāsarṣapapriyaṅgumārgaśākāny apraṇodyāni sarvebhyaḥ | pitṛdevagurubhṛtyabharaṇe vānyavṛttiś cen nāntareṇa śudrāt |
“abhyudyatam” abhimukhaṃ dātum ānītam edhādibhyo ‘nyad api | payodadhidhānāni tu anabhyudyatāny api dātur [241] gṛhādivartīni | ayācitadattāni na pratyākhyeyāni | “āvasatham” gṛham | “mārgam” mṛgamāṃsam | “pitṛdevagurubhṛtyabharaṇe cānyad” iti śrāddhādyartham anyad api dattam apratyākhyeyam ity arthaḥ |
sumantuḥ |
abhojyānnānām api puṣpaphalamūlaśākatṛṇakāṣṭhadhānyāni kṣetrasthāny ambu taḍāge goṣṭhasthaṃ payaś cādadatām adoṣaḥ |
“kṣetram” atrotpattisthānam |
āpastambaḥ |
madhv āmaṃ mārgaṃ māṃsaṃ bhūmir mūlaphalāni rakṣā gavyūtir niveśanaṃ yugyaṃ vāsaś cogrataḥ pratigrāhyāṇi | etāny api antevāsyuddhṛtānīti |
“rakṣā” trāṇam | “gavyūtiḥ” gomārgaḥ | “ugraḥ” ucchāstravartī | “antrevāsyuddhṛtāni” antevāsinā śiṣyeṇa nītāni |
hārītaḥ |
āmaṃ vā gṛhṇīran kṛtānnasya vā virasasya na subhikṣāḥ [242] syuḥ | svayam apy avṛttau suvarṇaṃ datvā paśuṃ vā bhuñjīta nātyantam avasyet vṛttiṃ prāpya viramet |
“āmaṃ” vrīhitaṇḍulādi | “kṛtānnasya” iti ṣaṣṭhīnirdeśāt kṛtānnasya vā viramasya lavaṇādirasarahitasya stokaṃ yāvad bhṛtyabharaṇārthaṃ tāvad ugrato ‘pi pratigrāhyam | “na subhikṣāḥ syuḥ” yadi na lokāḥ sukhena yācitānnadātāro bhaveyus tadā durbhikṣa ity arthaḥ |
ādityapurāne |
api pāpakṛtāṃ rājñāṃ pratigṛhṇanti sādhavaḥ |
prthivīṃ nānyad icchanti pāvanaṃ hy etad uttamam ||
manuḥ |
edhodakaṃ mūlaphalam annam abhyudyataṃ ca yat |
sarvataḥ pratigṛhṇīyān madhv athābhayadakṣiṇām ||
śayyāṃ gṛhaṃ kuśān gandhān apaḥ puṣpaṃ maṇīn dadhi |
dhānā matsyān payo māṃsaṃ śākaṃ caiva na nirnudet ||
[243] abhayasya mukhyadānāsaṃbhavād dṛṣṭāntārtham upādānam | “maṇayo” ratnāni |
aṅgiro-baudhāyanau prathame |
khalakṣetragataṃ dānyaṃ vāpīkūpagataṃ jalam |
abhojyād api tad grāhyaṃ yac ca goṣṭhagataṃ payaḥ ||
vicchinneṣu ca pakṣeṣu haviṣv āgrayaṇeṣu ca |
iṣṭiṃ tv avikṛtāṃ kuryāt sukṛtaṃ ca na lopayet ||
pakvānnavarjyaṃ viprebhyo godhānyaṃ kṣatriyād api |
vaiśyāt tu sarvadhānyāni śūdrād grāhyaṃ na kiṃcana ||
yat tu kṣetragataṃ dhānyaṃ khale vāsagṛhād bahiḥ |
sarvakālaṃ grahītavyaṃ śūdrebhyo ‘py aṅgiromatam ||
prativarṇam idaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ pratigrāhyaṃ dvijottamaiḥ |
tathāpi ca payaḥ peyaṃ dvijebhyo ‘py aṅgiromatam ||
“vicchinneṣu ca” pakṣādikaraṇakāleṣu | tathā “āgrayaṇeṣu” [244] iti saṃpūrṇeṣṭisaṃpattir yāvatā bhavati tāvat saṃpattyartham abhojyānnād api yathoktaṃ dhānyādi pratigrāhyam ity arthaḥ | “payaḥ” pānīyam atra |
tathā |
yat tu rāśīkṛtaṃ dhānyaṃ khalakṣetre ‘tha vā bhavet |
sarvakālaṃ grahītavyaṃ śūdrebhyo ‘py aṅgiromatam ||
supātre yat tu vinyastaṃ dugdhaṃ tac chuci nityaśaḥ |
pātrāntaragataṃ grāhyaṃ śūdrāt svagṛham āgatam ||
śūdraveśmani vipreṇa kṣīraṃ vā yadi vā dadhi |
nivṛttena na bhoktavyaṃ śūdrānnaṃ tad api smṛtam ||
āmaṃ māṃsaṃ madhu ghṛtaṃ dhānāḥ kṣīram athauṣadham |
guḍatakrarasā grāhyā nivṛttenāpi śūdrataḥ ||
śākamatsyamṛṇālāni kambūkāḥ saktavas tilāḥ |
ikṣuḥ phalāni piṇyākaṃ pratigrāhyāṇi sarvataḥ ||
“sarvakālam” sūtakādāv api sarvavarṇānāṃ cety arthaḥ | “rasā” lavaṇādayaḥ | “kambūkāḥ” taṇḍulānāṃ kaṇāḥ |
[245] yājñavalkyaḥ |
kuśāh kāśaṃ payo matsyā gandhāḥ puṣpaṃ dadhi kṣitiḥ |
māṃsaṃ śayyāsanaṃ dhānāḥ pratyākhyeyaṃ na vāri ca ||
ayācitaṃ hṛtaṃ grāhyam api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ |
anyatra kulaṭāṣaṇḍhapatitebhyas tathā dviṣaḥ ||
jābālaḥ |
abhyudyataṃ gṛhe bhakṣyaṃ grāhyaṃ tat sarvataḥ svayam |
ātmany eva śubhāyātaṃ niyuñjyād bhṛtake param ||
“śubhāyātam” aninditaprakāralabdham evātmopayogī kuryāt, ninditaprakārāyātaṃ tu avaśyabharaṇīyopayogi kuryād ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
āhṛtām udyatāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apracoditām |
mene prajāpatir grāhyām api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ ||
nāśnanti pitaras tasya daśa varṣāṇi pañca ca |
[246]        na ca havyaṃ vahaty agnir yas tām abhyavamanyate ||
cikitsakakṛtghnānāṃ śalyahartus tu vārdhuṣeḥ |
ṣaṇḍhasya kulaṭāyāś ca udyatām api varjayet ||
“śalyahartā” śastravaidyaḥ |
yamaḥ |
cikitsakasya mṛgayor vaiśyāyāḥ kitavasya ca |
ṣaṇḍhasūtakayoś caiva udyatām api varjayet ||
udyatām āhṛtāṃ bhikṣāṃ purastād apracoditām |
bhojyāṃ prajāpatir mene api duṣkṛtakarmaṇaḥ ||
śraddadhānasya bhoktavyaṃ caurasyāpi viśeṣataḥ |
na tv eva bahuyājyasya yaś copanayate bahūn ||
tathā |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ śalyakartuś ca pāśinaḥ |
ṣaṇḍhasya kulatāyāś ca udyātāpi na gṛhyate ||
[247] vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śastraṃ viṣaṃ surā cāpratigrāhyāṇi brāhmaṇasya |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na madyaṃ dadyān na pratigṛhṇīyād vā |
ādityapurāṇe |
kiṃ kariṣyaty asau vipro gṛhṇann ubhayatomukhīm |
sahasraṃ vāruṇāḥ pāśāḥ krūradhārāgnisannibhāḥ ||
pūrṇavarṣasahasreṇa pāśa ekaḥ pramucyate |
etām avasthāṃ prāpnoti gṛhṇann ubhayatomukhīm ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
vidyātapobhyāṃ hīnena na tu grāhyaḥ pratigrahaḥ |
gṛhṇan pradātāram adho nayaty ātmānam eva ca ||
[248] manuḥ |
na dravyāṇām anujñāya vidhiṃ dharmye pratigrahe |
prātaḥ pratigrahaṃ kuryād avasīdann api kṣudhā ||
hiraṇyaṃ bhūmim aśvaṃ gām annaṃ vāsas tilān ghṛtam |
avidvān pratigṛhṇāno bhasmībhavati dāruvat ||
hiraṇyam āyur annaṃ ca bhūr gauś cāpy oṣatas tanum |
aśvaś cakṣus tvacaṃ vāso ghṛtaṃ tejas tilāḥ prajāḥ ||
atapās tv anadhīyānaḥ pratigraharucir dvijaḥ |
ambhasy aśmaplavenaiva saha tenaiva majjati ||
tasmād avidvān bibhyād yasmāt tastāt pratigrahāt |
svapakenāpi gṛhṇan hi paṅke gaur iva sīdati ||
pratigrahasamartho ‘pi prasaṅgaṃ tatra varjayet |
pratigraheṇa hy asyāśu brahmatejaḥ praśāmyati ||
“pratigrahe dharmyo vidhiḥ” pratigrahe itikartavyatā | hiraṇyānne cāyur oṣato dahata ity arthaḥ | “yasmāt tasmāt” yataḥ kutaścid aviśuddhād api | “prasaṅgaḥ” atyāsaktiḥ |
[249] yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratigrahasamartho ‘pi nādatte yaḥ pratigraham |
ye lokā dānaśīlānāṃ sa tān āpnoti puṣkalān ||
yamaḥ |
sarvadevatābhigamanāt sarvayajñaniṣevaṇāt |
gavāṃ koṭipradānāc ca śreyāṃs tasyāpartigrahaḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
yaś ca yācanako nityaṃ na sa svargasya bhājanam |
udvejayati bhūtāni yathā cauras tathaiva saḥ ||
mahābhārate |
dharmārthaṃ yasya vittehā tasyānīhā garīyasī |
[250]        prakṣālanād dhi paṅkasya dūrād asparśanaṃ varam ||
“dharmārtham” iti āvaśyakakarmātiriktadharmābhiprāyam |
prayāgapratigrahe tīrthasya niṣphalatvam abhidhāya matsyapurāṇe |
tasmāt tīrthe na gṛhṇīyāt puṇyeṣv āyataneṣu ca |
nimitteṣu ca sarveṣu apramatto bhaved dvijaḥ ||
iti pratigrahavidhiḥ ||
[251]
14
atha kṣatriyavaiśyakarmavṛttayaḥ
tatra manuḥ |
trayo dharmā nivartante brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyaṃ prati |
adhyāpanaṃ yājanaṃ ca tṛtīyaś ca pratigrahaḥ ||
vaiśyaṃ prati tathaivete nivarterann iti sthitiḥ |
na tau prati hi tān dharmān manur āha prajāpatiḥ ||
śastrāstrabhṛttvaṃ kṣatrasya vaṇikpaśukēṣir viśaḥ |
ājīvanārthaṃ dharmas tu dānam adhyayanaṃ yajiḥ ||
vedābhyāso brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyasya tu rakṣaṇam |
vārtā karmaiva vaiśyasya viśiṣṭāni svakarmasu ||
brāhmaṇasya ime “brāhmaṇāḥ” | “vārtā” kṛṣipaśupālyavāṇijyāḥ | “viśiṣṭāni” śreṣṭāni |
parāśaraḥ [1.67.70] |
kṣatriyeṇa prajāḥ pālyāḥ śatrapāṇiḥ pracaṇḍavān |
[252]        nirjitya parasainyāni kṣitiṃ dharmeṇa pālayet |
lauhakarma tathā ratnapaśūnāṃ paripālanam |
vāṇijyaṃ kṛṣikarmāntaṃ vaiśyavṛttir udāhṛtāḥ ||
“pracaṇḍavān” vīrapuruṣopetaḥ |
dānam adhyayanaṃ yajña ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
pūrvadharma eva kṣatriyaḥ | śatrārjanakṣatatrāṇavyavahāradaṇḍarakṣaṇānu-śāsanam iti vṛttayaḥ |
pūrvadharma eva vaiśyaḥ | kṛṣiḥ paśupālyaṃ vāṇijyaṃ yonipoṣaṇaṃ vṛddhiprayoga iti vṛttiḥ |
“pūrvadharmaḥ” adhyayanayajñadānāni | śastreṇārjanaṃ “śastrārjanam” | kṣatāt trāṇaṃ “kṣatatrāṇam” | “vyavahāro” vādiprativādinoḥ saṃdehaharaṇam | “anuśāsanaṃ” varṇāśramāṇāṃ svadharme vyavasthāpanam | “yonipoṣaṇaṃ” vṛddhyarthe gavādipālanam | “paśupālyaṃ” vetanena paśurakṣaṇam |
hārītaḥ |
dhanurvedo vratasnātaḥ kṣatriyo ‘dhyayanayajanadānadharmā[253]śiṣṭanigrahato ‘bhijitaṣaḍbhāgavṛttiḥ | vedavrataikadeśī | vaiśyo ‘dhyayanayajanadānadharmā kṛṣipaśupālyavaṇigvṛttiḥ | vedavrataikadeśī | vaiśyo ‘dhyayanayajanadānadharmā kṛṣipaśupālyavaṇigvṛttiḥ |
“dhanurvedaḥ” snānādhigataśatravidyaḥ | evaṃ vedavratayor api | abhijitānāṃ śatror bhūmyādīnāṃ ṣaṣṭhabhāgagrahaṇam | “vedavrataukadeśī” vedavratayor ekadeśenāpi kṛtārthaḥ |
brahmādadhād ity anuvṛttau baudhāyanaḥ |
kṣatrabalam adhyayanayajanadānaśastrakośabhūtarakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kṣatrasya vṛddhyai | viṭsv adhyayanayajanadānakṛṣivāṇijyapaśupālanasaṃyuktaṃ karmaṇo vṛddhyai |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
adhyayanaṃ yajanaṃ dānaṃ tejaḥ saṃgrāme cāpalāyanam īśvarabhāvaś ca dharmaḥ kṣatriyasya |
“tejaḥ” pratāpitvam | “īśvarabhāvaḥ” aparavaśatvam |
āpastambaḥ |
svakarma brāhmaṇasyādhyayanam adhyāpanaṃ yajño yājanaṃ dānaṃ pratigrahaṇaṃ dāyādyaṃ śiloñcho ‘nyac cāparigṛhītam |
etāny eva kṣatriyasyādhāpanayājanapratigrahaṇānīti parihāpya daṇḍayuddhādhikāni |
[254]                kṣatriyavad vaiśyasya daṇḍayuddhavarjaṃ kṛṣigorakṣavāṇijyādhikam |
dvijātīnām adhyanejyādānam ity anuvṛttau “rājño ‘dhikaṃ rakṣaṇaṃ sarvabhūtānām” ityādi prakramya gautamaḥ |
kṣatriyaś ced anyaḥ tamupajīvet tadvṛttyā |
“anyaḥ” jātimātrasaṃbaddhaḥ | “tam” rājānam | “tadvṛttyā” śastradhāritvena |
devalaḥ |
kṣatradharmo ‘dhyayanejyādānair devapitṛpūjanam | brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣaṇaṃ vaiśyaśūdraparipālanam | balikaraśulkaṣaḍbhāgopajīvanam | varṇāśramasaṃsthāpanam, pāpanigraho guṇavatparigraho vyavahāravarjanam | dharmacāritrānupālanaṃ hastyaśvarathapraharaṇaśikṣābhyāsaḥ | śāstropāyāśrayatvaṃ kośadaṇḍāvekṣaṇaṃ paracakrāpaṇāṭavītaskaradūṣya-mānyāptakulīnādhyakṣapratiṣedhalabdhaśūranijaiśvaryadhairyam utsāhaśauryaṃ śaktatvam ayācanam [255] iti | vaiśyadharmo ‘dhyayanayajanapradānaiḥ dharmānuṣṭhānaṃ, brāhmaṇakṣatriyaśuśrūṣā, pāpavarjanam, kalatrapoṣaṇam, karṣaṇam, paśupālanam, bhārodvahanapaṇyavyavahāranartanagāyana-tālāvataraṇaraṅgopajīvanaṃ ceti |
“baliḥ” upāyanam | “karaḥ” kārukṛṣīvalebhyo niyatadhanādānam | “śulkaḥ” tarādipaṇyam | “ṣaḍbhāgopajīvanam” dhānyādīnām iti śeṣaḥ | “saṃsthā” maryādā | “śikṣā” śāstram | “upāyāḥ” sāmādayaḥ |”"gāyanam” atra gītam | “tālāvataraṇam” atra vādanam | “raṅgo” mallayuddhabhūmiḥ | iha tu mallavidyopalakṣyate |
baudhāyanaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ kusīdam upajīvet | pañcaviṃśatis tv eva pañcamāṣikī syāt |
“pañcaviṃśatiḥ” kārṣāpaṇānām |
manuḥ |
vaiśyas tu kṛtasaṃskāraḥ kṛtvā dāraparigraham |
vārtāyāṃ nityayuktaḥ syāt paśūnāṃ caiva rakṣaṇe ||
prajāpatir hi vaiśyāya sṛṣṭvā paridade paśūn |
brāhmaṇāya ca rājñe ca sarvāḥ paridade prajāḥ ||
[256]        na ca vaiśyasya kāmaḥ syān na rakṣeyaṃ paśūn iti |
vaiśye cecchati nānyena rakṣitavyāḥ kathaṃcana ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ lauhānāṃ tāntavasya ca |
gandhānāṃ ca rasānāṃ ca vidyād arghabalābalam ||
bīnānām uptivic ca syāt kṣetrabījaguṇasya ca |
mānayogāṃś ca jānīyāt tulāyogāṃśca sarvaśaḥ ||
sārāsāraṃ ca bhāṇḍānāṃ deśānāṃ ca guṇāguṇam |
lābhālābhaṃ ca paṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ ca vivardhanam ||
bhṛtyānāṃ ca bhṛtiṃ vidyād bhāṣāś ca vividhā nṛṇām |
dravyāṇāṃ sthānayogāṃś ca kravikrayam eva ca ||
dharmeṇa ca dravyavṛddhāv ātiṣṭhed yatnam uttamam |
dadyāc ca sarvabhūtānām annam eva prayatnataḥ ||
“lauhāḥ” tāmrādayaḥ | “balābale” nyūnādhike | “bījānām uptivit” samyak sasyotpattyanukūla-ghanaviralabījavapanavettā, svakṣetrabījaguṇasya vettā syād iti śeṣaḥ | “mānayogāḥ” mānaprakārāḥ | "tulāyogāḥ" tulādhāraṇaprakārāḥ | “bhāṇḍānāṃ” vikreyāṇām | “sāram” atra tadrūpam eva cirakālasyāpi | “bhṛtyāḥ” karmakarāḥ | “bhṛtiḥ” karmānurūpaṃ vetanam | “sthānayogāt” tadrūpāvasthānopāyāt |
brāhmaṇasya vaiśyavṛttividhānānantaraṃ manuḥ |
jīved etena rājanyaḥ sarveṇāpy ayanaṃ gataḥ |
[257]        na tv eva jyāyasīṃ vṛttim abhimanyeta karhicit ||
“ayanaṃ gataḥ” āpadgataḥ | “abhimanyeta” aṅgīkuryāt |
tathā |
vaiśyo ‘jīvan svadharmeṇa śūdravṛttyāpi vartayet |
anācarann akāryāṇi nivarteta ca śaktimān ||
“akāryāṇi” ucchiṣṭādyapasāraṇādīni |
tathā |
yo lobhād adhamo jātyā jīved utkṛṣṭakarmabhiḥ |
taṃ rājā nirdhanaṃ kṛtvā kṣipram eva pravāsayet ||
“utkṛṣṭaḥ” atra brāhmaṇaḥ |
kālikāpurāṇe |
kṛṣigorakṣavāṇijyaṃ kila vaiśyasya saṃmatam |
tathāpy anaḍudbhyāṃ (?) kṛṣiṃ naiva samācaret ||
puṅgavābhyāṃ dviṣaddvābhyāṃ praharārdhapramāṇataḥ |
dvau dvau yojyāntarekaikaṃ kārayed dharmato dvijaḥ ||
piṇyākaṃ tṛṇatoye tu teṣāṃ yatnāt prakalpayet |
dhūmakaṃ rūpanaṃ caiva kṛtvaivaṃ kārayet tataḥ ||
[258]        vaiṇavīm api bhūmiṃ tu adhomūtragamāya ca |
kurvann eva na sīdet tu kṛṣiṃ vaiśyo mahāmune ||
rājñaḥ ṣaḍbhāgam utsṛjya pañcamaṃ karṣakasya ca |
śilpinaḥ prakṛtīś caiva dāyādyāṃs tatra tarpya ca ||
śeṣam ātmani yuñjīta khalakṣetreṣu vṛddhimān |
paśūnāṃ pālanaṃ kuryān nivātaṃ ca niveśanam ||
viviktaṃ ca samaṃ śūbhraṃ stambhair yuktam akarkaśaiḥ |
ajāvikāṃś ca gaurīṇāṃ mahiṣīṇāṃ ca saṃgraham ||
kuryād yan na virodhi syād anyeṣāṃ vai kadācana |
śvasūkarakharoṣṭrāṇāṃ mārjāranakulādiṣu ||
svadharmam ācaran vaiśyo naiva kuryāt pratigraham |
lavaṇaṃ madhu madyaṃ ca tilaṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ payaḥ ||
madhūcchiṣṭaṃ tathā lākṣāṃ carma nīlīṃ tathā viṣam |
śastraṃ māṃsaṃ tathā hiṅgu tredhāṃ vā varṇikāṃ tathā ||
guḍapiṇyākasiddhānnaṃ vṛntākādiphalāni ca |
śastrāstram udakaṃ devān śuktāni vividhāni ca ||
śaileyān kutapān vaiśyas tv evamādi na vikrayet ||
mahābhārate |
tiladhānyarasāṃś caiva na vikrīṇīta vai kvacit |
vāṇijyārtham upāsīta vaiśyaḥ satpatham āśritaḥ ||
[259]
15
atha dravyāṇāṃ dharmādharmasvatvāni
tatra manuḥ |
sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ |
prayogaḥ karmayogaś ca satpratigraha eva ca ||
“vittāgamāḥ” dhanopāyāḥ | “dāyaḥ” anvayāgataṃ dhanam | “lābho” nidhyādeḥ | “jayaḥ” atra samgrāme śatror abhibhavaḥ | “prayogaḥ” kusīdam | “karmayogaḥ” kṛṣivāṇijyādi |
gautamaḥ |
svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu ca | brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdham, kṣatriyasya vijitam, nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ |
“svāmī” bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | “krayam” krītaṃ dhanam | “saṃvibhāgaḥ” mitradāya iti bhartṛyajñaḥ | “parigrahaḥ” ananyapūrva āraṇyamahiṣādeḥ | “adhigamaḥ” ākarādibhyo labdham, [260] etāni sarvavarṇasādhāraṇāni dharmyāṇi | “brāhmaṇasyādhikam” asādhāraṇam pratigrahādilabdham | “vijitam” saṃgrāme ‘dhikaṃ kṣatriyasya | “nirviṣṭam” karmakaraṇopāttam |
nāradaḥ [4.43–55] |
dhanamūlāḥ kriyāḥ sarvā yatnaḥ tasyārjane mataḥ |
rakṣaṇaṃ vardhanaṃ bhogas trayaḥ tasya vidhiḥ kramāt ||
tat punas trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ śuddhaṃ śabalam eva ca |
kṛṣṇaṃ ca tasya vijñeyo vibhāgaḥ saptadhā punaḥ ||
śrutaśauryatapaḥkanyāśiṣyayājyānvayāgatam |
dhanaṃ saptavidhaṃ śuddham udayo yasya tadvidhaḥ ||
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijyaśulkaśilpānuvṛttibhiḥ |
kṛtopakārād āptaṃ ca śabalaṃ samudāhrtam ||
pārścikadyūtacauryārtipratirūpakasāhasaiḥ |
vyājenopārjitaṃ yac ca tat kṛṣṇaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
tena krayo vikrayaś ca dānaṃ grahaṇam eva ca |
vividhāś ca pravartante kriyāḥ saṃbhoga eva ca ||
yathāvidhena dravyeṇa yat kiṃcit kurute naraḥ |
tathāvidham avāpnoti sa phalaṃ pretya ceha ca ||
tat punar dvādaśavidhaṃ prativarṇāśramāt smṛtam |
[261]        sādhāraṇaṃ syāt trividhaṃ śeṣaṃ navavidhaṃ viduḥ ||
kramāgataṃ prītidāyaḥ prāptaṃ ca saha bhāryayā |
aviśeṣeṇa varṇānāṃ sarvathā trividhaṃ dhanam ||
vaiśeṣikaṃ dhanaṃ vidyād brāhmaṇasya trilakṣaṇam |
pratigraheṇa yal labdhaṃ yājyataḥ śiṣyatas tathā ||
trividhaṃ kṣatriyasyāpi prāhur vaiśeṣiṅaṃ dhanam |
yuddhopalabdhaṃ kārāc ca daṇḍāc ca vyavahārataḥ ||
vaiśeṣikaṃ dhanaṃ jñeyaṃ vaiśyasyāpi trilakṣaṇam |
kṛṣigorakṣavāṇijyaiḥ śūdrasyāpi tv anugrahāt ||
sarveṣām eva varṇānām eṣa dharmyo dhanāgamaḥ |
viparyayād adharmyaḥ syān na ced āpad garīyasī ||
“śrutenopāttam” adhyayanādinā labdham | “śauryeṇa” jayādinā | “tapasā” japahomadevatārādhanādinā | “kanyāgatam” kanyayā saha śvaśurāder labdham | “śiṣyāgatam” dāyalabdham | “udayaḥ” phalam | “tadvidhaḥ” śuddhaḥ | “anuvṛttiḥ” sevā | “pārścikam” utkocādilabdham | “ārtiḥ” pīḍā | “pratirūpakam” dravyābhāsāḥ | “sāhasaṃ” protayātrādi | vyājo ‘tra dravyāntaramiśrīkaraṇam | “tena” trividhena | “śeṣam” asādhāraṇam | “kāraḥ” karaḥ | “vyavahāro” nyāyaḥ | “anugrahāt” śuśrūṣānibandhanāt |
[262] viṣṇuḥ [58.1–10] |
atha gṛhāśramiṇas trividho ‘rtho bhavati — śuklaḥ śabalaḥ sitaś ca | śuklenārthena yad aurddhvadaihikaṃ karoti tad devatvam āsādyāśnāti | yac chabalena tan mānuṣyam | yat kṛṣṇena tat tiryaktvam | vṛttyupāttaṃ sarvam eva śuklam | anantaravṛttyupāttaṃ śabalam | antaritavṛttyupāttam anuttamavṛtyupāttaṃ ca kṛṣṇam |
kramāgataṃ prītidāyaḥ prāptaṃ ca saha bhāryayā |
aviśeṣeṇa sarveṣāṃ dhanaṃ śuklam udāhṛtam ||
utkocaśulkasaṃprāptam avikreyasya vikrayāt |
kṛtopakārād āptaṃ sa śabalaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
pārścikaṃ dyūtacauryāptaṃ pratirūpakasāhasaiḥ |
vyājenopārjitaṃ yac ca tat kṛṣṇaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
budhaḥ |
nyāyāgatena dravyeṇa karma kuryād adhyayanayājanapratigraharikthakraya-saṃvibhāgapratigrahādhigamāḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [āpa. 24] |
vṛttyupāyena yal labdhaṃ kuryāt pālanavardhanam |
bhogaṃ ca bandhubhiḥ sārdhaṃ dīnānāthārthibhis tathā ||
[263] viṣṇupurāṇe |
pākasaṃsthā haviḥsaṃsthā somasaṃsthāś ca saṃsthitāḥ |
dhane yato manuṣyāṇāṃ yatetāto dhanārjane ||
mahābhārate |
arthena hi vihīnasya puruṣasyālpamedhasaḥ |
sarvāḥ kriyā vinaśyanti grīṣme kusarito yathā ||
durbhikṣād eva durbhikṣaṃ kleśāt kleśaṃ bhayād bhayam |
mṛtebhyaḥ pramṛtā yānti daridrāḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ ||
utsavād utsavaṃ yānti svargāt svargaṃ sukhāt sukham |
śraddadhānāḥ sudāntāś ca dhanāḍhyāḥ śubhakāriṇaḥ ||
ahiraṇyam adāsīkam alpānnādyam agorasam |
gṛhaṃ kṛpaṇavṛttīnāṃ narakasyāparo vidhiḥ ||
dharmeṇārthaḥ samāhāryo dharmalabdhaṃ tridhā dhanam |
kartavyaṃ dharmaparamaṃ mānavena prayatnataḥ ||
ekenāṃśena dharmas tu kartavyo bhūtim icchatā |
ekenāṃśena kāmaś cāpy ekam aṃśaṃ vivardhayet ||
[264] mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.11–12] |
pādenārthasya pāratryaṃ kuryāt saṃcayam ātmavān |
ardhena cātmabharaṇaṃ nityanaimittikānvitam ||
pādaṃ cārdhārdham arthasya mūlabhūtaṃ vivardhayet |
evam ācārataḥ putra arthaḥ sāphalyam ṛcchati ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
vittasādhyaṃ na yaḥ kuryād dharmam āḍhyo ‘pi hi dvijaḥ |
na sa tatphalam āpnoti pralobhākrāntamānasaḥ ||
tasmāt tribhāgaṃ vittasya jīvanāya prakalpayet |
bhāgadvayaṃ ca dharmārtham anityaṃ jīvanaṃ yataḥ ||
agarhitena karmaṇā dhanāny ādāya, svādhyāyaprakarṣopalabdhena vā brahmarṣinimittena vā adrisāgarā (?) gahanahavyaniyamābhyāsād eva vā gṛhastho gārhasthyaṃ pratipālayet |
[265]
16
atha śūdrakarmadharmavṛttayaḥ
tatrānupāttaśuśrūṣāvākyeṣu dvijātyadhikāro veditavyaḥ sāpekṣeṣu | dharma iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
tatra manuḥ |
viprāṇāṃ vedaviduṣāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ yaśasvinām |
śuśrūṣaiva tu śūdrasya dharmo naiḥśreyasaḥ paraḥ ||
śucir utkṛṣṭavarṇeṣu mṛduvāg anahaṃkṛtaḥ |
brāhmaṇāpāśrayo nityam utkṛṣṭiṃ jātito ‘śnute ||
utkṛṣṭāv atra kṣatriyavaiśyau | “brāhmaṇāpāśrayaḥ” vṛttyarthaṃ kṣatriyādiśuśrūṣāparo ‘pi brāhmaṇe niveditātmā | “jātita utkṛṣṭiḥ” jātyutkarṣaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [saṃ. 529] |
śaucaṃ brāhmaṇaśuśrūṣā nityam akrodha eva ca |
śūdrakarma tathā mantro namaskāro ‘tra veditaḥ ||
[266] āpastambaḥ |
śuśrūṣā śūdrasyetareṣāṃ ca varṇānām | pūrvasmin varṇe niḥśreyasaṃ bhūyaḥ |
parāśaraḥ [1.71] |
śūdrasya dvijaśuśrūṣā prathamaṃ dharmalakṣaṇam |
yad anyat kurute kiṃcit tad bhavet tasya niṣphalam ||
hārītaḥ |
na śūdrāya vṛttiṃ prāhus trivarṇaśuśrūṣaivāsya vṛttiḥ | dānam dharmam ity eke |
[manuḥ 10.121–123] |
śūdras tu vṛttim ākāṅkṣan kṣatram ārādhayed yadi |
dhaninaṃ vāpy upārādhya vaiśyaṃ śūdro jijīviṣet ||
svargārtham ubhayārthaṃ ca brāhmaṇān eva dhārayet |
jātabrāhmaṇaśabdo hi sā hy asya kṛtakṛtyatā ||
viprasevaiva śūdrasya viśiṣṭaṃ karma kīrtyate |
yad ato ‘nyad dhu kurute tad bhavaty asya niṣphalam ||
[267]        prakalpitāsya tair vṛttiḥ svakuṭumbād yathārhataḥ |
śaktiṃ cāvekṣya dākṣyaṃ ca bhṛtyānāṃ ca parigraham ||
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ jīrṇāni vasanāni ca |
pulakāś caiva dhānyānāṃ jīrṇāś caiva paricchadāḥ ||
“ārādhayet” dhārayet | “jātabrāhmaṇaśabdaḥ” prāpto brāhmaṇasyāyaṃ śuśrūṣaka iti vyapadeśo yena sa tathā | “ato ‘nyat” brāhmaṇasevanād anyad vratopavāsādi | “taiḥ” dvijātibhiḥ | “svakuṭumbāt” svakuṭumbārthād dhanāt | “śaktitaḥ” sāmartham | “dākṣyam” karyeṣu kauśalam | “bhṛtyasya parigraham” putradārādi | “ucchiṣṭam” atithyādibhuktaśeṣam | etac cocchiṣṭāśanam akṛtadāraparigrahasya | itarasya pākayajñādhikārāt | “pulakāḥ” asāradhānyāni | “paricchadāḥ” chatropānatprabhṛtayaḥ |
gautamaḥ |
śūdraś caturtho varṇaḥ ekajātis tasyāpi satyam akrodhaḥ śaucam ācamanārthe pāṇipādaprakṣālanam evaike | śrāddhakarma, bhṛtyabharaṇam, svadāravṛttiḥ, paricaryā cottareṣām, tebhyo vṛttiṃ lipseta | jīrṇāny upānacchatravāsaḥkūrcādīny ucchiṣṭāśanam, śilpavṛttiś ca yaṃ cārtham āśrayed bhartavyas tena kṣīṇo ‘pi | tena cottaras tadartho ‘sya nicayaḥ syād anujñāto ‘sya namaskāro mantraḥ | pākayajñaiḥ svayaṃ yajetety eke |
[268] “ekajātiḥ” ekajanmā, upanayanarūpajanmābhāvāt | “uttareṣām” utkṛṣṭānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnām | “kūrcaḥ” tṛṇamayam āsanam | “tena” arthena | “kṣīṇaḥ” vṛttihīnaḥ | “tena” śūdreṇa | “uttaraḥ” āryaḥ | kṣīṇa ity anuvartate | “tadarthaḥ” āryārthaḥ | “asya” śūdrasya | “nicayaḥ” dhanasaṃcayaḥ | “svayam” svayam eva kuryāt | na patnīputrādinā kārayed ity arthaḥ | “pākayajñaiḥ” iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
eteṣāṃ paricaryā śūdrasyāniyatā vṛttir aniyatakeśaveṣāḥ | sarveṣāṃ vā muktaśikhāvarjam |
“eteṣām” traivarṇikānām | “aniyatā” etāvad dātavyam iti niyamarahitā | “aniyatakeśaveṣāḥ” sarve aniyatāḥ | keśaveśāś ca śūdrasya ity anvayaḥ | “sarveṣām” brāhmaṇādīnām | “muktaśikhāvarjam” iti, muktaśikhātvaṃ na kenāpi kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
śūdrāṇām āryādhiṣṭhitānām ardhamāsi māsi vā vapanam | āryavad ācamanakalpaḥ |
[269] “ācamanakalpaḥ” ācamanetikartavyatā | “upavītam” vastravinyāsādiḥ | “āryaparatantrāṇām” na svatantrāṇāṃ śilpādijīvinām |
manuḥ |
śūdrāṇāṃ māsikaṃ kāryaṃ vapanaṃ nyāyavartinām |
vaiśyavac chaucakalpaś ca dvijocchiṣṭaṃ tu bhojanam ||
“nyāyavartinām” dvijaśuśrūṣāparāṇām | “śaucakalpaḥ” sūtakāpagamācamanādiprakāraḥ |
yājñavalkyo viśvāmitraś ca prathame |
śūdrasya dvijaśuśrūṣā tayājīvan vaṇiga bhavet |
śilpair vā vividhair jīved dvijātihitam ācaran ||
bhāryāratiḥ śucir bhṛtyabhartā śrāddhakriyārataḥ |
namaskāreṇa mantreṇa pañcayajñān na hāpayet ||
“bhāryāratiḥ” bhāryāyām eva param asya ratir na sāmānyastrīparastrīṣu | śrāddhakaraṇaṃ “namaskāreṇa mantreṇa” nama ityantena mantreṇa caturthyantadevatāsahitena |
[270] manuḥ |
aśaknuvaṃs tu śuśrūṣāṃ śūdraḥ kartuṃ dvijanmanām |
putradārātyayaṃ prāpto jīvet kārukarmabhiḥ ||
yaiḥ karmabhiḥ pracaritaiḥ śuśrūṣyante dvijātayaḥ |
tāni kārukakarmāṇi śilpāni vividhāni ca ||
“atyayaḥ” kṛcchram |
parāśaraḥ [1.72] |
lavaṇaṃ madhu madyaṃ vā takraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ payaḥ |
na duṣyec chūdrajātīnāṃ kuryāt sarveṣu vikrayam ||
kālikāpurāṇe |
vikrayaṃ sarvavastūnāṃ kurvan śūdro na doṣabhāk |
madhu carma surāṃ lākṣāṃ muktvā māṃsaṃ ca pañcamam ||
ayaṃ ca madhvādivikrayaniṣedhaḥ śūdrasya nātyantāpadi boddhavyaḥ |
[271] vyāsaḥ |
na ceha śūdraḥ patatīti niścayo
na caiva saṃskāram ihārhatīti ca |
śrutiprayuktaṃ na ca dharmam aśnute
na cāpi dharmāt pratiṣedhanaṃ śrutau ||
“na ceha śūdraḥ patatīti” māṃsādivikrayenāpi | “saṃskāraḥ” ihopanayanam | “ śrutiprayuktam” śrautam agnihotrādi | “dharmāt” śrutau kathitāt |
manuḥ |
na śūdra pātakaṃ kiṃcin na ca saṃskāram arhati |
nāsyādhikāro dharme ‘sti na dharmāt pratiṣedhanam ||
dharmepsavas tu dharmajñāḥ satāṃ dharmam anuṣṭhitāḥ |
mantravarjaṃ na duṣyanti praśaṃsāṃ prāpnuvanti ca ||
yathā yathā hi sadvṛttam ātiṣṭhaty anasūyakaḥ |
tathā tathemaṃ cāmuṃ ca lokam āpnoty aninditaḥ ||
śaktenāpi hi śūdreṇa na kāryo dhanasaṃcayaḥ |
śūdro hi dhanam āsādya brāhmaṇān eva bādhate ||
“mantravarjam” vaidikamantravarjam |
[272] āpastambaḥ |
pūjā varṇajyāyasāṃ kāryā, vṛddhatarāṇāṃ ca, hṛṣṭo darpati | dṛptaḥ svadharmam atikrāmati | dharmātikrame khalu punar narakaḥ |
“pūjā” namaskārādikā | “vṛddhatarāṇāṃ” sajātīyānām apīty arthaḥ |
gautamaḥ |
sarve cottarottaraṃ paricareyur āryānāryayor vyatikarme karmaṇaḥ sāmyam |
“āryānāryayoḥ” utkṛṣṭāpakṛṣṭajātīyayoḥ svakarmavyatikrame sāmyam | parasparaṃ pūjyapūjakabhāvo nāstīty arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
anāryam āryakarmāṇam āryaṃ cānāryakarmiṇaḥ |
saṃpradhāryābravīd dhātā na samau nāsamāv iti ||
“na samau” jātitaḥ ekasyotkṛṣṭavāt | “nāsamau” utkṛṣṭajāter api brāhmaṇādeḥ nīcakarmakāritvāt | tena tasyetareṇa śuśrūṣādikaṃ na kartavyam it tātparyārthaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
pāratantryaṃ samasteṣu teṣāṃ kāryeṣu vai tataḥ |
[273]        jayanti te nijān lokān kleśena mahatā dvijāḥ ||
dvijaśuśrūṣayaivaiṣa pākayajñādhikāravān |
nijān jayati vai lokān śūdro dhanyataras tataḥ ||
bhakṣyābhakṣyeṣu nāsyāsti preyāpeyeṣu vai punaḥ |
niyamo muniśārdūla tenāsau sādhv itīritam ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe śūdrādhikāre |
ayācitaḥ pradātā syāt kṛṣiṃ vṛttyartham āśrayet |
purāṇaṃ śṛṇuyān nityaṃ narasiṃhasya pūjanam ||
mahābhārate |
dūrāc chūdrenopacaryaḥ brāhmaṇo ‘gnir iva jvalan |
saṃspṛśya paricaryas tu vaiśyena kṣatriyeṇa ca ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
brāhmaṃ pājāpatyaṃ ca sthānam brāhmaṇasya, aindraṃ rājanyasya, mārutam vaiśyasya, gāndharvaṃ śūdrasya |
sve sve karmaṇy abhirataḥ svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ vrajanti te |
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsandhvigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau
gṛhasthakāṇḍe vṛttiparva samāptam ||
[274]
17
atha snātakavratāni
tatra manuḥ |
ato ‘nyatamayā vṛttyā jīvaṃs tu snātako dvijaḥ |
svargāyuṣyayaśasyāni vratānīmāni dhārayet ||
vedoditaṃ svakaṃ karma nityaṃ kuryād atandritaḥ |
tad dhi kurvan yathāśakti prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim ||
“anyatamayā vṛttyā” iti, anuvādakatvād ekatvam avivakṣitam | tenānekavṛttijīvane ‘py avirodhah | “vratam” idaṃ mayā kartavyam idaṃ na kartavyam iti śāstravihitaḥ saṃkalpaviśeṣaḥ | “vedoditam” pratyakṣānumeyavedabodhitam | “svakam” svāśramavihitam | “nityam” yāvajjīvam | “atandritaḥ” analasaḥ | “yathāśakti” asaṃbhave kiṃcidaṅgahānenāpi |
vyāsaḥ |
varṇāśrmāśrito dharmo vedakarmāśrito mataḥ |
vidvān hi vaidikaṃ karma kurvāṇo ‘bhīṣṭam aśnute ||
pradhānaṃ vaidikaṃ karma guṇabhūtaṃ tathetarat |
guṇaniṣṭho ‘tipramāṇaṃ bādhayan yāty adhogatim ||
[275] “vedakarmāśritaḥ” vaidikamarmāśritaḥ | “guṇaniṣṭhaḥ” smṛtyācāraniṣṭhaḥ | “atipramāṇam” vedam |
manu-vasiṣṭhau |
vedābhyāso ‘nvahaṃ śaktyā mahāyajñakriyā kṣamā |
nāśayanty āśu pāpāni mahāpātakāny api ||
“vedābhyāsaḥ” atra brahmayañjādiḥ |
āpastambaḥ |
yoktā ca dharmayukteṣu dravyaparigraheṣu ca pratipādayitā ca tīrthe yantā vātīrthe yato bhayaṃ na syāt | samgrahītā manuṣyān, bhoktā ca dharmāvipratiṣiddhān bhogān evam ubhau lokāv abhijayati |
“yoktā” udyogī | “dharmayukteṣu” vihiteṣu nādharmaniṣprayojanayoḥ | “tīrthe” pātre | “yantā” apātradānād uparataḥ | “yataḥ” iti, bhayahetos taskarāder apātrasyāpi dātavyam ity arthaḥ | “manuṣyān” karmakarādīn | “bhogān” kuṅkumādīn |
saṃvartaḥ |
kuryād gṛhyāṇi karmāṇi svabhāryāpoṣaṇam param |
ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt prāpnoti paramāṃ gatim ||
paraṃ dharmam āhur iti śeṣaḥ |
[276] sumantuḥ |
śeṣānnam ṛtugāmitvaṃ pañcayajñārthakarma ca |
tulyaiś ca saha saṃbandho gṛhasthasya vidhīyate ||
“tulyaiś ca saha sambandhaḥ” asaty uttame boddhavyaḥ |
manuḥ |
uttamair uttamair nityaṃ saṃbandhān ācaret saha |
ninīyuḥ kulam utkarṣam adhamān adhamāṃs tyajet ||
uttamān uttamān gacchan hīnān hīnāṃś ca varjayet |
brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭhatām eti pratyavāye tu śūdratām ||
“pratyavāye” viparītācaraṇe |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
satyaṃ samadhikaṃ kuryād upeyād īśvaraṃ sadā |
vairam nirhetukaṃ vādaṃ na kuryāt kenacit saha ||
jñātibandhuvivāhyebhyo mitram abhyadhikaṃ smṛtam |
samānam asamānaṃ vā na kurvīta paryatnataḥ ||
śokārātibhayatrāṇaprītivisrambhabhājanam |
āpaddhanaṃ ca mitraṃ ca loke bandhyadhikam hi tat ||
asamāno ‘dhivisrambho viśvāsaḥ tasya garhitaḥ |
[277] vasiṣṭhaḥ |
āhāranirhāravihārayogāḥ
susaṃvṛtā dharmavidā tu kāryāḥ |
vāgbuddhivīryāṇi tapas tathaiva
dhanāyuṣī guptatame tu kārye ||
“nirhāraḥ” mūtrapurīṣotsargaḥ | “vihāraḥ” strīsevā | “yogaḥ” samādhiḥ | “vāgguptiḥ” asatyālāpādiparivarjanena | “buddhiguptiḥ” aśubhasaṃkalpādiparivarjanena | vīryaṃ sāmarthyam | tasya rakṣā asthāne viniyogābhāvaḥ | “tapo” brahmacaryādi | tasya rakṣā vismayākaraṇam | “guptatame” aprakāśitatame |
dakṣaḥ |
āyur vittaṃ gṛhachidraṃ mantro maithunam auṣadham |
tapo dharmo ‘vamānaṃ ca nava gopyāni sarvadā ||
prāyogyam ṛṇaśuddhiś ca dānādhamanavikriyāḥ |
kanyādānaṃ vṛṣotsargo rahaḥpāpaṃ ca kutsitam ||
“gṛhachidram” anarthahetur abhyasūyādi | “prāyogyam” vṛddhyā dhanaprayogaḥ | “ṛṇam” ṛṇagrahaṇam | ṛṇasyaiva śuddhiḥ | “dānam” bhūmyādeḥ sthāvarasya | “ādhamanam” ādhiḥ |
manuḥ |
vayasaḥ karmaṇo ‘rthasya śrutasyābhijanasya ca |
veṣavāgbuddhisārūpyam ācaran vicared iha ||
[278] “sārūpyam” aucityam | tena vayaādyucitā veṣādayaḥ kartavyā ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [71.5–7] |
vayo’nurūpaṃ veṣaṃ ca kuryāt | śrutasyābhijanasya [dhanasya” ca deśasya ca | noddhataḥ |
manu-vasiṣṭhau [MDh 4.145–46] |
maṅgalācārayuktaḥ syāt prayatātmā jitendriyaḥ |
japec ca juhuyāc caiva nityam agnim atandritaḥ ||
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ nityaṃ ca prayatātmanām |
japatāṃ juhvatāṃ caiva vinipāto na vidyate |
“maṅgalācāraḥ” prātar gorocanālambhanādi | “vinipātaḥ” iṣṭaviyogādi |
viṣṇuḥ [23.57–59] |
gāvaḥ pavitraṃ māṅgalyaṃ goṣu lokāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ |
gāvo vitanvate yajñaṃ gāvaḥ sarvāghasūdanāḥ ||
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ dadhi ca rocanā |
ṣaḍaṅgam etat paramaṃ maṅgalaṃ sarvadā gavām ||
śṛṅgodakaṃ gavāṃ puṇyaṃ sarvāghaviniṣūdanam ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
atha rakṣoghnāni ajābalisuvarṇarajatatāmracandanamaṇayaḥ | sarpiḥ sarṣapakṣaumakṛṣṇājinanāgayavalājādarśapriyaṅgugoroca[279]nāmanaḥśilāḥ | gauḥ pūrṇakumbhanāgaśamīpalāśapuṣpadarbhāṇi ca sadā svādhyāyadhanyaḥ, trayāṇāṃ ca varṇānāṃ pūrvarātre jāgaraṇam |
“nāgaḥ” hastī | “darbhāḥ” kuśāḥ | “puṣpāṇi” nīlotpalādīni |
pṛthivīṃ prati lakṣmīvākye viṣṇuḥ |
vasāmy athārke ca niśākare ca
tārāgaṇāḍhye gagane vimeghe |
tathā lasallambapayodhare ca
śakrāyudhādye sataḍitprakāśe ||
tathā suvarṇe vimale ca raupye
ratneṣu vastreṣv amaleṣu bhūmau |
prāsādamālāsu ca pāṇḍurāsu
devālayeṣu dhvajabhūṣiteṣu ||
sadyaḥ kṛte vāpy atha gomaye tu
matte gajendre turage prakṛṣṭe |
vṛṣe tathā darpasamanvite ca
vipre tathaivādhyayanopapanne ||
siṃhāsane cāmalake ca bilve
chatre ca śaṅkhe ca sadaiva padme |
dīpte hutāśe vimale ca khaḍge
ādarśabimbe ca sadā sthitāham ||
pūrṇodakumbheṣu sacāmareṣu
satālavṛnteṣu ca bhūṣaṇeṣu |
bhṛṅgārapatreṣu manohareṣu
mṛdi sthitāhaṃ ca navoddhatāyām ||
[280]        kṣīre tathā sarpiṣi śādvaleṣu
kṣaudre tathā dadhni purandhrigātre |
dehe kumāryāś ca tathā surāṇāṃ
tapasvināṃ yajñakṛtāṃ ca dehe ||
śūre ca saṃgrāmavinirgate ‘haṃ
sthitā mṛte svargam itaḥ prayāte |
vedadhvanau cāpy atha śaṅkhaśabde
puṇyāhaśabde ca savādyaghoṣe ||
rājābhiṣeke ca tathā vivāhe
yajñe nare snātaśirasy aśuṣke |
puṣpeṣu śukleṣu ca parvateṣu
phaleṣu ramyeṣu saridvarāsu ||
saraḥsu pūrṇeṣu tathārjunīṣu
saśādvalāyāṃ bhuvi padmaṣaṇḍe |
bāleṣu vatseṣu śiśau prahṛṣṭe
sādhau nare dharmaparāyaṇe ca ||
ācāraseviny atha śāstravitve
vinītaveṣe ca tathā suveṣe |
suśuddhadante malavardhite ca
miṣṭāśane cātitipūjake ca ||
svadāratuṣṭe nirate ca dharme
sattvotkare cātyaśanād vimukte |
sadā supuṇyeṣu sugandhigātre
nityānulipte ca vibhūṣiteṣu ||
satye sthite bhūtahite niviṣṭe
kṣamāyute krodhavivarjite vā |
[281]        susaṃyatārthe parakāryadakṣe
kalyāṇacitteṣu sadā sunīte ||
“lambapayodhare” lambamānastanadhāriṇi | “kṛte” saṃskṛte | “arjunīṣu” goṣu |
mahābhārate |
vasāmi satye subhage sudānte
dakṣe nare karmaṇi vartamāne |
akrodhine daivapare kṛtajñe
jitendriye nityam udīrṇasatye ||
nākarmaśīle puruṣe vasāmi
na nāstike sāṅkṛtike kṛtaghne |
na bhinnasattve na nṛsaṃśavṛtte
na cāpi caure na ca nagnamūrkhe ||
na cālpatejo balasattvasārā
hṛṣyanti kupyanti ca yatra yatra |
na devi tiṣṭhāmi tathāvidheṣu
nareṣv asaṃprāptamanoratheṣu ||
yaś cātmanaḥ prārthayate na kiṃcid
yaś ca svabhāvopahṛtāntarātmā (?)
teṣv alpasantoṣapareṣu nityaṃ
nareṣu nāhaṃ nivasāmi devi ||
[282]        vasāmi dharmaśīleṣu dharmajñeṣu kṛtātmasu |
vṛddhaseviṣu dānteṣu darmajñeṣu mahātmasu ||
strīṣu dakṣāsu dāntāsu devadvijaparāsu ca |
vasāmi satyabālāsu svabhāvavinatāsu ca ||
prakīrṇabhāṇḍām anavekṣyakāriṇīṃ
sadā ca bhartuḥ partikūlavāsinīm |
parasya veśmābhiratām alajjām
evaṃvidhāṃ strīṃ parivarjayāmi ||
lolām adhokṣīm avagāhinīṃ ca
vyapetaśīlāṃ kalahapriyāṃ ca |
nidrābhubhūtāṃ satataṃ śayanām
evaṃvidhāṃ strīṃ praivarjayāmi ||
satyāsu nityaṃ priyadarśanāsu
saubhāgyayuktāsu guṇānvitāsu |
yāneṣu kanyāsu vibhūṣaṇeṣu
yajñeṣu medheṣu ca vṛṣṭimatsu ||
vasāmi phullāsu ca padminīṣu
nakṣatravedīṣu ca śāradīṣu ||
śaileṣu goṣtheṣu tathā raṇeṣu
saraḥsu phullotpalapaṅkajeṣu |
nadīṣu haṃsakharanādinīṣu
krauñcāvaguṣṭasvaraśobhitāsu ||
vistīrṇakūladrumaśobhitāsu
tapasvisiddhadvijasevitāsu |
vasāmi nityaṃ subahūdakāsu
sunakramakarākulitodakāsu ||
[283]        matte gajendre vṛṣabhe narendre
siṃhāsane satpuruṣe ca nityam |
yasmin gṛhe hāvyati hūyate ca
gobrāhmaṇe cārcyate devatāś ca ||
kāle ca puṣpair balayaḥ kriyante
tasmin gṛhe vāsam upaimi nityam |
svādhyāyayuktaṣu dvijeṣu nityaṃ
kṣatre ca dharmābhirate sadaiva |
vaiśye ca kṛṣyābhirate vasāmi
śūdre ca śuśrūṣānityayukte ||
nārāyaṇe tv ekamatā vasāmi
sarveṇa bhāvena śarīrabhūtā |
asmin dharmaḥ sumahān hi viṣṇau
brāhmaṇyatā cātra tathā priyatvam ||
nāhaṃ śarīreṇa vasāmi deva
naitan mayā śakyam ihābhidhātum |
yasmin svabhāvena vasāmi puṃsi
sa vardhate dharmayaśo ‘rthakāmaiḥ ||
tathā indraṃ prati śrīr uvāca |
asureṣv avasaṃ pūrvaṃ satvadharmanibandhanā |
viparītān sutān buddhvā tvayi vāsam arocayam ||
[284]        sabhāsadāṃ tu vṛddhānāṃ satyāḥ kathayatāṃ kathāḥ |
prāhasaś ca cābhyasūyaṃ ca sarvavṛddhān guṇāvarāḥ ||
uccaiś cāpy avadan rātrau nīcais tatrāgnim ajvalan |
putrāḥ pitṝn abhyavadan striyaś cābhyavadan patīn ||
nāśaucam anurudhyanta teṣāṃ mūḍhajanās tathā |
manasā karmaṇā vācā bhaktam āsīd anāvṛtam ||
viprakīrṇāni dhānyāni tathā mūṣikabhojanam |
apāvṛtaṃ payo ‘tiṣṭhad ucchiṣṭāś ca spṛśan ghṛtam ||
kuddāladātṛpiṭakaṃ prakīrṇaṃ kāṃsyabhājanam |
dravyopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ nānuvekṣat kuṭimbinī ||
prakārāgāravidhvastā na ca tat pratyavekṣataḥ |
āśramasthā vikarmasthāḥ pradviṣanti parasparam ||
ye ca vedavido viprā vispaṣṭam anṛtāś ca ye |
nirantaraviśeṣās te bahumānāvamānayoḥ ||
mahābhārate |
hāram ābharaṇaṃ veṣaṃ gatisthitim avikṣitim |
āsevante bhujiṣyān vai durjanācaritaṃ vidhim ||
striyaḥ puruṣaveṣeṇa narāḥ strīveṣadhāriṇaḥ |
krīḍārativihāreṣu parāṃ mudam avāpnuvan ||
prabhavabhyapurādāyān arhebhyaḥ pratipāditān |
nābhyavartanta nāstikyādṛtitaṃ (?) saṃbhaveṣv api ||
[285]        śvaśrūśvaśurayor agre vadhūḥ preṣyānuśāsanam |
anvaśāsac ca bhartāraṃ saṃmukhī yāti jalpanaiḥ ||
prayatnenāpi cārakṣed vittaṃ putrasya vai pitā |
vyabhajac cāpi saṃrambhād duḥkhavāsaṃ tathā vasan ||
taiś caiva sāditācārā nācāreṣu viparyaye |
nāhaṃ devendra vatsyāmi dānaveṣv iti me matiḥ ||
tathā |
gṛhe pārāvatā dhanyāḥ śukā vai sārikās tathā |
gṛha ete praśastāḥ syuḥ tathā vai tailapāyikāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
ādyā vyāhṛtayas tisraḥ svāhā svadhā namo vaṣaṭ |
yasyaite veśmani sadā brahmalokastha eva saḥ ||
tathā |
yasya vedamayaṃ cakṣuḥ vācaṃ ca vyāhṛtīmayam |
tejomayaṃ śarīraṃ ca sa kasmād avasīdati ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
hutāgnihotrasya kṛtāhnikasya
samāptajapyasya pathi sthitasya |
[286]        ye brāhmaṇasyopari divyalokāḥ
kāṅkṣanti ye vai ṛṣayaḥ samastāḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [81.90] |
śrutismṛtyuditaṃ samyak sādhubhir yaś ca sevitaḥ |
tam ācāraṃ niṣeveta dharmakāmo jitendriyaḥ ||
manu [4.178] |
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ |
tena yāyāt satāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchan na riṣyati ||
yenānuṣṭhānaprakāreṇa “na riṣyati” na bādhyate | idaṃ ca vaikalpikaviṣayam |
hārītaḥ |
karmaṇā prāpnuyāl lokān karmaṇā mucyate ‘śubhāt |
karmaṇā sarvam āpnoti yat kiṃcin manasecchati ||
ahiṃsre karmaṇi śubhe mokṣaṃ gacchati paṇḍitaḥ |
etad vaḥ kathaye viprā nākarmā kiṃcid āpnuyat ||
“ahiṃsre” avihitahiṃsārahite | “paṇḍito” ‘tra paramātmajñaḥ | “akarmā” cāśramakarmarahitaḥ |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
śvaḥ kāryaṃ sadyaḥ kurvīta pūrvāhṇe cāparāhṇikam |
[287]        na hi pratīkṣate mṛtyuḥ kṛtaṃ vāsya na vākrtam ||
idaṃ cādarātiśayārtham aniyatakālaviṣayam |
manuḥ [4.159–161] |
yad yat paravaśaṃ karma tat tad yatnena varjayet |
yad yad ātmavaśaṃ tu syāt tat tat seveta yatnataḥ ||
sarvaṃ paravaśaṃ duḥkhaṃ sarvam ātmavaśaṃ sukham |
etad vidyāt samāsena lakṣaṇaṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ ||
yat karma karvato ‘sya syāt paritoṣo ‘ntarātmanaḥ |
tat prayatnena kurvīta viparītaṃ tu varjayet ||
yat svavaśaṃ paraprārthnāyattam | etac ca yathā yathā kathaṃcid dhanasaṃbhave santoṣaparatāpratipādanārtham | “yat karma kurvataḥ” ityādinā ātmatuṣṭeḥ | punarvacanaṃ snātakavratatvajñāpanārtham |
laghuhārītaḥ |
vane ‘pi doṣāḥ prabhavanti rāgiṇāṃ
gṛheṣu pañcendriyanigrahas tapaḥ |
akutsite karmaṇi yaḥ pravartate
nivṛttarāgasya gṛhaṃ tapovanam ||
satkarmoddeśakrame dakṣaḥ |
saṃdhyāsnānaṃ japo homaḥ svādhyāyo devatārcanam |
[288]        vaiśvadevekṣaṇātithyam (uddhṛtaṃ) vāpy aśaktitaḥ ||
“īkṣaṇam” atithipradīkṣaṇam | “uddhṛtaṃ vāpy aśaktitaḥ” | atithibhojanāsāmarthye stokam apy annam uddhared ity arthaḥ |
yamaḥ |
kiṃ brahmacaryair yadi dārasevī
kiṃ copavāsaiḥ yadi śeṣabhojī |
maunena kiṃ syād yadi satyavādī
satyena kiṃ syād yadi vā nṛśaṃsaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
satyavādī, sadā tuṣṭaḥ, ṛtukālābhigāmī, nityaṃ snānaśīlo, nityaṃ cākrodhano, aparopatāpy akuhako, nityodakī, nityayajñopavītī, nityaṃ svādhyāyī, dharmānveṣī, patitānnavarjī, vītarāgalobhamoho, vittaśāṭhyavivarjito, anasūyako, gṛhastho bhavati |
“akuhakaḥ” amāyāvī | “kharvaḥ” vakraḥ (?) | “nityodakī” sodakakamaṇḍaludhārī |
kaśyapaḥ |
āhitāgniḥ, bhavat svadāranirataḥ, snānānn ṛtukālābhigāmī, devapitṛbhūtamanuṣyabrahmayajñān upasevamānaḥ, [289] ekadvitricaturṇāṃ brāhmaṇānām ātithyaṃ kurvāṇaḥ, vrataniyamajapyahomamantraparo, mātāpitṛbhaktaḥ, bhṛtyāpatyayoḥ śeṣānnabhojī, na cāsūyako, yat (kiṃcit) kiṃcāśī, tolakabhedakavedhakasūcakān pariharamāṇaḥ śreyasā yujyate |
“vratam” atra yamāḥ | “japyamantrāḥ” sāvitryādayaḥ | “yatkiṃcāśī” na rasalolupaḥ | “bhedakaḥ” samayādeḥ | “vedhakaḥ” paramarmaṇaḥ | “sūcakaḥ” piśunaḥ |
vāsiṣṭha-hārīta-baudhāyanāḥ | [8.17]
nityodakī nityayajñopavītī
nityasvādhāyī patitānnavarjī |
ṛtau ca gacchan vidhivac ca juhvan
na brāhmaṇaś cyavate brahmalokāt ||
uśanāḥ |
tatra vedavidyāvratasnātakaḥ śucir nityodakī nityayajñopavītī, ubhayakālaṃ nityasaṃdhyām upāsīta snānaśīlaḥ |
gautamaḥ [1.9.68] |
satyadharmā, āryavṛttaḥ, śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ, śaucaśiṣṭaḥ, śrutinirataḥ syāt, nityam ahiṃsro mṛdur dṛḍhakārī damadānaśīlaḥ | [290] evamācāro mātāpitarau pūrvāparān saṃbandhān duritebhyo mokṣayiṣyan snātakaḥ śaśvad brahmalokān na cyavate |
“satyadharmā” satyasandhaḥ | “āryavṛttaḥ” āryapriyakāricaritaḥ | “śiṣṭādhyāpakaḥ” sādhūnām adhyāpayitā | “śaucaśiṣṭaḥ” śauce yatnavān | “mṛduḥ” pāruṣyarahitaḥ | “dṛḍhakārī” ārabdhasya samāpayitā | “mokṣayiṣyan” mokṣayitum | evamācāra iti sambandhaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ |
trivargasevāṃ satatānnadānaṃ surārcanaṃ brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ ca |
svādhyāyasevāṃ pitṛtarpaṇaṃ ca kṛtvā gṛhī śakrasadaḥ prayāti ||
manuḥ [4.175] |
satyadharmāryavṛtteṣu śauce caivāramet sadā |
śiṣyāṃś ca śiṣyād dharmeṇa vāgbāhūdarasaṃyataḥ ||
“śiṣyā” śāsanīyāḥ putrādayaḥ | “bāhusaṃyamaḥ” bāhumūlena parasyābādhanam |
parāśaraḥ |
yāvaj juhoty adhīte ca dadāty adhyāpayaty api |
japaty arcati devāṃś ca tāvat tu dvija ucyate ||
[291] yamaḥ |
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ |
kiṃlakṣaṇo bhaved dharmaḥ kva vā dharmaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
kaḥ sarvasaṃmato dharmaḥ kiṃ ca dharmaparāyaṇam ||
yama uvāca |
tapaḥparamako dharmo dānejyālobhalakṣaṇaḥ |
maryādāyāṃ sthito dharmaḥ saṃsthayā ca pravartate ||
satyaṃ saduditaḥ dharmaḥ satyaṃ dharmaparāyaṇam |
“tapaḥparamakaḥ” tapaḥpradhānakaḥ | “saṃsthā” maryāadā | “satyam”bhūtārthavāditvam | dvitīyasatyaśabdenātra brahmocyate |
dakṣaḥ |
vibhāgaśīlatā yasya kṣamāyukto dayālukaḥ |
gṛhasthas tu samākhyāto na gṛheṇa gṛhī bhavet ||
dayā lajjā dhṛtiḥ śraddhā prajñā tyāgaḥ kṛtajñatā |
ete yasya guṇāḥ santi gṛhastho mukta ucyate ||
[292] yamaḥ |
śaucam adhyayanaṃ dānaṃ nityaṃ viṣayasaṃjñakaḥ |
atra yatnena saṃbhāvyaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ prāpya durlabham ||
atra śaucādiṣu yatnena yatnavatā [bhāvyam ity arthaḥ] |
śātātapaḥ |
bhavec chaucaparo nityaṃ tathā dehe jitendriyaḥ |
akrodhaḥ sarvabhūteṣu sādhukārī priyaṃvadaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
satyaṃ yajñas tapo dānam etad dharmasya lakṣaṇam |
dharmāt sukhaṃ ca jñānaṃ ca jñānān mokṣo viśiṣyate ||
tapo dharmaḥ kṛtayuge yajñaṃ tretāyuge smṛtam |
dvāpare cādhvaraḥ proktas tiṣye dānaṃ dayā damaḥ ||
“tiṣyaḥ” kaliḥ |
kṛte yad abdād dharmaṃ syāt tretāyāṃ tu ṛtutrayāt |
dvāpare tu tripakṣeṇa kalāv ahnā ca tatphalam ||
[293]        dayā dharmeṇa dānena tapasā brahmacaryayā |
śamayitvendriyagrāmaṃ brahmalokam avāpnuyāt ||
manuḥ [4.246] |
dṛḍhakārī mṛdur dāntaḥ krūrācārair asaṃvasan |
ahiṃsādamadānābhyāṃ japet svargaṃ tathā vrataiḥ ||
harītaḥ |
damo dayā dānaṃ dṛḍhavratatvaṃ ceti |
brāhmaṇasya kulaśrutaśīlavṛttāvasthānaṃ dama ity ācakṣate |
sarvabhūtānukampanaṃ dayā | saṃvibhāgo dānam |
trisaṃdhyam askannaṃ devatārcanajapahomadhyānaniyamānuṣṭhānaṃ dṛḍhavratatvam | trisaṃdhyaṃ vai niyamināṃ pāpma | śīryate tapobhūyastvam upaiti brahmaprakāśatāṃ gacchati | tasmāt trisaṃdhyaṃ niyamī syād brāhmaṇasyeti |
“brāhmaṇasya” brāhmaṇagrahaṇam eteṣu tasya yatnātiśayārtham | “śīlam” brāhmaṇyatādi | “askandanam” sarvāṅgopetaṃ [294] askhalitaṃ vā | devatārcanajapahomādhyayanāny eva niyamāḥ | “teṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ” [vā “niyamānuṣṭhānaṃ”] trisaṃdhyam iti saṃdhyātritayakāle | yathāyogam eṣām anuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
punar hārītaḥ |
sthitir avicchinnavedāveditā ayonisaṅkaritā avichinnārṣeyatvaṃ ceti kulaguṇāḥ | vedāṅgāni dharmo ‘dhyātmavijñānaṃ sthitiś ceti ṣaḍvidhaṃ śrutam |
kṣamā damo dayā dānam ahiṃsā gurupūjanam |
śaucaṃ snānaṃ japo homas tapaḥ svādhyāyaḥ satyavacanaṃ saṃtoṣo dṛḍhavratvaṃ ceti |
gautamaḥ |
sarvata evātmānaṃ gopāyīta |
yamaḥ |
dharmakṣetraṃ śarīraṃ hi yatnena paripālayet |
śarīrāt sravate dharmaḥ parvatāt salilaṃ yathā ||
vyāsaḥ |
viṣayeṣu na sajjeta manaḥsaṃyamam ācaret |
manaḥsaṃyamam ātiṣṭhan brahmalokam avāpnuyāt ||
[295] viṣṇuḥ |
damayanenātiṣṭhet | damaś cendriyāṇāṃ prakīrtitaḥ | dāntasyāyaṃ lokaḥ paraś ca |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.2–3] |
sadānupahate vastre praśastāś ca vanauṣadhīḥ |
gāruḍāni ca ratnāni bibhṛyāt prayato naraḥ ||
suśuddhāmalakeśaś ca sugandhiś cāruveṣadhṛk |
sitāḥ sumanaso hṛṣṭā bibhṛyāc ca naraḥ sadā ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.54] |
na cāpi raktavāsāḥ syāt citrāsitadharo ‘pi vā |
na ca kuryād viparyāsaṃ vāsasor na c bhūṣaṇe ||
varjyaṃ ca vidaśaṃ vastram atyantopahataṃ ca yat |
“atyantopahatam” surādiliptam |
mahābhārate |
anyad eva bhaved vāsaḥ śayanīye sadaiva tu |
anyad rathyāsu devānām arcāyām anyad eva hi ||
“rathyāsu” yatheṣṭaceṣṭāsu |
[296] precetāḥ |
na suvarṇam anagnyaṃ dhārayet |
kālikāpurāṇe |
kāyasthitena hemnā tu karma yat kusitaṃ naraḥ |
ācaret tad ayaṃ tasya vajrakalpaṃ bhaviṣyati ||
anāmāṅgulikāyāṃ tu dhārayed dakṣiṇe kare |
“kāyasthena” iti dakṣiṇakarānāmāṅgulikā | vyatiriktāṅgasthena suvarṇena kutsitakarma na kuryāt | tathākaraṇe pāpādhikyaṃ bhavatīti tātparyārthaḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
yaḥ kuryāt sarvakarmāṇi vastrapūtena vāriṇā |
sa muniḥ sa mahāsādhuḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
tathā |
sopānatko devagṛham ārohed yas tu mānavaḥ |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ tāmisraṃ nāma nāmataḥ ||
mahābhārate |
śiraḥsnātas tu tailena nāṅgaṃ kiṃcid api spṛśet |
tilapiṣṭaṃ ca nāśnīyāt tathāyur vidate mahat ||
[297]        snātasya varṇakaṃ hṛdyaṃ tat pradadyād viśāṃpate |
priyaṅgukalikābhyāṃ ca bilvena tagareṇa ca |
pṛthag evānuliptena kesareṇa ca buddhimān ||
padmapurāṇe saptarṣiśapatheṣu |
anṛtau maithunaṃ yātu divā svapnaṃ niṣevatu |
anyonyātithyam abhyetu bisastainyaṃ karoti yaḥ ||
tathā |
gobrāhmaṇāṃś ca tīrthāni parvatāṃś ca nadīṃs tathā |
devāṃś caiva hṛdā vandan na preto jāyate naraḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [2.255] |
gobrāhmaṇānalānnāni nocchiṣṭo na padā spṛśet |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
śleṣmamūtrapurīṣāṇi samutsṛjati yas tu vai |
devasyāyatane bhānoḥ sa gacchen narakān kramāt ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.30] |
ucchiṣṭo nālapet kiṃcit svādhyāyaṃ ca vivarjayet |
[298] tathā [34.44] |
notkṣiptapādajaṅghas tu prājñas tiṣṭhet kathaṃcana |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.8–9, 12, 16, 19, 29] |
pradīptaṃ veśma na viśen nārohec chikharaṃ naraḥ |
na kuryād dantasaṃgharṣaṃ na kuṣṇīyāc ca nāsikām ||
nāsaṃvṛtamukho jṛmbhet chvāsakāsau na vartayet |
noccair haset saśabdaṃ ca na muñcet pavanaṃ budhaḥ ||
na huṃkuryāc chavaṃ ghrātvā śavasaṅgho hi śobhanaḥ |
upasarpen na ca vyālaṃ ciraṃ tiṣṭhen na cotthitaḥ ||
atīva jāgarasvapne tadvat sthānāśane budhaḥ |
na seveta tathā śayyāṃ vyāyāmaṃ ca nareśvara ||
daṃṣṭriṇaḥ śṛṅgiṇaś caiva prājño dūreṇa varjayet |
avaśyāyaṃ ca rājendra puro vātātapau tathā ||
muktakacchaś ca nācāmed devādyarcāṃ ca varjayet |
śleṣmasiṃhānikotsargo nānnakāle praśasyate ||
balimaṅgalyajapyādau na home na mahājane |
“devādyarcāṃ muktakacchaḥ” ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | “mahājane” mahājanamadhye |
devīpurāṇe |
na viṣaṃ bhakṣayet prājño na bujaṃgaiś ca krīḍayet |
[299]        netrāñjanaṃ niṣeveta dantadhāvanapūrvakam ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.6–7] |
didhiṣūpatitonmattabahuvairātikīṭakaiḥ |
bandhakī bandhakībhartṛkṣudrānṛtakair (?) nartakaiḥ (?) saha ||
tathātivyayaśīlaiś ca parivādarataiḥ śaṭhaiḥ |
budho maitrīṃ na kurvīta naikaḥ panthānam āvrajet ||
“atikīṭakaḥ” atikṛpaṇaḥ | “bandhakī” vyabhicāriṇī |
na kuryān maitrīm ity anuvṛttau mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.89] |
na sarvaśaṅkibhir nityaṃ na ca devaparair naraiḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
yadākāśaḥ smṛto bhīmas tasmān nāsaṃvṛte kvacit |
kuryān mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā na bhuñjīta pibeta vā ||
maithunaṃ cāpi na cared ucchiṣṭāni ca varjayet |
“ucchiṣṭāni” upabhuktaśiṣṭāni |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.7] |
na yajñaṃ na tapāṃsī vā puruṣasya vibhūtaye |
bhavanti yaḥ sadācāraṃ samullaṅghya pravartate ||
[300] vāyupurāṇe |
ekasyārthāya yo hanyād ātmano vā parasya vā |
ekaṃ prāṇaṃ bahūn vāpi kāmaṃ tasyāpi pātakam ||
yasmiṃs tu nihate ‘bhadre edhante bahavaḥ sukham |
tasmin hate ‘śubhe nāsti pātakaṃ copapātakam ||
mahābhārate |
yaḥ pravṛttiṃ śrutiṃ mohāc chāstraṃ ca munibhāṣitam |
dveṣayaty anabhijñāya taṃ vidyād brahmaghātakam ||
mahābhārate |
baliṃ prati śrīvākyāni |
yadā śuśrūṣayā vṛddhāṃ parivāreṇa yojyate |
putraś ca pitaraṃ mohāt preṣayiṣyati karmasu ||
brāhmaṇaiḥ kārayiṣyanti vṛṣalāḥ pādadhāvanam |
śūdraś ca brāhmaṇīṃ bhāryām upayāsyati nirbhayaḥ ||
viyoniṣu ca bījāni mokṣyante puruṣāḥ sadā |
saṅkaraṃ kāṃsyabhāṇḍaiś ca baliś cāpi kupātrakaiḥ ||
cāturvarṇyaṃ yadā kṛtsnam unmaryādaṃ bhaviṣyati |
ekaikas te tadā pāśaḥ kramaśaḥ parimokṣyate ||
[301] viṣṇupurāṇe |
devādiniśvāsahataṃ śarīraṃ yat tasya veśma ca |
na tena saṅkaraṃ kuryād gṛhāsanaparicchadaiḥ ||
“devādiniśvāsahatam” yathocitapūjāvirahāt |
[iti śrīgṛhasthakāṇde snātakavratāni]
[302]
18
atha yamaniyamāḥ
tatra manuḥ [4.204] |
yamān seveta satataṃ na nityaṃ niyamān budhaḥ |
yamān pataty akurvāṇo niyamān kevalān bhajan ||
idaṃ ca yamaniyamayor yugapadupanipāte vihitatvāviśeṣād vikalpaprasaṅge boddhayam | “satata”" āpady api |
vyāsaḥ |
ahiṃsā satyavacanaṃ brahmacaryam akalkatā |
asteyam iti pañcaite yamāś caiva vratāni ca ||
akrodho guruśuśrūṣā śaucam āhāralāghavam |
apramādaś ca niyamāḥ pañcaivopavratāni ca ||
brahmacaryam atra pratiṣiddhamaithunavarjanam | “akalkatā” dambharahitatvam |
[303] śaṅkhalikhitau |
akrodha ārjavaṃ śaucaṃ vyavahāro guruśuśrūṣā ceti niyamāḥ pañca |
“vyavahāraḥ” śāstrārthācaraṇam | akrodhādīnām atrāntarbhāve ‘py ādarārthe punarvacanam |
yājñavalkyaḥ [3.312–13] |
brahmacaryaṃ dayā kṣāntir dānaṃ satyam akalkatā |
ahiṃsāstreyamādhurye damaś ceti yamāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
snānamaunopavāsejyāsvādhyāyopasthanigrahāḥ |
niyamā guruśuśrūṣā śaucākrodhāpramādatā ||
“dhyānam” paramātmacintanam | “mādhuryam” priyavāditvādi | “apramādatā” svakartavyeṣv avadhānam |
hārītaḥ |
ahiṃsā satyam asteyaṃ śaucaṃ brahmacaryam iti niyamāḥ | [304]
kūrma ivāṅgāni karmabhyaḥ saṃhṛtya rūpebhyo dṛṣṭim, mūka iva vācyebhhyo vācam, karmendriyabuddhīndriyāṇi manaś ca saṃyamyāhiṃsā bhavati |
evaṃ hy āha –
karmanā cakṣuṣā caiva vācātha manasā tathā |
yo vai na kurute pāpaṃ sā cāhiṃsā caturvidhā || iti |
devalaḥ |
tatra daśavidhā hiṃsā | udvegajananam, santāpajananam, rujākaraṇam, śoṇitotpadanam, paiśunyakaraṇam, sukhāpakaraṇam, atikramaḥ, saṃrodho, hitapratiṣedho, vadha iti |
tatra ślokaḥ |
kāyakleśaṃ manoduḥkhaṃ vadhaṃ vā prāṇināṃ punaḥ |
yatra vardhayati dveṣāt sā hiṃseti samāsataḥ ||
“udvego” manaso ‘svāsthyam | “santāpo” yasmin duḥkhe utpanne ākulitam evātmānaṃ manyate | “rujā” śarīraduḥkham | “atikramo” manyāvamānanam | “saṃrodhaḥ” svecchācāraniṣedhaḥ | “hitapratiṣedhaḥ” duḥkhāpanāyakabheṣajaniṣedhaḥ |
mahābhārate |
yūkamatkuṇadaṃśādīn khādataś cātmanas tanum |
putravat parirakṣanti te narāḥ svargagāminaḥ ||
[305] hārītaḥ |
ātmā sannasyaiṣā vāk satī yayā vadati yat sataḥ |
[satī vadati tad etat satīyaṃ satyam ity ācakṣate ||
yathāgniḥ ghṛtāhutihuto dīpyate, evaṃ satyābhidhāpy ātmā brahmānnadattam āptiṃ prativadan dīpayati | atha yathādbhir agniṃ dhvaṃsayaṃs tamāṃsi pratipadyate evam anṛtaṃ dhruvaṃ vadan tamāṃsi pratipadyate] tasmān nānrtaṃ brūyāt | somakrayakanyāvivāhabhayamaithunabālasaṃjñapana[gobrāhmaṇahite ca bruvan mithyān na lipyate | evaṃ hy āha |
yatra prāṇivadho nāsti yac ca nityam aninditam |
svabhāvataś ca yat satyaṃ tat satyavacanaṃ smṛtam || ]
yathā vācā ātmā vadatīty anvayaḥ | “ātmano hitam” yad va[stu satī vadati tat satyam | “bālasaṃjñapanam” bālasaṃtoṣaṇam] “aninditam” aślīlatādirahitam | svabhāvataś chalarahitam |
manu-yamau [4.138] |
satyaṃ brūyāt priyaṃ brūyān na brūyāt satyam apriyam |
[306]        priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
etena priyaṃ satyaṃ ca militaṃ dvayaṃ vaktavyam ity uktaṃ bhavati |
yamaḥ |
subhāṣitasahasrāṇi subhāṣītaśatāni ca |
sarvāṇy atyāhṛtasyeha kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
dayā hi sarvabhūtānām anṛtena ca śāmyati |
satyenāpi vinaśyeta hiṃsā yatra pravartate ||
etena satyānṛtanindayā maunaprāśatyamuktam |
hārītaḥ |
ṛtam iti sadbhūtābhidhānaṃ bhavati | nātra ṛtam astīty anṛtam | tac caturvidhaṃ bhavati | paṇitānṛtam, pratyayānṛtam, sukṛtānṛtam, sādhāraṇānṛtam iti | krayavikrayakitavavṛttivṛttitvaṃ paṇitānṛtam | tasmān na vyasanādibhiḥ krīṇīyāt na vikrīṇīyāt | na kitavavṛttiḥ syāt | evaṃ hy āha |
vikrayī śaṃsate paṇyaṃ krayī nindati sadguṇam |
[307]                vyājayogāc ca kitavam aśraddheyaṃ prajāpatiḥ || iti |
viśrambhānṛtaṃ sākṣyanṛtaṃ vyavahārānṛtam iti trividhaṃ pratyayānṛtam |
yad viśraṃbhād vaktrāt kīrtiṃ karoti kārayati vā viśraṃbhānṛtam ity ucyate |
yat sākṣikarmaṇy avasthito ‘nṛtam adhyavasyati jñānalabhyapalapati kūṭasākṣitāṃ copaiti tat sākṣyanṛtam ity ucyate |
dvayor vivadamānayor maitryā dveṣāl lobhād bhayāt pramādād vā yat pakṣāntaram upaiti, yat saṃbhūtaṃ vyudasyāsaṃbhūtaṃ sthāpayati tat saṃvyavahārānṛtam ity ucyate | evaṃ hy āha |
viśaṃbhāt tritayaṃ hanti pañca mithyāvasāyataḥ |
kṣaṇāt saptayugaṃ hanti tv anṛte vyavahārataḥ ||
“vyasanam” vañcanā | “mithyāvasāyo” anṛtavacanam | “jñānāpalāpo” mṛṣāsākṣitam | “yugaṃ
janma |
[308] yamaḥ |
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte ||
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca hiraṇyārthe ‘nṛtaṃ vadan |
sarvaṃ bhūmyanṛte hanti mā sma bhūmyanṛtaṃ vadet ||
apsu bhūmivad ity āhuḥ strīṇāṃ bhoge ca maithune |
paśuvat kṣaudraghṛtayor yac cānyapaśusaṃbhavam ||
govad vastreṣu sarveṣu śayane ‘thāsaneṣu ca |
aśvavat sarvayāneṣu kharoṣṭrāśvatareṣu ca ||
ādityacandrāv analānilau ca
dyaur bhūmir āpo hṛdayaṃ yamaś ca |
paśyanti nityaṃ puruṣasya vṛttaṃ
tasmān na vācyaṃ tv anṛtaṃ kadācit ||
yasya vāṅmanasī śuddhe samyag yupte ca sarvadā |
tapo vedāḥ smṛtis tyāgaḥ sa tebhyaḥ sarvam aśnute ||
“pañca paśvanṛte hanti” iti paśvādiviṣayam anṛtaṃ vadan pañcādisaṃkhyākān bāndhavān “hanti” narake pātayati | uttarottaraṃ nindātiśayadarśanaṃ doṣabhūyastvakhyāpanārtham | “bhoge ca maithune” mithunasaṃpādye bhoga ity arthaḥ | “sarvaṃ ca paśusaṃbhavam” kṣīradadyādi | “sarveṣu” āvikādiṣu |
[309]  devalaḥ |
anṛtaṃ dvividham asatyaṃ visaṃvādaś ceti | tantra [tatra?] dṛṣṭaśrutakṛtānām anyathāvacanam asatyam | prāg abhyupagatānām asaṃkīrtanaṃ visaṃvāda iti |
rogād dveṣād bhayāl lobhād dākṣiṇyāc chaṅkayā kriyā |
dambhāt pramādataś cāpi narasyotpadyate ‘nṛtam ||
paraprāṇopaghātārthe paradravyāpahārataḥ |
viśiṣye ‘nṛtavādas tu na vṛthā kathanādiṣu ||
tasmād anṛtahetūṃs tān ātmany utpādayet kvacit |
utpannāṃ vā praśamayet kāraṇais tadvighātibhiḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
dānānṛtam ijyānṛtaṃ tapo’nṛtam iti trividhaṃ sukṛtānṛtam | annadhānyārthavāso-ratnahiraṇyapaśugo’śvakanyābhūmyabhayadānānām ekatamaṃ datvā yo na dadāti na pratipadyate mayā dattam iti tad dānānṛtam ity ucyate |
[310]        evaṃ hy āha –
annānṛte janma hanti dve tu vāso’nṛte tathā |
trīṇi ratnānṛte hanyāc caturaṃ kāñcanānṛte ||
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte ||
etat tu dviguṇaṃ hanti kṛtvā bhūmyanṛtaṃ naraḥ |
tataś ca dviguṇaṃ bhūyo ‘bhayaṃ datvā hinasti yaḥ ||
yo yakṣya ity uktvā na yajeta, mahāntaṃ vā kratum abhisamīkṣya svalpenaiveṣṭvā, na sarvasvam upayunakti tatra traidhātavī prāyaścittiḥ |
yas tu tapa upetya na yathārthaṃ tapasyati anyathā vā carate asamāptako vā nivarteta tatrāgneyo vidhiḥ | cāndrāyaṇaṃ vā sādhāraṇaṃ nāma | yathā gobrāhmaṇahitam āpatkalpaḥ svairābhidhānaṃ bhavati | evaṃ tv āha ¯
na marmayuktaṃ vacanaṃ hinasti
na svairavākyaṃ na ca maithunārthe |
prāṇātyaye sarvadhanāpahāre
pañcānṛtāny āhur apātakāni ||
artho tatra pratiuktadhanād anyaḥ | “datvā” pratiśrutya | “sādhāraṇatvam” guṇadoṣāṇām anubandhāt | yathā gobrāhmaṇa[311]hitānṛtam anujñātam api guror hitaṃ sadoṣam itaratrādoṣaṃ  evam āpadanṛtacaurābhidhānānṛtayor api |
yamaḥ [MBh vanaparva 315.10ca] |
na narmayuktaṃ vacanaṃ hinasti
na strīṣu rājan na vivāhakāle |
prāṇātyaye sarvadhanāpahāre
pañcānṛtāny āhur apātakāni ||
devalaḥ |
vivāhe brahmahāsārthaṃ prāṇināṃ jīvitāya vā |
ātmanaḥ prāṇahetor vā na sīded anrtaṃ vacan ||
vivāre [vivāhe?] maithune | “brahmahāsārtham” brāhmaṇarthaṃ hāsārthaṃ ca |
gautamaḥ [3.5.29] |
vivāhe maithunanarmāntasaṃyogeṣv adoṣam eke ‘nṛtaṃ nanu khalu gurvartheṣu, sapta puruṣān itaś ca parataś ca hanti manasā guror anṛtaṃ vadann alpeṣv apy artheṣu |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.132] |
na saṃśayaṃ prapadyeta nākasmād apriyaṃ vadet |
nāhitaṃ nānṛtaṃ caiva na stenaḥ syān na vārdhuṣī ||
[312] atha devalaḥ |
tataḥ sthūlamadhyakṣudrāṇāṃ trividhānāṃ padarthānāṃ prasahya tūṣṇīṃ vā pracchannaṃ vā haraṇaṃ steyam |
rakṣakṣetraveśmahemarūpyābharaṇādīni sthūlāni, catuṣpadadvipadānna-dhānyaṃ lohavastrakārṣāpaṇopakaraṇādīni madhyāni | dantaśṛṅgadārucarma-pāṣāṇamṛṇmayādīni kṣudrāṇi |
“prasahya” balātkāreṇa | “tūṣṇīṃ haraṇam” svāminādṛśyamānasya balātkāraṃ vinā yad dharaṇam |
hārītaḥ |
dvividham eva dravyam āhuḥ – sacetanaṃ niścetanaṃ ca | saṃgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ ca | sacetanaṃ saṃgamam | niścetanam apy ataijasamaṇiśailauṣadhivānaspatyaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ rasamayaṃ caivaṃbhūtasya daśavidhānāt steyaṃ bhavati |
adattādānād anatispṛṣṭagrahanāt, anirdeśopayogāt, bhṛtyebhyo bhṛtyadānāt, yācitā niryātanā nyāsāpalāpanāt, chadmavyasanayoḥ anupāyagrahaṇāc ceti pratiśrutyādānād devatānabhisaṃbandhāc caitad daśavidhaṃ bhavati | evaṃ hy āha –
pratiśrutyāpradānena dattasyācchādanena ca |
vividhān narakān yāti tiryagyonau ca jāyate ||
[313]                vācā yac ca yat pratijñātaṃ karmaṇā nopapāditam |
ṛṇaṃ tad dharmasaṃyuktam iha loke paratra ca ||
svāminādattasyādānaṃ “adattādānam” | parimāṇād atiriktagrahaṇaṃ “anatisṛṣṭagrahaṇam” | devapitṛmanuṣyārthe bhikṣitvā svayam upayuñjāno “anirdeśopayogaḥ” | bhṛtyānāṃ karma kārayitvā “bhṛter dānam” |
yāvitvā necchāmīty aniryātanaṃ “yācitāniryātanam” | vidyāmitrakulapratyayāt nyastasyāpalapanaṃ “nyāsāpalapanam” | kūṭamānatulātaskaratvaṃ “chadma” | “vyasanād” abrahmacāriṇo hiraṇyādīnām ātmārthaṃ pratigraho, yatīnāṃ cānupāyagrahaṇam |
steyino hi pramītāḥ pūrvadhaninām upakārāya jāyante | tasmāt steyam na kuryān na kārayen nānumanyeta |
“sacetanaṃ jīvādhiṣṭhitam” | tac ca dvividhaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ ca | gatimat puruṣagavādi jaṅgamam, jarāyujāṇḍajasvedajarūpam | vṛkṣādi udbhijjam, sthāvaram “niścetanam” jīvānadhiṣṭhitam | “āpyam” śaṅkhādi | “taijasam” suvarṇādi | vrīhiyavādi “auṣadham” | “vānaspatyam” tṛṇakāṣṭhādi | “mṛṇmayam” ghaṭādi | “rasamayam” kṣīraguṣādi | daśavidhatvaṃ vakṣyamāṇā dattādānādiprakāreṇa | “pratiśrutyādānam” “tavedaṃ mayā deyam” iti pratijñāya yad adānam | “devatānabhisaṃbandhaḥ” “idaṃ yāgāya dravyam” iti vidhāya, tasya devatayānabhisaṃbandho yāgākaraṇāt | “dharmasaṃyuktam” yat pratijñātam ity [314] anvayaḥ | “kārayitvā” karmeti śeṣaḥ | “aniryātanam” apratyarpaṇam | upakārāya jāyante paśudāyādibhāveneti śeṣaḥ |
yamaḥ |
suvarṇam ekaṃ gām ekāṃ bhūmer apy ekam aṅgulam |
haran narakam āpnoti yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
hiraṇyaṃ paśudānāni striyo vāsāṃsi yo haret |
saparyāyeṇa yātīmān narakān ekaviṃśatim ||
“narakāṃś ca” prāyaścittakāṇḍe vakṣyāmaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.2.6] |
steno ‘bhiśasto brāhmaṇo rājanyo vaiśyo vā parasmin loke ‘parimite niraye vṛtte jāyate | caṇḍālo brāhmaṇaḥ paulkaso rājanyaḥ, vaiṇo vaiśyaḥ |
stenaḥ san loke yo prasiddhaḥ |
gautamaḥ [2.4.14–22] |
kṣudrapaśvanṛte sākṣī daśa hanti | go’śvapuruṣabhūmiṣu daśaguṇottaram | sarvaṃ vā bhūmau | haraṇe narakaḥ | bhūmivat apsu | mithunasaṃyoge ca | paśuvan madhusarpiṣoḥ | govad vastrahiraṇyadhānyabrahmasu | yāneṣv aśvavat haraṇe |
[315] viṣṇuḥ [52.15–17] |
yad yat parebhyas tv ādadyāt puruṣas tu niraṅkuśaḥ |
tena tenātha hīnaḥ syād yatra yatrābhijāyate ||
jīvitaṃ dharmakāmau ca dhane yasmāt pratiṣṭhitau |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena dhanahiṃsāṃ vivarjayet ||
prāṇihiṃśāparo yas tu dhanahiṃsāparas tathā |
mahad duḥkham avāpnoti dhanahiṃsāparas tayoḥ ||
yamaḥ |
devadravyāpahartāraṃ brahmasvaharaṇe ratam |
puruṣaṃ pāpakarmāṇaṃ narakaḥ pratipadyate ||
yas tu labdhaṃ punar dadyād devadravyaṃ yatas tataḥ |
gurudravyaṃ dvijadravyaṃ so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
yad etad dhanam ity āhuḥ prāṇā hy ete bahiścarāḥ |
sa tasya harate prāṇān yo yasya harate dahnam ||
apahṛtya parasyārthaṃ yas tu dānaṃ prayacchati |
sa dātā narakaṃ yāti yasyārthas tasya tat phalam ||
[316]        parasvaparituṣṭās tu jīvitā narakāś ca ye |
teṣāṃ rajaḥ pated yatra tad amedhyataraṃ bhavet ||
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca |
brahmasvahartā narake patnaṃ parivartate ||
tathā |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi brahmahā narakaṃ patet |
yaś ca brāhmaṇavṛttighno yo vai patati yājakaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
śodhyaṃ śudhyati mṛttoyaiḥ prāyaścittaiś ca pātakam |
brahmasvanyāsahartārau tam adatvā na śudhyataḥ ||
brahmahā vittahā cobhau samau syātāṃ na vāsamau |
kalpānte brahmahā śudhyed vittahā na kadācana ||
evaṃ jñātvā parasvāni manasāpi na cintayet |
parastriyo viśeṣeṇa dharmasāram idaṃ smṛtam ||
jātyandho badhiraś caiva mūkaḥ saṅkucitāṅguliḥ |
paradārāpavādeṣu parachidrānuhiṃsane ||
evaṃ vṛttas tu pitarau trāyate mahato bhayāt |
vitatyeha yaśo dīptaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate ||
[317] yamaḥ |
devadravyaṃ gurudravyaṃ yad dravyaṃ pārṣadaṃ bhavet |
hṛtvāmutra naro lobhād gṛdhrocchiṣṭena jīvati ||
cāṇḍalo nijagau gāthāṃ manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvaḥ purā |
prabhavāmīti yo ‘śnāti bahusāmānyam ekakaḥ ||
śunāṃ rajasvalānāṃ ca charditaṃ maladūṣitam |
tasya saṃsarato loke vṛttir eṣā vidhīyate ||
caṇḍālo bhūtvā jīvatīty anvayaḥ |
dānabṛhaspatiḥ |
harate hārayed bhūmiṃ mandabuddhis tamovṛtaḥ |
sa ruddho vāruṇaiḥ pāśais tiryagyoniṣu jāyate ||
svadattāṃ paradattāṃ vā yo hareta vasuṃdharām |
ṣaṣṭhivarṣasarhasrāṇi viṣṭhāyāṃ jāyate kṛmiḥ ||
ṣaṣṭhivarṣasahasrāṇi svarge gacchati bhūmidaḥ |
[318]        āhartā cānumantā ca tāny eva narakaṃ vaset ||
taḍāgānāṃ sahasreṇa aśvamedhaśatena ca |
gavāṃ koṭipradānena bhūmihartā na śudhyati ||
anyāyena hṛtā bhūmir anyāyena ca hāritā |
haranto hārayantaś ca hanyus tripuruṣaṃ kulam ||
brahmasvaṃ praṇayād bhuktaṃ hanyād āsaptamaṃ kulam |
vikrameṇa ca bhuktaṃ tad daśapūrvān daśāparān ||
brahmasve mā matiṃ kuryāt prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatair api |
agnidagdhāni rohanti brahmadagdhaṃ na rohati ||
na viṣaṃ viṣam ity āhur brahmasvaṃ viṣam ucyate |
viṣam ekākinaṃ hanti brahmsvaṃ putrapautrakam ||
lohacūrṇāśvacūrṇe ca viṣaṃ ca jarayen naraḥ |
brahmasvaṃ triṣu lokeṣu kaḥ pumāṇ jarayiṣyati ||
vindhyāṭavīṣv atoyāsu śuṣkakoṭaravāsinaḥ |
kṛṣṇasarpāś ca jāyante brahmadāyāpahārakāḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
sāmānyāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ labdhvā gṛhṇāty eko vimohitaḥ |
[319]        nāstikyabhāvanirataḥ sa ca preto ‘bhijāyate ||
hārītaḥ |
asteyaṃ paramo dharma iti dharmavido viduḥ |
paradravyāpahartṝṇāṃ na śubhā vidyate gatiḥ ||
