ath steyāpavādaḥ
tatra āpastambaḥ [1.28.1–5] |
yathā kathā ca paraparigraham abhimanyate steno ha bhavatīti kautsa-hārītau | tathā kāṇva-pauṣkarasādī | santy apavādāḥ paraparigraheṣv iti vārṣyāyaṇiḥ | śamyoṣā yugyaghāso na svāminaḥ pratiṣedhayanti | ativyavahāro vyaruddho bhavati | śivoṣāyugyaghāso ‘svāminaḥ pratiṣedhe | sarvatrānumatir pūrvam iti hārītaḥ |
yathā kathaṃcid ity arthe yathākatheti chāndasaḥ prayogaḥ | evaṃ vidhiṃ paraparigṛhītam adīyamānam, pareṇa parigrahaḥ parasvam, “abhimanyate” svīkaroti | “śamyoṣā” mudgacaṇakādīnāṃ bījakoṣaḥ | “uṣā” abhyaṣākhyāyavādikaṇiśāḥ | “yugyānām” vāhanānām | “ghāso” yavasaḥ | evaṃ vidhiṃ yas tu prigṛhītam ādīyamānaṃ pareṇa tatsvāmināṃ “pratiṣedhayanti” nivārayanti | “ativyavahāro” atimātragrahaṇam | “viruddho” niṣiddhaḥ |
[321] yamaḥ |
asteyam agnaye kāṣṭham asteyaṃ ca tṛṇaṃ gave |
kanyāharaṇam asteyaṃ yo haraty analaṃkṛtām ||
puṣpe śākodake kāṣṭhe tathā mūlaphale tṛṇe |
adattādānam eteṣām asteyaṃ tu yamo ‘bravīt ||
tṛṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ phalaṃ puṣpaṃ prakāśaṃ vai hared dvijaḥ |
gobrāhmaṇārthe gṛhṇan vai na sa pāpena lipyate ||
manuḥ [8.339] |
vānaspatyaṃ mūlaphalaṃ dārvagnyarthe tathaiva ca |
tṛṇaṃ ca gobhyo ghāsārtham asteyaṃ manur abravīt ||
“vānaspatyam” vanaspatiprabhavaṃ patrādi | “agnyartham” āhitasyāgneḥ samindhanārtham |
[322] devalaḥ |
asteyam agnaye kāṣṭham asteyaṃ paśave tṛṇam |
asteyaṃ phalam āraṇyam asteyaṃ pārthivaṃ jalam ||
gautamaḥ |
go’gnyarthye tṛṇamedhan (dhān?) vīrudvanaspatīnāṃ puṣpāṇi svayam ādadīta | phalāni vāparivṛttānām |
“aparivṛttānām” aparirakṣitānām |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
dvijas tṛṇaidhaḥpuṣpāṇi (?) sarvataḥ samupāharet |
“sarvataḥ” rakṣitād api |
hārītaḥ |
pūrvāśramayor nekṣudvayagrahaṇe stainyam | tathā mūtrapurīṣādīnāṃ pāṇyoḥ śākaparimāṇe ekapāṇeḥ | śamīnāṃ bahavas tadvat eke | rakṣyavṛkṣāṇāṃ tatsthāntakṣaṇe stokagrahaṇe vā anyeṣāṃ sumanasāṃ vārcāpinahane agnyarthe tṛṇakāṣṭhānāṃ yavasodakena gavāṃ ca astainyam |
[323] “pāṇyoḥ śākaparimāṇe” pāṇidvayaparimite śāke | pāṇiḥ śamīnāṃ parimāṇa ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | ekapāṇiparimitānāṃ śamīnām ity arthaḥ | “rakṣyo” rakṣaṇīyaḥ | “arcāpinahanam” pratimācchādanam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tilamudgamāṣayavagodhūmādīnāṃ śasyamuṣṭigrahaṇeṣv adoṣaḥ pathikānām apy apathyadalānāṃ tathekṣor mulakayoḥ śākapuṣpaphalapracāyane ca | viditasya prakāśaṃ gṛhṇato na doṣaḥ |
“śasyam” phalam | “apathyadalānām” saṃbalarahitānām | “viditasya” ākhyātasya sādhutveneti śeṣaḥ |
bṛhaspati-kātyāyanau |
trapuṇervāruke (?) dve tu pañcāmraṃ pañcadāḍimam |
kharjūrabadarādīnāṃ muṣṭiṃ gṛhṇan na doṣabhāk ||
“urvārukam” karkaṭī |
nāradaḥ |
śālivrīhiprasūtānāṃ muṣṭir grāhyo vidhīyate |
[324]        yavagodhūmayor vāpi yadi vā mudgamāṣayoḥ ||
aniṣiddhair (?) gṛhītavyo muṣṭir ekaḥ pathi sthitaiḥ |
śākaṃ śākapramāṇena gṛhyamāṇaṃ na duṣyati ||
grahītavyāni puṣpāṇi devatārcanakāraṇāt |
adattādāyinaṃ vidyād yadi tv adhikam icchati ||
“śākapramāṇam” hārītoktaṃ muṣṭidvayam |
vṛddhamanuḥ |
caṇakavrīhigodhūmayavānāṃ mudgamāṣayoḥ |
anirbaddhair gṛhītavyo muṣṭir ekaḥ pathi sthitaiḥ ||
“anirbaddhaiḥ” asaṃbaddhaiḥ asvāmibhir iti |
yamaḥ |
pathikaḥ kṣīṇavṛttis tu dvāv iṣkṣū dve ca mūlake |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetre na doṣaṃ brāhmaṇo ‘rhati ||
[325]
20
atha śaucam
atra hārītaḥ |
śaucaṃ nāma dharmādipatho brahmāyatanaṃ śriyodhivāso, manasaḥ prasādanam, devānāṃ priyam, śarīre kṣetrajñadarśanaṃ buddhiprabodhanam | tac ca śaucaṃ dvividham upariṣṭād vakṣyāmaḥ | evaṃ hy āha –
śuciṃ devā hi rakṣanti pitaraḥ śucim anvayuḥ |
śucer bibhyati rakṣāṃsi ye cānye duṣṭacāriṇaḥ ||
“dharmādipathaḥ” dharmasya prathamo mārga upāya iti yāvat | śucer eva kamaṇy adhikārāt | “brahmāyatanam” vedāśrayaḥ | kṣetrajñam ātmānaṃ darśayatīti “kṣetrajñadarśanam” | “dvividham” hārītenaiva vakṣyamāṇaṃ bāhyam abhyantaraṃ ca |
tatra bāhyaṃ kulaśaucam arthaśaucaṃ śārīraṃ ca | tatra kulaśaucaṃ sūtakādyaśaucaviraḥ | arthaśaucaṃ bhojanādidravyāṇāṃ śuddhiḥ | śārīraṃ śaucam amedhyādilepaprakṣālanam |
[326]
ābhyantaraśaucaṃ pañcavidham | tad yathā mānasaṃ cākṣuṣaṃ ghrāṇaṃ vācyaṃ khādyaṃ ceti | tatra mānasam – apuṇyābhidhānadoṣavirahaḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
śauce yatnaḥ sadā kāryaḥ śaucamūlo dvijaḥ smṛtaḥ |
śaucācāravihīnasya samastā niṣphalāḥ kriyāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
bhraṣṭaśaucaṃ naraṃ dṛṣṭvā praharantīha rākṣasāḥ |
yakṣāḥ piśācā bhūtāni ye cānye ‘py evamādayaḥ ||
jñānaṃ dānaṃ tapas tyāgaḥ mantrakarmavidhikriyāḥ |
maṅgalācāraniyamāḥ śaucabhraṣṭasya niṣphalāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ niveditaṃ dattaṃ bhuktaṃ japtaṃ tapaḥ śrutam |
yātudhānāḥ pralupyanti śaucabhraṣṭād dvijanmanaḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
śaucahīnāś ca ye viprā na ca yajñopavītinaḥ |
hutaṃ dattaṃ tapas teṣāṃ vinaśyen nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
[327]
21
atha brahmacaryam
tatra hārītaḥ |
brahmacaryaṃ nāma divyamānuśīṇāṃ tathā citrārthakāṣṭhapotasthamṛṇmayā-dīnām apy aprārthanam asmaraṇam asaṅkalpanam anabhiprekṣaṇam asaṅkīrtanam anabhibhāṣaṇam anabhigamanam asaṃdarśanam asamāgamaś cāsām |
tac ca brahmacaryaṃ caturvidhaṃ bhavati | tatra paradāravarjanaṃ nityam | svadārābhigamanaṃ kṛṣṇam | parvaniyamavarjaṃ raktam | ṛtukālābhigamanaṃ śuklam | ūrdhvaretastvaṃ vimalam | evaṃ hy āha |
kṛṣṇenemaṃ jayel lokaṃ madhyaṃ raktena vai jayet |
śuklena tu jayet svargaṃ vimalān mokṣam āpnuyāt ||
“divyā” ūrvaśyādyāḥ śāstrāvagatāḥ | “potasthāḥ” potaṃ pratimākaraṇaṃ rajatādi tatsthāḥ tannirmitāḥ | “aprārthanam” sā me bhūyāt ity āśaṃsāvirahaḥ | “asaṃkalpam” evaṃ kariṣyāmīty abhidhānābhāvaḥ | “anabhiprekṣaṇam” abhimukhānabhilokanam | “asaṃdarśanam” sakalāvayavānavalokanam | “asamāgamaḥ” asaṃnidhānam | parvaniyamavarjam, parvaṇi amāvāsyādau, niyame vratādau iti yamāḥ |
[328]
22
atha niyamāḥ
tatra hārīto yamaś cāntyaśloke |
krodhaniyamaś cārjavam akalpatā saṃtoṣo guruśuśrūṣā ceti niyamāḥ | tato niyamāḥ, vāṅniyamaḥ karmaniyamaś ceti | krodhaniyamāḥ mānasāt krodhāt cittaparitāpāt pīḍyamānasya kārśyavaivarṇyavairūpyādayo vyādhayaḥ saṃbhavanti |
ahaṃkārerṣyāsūyādveṣaparopatāpādīna ca krodharūpāṇi | vāglakṣaṇād apriyaparuṣavāditā nānāvidhavākpradānādayaḥ | karmalakṣaṇāḥ keśagrahaṇasaṃprahārādayaḥ | aparaśarīraprayogakarāś ceti |
yair upasṛṣṭaḥ san vidvān apy avasādam upaiti tasmād etān varjayet |
[329]        evaṃ hy āha |
ākruṣṭas tāḍitaḥ kṣiptaḥ kṣamate yo na kupyati |
sa tasya duṣkṛtaṃ datvā sukṛtaṃ sarvam āpnuyāt || iti |
“tasya” ākrośanādeḥ |
yamaḥ |
krodhano yajati juhoti yad vā tapas tapyati vairocanes tad dharate asya sarvaṃ moghaśramo bhavati |
“asya” krodhanasya | “vairocaniḥ” baliḥ |
hārītaḥ |
niṣkṛtir niyamo ‘jaihmaṃ niyamaś cārjavam | chadma māyā vyājayuktādhikṛtiḥ | kāṭhinyaśāṭhyavaiṣamyādauṣṭyaṃ sadbhāvayuktaṃ jīhmaṃ tad ete saṃniyamyārjavaṃ bhavati | evaṃ hy āha |
jaihmaṃ mṛtyupadaṃ prāhur niṣkṛtiṃ tamasaḥ padam |
sarvabhūtātmabhūtatvād ārjavaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padam ||
smayaniyamaḥ sūnāniyamaḥ sevāniyamo ‘daṃbhaniyamaḥ cety akalkatā |
[330] jñāna-dāna-tapa-aiśvarya-bhoga-viśeṣaiḥ smayaṃ varjayet | smayopapannās tejasāvapannāḥ saṃbhavanti | pañcasūnāsaṃbhūtaṃ pāpamayam ity ucyate | arthino hi pretabhūtā bījavaj jāyante | tasmān nātmarthe sūnāḥ kārayet | trividhaśilpaprayogaiḥ sevāviśeṣaiḥ | parān annārthaṃ nopacaret | avidvān vidvattāṃ khyāpayaty adātā dānam, ayajvā yajñam, atapasvī tapo nāśramy āśramārthaṃ lipsan | tenaiva vṛttānāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ bhavati | evaṃ hy āha |
kiṃ tena na kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ caureṇātmāpahāriṇā |
yo ‘nyathā santam ātmānam anyathā pratipadyate ||
“chadma” chalam | “māyā” kauṭilyam | “vyājaḥ” arthāntaropanyāsaḥ | “kāṭhinyam” niṣkaruṇatā | “śāṭhyam” apārthāya hṛdaye rāgādipradarśanam | “vaiṣamyam” nigūḍhaparadrohābhisaṃdhānam | “dauṣṭyam” parasukhāsahiṣṇutvam | ete niṣkṛtiḥ jaihmaṃ smayo darpaḥ | “avapannāḥ” hīnāḥ “bījavaj jāyante” iti | yathā bījāt samānam eva kāryaṃ jāyate, tathā ādhino ‘pi samānarūpā eva jāyante | arthagrahaṇād vidyādyartham adoṣaḥ | evaṃ vṛttānāṃ vidvadānīnāṃ [-dīnāṃ?] pratipadyata ity antarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ |
[331] manuḥ |
na dharmasyāpadeśena pāpaṃ kṛtvā vrataṃ caret |
vratena pāpaṃ saṃchādya kurvan strīśūdradambhanam ||
pretyeha cedṛśā vipro garhyate brahmavādibhiḥ |
chadmanācaritaṃ tac ca vrataṃ rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
aliṅgī liṅgiveṣeṇa yo vṛttim upajīvati |
sa liṅgināṃ haraty enas tiryagyonau ca jāyate ||
yamaḥ |
sarvatra vidyate vyājo dharme vyājo na vidyate |
vyājopahatadharmāṇāṃ svarge vāso na vidyate ||
tapo na kalko ‘dhyayanaṃna kalkaḥ
svābhāviko vedavidhir na kalkaḥ |
prasahya vittāharaṇaṃ na kalkaḥ
tāny eva bhāvopahatāni kalkaḥ ||
“kalkaḥ” pāpam | “svābhāvikaḥ” naupādhikaḥ | “prasahya vittāharaṇam” atra bhrāntyā na karmaṇā vā svāmyananumatavastugrahaṇam |
atra hārītaḥ |
sūnāniyamī sevāniyamī steyāvikreyavikrayī [332] ayājyayājanāsatpratigraha-śūdraśrāvaṇabhṛtakādhyāpanānuyogapratigrahanivṛttyālobhaniyamī saṃtuṣṭo bhavati | evaṃ hy āha |
saṃtoṣaḥ paramo dharmaḥ saṃtoṣaḥ paramaṃ sukham |
saṃtoṣaḥ paramaṃ śreyaḥ saṃtoṣaḥ paramaṃ yaśaḥ ||
saṃtoṣaḥ paramā siddhiḥ saṃtoṣaḥ paramaṃ sukham || iti |
“śūdraśravaṇam” [supātratākhyāpanārthe śūdrasyāgre vedādipāṭhaḥ] | “anuyogaḥ pratigrahaḥ” pāṭanikāpūrvakaḥ “pratigrahasaṃtoṣaḥ paramo” dharma iti dharmādīnāṃ saṃtoṣamūlakatvāt tadabhedenopacāraḥ |
guruśuśrūṣā nāma śāsanadharmanimittaṃ kriyate | tatra jñānaniṣkriyārthaṃ guruśuśrūṣāṃ kuryāt | śuśrūṣāsvarūpanivṛttau brahmacārikāṇḍe abhihitau |
punar hārītaḥ |
yameṣu niyameṣu vā prasannas
tiṣṭhati [yāti] lokān variṣṭhān |
bhraṣṭas tebhyo vividhā yāti yonīḥ
yāsūtpanno majjate yātanāsu ||
[333]        tasmād vīraḥ śuśruvān nyāyavṛtto
yasmin yaśo na vyathate nāpi dharmaḥ |
dharmo hy ekaḥ susahāyo janānāṃ
ya etān ghorān trāyate mṛtyudurgāt || iti |
iti bhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite gṛhasthakāṇḍe yamaniyamāḥ |
[334]
23
atha kulanāśakāni
tatra yama-śātātapa-dānavyāsāḥ |
devadravyavināśena brahmasvaharaṇena ca |
kulāny akulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca ||
“devadravyam” devatoddeśena yat tyaktaṃ vastu |
yamaḥ |
gobhiḥ paśubhir aśvaiś ca kṛṣyā vasusamṛddhayā |
kulāny akulatāṃ yānti brāhmaṇātikrameṇa ca ||
[manuḥ] |
śilpena vyavahāreṇa śūdrāpatyaiś ca kevalaiḥ |
gobhir aśvaiś ca yānaiś ca kṛṣyā rājopasevayā ||
ayājyayājanaṃ caiva nāstikyena ca karmaṇā |
kulāny akulatāṃ yānti yāni hīnāni mantrataḥ ||
mantratas tu samṛddhāni kulāny alpadhanāny api |
kulasaṃkhyāṃ ca gacchanti karṣanti ca mahad yaśaḥ ||
[335] “śilpam” citrādikarma | “vyavahāraḥ” ṛṇapradānādi | “yānaiḥ” śakaṭādibhiḥ | “sevā” anyabhāvena parārādhanam | “nāstikyakarma” nāsti svargādiphalam etena karmaṇeti buddhiḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
anṛtaṃ pāradāryaṃ ca tathābhakṣyasya bhakṣaṇam |
agamyāgamanaṃ caiva hiṃsā steyaṃ tathaiva ca ||
aśrautadharmācaraṇaṃ maitradharmabahiṣkṛtam |
etāni nava karmāṇi pātakāni ca varjayet ||
“maitradharmabahiṣkṛtam” maitrīdharmayor bahiṣkaraṇaṃ tyāga ity arthaḥ |
[336]
24
atha nivāsaḥ
tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
nivāsamukhyo viprāṇām ācāraḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
guṇadoṣasamudbhūtir loke saṃsargajā smṛtā ||
bhūmimālyakuśadhānyaśaṣpāmbudvijanaigame |
niṣkaṇṭake dhārmike ca vaset sthāne nirāmaye ||
“śaṣpa” atra ghāsaḥ | “naigamo” vāṇijakaḥ | “kaṇṭako” atra caurādiḥ | “dhārmike” dhārmikabahule | “nirāmaye” deśe svabhāvajarogaśūnye |
āpastambaḥ [1.15.22] |
prabhūtaidhodake grāme yatrātmādhīnaṃ prayamaṇaṃ tatra vāso dharmyo brāhmaṇasya |
[337]  “prayamaṇam” pavitrīkaraṇaṃ jalagomayakuśādi |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.6.31–33] |
prabhūtair yavasaidhodaka[samit]kuśamālyopaniṣkramaṇam āḍhyajanākulam analasasusamṛddham āryajanādhiṣṭhitam adasyuprativeśaṃ grāmaṃ vāsayituṃ yateta dhārmikaḥ ||
udapānodake grāme brāhmaṇo vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
uṣitvā dvādaśa samāḥ sa śaudraṃ dharmam ṛcchati ||
purareṇuguṇḍhitaśarīras tatpūrṇanetravadanaś ca nagare vasan saṃniyatātmā siddhim avāpsyatīti | na tad asti |
rathāśvagajadānyānāṃ gavāṃ caiva rajaḥ śubham |
apraśastaṃ samūhanyāḥ śvājāvibhir avāsasām ||
“upaniṣkramaṇam” nirgamanamārgaḥ | “analasaḥ” udyamī | “adasyuprativeśaṃ” asaṃnihitacauram | “udapānodake” kūpa evodakaṃ yatrety arthaḥ | “nagare” pure | “puraḥ” [338] mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇoktaṃ [49.43] yathā |
sotsedham uccaprākaraṃ sarvataḥ parikhāvṛtam |
yojanārddhārdhaviṣkambham aṣṭabhāgāyataṃ puram |
iti tātparyārthaḥ |
yamaḥ |
ekakūpodake grāme brāhmaṇo vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
varṣeṇa śūdrī bhavati kṛṣṇavarṇam upāśritaḥ ||
“kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ” śūdraḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.32.18–21] |
kṣudrān kṣudrācaritāṃś ca deśān na sevetādasatāṃ (?) samājāṃś ca | samājaṃ ced gacchet pradakṣiṇīkṛtyopeyāt | nagarapraveśanāni varjayet |
“kṣudrān” jalādinā saṅkucitapracāradeśān | “kṣudrācaritān” niṣādādibhir adhyuṣitān | “nagarapraveśanāni” nagaraṃ praviśyate yaiḥ pradeśaiḥ tāni | vāsārthaṃ na sevetety arthaḥ |
manuḥ [4.60–61, 79] |
nādhārmike vased grāme vyādhyādibahule bhṛśam |
naikaḥ prapadyetādhvānaṃ na ciraṃ parvate vaset ||
na śūdrarājye nivasen nādhārmikajanāvṛte |
[339]        na pāṣaṇḍajanākrānte nopasṛṣṭe ‘ntyajaiḥ nṛbhiḥ ||
na saṃvasec ca patitair na cāṇḍālair na pulkasaiḥ |
na mūrkhair nāvaliptaiś ca nāntyair nāntyāvasāyibhiḥ ||
“adhārmikajanāvṛte” adhārmikajanā yeṣu grāmeṣu tair veṣṭiteṣu |
viṣṇuḥ [71.64–68] |
na śūdrarājye nivaset | nādhārmikajanākīrṇe |
na saṃvaset vaidyahīne nopaspṛṣṭe na ciraṃ parvate |
uśanāḥ |
naṣṭapatitābhiśastalokaninditācāraiḥ na saha saṃvaset |
vyāsaḥ |
tatra tena na vastavyaṃ yatra nāsti catuṣṭayam |
ṛṇapradātā vaidyaś ca śrotriyaḥ sajalā nadī ||
tatra tāvan na vastavyaṃ yatraitat tritayaṃ sadā |
jigīṣuḥ pūrvavairī ca janaś ca satatotsavaḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.113–116] |
jitāmitro nṛpo yatra balavān dharmatatparaḥ |
tatra nityaṃ vaset prājñaḥ kutaḥ kunṛpatau sukham ||
[340]        paurāḥ susaṃhatāḥ yatra satataṃ nyāyavartinaḥ |
yatrāmātsariṇo lokās tatra vāsaḥ sukhodayaḥ ||
yasmin kṛṣīvalā rāṣṭre prāyaśo nātibhoginaḥ |
yatrauṣadhāny aśeṣāṇi vaset tatra vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
tathā |
vasen nityaṃ suśīleṣu sahavāsiṣu paṇḍitaḥ |
yatra apradhṛṣyo nṛpatir yatra sasyapradā mahī ||
mahābhārate |
yatrāgamayamānānām asatkāreṇa pṛcchatām |
prabrūyād brāhmaṇo dharmaṃ tyajet taṃ deśam ātmavān ||
“āgamayamānānām” tarjanaṃ kurvatām |
śiṣyopādhyāyikāvṛttir yatra syāt susamāhitā |
yathāvac chāstrasaṃpannā kas taṃ deśaṃ parityajet ||
ākāśasthā dhruvaṃ yatra doṣaṃ brūyur vipaścitaḥ |
ātmapūjābhikāmā vai ko vaset tatra paṇḍitaḥ ||
“ākāśasthā” apraśastubhāḥ |
tathā |
yatra santāpitā lubdhaiḥ prāyaśo dharmasetavaḥ |
pradīptam iva cailāntaṃ kas taṃ deśaṃ na saṃtyajet ||
[341] “dharmasetavaḥ” dharmamaryādāvyavasthāpakāḥ | “cailam” vastram |
yatra dharmam anāśaṅkāś careyur vītamatsarāḥ |
caret tatra vasec caiva puṇyaśīleṣu sādhuṣu ||
dharmam arthanimittaṃ ca careyur yatra mānavāḥ |
na tatra tu vasej jātu te hi pāpakṛto janāḥ ||
karmaṇā yatra pāpena vartante jīvitepsavaḥ |
vyavadhāvet tatas tūrṇaṃ saparyāstaraṇād iva ||
“vyavādhāvet” (?) vegenāpasaret |
yatra rājā na rājñaś ca puruṣāḥ pratyanantarāḥ |
kuṭumbinām agrabhujas tyajet tad rāṣṭram ātmavān ||
śrotriyās tv agrabhoktāro dharmanityāḥ sanātanāḥ |
yājanādhyāpanabhujo yatra tad rāṣṭram āvaset ||
svāhā svadhā vaṣaṭkārā yatra samyag anuṣṭhitāḥ |
ajasraṃ caiva vartante vaset tatrāvicārayan ||
aśucīn yatra paśyeta brāhmaṇān vṛttikārṣitān |
tyajet tad rāṣṭram āsannam upaspṛṣṭam itvāmiṣam ||
prīyamāṇā narā yatra papraccheyur ayācitāḥ |
svasthacitto vaset tatra kṛtakṛtya ivātmavān ||
daṇḍo yatrāvinīteṣu satkāraś ca kṛtātmasu |
[342] devalaḥ |
araṇyaṃ devatāsthānaṃ tīrthāny āyatanāni ca |
tasmāt teṣu mṛtā loke yānti lokān divaukasām ||
“araṇyam” naimiṣādi | “tīrtham” gaṅgādi | “āyatanam” avimuktādi | etāni devasthānāni | tasmāt teṣu tiṣṭhantīty anvayaḥ |
iti nivāsaḥ |
[343]
25
atha vācyāvācyam
tatra manuḥ [4.139] |
bhadraṃ bhadram iti brūyād bhdram ity eva vā vadet |
śuṣkavairaṃ vivādaṃ ca na kuryāt kenacit saha ||
“bhadram bhadram” iti prathamaṃ bhadrapadaṃ bhadretaravastuparam | dvitīyaṃ bhadrapadaṃ tatparyāyaparam, “na bhadraṃ bhadram iti brūyāt puṇyaṃ praśāstam iti eva brūyāt” iti āpastambadarśanāt [1.31.13–14] | “bhadram ity eva” iti | bhadrapadam eva tatrārthe prayoktavyam | “śuṣkam” niṣprayojanam |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.22–23] |
virodhaṃ nottamair gacchen nādhamaiś ca sadā budhaḥ |
vivāhaś ca vivādaś ca tulyarūpaiḥ saheṣyate ||
nārabheta kaliṃ prājñaḥ śuṣkaṃ vairaṃ vivarjayet |
apy alpahāniḥ soḍhavyā vaireṇārthāgamaṃ tyajet ||
[344] viṣṇuḥ [72.72–75] |
nāślīlaṃ kīrtayet nānrtaṃ nāpriyaṃ na kāṃcit marmaṇi spṛśet |
“marma” atra gopyadūṣaṇam |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.8–11] |
gor dakṣiṇānāṃ kumāryāś ca parīvādān vivarjayet | spṛhayantīṃ gāṃ nācakṣīta | saṃspṛṣṭāṃ ca gāṃ vatsenānimitte | nādhenuṃ adhenur iti brūyāt | dhenuṃbhavyety eva brūyāt |
goḥ adakṣiṇāyā api dakṣiṇānāṃ vastrahiraṇyādīnāṃ dakṣiṇātve kalpitānām | “spṛhantī” dhānādi bhakṣayituṃ pravṛttām | “saṃspṛṣṭāṃ” vatsena pāyayitum iti śeṣaḥ | “animitte” ṛtuvaiguṇyanimittābhāve | sattābhyo dharmavādohanvaprakathane sati na doṣaḥ | dhenuṃ bhavyaśabdo dhenur bhaviṣyatīty artho “dhenor bhavyāyāṃ ca num vaktavyaḥ” iti numāgamaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nendradhanuś candrasūryapariveṣolkaḥ parasmai kathayet | ātmanaḥ pratikūlaṃ na mahājanavirodhaṃ kuryāt | na goṣṭhibhiḥ sambadhyet |
[345] “mahājanavirodham” mahājanapratikūlam | mahatvaṃ vedārthajñānādinā | “goṣṭhībhiḥ” vṛthā saṅkathābhiḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.17] |
nāsaume sapatna iti brūyāt yady asaume sapatna iti brūyāt dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ janayet |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mattapramattonmattaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣāṃ na kurvīta na parastriyaṃ rahasi saṃbhāṣet |
“apramatto” anavahitaḥ |
sāṅkhyāyanagṛhyam [4.11.6] |
sūtikodakyābhyāṃ na saṃvadet |
gautamaḥ [1.9.17–25] |
na mlecchāśucyadhārmikaiḥ saha saṃbhāṣeta | saṃbhāṣya puṇyakṛto manasā dhyāyet | brāhmaṇena saha saṃbhāṣeta | adhenuṃ dhenubhavyeti | abhadraṃ bhadram iti | kapālaṃ bhagālam iti | maṇidhanur itīndradhanuḥ | gāṃ dhayantīṃ parasmai nācakṣīta na caināṃ dhārayet |
[346] pāraskaraḥ [2.7.10.11] |
garbhiṇīṃ vijanyeti brūyāt | sakulam iti nakulam |
yamaḥ |
tithiṃ pakṣaṃ ca na brūyān na nakṣatrāṇi nirdiśet | adya dvitīyā idaṃ nakṣatram iti akāraṇe na brūyāt |
devalaḥ |
jīveti kṣuvato brūyāj jīvety uktaḥ saheti ca
saheti ceti ekavacanaprayogāc janitakrodhapratiṣedhaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na parakṣetrāgāre gāṃ carantīṃ yāpayantīṃ cānyasmai kathayet | na gobrāhmaṇoparodhe aśnīyāt | na vāhyamāne vāhyatām iti brūyāt |
“uparodhe” āsevane |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.6.20] |
ayuktā rūkṣāḥ paruṣā vāco na brūyāt |
hārītaḥ |
na gāṃ dhayantīṃ paravallabhakṣetrāvatīrṇāṃ vācakṣīta |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
na parakṣetre gāṃ carantīm ācakṣīta pibantaṃ vā paravatsam |
[347] āpastambaḥ [1.36.10] |
varjayet stutiś ca guroḥ prasaktaṃ yathā sussnātam iti |
“sussnātm” iti snānapraśaṃsā |
punar āpastambaḥ [2.5.11–13] |
vākyena vakyasya pratīghātam ācāryasya varjayec chreyasāṃ ca | sarvabhūtaparīvādāṃ ākrośāṃś ca | vidyayā ca vidyānām ||
śreyasīṃ praśastānāṃ vidyayā ca vidyānāṃ pratīghātam ity anvayaḥ |
sāṅkhyāyanagṛhyam [4.12.9–10] |
na janasamavāyaṃ gacchen nopaviśet | sametya nākrośane ‘pi piśuno ‘kulam kulaṃ neti heti ha syāt |
kulaṃ kulaprayojanaṃ paragṛhagamanaśīlaḥ | iti hetiha vṛttam iti prakāśanaparaḥ |
mahābhārate |
paṇyānāṃ śobhanaṃ paṇyaṃ kṛṣīṇāṃ sidhyatāṃ kṛṣiḥ |
bahukāraṃ ca sasyānāṃ bāhye ‘vāhyaṃ tathā gavām ||
saṃpannaṃ bhonaje brūyāt pānīye tarpaṇaṃ tathā |
amṛtaṃ pāyase brūyād yavāgvāṃ kṛṣare tathā ||
[348]        śmaśrukarmaṇi saṃprāpte kṣute snāne ca bhojane |
vyādhitānāṃ ca sarveṣām anyṣyam abhinandanam ||
mahātmanāṃ ca guhyāni na vaktavyāni karhicit |
tvaṃkāraṃ nāmadheyaṃ ca jyeṣṭhānāṃ parivarjayet ||
adharāṇāṃ samānānām ubhayeṣāṃ na duṣyati |
“paṇyāṇāṃ śobhanaṃ paṇyam” iti, paṇyānāṃ kṛte śobhanam etat paṇyam iti brūyāt | “bahuprakāraḥ” bahuśasyam astv ityādi | “vāhyāvāhyan tathā gavām” iti, gavāṃ madhye vāhyaṃ dāntaṃ vāhayogyaṃ vāhyam iti brūyāt | “āyuṣyam” iti nandanaṃ brūyād ity arthaḥ | “avarāṇām” kanīyasām |
viṣṇuḥ |
na vigṛhya kathāṃ kuryāt |
“vigṛhya” ākṣipya |
yama-paiṭhīnasī |
anindyā brāhmaṇā gāvaḥ kāñcanaṃ salilaṃ striyaḥ |
pṛthivī ca ṣaḍ etāni yo nindanti sa ninditaḥ ||
[349] hārītaḥ |
na devagurubrāḥmaṇaparivāhaṃ (-vādaṃ?) kuryān na caiṣāṃ dīyamānaṃ prativārayet |
cāṇḍālaśapatheṣu varāhapurāṇe |
pūrvabhuktāṃ striyaṃ yas tu nindet sarvāṅgasundarīm|
nindate yauvanabhraṣṭāṃ tena pāpena lipyatām ||
bhuktvā parasya cānnāni yas tu nindati nirghṛṇaḥ |
tena pāpena lipate ‘haṃ yady ahaṃ nāgame punaḥ ||
śrutvā parasya vākyāni bhaved yan mantrabhedanāt |
tena pāpena lipayehaṃ yady ahaṃ nāgame punaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
māsmai dehīti caivaṃ yaḥ pravravīti durātmavān |
api jātiśataṃ gatvā na sa mucyeta kilbiṣāt ||
viṣṇuḥ [71.83] |
devabrāhmaṇaśāstramahātmanāṃ parivādaṃ pariharet |
“śāstram” vidhāsthānam |
[350] mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.83–85] |
devadeva dvijātīnāṃ sādhu satyamahātmanām |
guroḥ prativratānāṃ ca tathā [yajvitapasvinām] ||
parivādaṃ na kurvīta parihāse ‘pi putraka |
kurvatām avinītānāṃ na śrotavyaṃ kathaṃcana ||
mahābhārate |
sadā nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham |
nindyamāno naraḥ śīghraṃ narakeṣu prapadyate ||
varāhapurāṇe |
viṣṇuṃ rudrāntaraṃ brñyād yā śrīr gaury antaraṃ tathā |
tan nāstikānāṃ martyānāṃ kāvyaṃ śāstravigarhitam ||
“antaraṃ” bhedam | “kāvyam” kutsitapramāṇam ity arthaḥ |
yamaḥ |
yas tu nindyāt paraṃ jīvan praśaṃsaty ātmano guṇān |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma vedavādiṣu garhitaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
gṛhe pararahasyāni naivopaśṛṇuyād budhaḥ |
[351]        na brūyāt paratantrāṇi prakāśaṃ janasaṃsadi ||
yamaḥ |
parokṣāṇi pareṣāṃ ca parivādaṃ ca varjayet |
naiva brūyān na śṛṇuyād duṣṭavākyaṃ kathaṃcana ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣeṇa kadācid api kenacit |
duṣṭavākyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kathaṃcana ||
tūṣṇīm āsīta nindāyāṃ na brūyāt kiṃcid uttaram |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādaṃ na kuryān naiva kārayet ||
yad brāhmaṇeṣu kuśalaṃ tad eva śubham ācaran |
brāhmaṇānāṃ parīvādo nindā yatra pravartate |
karṇau tatra pidhātavyau gantavyaṃ vā tato ‘nyataḥ |
syād rāsabhaḥ parīvādāt śvā syāt sūkar eva ca |
paribhūya kṛmiś caiva kīṭaḥ syād api matsarī ||
“parokṣāṇi” duṣṭavāyānīti śeṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
parīvādaṃ tyajed vipro ‘savarṇeṣu ca sūcanam |
paribhogaṃ yathokteṣu matsaraṃ putraśiṣyayoḥ ||
“sūcanam” paiśunyam | “paribhogaḥ” paribhūya dhanādyupabhogaḥ | “yathokteṣu” brāhmaṇeṣu sarvarṇeṣu ca |
[352] apa ity anuvṛttau vāyupurāṇe |
naitāḥ paricakṣīta vahantyo ‘tha vāthavā sthitāḥ |
“paricakṣīta” parivahet |
tasmān naitaṃ parivaded vāyuṃ taṃ vāyum īśvaram |
ādatte sukṛtaṃ teṣāṃ ye vai parivadanti tam ||
tathā |
tāvatkālaṃ smṛto deva ugro devas tu dīkṣitaḥ |
tasmān naivaṃ parivaded aślīlaṃ nāsya kīrtayet ||
“tāvatkālaṃ” yāvaddīkṣākālam | “ugro” rudraḥ |
tathā |
yogaṃ parivaded yas tu dhyāninaṃ mokṣakāṅkṣiṇam |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ śrotāraś ca na saṃśayaḥ ||
manuḥ |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryamātulātithisaṃśritaiḥ |
vṛddhabālāturair viprair jñātisaṃbandhibāndhavaiḥ ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ jāmībhiḥ bhrātrā putreṇa bhāryayā |
duhitrā dāsavargeṇa vivādaṃ na samācaret ||
[353]        etais tyajed vivādāṃs tu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
etair jitas tu jayati sarvān lokān imān gṛhī ||
ācāryo brahmalokesaḥ prājāpatye pitā prabhuḥ |
atithis tv indralokeśo devalokasya cartvijaḥ ||
jāmayo ‘psarasāṃ loke vaiśvadevasya bāndhavāḥ |
saṃbandhino ‘pāṃ lokeṣu pṛthivyāṃ mātṛmātulau ||
ākāśeśās tu vijñeyā bālavṛddhakṛśāturāḥ |
bhrātā jyeṣṭhaḥ samaḥ pitrā bharyā putraḥ svakā tanuḥ ||
chāyā svā dāsavargas tu duhitā kṛpaṇaṃ param |
tasmād etair adhikṣiptaiḥ sahetāsaṃjvaraḥ sadā ||
“kṛpaṇam” kṛpāpātram |
devīpurāṇe |
na kuryāj janavādaṃ tu na viprair na ca vatsalaiḥ |
na pradhānajane vādo nṛpākṣepaḥ kadācana ||
nṛpabandhugurvamātyabhiṣagjyotiḥpurohitaiḥ |
virodhāt sumahadduḥkhaṃ prītyā ca sukham āpnuyāt ||
tathā ca |
na ca vācaṃ vaded duṣṭāṃ nadīnāṃ na ca karkaśām |
[354] bandhvādayo nṛpasaṃbandhinaḥ | “jyotiḥ” atra jyautiṣikaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.37] |
śamaṃ nayati yaḥ kruddhān sarvabandhur amatsarī |
bhītāśvāsanakṛt sādhuḥ svargas tasyālpajaṃ phalam ||
śamam upayānti |
matsyapurāṇe [41.50–52] |
kārtavīryārjuno nāma rājā bāhusahasrabhṛt |
yo ‘sya saṃkīrtayen nām kalyam utthāya mānavaḥ ||
na tasya vittanāśaḥ syān naṣṭaṃ ca labhate punaḥ |
skandapurāṇe nandinaṃ prati maheśvaraḥ |
anāmayaś cetanavān nāmakīrtayitātha vā |
so ‘śvamedhaphalaṃ caiva labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
pāpadoṣā ity anuvṛttau devalaḥ |
tataḥ paruṣavacanam apavādaḥ, paiśunyam anṛtam, vṛthālāpo, niṣṭuram iti vāṅmayāḥ ṣaṭ | pareṣāṃ deśajātikulavidyāśilparūpavṛttācāraparicchadāśarīra-karmājīvānāṃ tat pratyakṣaṃ doṣavacanaṃ paruṣam |
ya cānyat krodhasaṃtāpatrāsasaṃjananaṃ vacaḥ |
paruṣaṃ tac ca vijñeyaṃ yathānyac ca tathāvidham ||
[355]                cakṣuṣmān iti luptākṣaṃ cāṇḍālaṃ brāhmaṇeti ca |
vṛṣalaṃ vṛṣalety uktvā patitaṃ patiteti ca |
sateynāpi ca doṣaḥ syān mithyā dviguṇabhāg bhavet ||
teṣām eva paruṣavacanānāṃ parokṣam udāharaṇam apavādaḥ | gurunṛpatibandhu-bhṛtyasakāśe ‘rthopaghātārthaṃ doṣopākhyānaṃ paiśunaym | anṛtaṃ dvividham – asatyam visaṃvadaś ceti |
tathā |
deśe rājapravādāc ca pravākṣaparikalpanāt |
narmahāsaprasaṅgāc ca vijñeyaṃ vyarthabhāṣaṇam ||
guhyāṅgāmedhysaṃjñānāṃ vacanaṃ niṣṭhuraṃ viduḥ |
yad anyad vā vaco nīcaṃ strīpuṃsor mithunāśritam ||
ity evaṃ ṣaḍvikalpasya duṣṭavākyasya bhāṣaṇam |
ihāmutra ca sa krūram anartham adhigacchati ||
hitāhitaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ kevalaṃ vacasi sthitam |
tasmāt saṃyojayed vācam apramattaḥ parīkṣya ca ||
duścikitsyatamaṃ paścād duruktaṃ vaco bhavet |
kṣipraṃ vinipatec cāpi vacanaṃ doṣasaṃhitam ||
[356] “vṛttam” śīlam | “ācāro” nityanaimittikānuṣṭhānam | “ājīvo” jīvikā |
yamaḥ |
apāpaṃ pāpam ity uktvā cauraṃ caureti vā dvija |
vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syān mithyā dvirdoṣatāṃ vrajet ||
pratyakṣaṃ vā parokṣaṃ vā patitaṃ yadi paśyati |
pratyādeśo na kartavyo rakṣed ātmānam ātmanā ||
yāni mithyābhiśantānāṃ patanty aśrūṇi rodanāt |
tāni putrān paśūna heti tena mithyābhiśaṃsinām ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [3.263] |
mithyābhiśaṃsino doṣo dviḥsamo bhūtavādinaḥ |
mithyābhiśastadoṣaṃ ca samādatte mṛṣāṃ vadan ||
āpastambaḥ [1.21.20] |
doṣaṃ budhvā nu pūrvaḥ parebhyaḥ patitasya samākhyāne syād varjayec cainaṃ dharmeṣu |
[357] śātātapaḥ |
snātasya varuṇas tejo juhvato ‘gniḥ śriyaṃ haret |
bhuñjānasya yamas tv āyus tasmān na vyāhares triṣu ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.18.104] |
ete pāṣaṇḍinaḥ pāpā na hy enāt tālayed budhaḥ |
puṇyaṃ naśyati saṃbhāṣād eteṣāṃ dantinodbhavam ||
iti vācyāvācyāni |
[358]
26
atha gamanapraveśanavidhiḥ
tatra manuḥ [4.67–68, 140] |
nāvinītair vrajed dhuryair na vāladhivirūpitaiḥ |
na bhinnaśṛṅgākṣikhurair na cakṣurvyādhipīḍitaiḥ ||
vinītais tu vrajen nityam āśugair lakṣaṇānvitaiḥ |
varṇarūpopasaṃpannaiḥ pratodenātudan bhṛśam ||
nātikalyaṃ nātisāyaṃ nātimadhyaṃdine sthite |
nājñānena samaṃ gacchen naiko na vṛṣalaiḥ saha ||
“avinītaiḥ” duṣṭaiḥ | “vāladhivirūpitaiḥ” chinnapucchaiḥ | “dhuryaiḥ” aśvādibhiḥ | “nātimadhandine sthite” savitarīti śeṣaḥ |
[359] tathā |
gavāṃ ca yānaṃ pṛṣṭhena sarvathaiva vigarhitam |
viṣṇuḥ [63.2–17] |
naiko ‘dhvānaṃ prapadyeta nādhārmikaiḥ sārdham | na vṛṣalaiḥ | na dviṣadbhiḥ | nātipratyuṣasi nātisāyam na saṃdhyayoḥ | na madhyāhne | nāsaṃnihitapānīyam | nāti[tūrṇam | na rātrau | na santataṃ] vyādhitārtair vāhanaiḥ [na hīnāṅgaiḥ na dīnaiḥ | ] na gobhiḥ nādāntaiḥ |
asaṃnihitapānīyam iti kriyāviśeṣaṇam | “adāntaiḥ” aśikṣitaiḥ | “santatam” aviśrameṇa | “vyālo” duṣṭaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
pūrvavṛttiṣu vṛttyā tan nāsūryam adhvānaṃ gacchet | na niyamavelāyām | nānudako yajñopavītī naiko na vṛṣalaiḥ saha |
“pūrvavṛttiṣu” vidyamānāsv iti śeṣaḥ | “asūryaṃ” rātrāv ity arthaḥ | “niyamavelā” nityakarmakālaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
naiko ‘dhvānaṃ prapadyeta nānupātavagne (?) nāyajñopavīti na śūdrādisahāyaḥ |
[360] baudhāyanaḥ [1.7.7–8] |
[tasmād] vinā kamaṇḍalunā nādhvānaṃ vrajen nāsīmāntaṃ [na] gṛhād gṛham | padam api na gacched iṣumātram ity eka |
“vinā kamaṇḍalunā” iti sarvatra yojanīyam | kamaṇḍalugrahaṇam udapātropalakṣaṇārtham | “iṣumātram” iṣuvikṣepaparimitadeśād adhikaṃ padam ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.38, 39.5.14] |
varṣātapādiṣu chatrī daṇḍī rātryaṭavīṣu ca |
śarīratrāṇakāmo vai sopānatkaḥ sadā vrajet ||
nordhvaṃ na niryag dūraṃ vā nirīkṣan paryaṭed budhaḥ |
yugamātraṃ mahīpṛṣṭaṃ naro gacchan na doṣabhāg ||
na duṣṭaṃ yānam ārohet kulachāyaṃ na saṃśrayet |
naikaḥ śūnyāṭavīṃ gacchen na tu śūnyagṛhe vaset ||
devīpurāṇe |
nāsahāyo vrajed rātrau notpathe ca catuṣpathe |
skandapurāṇe |
saunandīti yaḥ proktvā kāntāraṃ pratipadyate |
na tasya dvijaśārdūla hiṃsrāḥ kuryuḥ bhayaṃ kvacit ||
[361] jābālaḥ |
naiko ‘dhvānaṃ prapadyeta na rātrau nāriṇā saha |
na mahāvṛkṣam ārohen na bāhubhyāṃ nadīṃ taret ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2.6.21–23] |
naiko ‘dhvānaṃ prapadyeta | na patitair nābhiśastair na striyā na śūdreṇa | na pratisāyaṃ gacchet |
vyapeyād ity anuṣaṅgaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
na gobrāhmaṇāntarenopeyāt |
yamaḥ |
saṃnikṛṣṭam adhīyānaṃ yo ‘ntarā vyāhared dvijaḥ |
śvabhāsarāsabhānāṃ ca yoniṃ vrajati pāpakṛt ||
“saṃnikṛṣṭaḥ” ekasyāṃ sabhāyām iti tātparyārthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.6.13–15] |
puradvārendrakīlaparighāntareṇa nātīyāt | preṣayor antareṇa na gacchet vatsatantrīṃ nopari gacchet |
[362]  “indrakīlam” indrayaṣṭiḥ | “parighaḥ” argalā | “preṣā” hindolāḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.32.25–27] |
gārdabhaṃ yānam ārohaṇe viṣamārohaṇāvarohaṇāni varjayet | bāhubhyāṃ ca nadītaraṃ nāvaṃ ca sāṃśayikīm |
ārohaṇe na kartavya iti śeṣaḥ | “tara” taraṇam |
viṣṇuḥ [63.43–47] |
pravarṣati na dhāvet | na vṛthā nadīṃ taret | devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś codakam apradāya ca | bāhubhyāṃ bhinnayā nāva |
“na taret” iti pratyekam anuṣajyate |
manuḥ |
acakṣurviṣayaṃ durgaṃ na prapadyeta karhicit |
“acakṣurviṣayaṃ durgaṃ” pramāṇena virodhirahitatattvenājñātm araṇyādi |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
ayaṃ me vajra ity evaṃ mantraṃ kṛtsnam udīrayet |
[363]        varṣaty aprāvṛto gacchet svapet pratyakśirā na ca ||
“ayaṃ me vajraḥ sarvaṃ pāpmānam apahanti” iti sarvamantraṃ paṭhan, varṣaty aprāvṛto gacchet yāvan mantrasamāptiḥ | ūrdhvam aniyamas tāvataiva pāpnamo hatatvād iti viśvarūpaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.12.11] |
abhimukham agnaye kriyamāṇaṃ (?) nāmapratiṣṭhitaṃ (?) bhūmau pradakṣiṇīkṛtyopeyāt |
bhūmau pradakṣiṇīkṛtyaiva gacched ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ |
mṛdaṃ gāṃ daivataṃ vipraṃ ghṛtaṃ madhu catuṣpatham |
pradakṣiṇāni kurvīta prajātāṃś ca vanaspatīn ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.42] |
vidyādhikaṃ guruṃ caiva dvijaṃ kuryāt pradakṣiṇam |
[364] śaṅkhalikhitau |
gobrāhmaṇasamavāhendrakīlacatuṣpathāny abhijñātāṃś ca vanaspatīn pradakṣiṇāni kuryāt |
pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryād ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ [63.29–32] |
agnibrāhmaṇagaṇikāpūrṇakumbhādarśachatradhvajapatākāśrīvṛkṣavardhamāna-nandyāvartāṃś ca tālavṛntacāmarāś ca, gajājagokṣīradadhighṛtamadhu-siddhārakāṃś ca | vīṇābakulārdragomayaphalapuṣpārdraśākagorocanādūrvā-prahorāṃś ca uṣṇīṣālaṃkaraṇamaṇikanakarajatavastrāsanayānāmiṣāṇi ca bhṛṅgāroddhṛto bījabandhaikakumārī mīnāṃś ca [dṛṣṭvā prayād iti] |
śrīvṛkṣavṛddhamānaṃ nandyāvartāḥ “saṃniveśāḥ” suvarṇadārvādivinirmitāḥ jyotiḥśāstrādiprasiddhāḥ | “uṣṇīṣālaṃkāro” mukuṭam |
matsyapurāṇe [243.14–28] |
atheṣṭyādi pravakṣyāmi maṅglyāni yathānagha |
śvetāḥ sumanasaḥ śreṣṭhāḥ pūrṇakumbhas tathaiva ca ||
jalajāni ca puṣpāṇi māṃsaṃ madyaṃ ca pārthiva |
gāvas turaṅgamā nāgā baddha eka paśus tv ajaḥ ||
[365]        tridaśāḥ suhṛdo vijñāḥ jvalitaś ca hutāśanaḥ |
gaṇikā ca mahābhāga dūrvā cārdraṃ ca gomayam ||
rukmaṃ raupyaṃ tathā vastraṃ sarvaratnāni cāpy atha |
auṣadhāni ca dharmajñā yavāḥ siddhārthakās tathā ||
nṛvāhyamānaṃ yānaṃ ca bhadrapīṭhaṃ tathaiva ca |
khaḍgaṃ cakraṃ tathā kāñcamṛdam āyudham eva ca ||
ghṛtaṃ dadhi payaś caiva phalāni vividhāni ca |
svastikaṃ vardhamānaṃ ca nandyāvartaṃ ca kaustubham ||
sanyastacitravinyastamaṅgalāṇy aparāṇi ca |
akṣatāḥ śvetapuṣpāś ca tathā darpaṇa eva ca ||
añjanaṃ rocanā caiva bhṛṅgāro mākṣikaṃ tathā |
vāditrāṇāṃ mukhaḥ śabdo gambhīraś ca manoharaḥ ||
gāndhāraṣaḍjaṛṣabhā ye ca śastās tathāsurāḥ |
meghāḥ śastāḥ ghanāḥ snigdhāḥ gajabṛṃhitaniḥsvanāḥ ||
anulomā taḍicchastāḥ śakracāpas tathaiva ca |
anulomā gṛhāḥ śastāḥ dikpatis tu viśeṣataḥ ||
āstikyaṃ śraddadhānatvaṃ tathā pūjyābhipūjanam |
[366]        śastāny etāni dharmajña yaś ca syān manasi priyam ||
manasas tuṣṭir evātra paramaṃ jayakāraṇam |
ekataḥ sarvaliṅgāni manastuṣṭikaraṃ tathā ||
yatrotsukatvaṃ manasaḥ parharṣaḥ
śubhasya lābho vijayasya vādaḥ |
māṅgalyalabdhiḥ śravaṇaṃ ca rājan
jñeyāni nityaṃ vijayāvahāni ||
“nṛpavardhamānaṃ” puruṣayuktam | yānaṃ bhadrapīṭhaḥ | svastikakaustubhāḥ suvarṇādighaṭitāḥ saṃniveśaviśeṣāḥ | “anulomāḥ” pṛṣṭhasthāḥ | “dikpatayo” ravisitakujādyāḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [63.34–38] |
atha mattonmattavyaṅgān dṛṣṭvā nivarteta | vāntaviriktamuṇḍajaṭilavāmanāṃś ca | kāṣāyipravrajitamalināṃś ca | tailaguḍaśuṣkagomayendanatṛṇaparṇabhasmāṅgāra-kacāṃś ca lavaṇaklībāsavanapuṃsakakārpāsarajjugaḍitamuktakeśāṃś ca |
[367] “kacāḥ” keśāḥ | “klībo” niretaskaḥ | [“āsavaḥ” sandhānakaḥ | napuṃsakaḥ strīsaṃvyañjanarahitaḥ] |
matsyapurāṇe [243.2–12, 22-25] |
auṣadhāni ca yuktāni dhānyaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca yad bhavet |
kārpāsaṃ ca tṛṇaṃ śuṣkaṃ gomayendhanam eva ca ||
indhanaṃ ca tathāṅgāraṃ laguḍāś ca tathāśubhāḥ |
abhyaktaṃ malinaṃ muṇḍe tathā nagnaṃ ca mānavam ||
muktakeśaṃ jarārtaṃ ca kāṣāyāmbaradhāriṇam |
unmattakaṃ tathā ṣaṇḍaṃ dīnaṃ cātha napuṃsakam ||
ayaḥ paṅgus tathā carmakeśabandhanam eva ca |
tathaivoddhṛtasārāṇi piṇyākādīni yāni ca ||
riktāṇi caiva bhāṇḍāni mṛtaṃ sāṅkratam eva ca |
evamādīni cānyāni praśastāny abhidarśane ||
aśasto vāyuśtaś ca bhinnabhairavajarjaraḥ |
ehīti purataḥ śabdaḥ śasyate na tu pṛṣṭhataḥ ||
[368]        gaccheti paścād dharmajña purastāt tu vigarhitaḥ |
kva yāsi tiṣṭha mā gaccha kiṃ te tatra gatasya tu ||
anye śabdāś ca ye ‘niṣṭhās te vipattikarāḥ pathi |
dhvajādiṣu tathā sthānaṃ kravyādānāṃ vigarhitam ||
calanaṃ vāhanānāṃ ca vastrasaṅgas tathaiva ca |
nirgatasya ca dvārādau śirasaś cābhighātitā ||
chatradhvajānāṃ śastrāṇāṃ patākāṃ ca tathāśubham |
vāyuḥ saśarkaraḥ rūkṣaḥ sarvadigbhyaḥ samutthitaḥ ||
pratilomas tathā nīco vijñeyo bhayakṛd dvijaḥ |
anukūlo mṛduḥ snigdhaḥ sukhasparśaḥ sukhāvahaḥ ||
rūkṣā rūkṣasvarāś caṇḍāḥ kravyādāś ca vigarhitāḥ |
apraśaste tathā jñeye pariveṣaparvarṣaṇe ||
“auṣadhāni yuktāni” militāni daśamūlībhṛtīni | “kṛṣṇadhānyaṃ” tilamāṣādi | “bandhanaṃ” nigaḍādi | “kravyādānāṃ” gṛdhrādīnām |
tathā [243.13–14] |
dṛṣṭe nimitte prathamam animittaṃ vināśanam |
śaṅkaraṃ pūjayed vidvāṃs tathaiva madhusūdanam |
dvitīye tu tathā dṛṣṭe pradīpaṃ praviśed gṛham ||
[369] vasiṣṭhaḥ [12.42] |
nāvṛto yajñaṃ gacchet yad vrajet pradakṣiṇam punar āvrajet |
“avṛtaḥ” anāhūtaḥ |
vṛddhamanuḥ |
namaskartuṃ vrajet kāmaṃ didṛkṣur yajñakarmaṇaḥ |
kulāt kulaṃ rahasy api samcaren nānimittataḥ ||
“kulāt kulam” gṛhād gṛham | “rahasyam” gopyam | “animittataḥ” saṃprayojanaṃ vinā |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na vadhabandhasamavāyam gaccheta na dāvaṃ na dampatyor antaram na ṛṣīṇāṃ prabhavam | na ṛṣīṇāṃ na vadhabandhanasamavāye |
“na vadhabandhananimitto” janasmūhaḥ | “dāvo” vanavahniḥ | tatsamīpaṃ na gacchet | “ṛṣīṇāṃ prabhavam” utpattisthānam | kapālagrāmādikam iti mādhavasvāmī |
[370] manuḥ [4.73] |
advāreṇa ca jānīyān grāmaṃ vā veśma cāvratam |
rātrau ca vṛkṣamūlāni dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na rātrau vicaret |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.23] |
sṛtyā grāmam praviśet | yadi praviśet “namo rudrāya vāstoṣpataye” ity etām ṛcaṃ japed anyāṃ vā raudrīm |
“sṛtyā” mārgeṇa |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na bāhyair ayuktair apītapānīyaiḥ pibet | na grāsaṃ adatvāśnīyāt |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [14.56] |
ekasārthaprayātā ye niḥsvam annārthinaṃ naram |
[371]        apāsya svānnam aśnanti ta ime śleṣmabhojinaḥ ||
dakṣaputrāṇāṃ bhrātranveṣaṇāya gatānām anivṛttim abhidhāya viṣṇupurāṇe [1.15.100] |
tataḥ prabhṛti vai bhrātā bhrātur anveṣaṇe dvijaḥ |
prayāto naśyati tathā tan na kāryaṃ vijānatā ||
iti gamanapraveśanavidhiḥ |
[372]
27
athānākramaṇīyāny anadhiṣṭheyāni
tatra manuḥ |
devatānāṃ guror prājñaḥ snātakācāryamantriṇām |
nākrāmet kāmataś chāyāṃ kapilasya ca gor api ||
svāṃ tu nākramec chāyāṃ klībena patitena ca |
cāṇḍālena dviṣadbhiś ca nityaṃ rogānvitena ca ||
“prājñaḥ” praśastabuddhiḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
devartviksnātakācāryarājñāṃ chāyāṃ parastriyāḥ |
nākrāmed raktaviṇmūtraṣṭhīvanodvartanāni ca ||
[373] manuḥ |
madhyandine ‘rdharātre ca śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā tu sāmiṣam |
saṃdhyayor ubhayoś caiva na seveta catuṣpatham ||
udvartanam apasnānaṃ viṇmūtraṃ raktam eva ca |
śleṣmaniṣṭhyūtavāntāni nādhitiṣṭhet tu kāmataḥ ||
vairiṇaṃ nopaseveta sahāyaṃ caiva vairiṇaḥ |
adhārmikaṃ taskaraṃ ca parasyaiva ca yoṣitam ||
“udvartanam” kṛtamalāpakarṣaṇaṃ kuṅumādi | “apasnānam” snānopayuktaṃ jalam | “niṣṭhyutam” udgīrṇaṃ tāmbūlādi |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
noddhatakuhakaiḥ sahaikatas tiṣṭhet |
“uddhataḥ” avinītaḥ | “kuhakaḥ” vaśīkaraṇādiniṣthaḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [63.19–23] |
na catuṣpatham adhitiṣṭhet | na rātrau vṛkṣamūlaṃ na śūnyālayam | [na tṛṇam] na paśūnāṃ bandhanāgāram |
hārītaḥ |
āvapanāpramārjanāpasnānatuṣabhasmāsthikapālakeśasaṃyānādhitiṣṭhet |
[374] “āvapanam” goṇmādibhāṇḍam | “apramārjanam” mārjanopayuktakuśādi | “kapālaḥ” kūrparaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nāheḥ padam ādhitiṣṭhet | na tuṣakapālakeśabhasmāsthitṛṇasaṅkarakūṭe tiṣṭhet |
“saṅkaraḥ” avakaraḥ |
manuḥ [4.78–79] |
adhitiṣthen na keśāṃs tu na bhasmāsthikapālikāḥ |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.25] |
tuṣāṅgārāsthiśīrṇāni rajjuvastrādikāni ca |
nādhitiṣṭhet tathā prājñaḥ pathi patrāṇi vā bhuvi ||
āpastambaḥ [2.12.5] |
pāṇisamūhanaṃ brāhmaṇasya nāprokṣitam adhitiṣṭhet | na bhūmāv anantarhitāyām āsīta |
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.13–15, 28] |
catuṣpathaś caityataruḥ śmaśānopavanāni ca |
duṣṭastrīsaṃnikarṣaś ca varjyāni niśi sarvadā ||
pūjyadevadhvajājyośchāyāṃ nātikramed budhaḥ |
[375]        keśāsthikaṇṭakāmedhyabalibhasmatuṣāṃ tathā ||
snānārdrāṃ dhāriṇīṃ caiva dūrataḥ parivarjayet |
śleṣmaviṇmūtraraktāni sarvadaiva na laṅghayet ||
devīpurāṇe |
śūnyāgāre na tiṣṭhec ca na ca parvatamastake |
na śmaśāne na devānāṃ prāsādeṣu kadācana ||
na caiva goḥ prasūtāyāḥ viśvaset strījaneṣu ca |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [34.85] |
notkṛṣṭaśayyāśayano na nikṛṣṭasya cāruhet |
tathā [35.30] |
nodyānādau vikāleṣu prājñas tiṣṭhet kathaṃcana |
na cālapej janaṃ duṣṭaṃ vīrahīnāṃ na ca striyam ||
ity anākramaṇīyāny anadhiṣṭheyāni |
[376]
28
athāvalokanīyāni
tatra viṣṇuḥ [71.17–21] |
ādityam udyantaṃ nekṣeta | nāstaṃ yāntam noparaktam | na vāsasā nirohitam | na cādarśadarśanābhyupagatam | na madhyāhne |
“uparaktaḥ” rāhugrastaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nodyantam ādityam īkṣeta | nāstaṃyāntam āpyāyanaṃ ca | tasmai vidhivad vidadhati |
“āpyāyanam” arghyādinā pūjanam | “tasmai” ādityāya |
hārītaḥ |
na nagnāṃ striyaṃ puruṣaṃ vā īkṣeta | nodayāstamayau candrārkau |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
nekṣetārkaṃ na nagnāṃ strīṃ na ca saṃspṛṣṭamaithunām |
[377]        na ca mūtraṃ purīṣaṃ vā nāśucī rāhutārakāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
sūryācandramasau cobhau nakṣatrāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ |
nodīkṣetāśucir nityaṃ grahān anyāṃś ca mānavāḥ ||
patitavyaṅgacāṇḍālocchiṣṭādyā na vilokayet |
arthadṛṣṭimatair etaiḥ saṃbhāṣeta nṛṇāṃ varaḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3.12.11] |
jyotīṃṣy amedhyāsaktāni nābhivīkṣeta ca prabho |
tathā |
saṃvatsaraṃ kiryāhānir yasya puṃso ‘bhijāyate |
tasyāvalokanāt sūryo nirīkṣyaḥ sādhubhiḥ sadā ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [35.33, 35] |
devatāpitṛsacchāstrayajñasaṃdhyādinindakaiḥ |
kṛtvā tu sparśanālāpaṃ śudhyetārkāvalokanāt ||
avalokya tathodakyām antyajaṃ patitaṃ śavam |
vidharmisūtikāṣaṇḍaṃ vivastrāntyavasāyinaḥ ||
mṛtaniryātakāś caiva paradāraratāś ca ye |
etad eva hi kartavyaṃ prājñaiḥ śodhanam ātmanaḥ ||
[378] skandapurāṇe |
kṛtaghnaṃ mānavaṃ dṛṣṭvā narakeṣv api kutsitam |
śuddhyarthaṃ devi draṣṭavyāḥ somānaladivākarāḥ ||
somādīnāṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ vikalpaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
avaguṇṭhita ucchiṣṭo ‘medhyāntaro nāvabhāṣeta | nordhvaṃ jyotīṃṣi vīkṣeta na ca devavanaspatīn |
“avaguṇṭhita” atra avaguṇṭhitaśirāḥ |
gautamaḥ |
na nagnāṃ parayoṣitam īkṣeta |
sāṅkhyāyanagṛhyam [4.11.1] |
na nagnāṃ striyam īkṣetānyatra maithunāt | tathānāptam akāryakāriṇam |
“anāptam” śatrum | “akāryakāriṇam” pātakinam | nekṣetety anuṣaṅgaḥ |
īkṣetety anuvṛttau [379] viṣṇuḥ [71.22–31] |
na kruddhasya guror mukham | na tailodayoḥ svacchāyām | na malavaty ādarśe | na patnīṃ bhojanasamaye | na striyaṃ nagnām | na kaṃcana mehamānam | na cālānam | bhraṣṭakuñjaram na viṣamastho vṛṣādiyuddham | nonmattam | na mattam |
manuḥ |
nāśnīyād bhāryayā sārdhaṃ nainām īkṣeta cāśnatīm |
kṣuvatīṃ jṛmbhamāṇāṃ vai na cāsīnāṃ yathāsukham ||
nāñjayantīṃ svake netre na cābhyaktām anāvṛtām |
na paśyet prasavantī ca tejaskāmo dvijottamaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na kūpaśvabhrāṇy atiṣṭhet [ātiṣṭhet?] | na kūpam avalokayet | na laṅghayet |
manuḥ |
na viṇmūtram udīkṣeta |
ity avalokanīyāni |
[380]
29
atha śūdrāpradeyāni
tatra manu yama-vasiṣṭhau prathame [Manu 4.80–81] |
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyān nocchiṣṭaṃ na haviṣkṛtam |
na cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ na cānyavratam ādiśet ||
yo hy asya dharmam ācaṣṭe yaś caivādiśate vratam |
so ‘saṃvṛtaṃ nāma tamaḥ saha tenaiva gacchati ||
“matiḥ” adēṣṭārthakāryākāryaviṣayā | “ucchiṣṭam” bhojanapātrasthaṃ bhaktaśiṣṭam | “haviṣkṛtam” havistvena kalpitaṃ puroḍāśādi | “dharmaṃ” pañcayajñādi | ayaṃ cocchiṣṭadānaniṣedho ‘nāśritaśūdraviṣayaḥ, āśritaṃ prati,
ucchiṣṭam annaṃ dātavyaṃ jīrṇāni vasanāni ca | [10.115]
iti manunaivodāhṛtatvāt | itare tu pratiṣedhāḥ sarvaśūdraviṣayāḥ | varṇadharmasya tu lobhādinā atikrāntapratiṣedhenaivopapatteḥ na tadvidhānāpekṣā |
[381] yamaḥ |
vṛṣalādhyāpako yas tu vṛṣalādhyāpitaś ca yaḥ |
tāv ubhau narake magnau vasetāṃ śaradāṃ śatam ||
viṣṇuḥ [71.48–52] |
na śūdrāya matiṃ dadyāt | nocchiṣṭahaviṣī | [na tilān] | na cāsyopadiśed dharmaṃ na vartam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
kṛśarapāyasāpūpamāṃsadadhimadhughṛtakṛṣṇājināni na śūdrebhyo dadyāt | na cocchiṣṭaṃ haviṣkrtaṃ nopakṛtaṃ kiṃcid anyāprakṛtāt ||
prakṛtam utsavaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.25–26] |
nābrāhmaṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ dadyād yadi prayacched dantān kṛṣṭvā tasminn avadhāya prayacchet |
abrāhmaṇo ‘tra śūdraḥ | vilikhya tasminn ucchiṣṭe avadhāya niḥkṣipya dantamalānīti śeṣaḥ |
[382]
30
atha krodhādīnāṃ varjanam
tatra āpastambaḥ [1.31.28] |
krodhādīṃś ca bhūtadāhīyān doṣān varjayet |
“bhūtadāhīyān” śarīradāhahetūn |
punar āpastambaḥ [1.23.4–5] |
atha bhūtadāhīyān doṣān udāhariṣyāmaḥ | krodho harṣo lobho moho dambho droho mṛṣodyamātyāśaparīvādāvasūyā kāmamanyū anātmyam ayogaḥ | [teṣāṃ yogamūlo nirghātaḥ] |
“atyāśaḥ” bahutarabhakṣaṇam | “manyuḥ” parābhibhavotthadoṣānuvṛttiḥ | “anātmyam” avaśitvam | “ayogo” anuyogaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
audararaitasakāmajakrodhajaharṣajānagrān yathāvan niyacchet |
[383] audarāḥ sarvakarmendriyabhāvadoṣāḥ saṃbhavanti yair upasṛṣṭo vividhām āpadam upaiti, tasmāc chucir yathā parimitāhāraḥ syāt | āhāraśuddhau sattvaśuddhir ity ācāryāḥ | lokadvayasantāpakaratvād agnitam eteṣām | niyacchet śamayet
punar hārītaḥ |
paustā sāhasaṃ retasya bījaṃ tad amṛtāv utsargadoṣaḥ syād ṛtau votsarge tad brahmacaryeṇa vyākhyātam | devādipravṛttatvāj jagataḥ prathamam, yajñaniyamārthaṃ dvitīyam, tapaḥsiddhyarthaṃ tṛtīyam, mokṣasiddhyarthaṃ caturtham, samkalpāt kāmasaṃbhavatyāśayāt pravartate snehān nibadhnāti | sa icchālakṣaṇo ‘nekavidhaḥ kāmo yenābhibhūtaḥ tṛpta eva kāmānāṃ lokeṣv anekavidhajanmasaṃsārakāmāvarte nimajjati sa evānalaḥ | kāmo hi bhagavān vaiśvānara iti śrutiḥ | tasya saṃkalpo niyamanam, sa vyākhyātaḥ |
kāmagītau cātra ślokau bhavataḥ |
pravṛttau ca nivṛttau ca sarvataḥ pratibhūr aham |
sarvārtheṣu yathārtho ‘haṃ madṛte nāśti kiṃcana ||
yo māṃ yathārthaṃ sarvārthe sarvabhūteṣu paśyati |
[384]                so ‘dhvanaḥ paramāṃ yāti netare kāmavādinaḥ || iti |
krodhāgninābhibhūtaḥ sveṣām apy abahumato nābhibhavati yo ‘viśvasanīyaś ca bhavati | kāryākāryavācyāvācyāni na vitarkayati | [hitavādino gurūn paśyati kramety āviṣṭaḥ | pretalokāyātmānaṃ gamayati] | tatra ghorāṃ nirayaprāyāṃ yātanām anubhūya krūrakrayādaśvatiryagyoniṣu jāyate | tatra sarvānāṃ prajānāṃ vaśyo bhavati | kramaśo manuṣyatāṃ prāpya sarvajanavidviṣṭatām upaiti | krodho hi tamorūpas tasyā kṣamā niyamanaṃ sā vyākhyāteti | lobhāgninābhibhūtaḥ puṇyārtheṣv apy upayujyamānam atikrāmantam manyate | naṣṭaṃ putraṃ dārabandhūnām upayuktaṃ suhṛdbandhusaṃbandhinām apy anujīvyatamo bhavati | asadvṛttānām apy ādānam icchati | tatrārthānubandhāt tiryagyoniṣu jāyate lobho hi tamorūpas tasya saṃtoṣo niyamanaṃ so ‘pi vyākhyātaḥ | evaṃ hy āha —
audaro retasaś cāpi kāmajaḥ krodhajo ‘ṅgajaḥ |
ete vai nirjitā yena sa vai pañcatapā smṛtaḥ |
[385]                vācovegaṃ manasaḥ krodhavegaṃ
jihvāvegaṃ tūdaropasthavegam |
etān vegān dhārayed yas tu vipras
taṃ prabrūyuḥ brahmiṣṭaṃ vā muniṃ ca || iti |
paurastān puruṣavyāpārān sāhasaṃ balavantasya puruṣasya bījaṃ yatas tat tasmād brahmacaryeṇa bahurūpatayā kṛṣṇādi tat sarvāḥ sarvam udāragamanarūpam | prathamaṃ jagannivāsinaḥ sarvasya bhavati devam ārabhya tiryakparyantaṃ svabhāvapravṛttatvāt | dvitīyaṃ sarvavarjanarūpaṃ yajñasāṅgatāsiddhyarthaṃ tṛtīyam ṛtāv eva gamanam ūrdhvaretaskaṃ saṃkalpaḥ viṣayasaundaryajñānam | “kāmo” viṣayecchā | “āśayaḥ” paunaḥpunyena bhāvanā | “snehaḥ” tatraivāsaktiḥ | “adhvanaḥ” arcirādimārgāt paraṃ tripālakṣaṇam |
viṣṇuḥ [33.6] |
trividhaṃ narakasyedaṃ dvāraṃ nāśanam ātmanaḥ |
kāmaḥ krodhas tathā lobhaḥ tad etat tritayaṃ tyajet ||
jābālaḥ |
tyajed indriyacāpalyaṃ brahmanindāṃ tathaiva ca |
[386]        māyāmātsaryalobhāṃś ca krodhavegān balāya te ||
parāpavādamohāṃś ca ātmārthe yācanāṃ tathā |
vṛthā kalaham ālasyaṃ tathā maithunam eva ca ||
āpastambaḥ [2.5.18] |
manasā vācā ghrāṇena cakṣuṣā śrotreṇa tvakśiśnodarārambhan āsrāvān paribhuñjāno ‘mṛtatvāya kalpate \
tvakśiśnodarārambhaṇānān (?) āsrāvān (?) pañcabhir indriyaiḥ “paribhuñjāno” varjayet |
manuḥ [4.163] |
nāstikyaṃ vedanindāṃ ca devatānāṃ ca kutsanam |
dveṣaṃ dambhaṃ ca mānaṃ ca krodhaṃ taikṣṇyaṃ ca varjayet ||
hārītaḥ |
sarvābhakṣyābhakṣaṇam abhojyabhojanam apeyapānam ayājyayājanam asatpratigraho paradārābhigamanaṃ paradravyāpaharaṇaṃ prāṇihiṃsā ceti śarīrāṇi [śārīrāṇi?] | pāruṣyam anṛtaṃ vivādaḥ śrutivikrayaś ceti vācyāni | paropatāpaḥ parābhidrohaḥ krodho lodho moho ‘haṃkāraś ceti tad etāny aṣṭādaśa ca yāni karmāṇi |
“abhojyabhojanam” patitādyannabhakṣaṇam | “naireyāṇi” narakahetavaḥ (?) |
[387] devalaḥ |
athātaḥ pāpadoṣān manovākśarīrajān vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tatra moharāgadveṣamānalobhamadaśokamamatvāhaṃkārabhayaharṣamodhacintāś ceti dvādaśa mānasāḥ |
teṣāṃ ca trividho mohaḥ saṃbhavaḥ sarvapāpmanām |
ajñānaṃ saṃśayajñānaṃ mithyājñānam iti trikam ||
vijñānaṃ sarvavidyānām arthānāṃ svayam ūhanam |
doṣair adharṣaṇaṃ ceti jñānam ajñānam anyathā ||
aśreyaḥśreyasor madhye bhramaṇe saṃśayo bhavet |
mithyājñānam iti prāhur ahite hitadarśanam ||
bhavaty adharmā ajñānād avidyātaś ca saṃśayaḥ |
ubhābhyām anyathājñānaṃ pāpiṣṭham upajāyate ||
ajñānād duḥkhasaṃprāptiḥ saṃśayād vartate punaḥ |
mithyājñānāt pataty eva mūḍhaḥ tāmisrayoniṣu ||
āvṛttāḥ viṣayāḥ sarve dharmadoṣair yathā tanuḥ |
guṇato hṛdi tān śrutvā rāgorāgasya lakṣaṇam ||
asaṃnikarṣād ajñānāt saṃkalpād abhimānataḥ |
[388]                sukhāc ca jāyate rāgo viṣayeṣu śarīriṇām |
jñānād dveṣād asāmarthyāt kleśād anyaprasaṅgataḥ |
viyogāt kāmataḥ śokād dharmāc caiva nivartate ||
nivartate ced rāgo ‘yaṃ puṃso dveṣādihetubhiḥ |
bhūyo ‘py utpadyate saṅgāj jñānān naśyati kevalam ||
atiprasaktaḥ bhogeṣu rāgo bhavati doṣakṛt |
parastrīṣu parasveṣu kiṃcit prārthanayāpi ca ||
roṣo ‘marṣo ‘bhyasūyā ca droho mithyā vitarkitam |
duḥkhārhaṃ ceti tattvajñair dveṣaḥ ṣoḍhā vidhīyate ||
yat tasmād hṛdaye jantoḥ samuttiṣṭhati vaikṛtaḥ |
parāniṣṭacikīrṣā ca saroṣa iti saṃjñitaḥ ||
parābhavasamutthena doṣeṇaivābhicoditaḥ |
kiṃcid dṛṣṭvaiva yo dveṣas tad amarṣasya lakṣaṇam ||
parasya śuddhān dṛṣṭvā tu svaguṇābhyadhikān guṇān |
nābhinandati yo dveṣāt sābhyasūyety udāhṛtā ||
suhṛdām api yo nityam arthān upajighāṃsati |
[389]                asauhṛdyamatiḥ pāpas tad āhur drohalakṣaṇam ||
yaḥ sādhūn paradoṣāṃś ca manyate dveṣakāriṇaḥ |
mithyā vitarkitaṃ ceti tad āhur bhāvadoṣajam ||
dveṣāl lobhāc ca saṃkalpāc cintanaṃ pāpakarmaṇām |
duḥkhārham iti vijñeyaṃ pūrvaduṣkṛtasaṃbhavam ||
duḥkhān mithyā vighātāc ca duḥkho ‘yaṃ dvividho bhavet |
jñānāt kālātyayād dharmāt prīteś ca vinivartate ||
atattvagrahaṇaṃ tāpo vepathur jñānasaṃplavaḥ |
svedo ‘kṣirogaḥ pāruṣyaṃ śvāsas tu dveṣadoṣajāḥ ||
sarvatrotkṛṣṭam ātmānaṃ sarvebhyo yo ‘bhimanyate |
sa māna iti vijñeyo yatheṣṭasya surākṛyaḥ (?) ||
narasyātmābhimānena yā utkṛṣṭir na śobhate |
utkṛṣṭir yācanā prāhur utkṛṣṭiḥ sāvamānataḥ ||
parārthaṃ parabhogāṃś ca parasāmṛdhyam eva ca |
dṛṣṭvā śrutvā ca yā tṛṣṇā jāyate lobha eva saḥ ||
alobhān mūlatarkāc ca pipāsā parivardhitaḥ |
[390]                anartheṣv eva saṃmohya lobhaḥ pacati durnaram ||
nāsti tṛṣṇāsamaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāsti tyāgasamaṃ sukham |
tasmāc ca lobhasaṃjñātāṃ tṛṣṇāṃ vidvān parityajet ||
prāpyāpi medinīṃ kṛtsnāṃ tṛṣṇā vai nopaśāmyati |
vivardhate kiṃ bhūyo ‘pi haviṣeva vibhāvasuḥ ||
duḥkhād ity āhur antatvād anityatvād atṛptitaḥ |
paścāc chokāvahatvāc ca na kvacic chreyasī tṛṣā ||
kulavṛddhivapurvidyāvittaiśvaryavibhūtibhiḥ |
svair etair bādhyate yo ‘nyān unmādasyeha lakṣaṇam ||
devāsuramanuṣyāṇām utsiktāmadahetavaḥ |
madenaiva vineśus te tasmāt tyājyo mado budhaiḥ ||
harṣaiḥ śokāvalokāc ca dambhād darpāc ca garvataḥ |
anye ca tadvidhā bhāvās te madasya vikalpajāḥ ||
aniṣṭayogajād dveṣāt snehāviṣṭāc ca yogajāt |
kleśād vā hṛdi duḥkhaṃ ca sa śoka iti padyate ||
anityāḥ sarvabhāvāś ca śokas tatra na vartate |
atha vā yadi nityāḥ syuḥ kutaḥ śokaḥ pravartate ||
[391]                naivaṃ hi śocet sattvasthas tāmaso vāpi kevalam |
śokād duḥkham avāpnoti samāseneha rājasaḥ ||
idaṃ mameti yaḥ svāmyam ātmānaṃ sveṣu manyate |
ajānantam anityatvaṃ mamatvam iti tad viduḥ ||
aham ity abhimānena yaḥ kriyāsu pravartate |
kāryakāraṇayuktāsu tad ahaṃkāralakṣaṇam ||
ahaṃkāramamatvābhyāṃ bādhyante sarvadehinaḥ |
saṃsāraviniyogeṣu tābhyāṃ muktas tu mucyate ||
ātmabādhākaraṃ śrutvā dṛṣṭvā vā pratyupasthitam |
udvego jāyate yas tu kṛdaye tad bhayaṃ smṛtam ||
tāvad bhaye na bhetavyaṃ yāvad bhayam anāgatam |
āgataṃ tu bhayaṃ dṛṣṭvā pravartavyam abhītavat ||
bhavitavyaṃ bhavaty eva nāsti karmavyatikramaḥ |
ity evaṃ dṛṣṭatattvānāṃ na kvacij jāyate bhayam ||
ātmano hṛdayaṃ dṛṣṭvā parebhyo vyasanāni ca |
ānando mānaso jantor harṣa ity abhidhīyate ||
sarvadravyeṣv anityeṣu kṣaṇikeṣv asukheṣu ca |
na harṣasyāvakāśo ‘sti vimṛśya yadi paśyati ||
[392]                mādhyasthyam api doṣāya pareṣāṃ vyasanāgame |
kiṃ punar mūḍhabhāvena pramodaḥ parividrave ||
yo vṛthā cintayaty arthān manasā hi parasparam |
moghacinteti tām āhur dharmakarmopaghātinīm ||
bhogaiśvaryadhanādīni lobhād yaḥ saṃsared vṛthā |
sa moghacintayātmānaṃ hanti buddhiṃ ca karma ca ||
tasmād vidūragāṃ cintāṃ tyaktvā śokaṃ ca karmajam |
ārabheta śubhaṃ karma dhiyaṃ prāptuṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||
rāgasaṃkalpajān hanti hṛdayeṣu dṛḍārpitān |
ucchidya caitān medhāvī tattvajñānāsinā hutam ||
ātmano rakṣaṇopāyam anityāmadhyatāṃ tanoḥ |
hitaṃ ca sarvabhūtānāṃ cintayen manasā saha ||
ete manobhavā doṣāḥ dvādaśāśivahetavaḥ |
mohādayaḥ samākhyātā devāsuranṛmohanāḥ ||
tatsaṃyogān mano gupter jñānadharmaprasaṅgataḥ |
tyāgāc ca vyasanādīnāṃ hāniṃ gacchanti mānavaḥ ||
[393] tathā |
aprakāśakaro buddhiṃ tam āvṛtya sarvataḥ |
prathamaṃ jāyate mohād dehināṃ hṛdi pāpmajaḥ ||
mohād utpadyate rāgo rāgād dveṣaḥ prajāyate |
tribhir etair bhavaty evaṃ pāpaṃ śārīravāṅmayam ||
tasmād garīyaḥ sarveṣu mānaseṣv api ca trayam |
sarvān doṣān parityaktuṃ trīn etān prathaṃ tyajet ||
vāṅmayān abhidhāya pāpadoṣānuvṛttau punar devalaḥ |
[tato hiṃsāpacāraḥ steyam arthadūṣaṇam iti caturvidhāḥ śarīrajāḥ | kṛtapraṇāśanaṃ dravyāpadhvaṃsanaṃ cārthdūṣaṇam ity evam] | veśmakṣetraśasyadahanaṃ setubhedanam ājīvopaghātanaṃ kṛtapraṇāśanam | dravyāpadhvaṃsanaṃ cārthadūṣaṇam ity evam |
kṛtvā tv arthajātān yāyāt pūrvān svalakṣaṇaiḥ |
ākhyātāni viśeṣeṇa pāpāni pariśeṣataḥ ||
manuḥ [4.162, 165–169] |
ācāryaṃ ca pravaktāraṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
[394]        na hiṃsyād brāhmaṇān gāś ca sarvāṃś caiva tapasvinaḥ ||
brāhmaṇādyāvagūryaiva (?) dvijātir vadhakāmyayā |
śataṃ varṣāṇi tāmisre narake parivartate ||
tāḍayitvā tṛṇenāpi saṃrambhān matipūrvakam |
ekaviṃśatim ajātiḥ pāpayoniṣu jāyate ||
ayudhyamānasyotpādya brāhmaṇasyānṛśaṃsinaḥ |
duḥkhaṃ sumahad āpnoti pretyāprājñatayā naraḥ ||
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti mahītalāt |
tāvato ‘bdān amutrādyaiḥ śoṇitotpādako ‘dyate ||
na kadācid dvijaṃ tasmād vidvān avagured api |
na tāḍayet tṛṇenāpi na gātrāt srāvayed asṛk ||
tathā [manuḥ 11.206] yamaḥ |
avagūryas tv abdaśataṃ sahasram abhihatya tu |
jighāṃsayā brāhmaṇasya narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
tathā [11.207] |
śoṇitaṃ yāvataḥ pāṃsūn saṃgṛhṇāti dvijanmanaḥ |
[395]        tāvanty (?) abdasahasrāṇi tatkartā narake vaset ||
“ajātiś” ca janmāni | “amutra” paraloke | “adyate” bhakṣyate |
uśanāḥ |
na brāhmaṇasyāpadaṃ kuryāt |
yamaḥ |
vādena brāhmaṇaṃ jitvā harṣaṃ vai yo ‘dhigacchati |
śmaśāne pādapaḥ sasyād gṛdhrakaṅkaniṣevitaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
devā lokās tathā vedā gāvo yajñās tathaiva ca |
sarvasthāneṣu bhūyiṣṭhaṃ brāhmaṇeṣu vyavasthitāḥ ||
pūjitaiḥ pūjitā hy ete dviṣṭair dviṣṭā bhavanty uta |
ninditair ninditā hy ete tasmād vipro ‘dhidaivatam (?) ||
punar hārītaḥ |
samīpastho dahaty agniḥ tejasaivendriyaṃ tanum |
manyunā tad dahed vipraḥ kuśalam āprakṛtiṃ yaśaḥ ||
āsannaṃ pradahaty agniḥ dūrāc ca dahati dvijaḥ |
tasmāt taṃ pūjayen nyāyāt sa hi tīvrataro ‘nalāt ||
[396]        chinnaṃ rohati śastreṇa dagdhaṃ rohati vahninā |
vipramanyupradagdhasya rohaṇaṃ naiva vidyate ||
brāhmaṇān bhojayen nityaṃ brāhmaṇān arcayet sadā |
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasādena sarvataḥ śāntim āpnuyāt ||
yathārṇave naur mahatī prayuktā
sat gārayanty [santārayty?] āśu tato durgāt |
hṛṣṭā dvijā tadvad iha kṣaṇena
santārayeyur mahato ‘pi durgāt ||
“pūjayet” praṇāmādinā \
manuḥ [4.164] |
parasya daṇḍaṃ no yacchet kruddho (?) nainaṃ nipātayet |
anyatra putraśiṣyād vā śiṣṭyarthaṃ tatra pātayet ||
“enaṃ” daṇḍam | “śiṣṭyartham” śikṣārtham | “tatra” putraśiṣyayoḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.155] |
na nindātāḍane kuryāt putraṃ śiṣyaṃ ca tāḍayet |
[manuḥ 4.141] |
hīnāṅgān atiriktāṅgān vidyāhīnān yavo’dhikān |
[397]        rūpadravyavihīnāṃś ca jātihīnāṃśca nākṣipet ||
viṣṇuḥ [71.1–3] |
na ca nāvamanyeta | na hīnāṅgam adhikāṅgaṃ vā | na ca [mūrkhān] dhanahīnān vā upahaset | evaṃ strīṃ nāvamanyet | na hīnān avamanyeta |
manuḥ [4.135–137] |
kṣatriyaṃ caiva sarpaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ ca bahuśrutam |
nāvamanyeta vai dṛṣṭaṃ kṛśān api kadācana ||
etat trayaṃ hi puruṣaṃ nirdahaty avamānitam |
tasmād etat prayatnena nāvamanyeta buddhimān ||
nātmānam avamanyeta pūrvābhir asamṛddhibhiḥ |
ā mṛtyoḥ śriyam anvicchen naitāṃ manyeta durlabhām ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na śiṣṭān asatkuryāt |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.4.25–27] |
dainyaṃ śāṭhyaṃ (?) jaihmyaṃ ca parivarjayet |
[398]        athāpy atrośanasoś ca vṛṣaparvaṇaś ca duhitros saṃvāde gāthām apy udāharanti |
stuvato duhitā tvaṃ vai yācataḥ pratigṛhṇataḥ |
athāhaṃ stūyamānasya dadato ‘pratigṛhṇataḥ ||
devalaḥ |
ātmānaṃ na śape doṣān nīrujo na svaped divā |
na nakhair vilikhed bhūmiṃ gāṃś ca rātrau na saṃviśet ||
na nadīṣu nadīṃ brūyāt parvateṣu ca parvatam |
nānyat praśaṃset tatrasthas tīrtheṣv āyataneṣu ca ||
āvāse bhojane vāpi na tyajet sahayāyinam |
devān nṛpān ṛṣīṃś caiva anusaptapadaṃ vrajet ||
brāhmaṇān nṛpatīn vidvān gāś ca sādhūn śiśūn api |
nākrośen nāvamanyeta pūjayed daivataṃ yathā ||
nāvagāhed apo nagno vahniṃ nātikramet padā |
śiro’bhyaktāvaśiṣṭena tailenāṅgaṃ vilepayet ||
atra na iti anuṣaṅgaḥ |
[399] viṣṇuḥ [71.78] |
na sarpair na śastraiḥ krīḍet \
manuḥ [4.144, 177] |
anāturaḥ svāni khāni na spṛśed animittitaḥ |
romāṇi ca rahasyāni sarvāṇy eva vivarjayet ||
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo ‘nṛjuḥ |
na syād vākcapalaś caiva na paradohakarmadhīḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [6.33, 35, 41, 42] |
nāṅgana[kha]vādanaṃ kuryāt, na cāpo ‘ñjalinā pibet | na mlecchabhāṣāṃ śikṣeta | athāpy udāharanti |
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo bhavet |
na ca vākaṅgacapala it śiṣṭasya gocaraḥ ||
gautamaḥ [19–49–51] |
na padā manasā karṣet | na śiśnodarapāṇipādacapalaṃ [400] kuryāt | chedanabhedanavilikhanavimardanāṅgasphoṭanāni nākasmāt kuryāt |
“avasphoṭanam” hastādyaṅgasandhisphoṭanam |
manuḥ [4.63–64] |
na kurvīta vṛthāceṣṭāṃ na vāry añjalinā pibet |
notsaṅge bhakṣayed bhakṣyaṃ na jātu syāt kutūhalī ||
na nṛtyed atha vā gāyen na vāditrāṇi vādayet |
na cāspoṭen na vā kṣveḍen na saṃrakto virāvayet ||
“āsphoṭanam” pāṇinā bāhumūlābhighātanam | “kṣveḍanam” siṃhanādaḥ | “saṃrakto” harṣaṇaḥ | “virāvo” vikṛtaśabdaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na bhūmiṃ vilikhen na padābhihanyāt |
yamaḥ |
na gāyen na ca nṛtyeta nākṣaiḥ krīḍet kadācana |
na vādayet vāditraṃ na (ca) kṣveḍed kadācana ||
[401]        nāsphoṭayen na dhāvet tu nātisāhasam ācaret |
hārītaḥ |
na pathi mūtrapurīṣaṃ śilāṃ cotsṛjet | vrīhiniṣkṛṣaṇaloṣṭamardanalekhālikhanaṃ karajadaśanavīṇāvādanāni varjayet | dainyam ātmanaḥ | tathā mālāvalepapramādakaraṇavividhāṃ ceṣṭāṃś ca madyaśuklavikārāṃś ca varjayet |
“niṣkṛṣaṇam” nakhair vituṣīkaraṇam | “avalopo” garvaḥ |
yamaḥ |
naikavastreṇa bhoktavyaṃ na nagnaḥ sthānam arhati |
suptavyaṃ naiva nagnena ucchiṣṭaṃ tu na saṃviśet ||
na gacchen na paṭhec caiva na spṛśec ca tathā śiraḥ |
na dadhisumanasāṃ pratyācakṣīta nātmanaḥ ||
srajaṃ ca nāpakarṣet suptaṃ na pratibodhayet ||
manuḥ [4.69–71] |
bālātapaḥ pretadhūmo varjyaṃ bhinnaṃ tathāsanam |
[402]        na chindyān nakharomāṇi dantair notpāṭayen nakhān ||
na mṛlloṣṭhaṃ vigṛhṇīyān na chindyāt karajaiḥ tṛṇam |
na niṣphalaṃ karma kuryān nāyatyām (?) āmasukhodayam ||
loṣṭabhakṣī tṛṇacchedī nakhakhādī ca yo naraḥ |
sa vināśaṃ vrajaty āśu sūcako śucir eva ca ||
varjayed ity anuvṛttau āpastambaḥ [1.32.28] |
tṛṇacchedanaloṣṭavimardananiṣṭīvanāni cākāraṇād yac cānyat paricakṣate |
“akāraṇāt” prayojanaṃ vinā, yadṛcchayā ity arthaḥ | “paricakṣate” varjayanti śiṣṭā iti śeṣaḥ |
punar āpastambaḥ [2.12.12] |
pṣṭhataś cātmanaḥ pāṇī na saṃśleṣayet |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.138] |
nākṣaiḥ krīḍan na dharmaghnair vyādhitaiś ca na saṃviśet |
viruddhaṃ varjayet karma pretadhūmaṃ nadītaram ||
[403] “nadītaram” nadītaraṇam, bāhubhyāṃ varjayed ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ [4.74] |
nākṣair krīḍet kadācit tu svayaṃ nopānahau haret |
tathā [4.57] |
naikaḥ supyāc chūnyagṛhe śayānaṃ na prabodhayet |
nodakyābhibhāṣeta yajñaṃ gacchen na cāvṛtaḥ ||
āpastambaḥ [2.20.12–17] |
padā pādasya prakṣālanam adhiṣṭhānaṃ ca varjayet | preṅkholanaṃ ca pādayoḥ jānuni cātyādhānaṃ jaṅghāyāḥ nakaiś ca nakhavādanaḥ | sphoṭanāni cākāraṇāt | yac cātnyat paricakṣate |
“preṅkholanam” itas tataś cālanam | “atyādhānam” āropaṇam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
[na pādau pādena prakṣālayet | na darbhaiḥ parimṛjyāt |
[404] viṣṇuḥ [71.37–39] |
na pādau pratāpayet | na kuśeṣu parimṛjyāt | nākāṃsyabhājane pādau dhāvayet |
yamaḥ |
na kāṃsye śaucayet pādau yatra syād api bhojanam | ]
gautamaḥ [9.12–13] |
(na?) vāyvagniviprādityo devatā gāś ca prati paśyan vā mūtrapurīṣāmedhyān vyudasyet | naitā devatāḥ prati pādau prasārayet |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.25] |
agnim ādityam āpo brāhmaṇaṃ gā devatādvāraṃ prati pādaṃ ca śaktiviṣaye nābhiprasārayet |
pādau iti śeṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
na devagurugobrāhmaṇasamīpe pādau prasārayet |
devalaḥ |
aśuddhaḥ śayanaṃ snānaṃ svādhyāyaṃ yānavāhanam |
bahirniṣkramaṇaṃ (?) caiva na kurvīta kathaṃcana ||
svapnam adhyayanaṃ snānam adhvānaṃ bhojanaṃ gatim |
ubhayoḥ saṃdhyayoḥ nityaṃ madhyāhne ca vivarjayet ||
[405] yājñavalkyaḥ [1.155] |
gobrāhmaṇānalānnāni nocchiṣṭo na padā spṛśet |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nāgniṃ gāṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ca pādenādhitiṣṭhet |
āpastambaḥ [1.31.4–7] |
varjayed devatābhidhānaṃ cāprayataḥ | paruṣaṃ cobhayor devatānāṃ rājñāś ca | brāhmaṇasya ca gaur iti padopasparśanaṃ varjayet | hastena cākāraṇāt |
devatānām iti jātyākhyāyāṃ bahuvacanam | “ubhayor” devatārājñoḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nāśucir agniṃ paricaret | na ṛṣipitṛdevatāḥ kīrtayet | na gādhodakam avagāhet | nāpo ‘ñjalinā pibet | nāpo ‘gniṃ caikato dhārayet |
“pitaro” agniṣṭāttādayaḥ | “devatā” viṣṇvādayaḥ | “ekataḥ” ekasmin hastādau |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
vidyamānena hastena brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
toyaṃ pibati vaktreṇa śvāsau jāyeta nānyathā ||
[406]        uddhṛtya vāmahastena yat toyaṃ pibati dvijaḥ |
surāpānena tat tulyaṃ manur āha prajāpatiḥ ||
abhojya ity anuvṛttau vasiṣṭhaḥ [14.36] |
antarnāvy udakam |
nausthyaṃ jalaṃ na peyam ity arthaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2.12.9–10] |
agnim apaś ca na yugapad dhārayīta | nānāgnīnāṃ ca saṃnivāpaṃ varjayet |
“saṃnivāpaḥ” saṃsargaḥ |
gautamaḥ [9.9–11]
nāpo ‘medhyena saṃsṛjet | nāñjalinā pibet | nātiṣṭhet |
hārītaḥ |
na nagno jalam upetyāt | na niṣṭhīvet | nottared anupaspṛśya nāpaḥ pibed añjalinā | nānuddhṛtābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kuryāt | nāpsu retomūtrapurīṣāṇi cotsṛjet |
manuḥ [4.56] |
nāpsu mūtrapurīṣaṃ vā ṣṭīvanaṃ vā samutsṛjet |
amedhyaliptam anyad vā lohitaṃ vā viṣāṇi vā ||
[407] āpastambaḥ [1.30.22] |
ṣṭīvanamaithunakarmāṇy apsu varjayet |
yamaḥ |
vyatikrāmen na (?) sravantīṃ jvalantījvalanaṃ tathā |
caityavṛkṣaṃ na vai chindyān nāśuṣkeṣmāṇam utsṛjet ||
na śukraṃ na viṣaṃ na mūtraṃ na lohitam |
nāsthibhasmakapālaṃ ca na keśān na vinnakhān ||
manuḥ [4.53–54] |
nāgniṃ mukhenopadhamen nagnāṃ nekṣeta ca striyam |
nāmedhyaṃ prakṣiped agnau na ca pādau pratāpayet ||
adhastān nopadadhyāc ca na caivam avalaṅghayet |
na cainaṃ vādataḥ kuryān na prāṇābādham ācaret ||
āpastambaḥ [1.15.18–21] |
agniṃ cāprayata āsīdet | iṣumātrād ity anye | na cainam upadhamet | khaṭvāyāṃ ca nopadadhyāt |
[408] iṣumātrādiṣu parimitadeśād upadhametya saprāyata (?) ity anuṣaṅgaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
nāpuṣpitaṃ vṛkṣam ārohet |
devalaḥ |
nāvekṣetāśuciḥ pūrvaṃ gharṣayen na padaṃ padā |
nagnau (?) na prakṣiped agnau adbhiḥ praśamayet tathā ||
prāṇopaghātam anyeṣāṃ na śuddhādibhir ācaret |
suhṛnmaraṇārtiṃ vānasūyaṃ (?) śrāvayet parān ||
abhiyogeṇa mitrāṇi bāliśyāc caiva pīḍayet |
dākṣiṇyād dhi daridro ‘pi sahate mitrapīḍanam ||
prāṇino hiṃsitavyāś ca kliṣṭaghātaṃ na ghātayet |
apaṇyaṃ kūṣṭapaṇyaṃ (?) vā vikraye na prayojayet ||
na ca vahniṃ viniśvāsair jvālayed dharmatattvavit |
navād vṛthā (?) śastraṃ gogurustrīkumārake ||
[409]        puṇyasthānodakasthāne sīmāntaṃ vā kṛṣen na ca |
na bhindyāt samayaṃ pūrvam abhyupetaṃ kadācana ||
aviśvāso hi pāpiṣṭho bhavet samayabhedanāt |
nikṣepopanidhī prāptau tathaiva punar arpayet ||
datvāpanayanaṃ cāryair narakadvāram ucyate |
yaḥ pāpiṣṭho naraḥ kuryāt tiryagyoniṣu maithunam ||
viyonigamanād bhūyaḥ sa viyoniṣu jāyate |
parasparaṃ paśūna bālān pakṣi[ṇor] vā na yodhayet ||
parabādhān na kurvīta jalavātāyanādibhiḥ |
kārayitvā svakarmāṇi kārūn paścān na vañcayet ||
karmānurūpam eteṣāṃ prayacched bhaktavetanam |
prativeśmānuveśmābhyām anyatra na nimantrayet ||
pradīptaṃ gṛham āsanna adbhir yāyāt tvarānvitaḥ |
sāyaṃ prātar gṛhadvāraṃ bhikṣārthaṃ nāvaghaṭṭayet ||
na ciraṃ vāsayet kanyāṃ svāmī prāptaphalāṃ gṛhe ||
“kliṣṭāghātam” bahukleśapūrvakaṃ vṛthāmohārtham iti | “nikṣepam” prakāśam arpitaṃ dravyam | “upanidhiḥ” vastrādipihitam anākhyāta[410]svarūpaṃ samarpitam | “jalāyanam” prāṇālikādi | “vātāyanam”gavākṣaḥ | “kārūn” karmakārān | “prativeśmānuveśmābhyām” iti samīpagṛhopalakṣaṇam | “bhikṣārtham” bhikṣānivṛttyartham | “nāvaghaṭṭayet” na saṃvṛṇuyāt | svāmy atra pitrādiḥ | “prāptaphalām” ratiputraphalayogyām |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
na khādaṃs tiṣṭhet na hasan jalpeta | nāgniṃ laṅghayet | [na mukhenopadhamet] | samṛddham agniṃ hastābhyāṃ na spṛśet | nodake ciraṃ snāyāt |
uśanāḥ |
pūranadīvṛkṣanikuṭacatuṣpathaparastrīṇāṃ pratāraṇāvagāhanātirohaṇapraśleṣana-saṃbhāṣaṇāni varjayet |
“pūraḥ” pravṛddhaḥ jalapravāhaḥ | “nikuṭaḥ” koṭaraḥ | pūranadyoḥ prataraṇāvagāhane | nikuṭacatuṣpatheṣu “atirohaṇam” atyantākramaṇam | “parastrīṇām” āliṅginasaṃbhāṣaṇe | etāni vartajet |
[411] āpastambaḥ [1.30.14] |
divā ca śirasaḥ prāvaraṇaṃ varjayet | mūtrapurīṣayoḥ parihāpya |
gautamaḥ [9.36–37] |
na prāvṛtya śiro ‘hani paryaṭet | prāvṛtya rātrau | tathā na vastreṇa vījayed iti |
