31
atha paradārādivarjanam
tatra manuḥ [4.134] |
na hīdṛśam anāyuṣyaṃ loke kiṃcana vidyate |
yādṛśaṃ puruṣaseha paradāropasevanam ||
vyāsaḥ |
svakarmanirato nityaṃ svadāranirato bhavet |
sevanāt parakīyānāṃ narako dārakarmaṇām ||
uśanāḥ |
nānyapatnīm abhigacchet |
yamaḥ |
paradārān na seveta |
devalaḥ |
agamyāgamanam apacāraḥ | parabhāryāt tu varṇottamā svasā duhitā sagotrā caṇḍālī patitā pravrajitā tiryagyonyaś cāgamyāḥ |
[413]                gamyānām api kanyānāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ pāpam ucyate |
punas tāṃ yadi gṛhṇāti gandharvaḥ samayo bhavet ||
“gamyānām” vivāhārhāṇām | “dūṣaṇam” samāliṅganacumbanādi |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1.160] |
paraśayyāsanodyānagṛhayānāni varjayet |
adattāny agnihīnasya na cāśnīyād anāpadi ||
“adattāni” ananujñātāni |
manuḥ [4.202] |
yānaśayyāsanāny asya kūpodyānagṛhāṇi ca |
adattāny upabhuñjāna enasaḥ syāt turīyabhāk ||
iti paradārādivarjanam
[414]
32
atha saṃkaravarjanam
bṛhaspatiḥ [āpaddharma, 52] |
viprayoniṃ samāsādya saṃkaraṃ parivarjayet |
mānuṣyam adhikaṃ loke brāhmaṇyam adhikaṃ tataḥ ||
[prāyaś. 24–25]
ekaśayyāsanaṃ paṅktir bhāṇḍapakvānnamiśraṇam |
yājanādhyāpane yonis tathā ca sahabhojanam ||
navadhā saṃkaraḥ prokto na kartavyo ‘dhamaiḥ saha |
uttamair uttamaiḥ sārdhaṃ kartavyas tu samena vā ||
“yonir” atra yaunaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | “sahabhojanam” ekapātrabhojanam |
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇyaṃ parirakṣeta nānāyoniniṣevaṇāt |
duṣṭebhyo vyavahārāc ca tathā duṣṭapraigrahāt ||
[415]        aśubhebhyaś ca karmabhyaḥ saṃsargād aśubhaiḥ saha |
saṃsargagāmino hy ete hetavaḥ sadbhir īritāḥ ||
saṃbandhād gātrasaṃsparśāc chayanāt sahabhojanāt |
saṃlāpāc cāpi saṃvāsāt pāpaṃ saṃkramate nṛṇām ||
tathā |
bhāṇḍasaṃsargasaṃkīrṇā yonisaṃkaradūṣitāḥ |
annasaṃkarasaṃkīrṇā viyoniṃ yānti vai dvijāḥ ||
tathā |
durlabhā brahmayonis tu saṃkareṇa tu nānyathā |
brahmabhāvo na sulabhas tasmād budhyeta brāhmaṇaḥ ||
“duṣṭāḥ” patitādayaḥ | “vyavahāraḥ” parasparagṛhagamanādi | “aśubhebhyaś” caurādibhyaḥ | “aśubhaiḥ” apavitraiḥ nīlaraktādibhiḥ | “saṃsargagāminaḥ” saṃsargeṇa caramāṇasya pāpasya vekṣyamāṇāḥ | “saṃbandho” yaunādi |
hārītaḥ |
ālāpād gātrasaṃsparśān niśvāsād api caiva tu |
ekayānāsanābhyāṃ tu pāpaṃ saṃkramate nṛṇām ||
hanyād aśuddhaś śuddhaṃ tu śuddho ‘śuddhaṃ tu śodhayet |
[416]        aśuddhas tu tamobhūtaḥ śuddhavāsena śudhyati ||
tayor yady atra bhūyitṣṭhaṃ śuddhyaśuddhī tataḥ smṛte ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2.5.9] |
gurusaṃkariṇaś caiva śiṣyasaṃkariṇaś ca ye |
āhāramātrasaṃkīrṇā dīrghaṃ tama upāsate ||
hārītaḥ |
pānaśayanāsanāny aparihāryāny eke manyante | tatra varṇaviśeṣāt, śuklamalinasaṃsargadarśanāt, pāpasaṃsargād vyādhisaṃkramaṇayogāc ca | tasmāt prathak śaucāḥ śreyāṃsa iti | svānupapattau śuddhyantardhāya samāmananti | atyantsaṃsparśe sacelasnānam | evaṃ hy āha –
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ pānam antardhāya samācaret |
brahmasūtropānadvastraṃ dhṛtam anyair na dhārayet ||
na caikabhojanaṃ prāhur brāhmaṇānāṃ svakair api |
prasaṅgāt karmadoṣāc ca prthak śaucās tathāparāḥ ||
vastuviśeṣāt śuklamalinayoḥ | saṃsargeṇa varṇaviśeṣo dṛṣṭaḥ | “svānupapattau” svakīyādyāsanāsaṃbhave | “śuddhyantar[417]dhāya” śuddhivastrādikam | “brahmasūtram” yajñopavītam | “anyadhṛtam” sarvathaiva na dhārayet | “upānadvastre” tu kṛtanirṇejane upari dhārye | “nirṇiyāśaktau” iti gautamavacanāt | “ekabhojanam” ekasmin pātre paṅktau vā | “svakair" jñātibhiḥ | “prasaṅgāt” anyena pāpakarmaṇāpi saha | kadācid uparodhādibhojanaprasakteḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [ācāra 93, 96] |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭā ye na spṛśanti parasparam |
bhasmanā kṛtamaryādā na teṣāṃ saṃkaro bhavet ||
agninā bhasmanā caiva salilena viśeṣataḥ |
dvāreṇa staṃbhamārgeṇa ṣaḍbhiḥ paṅktir vibhidyate ||
iti saṃkaravarjanam |
[418]
33
atha tyājyātyājyāḥ
atra śaṅkhalikhitau |
atyājyā mātā pitā sapiṇḍās tathā guṇavantaḥ | sarve vātyājyāḥ |
“guṇavantaḥ” vidyākarmaśālinaḥ |
devalaḥ |
pitaraṃ bhrātaraṃ putraṃ putrikāṃ bhaginīṃ snuṣām |
na tyajed dveṣalobhābhyāṃ dārān apatitān api ||
mātṛtyāgo hi śāstreṣu nopadiṣṭaḥ kathaṃcana |
patitā vābhiśastā vā mātā na tyāgam arhati ||
kulavṛddhā jñānavṛddhā vayovṛddhāś ca mānavāḥ |
śīlavṛddhāś ca saṃpūjyā tathā svaguṇagauravāt ||
“apatitān” iti sarvatra viśeṣaṇam |
[419] hārītaḥ |
pitā tu janako martyaiś chandaḥ svācārya ucyate |
heveṣṭvonaṃ janayati tv agnīṃ nadhvaryur vā dadhāt (?) ||
tasmād etān sadā vṛddhān pūjayen na parityajet |
parityāgāt tathaiteṣām iṣṭāṃl (?) lokān na vindati ||
aśuddhau tu parityāgaḥ pātake ‘yājyayājine |
upādhyāye ca yājye ca na pitus tyāga ucyate ||
parityajanti ye rāgād upādhyāyaṃ kathaṃcana |
avamānayanti ye mohān na teṣām niṣkṛtiḥ smṛtā ||
“chandaḥsu” svavedeśu | aśucir atra pātakāyājyayājanarūpā | “upādhyāye ca yājye ca” anantroktāśuddhau na parityāga iti saṃbandhaḥ | na pitus tyāgaḥ pūrvoktāśuddhu |
viṣṇuḥ |
bhāryā śiṣyaś ca putraś ca saṃsṛṣṭāḥ pāpakarmabhiḥ |
paribhāṣya parityājyā patito yo ‘nyathā bhavet ||
[420] ṛtvigācāryān yājakādhyāpakau heyāv anyatra hānāt patitaṃ bhāṣya vibhāṣya viravyāpyaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.21.5–6] |
na patitaiḥ saṃvyavahāro vidyate | tathāpapātraiḥ |
“apapātraiḥ” śavarādibhiḥ |
gautamaḥ [21.12–16] |
ajñānādhyāpanād ṛtvigācāryau patanīyasevāyāṃ ca heyau | anyatra hānāt patati | tasya ca parigṛhītety eke | na kadācana mātā pitror avṛttiḥ | dāyaṃ tu na bhajeran |
“ajñānādhyāpanād” iti samāhāradvaṃdvam | kṛtvā paścāt nañ saṃbandhanīyaḥ | “ajñānāt” karmaviśeṣe āvirbhāvyājñānāt tasya akaraṇaṃ ṛtvigācāryau tyajato ‘nyaḥ svīkartā so ‘pi patati | “avṛttiḥ” anukūlavṛttir aśuśrūṣeti yāvat | “dāyaṃ tu na bhajeran” putrā iti śeṣaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [1.28.6–10] |
na patitam ācāryaṃ jñātiṃ vā darśanārtho gacchet | na cāsmād bhogān upayuñjīta | yadṛcchāsaṃnipāte upasaṃgṛhya [421] tūṣṇīṃ [vyati]vrajet | mātā putra[tva]sya bhūyāṃsi karmāṇi prārabheta, tasyāṃ śuśrūṣā nityā patitāyām api | na tu dharmasaṃnipātaḥ syāt |
“darśanārtham” darśanam artho yasyeti sa tathoktaḥ | “putratvasya” āhitagarbhasya putratvasiddhyarthaṃ garbhasaṃnipātaḥ | “vāpaḥ” (?) dharmakṛtye śrāddhādau saṃmilanam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [13.47] |
patitaḥ pitā na parityājyo mātā tu putraṃ prati na patati |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.3.42–43] |
na patitaiḥ saṃvyavahāro vidyate patitām api tu mātaraṃ bibhṛyād anabhibhāṣamāṇaḥ |
gautamaḥ [20.1–7] |
tyajet pitaraṃ rājaghātakaṃ śūdrānuyājakaṃ vedaviplāvakaṃ bhrūṇahanaṃ yaś cāntyāvasāyibhiḥ saha saṃvaset antyāvasāyināṃ [422] vā | tasya vidyāgurūn yonisaṃbandhāṃś ca saṃnipātya sarvāṇy udakāni pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ | pātraṃ cāsya viparyasyeyuḥ | dāsaḥ karmakaro vāvakarād amedhyapātram ānīya dāsīghaṭāt pūrayitvā dakṣiṇābhimukhaḥ padā viparyasyedam anudakaṃ karomīti nāmagrāham | te sarve ‘py ārabheta prācīnāvītīno muktaśikhāḥ | vidyāguravo yonisaṃbandhāś ca vīkṣeran apa upaspṛśya grāmaṃ praviśanti |
“śūdrārthaṃ yājakaḥ” śūdrād artham adhigamya yo yajeta | “vedaviplaāvakāḥ” anadhyāyādhyāpakāḥ | “antyāvasāyibhiḥ” caṇḍālādibhiḥ | “antyāvasāyino saṃvāso” maithunādikaḥ | “tasya” tyājyasya jīvata eva “pretakarmāṇi kuryuḥ” | putrādayaḥ iti śeṣaḥ | “saṃnipātya” melayitvā | “udakādīni” ādiśabdena pretaśrāddhopapādānam | “viparyāso” nyubjīkaraṇam | "avakaraḥ" mārjanīnirastadhūlyādisaṃcayaḥ | taddāsaṃ karmakaraṃ vā | sarve pūvoktāḥ putrādayaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
patitābhiśastaduṣṭātmakulakṣayakarasya vā tasya guror bāndhavānāṃ rājñaś ca samakṣaṃ doṣam anuravyāpya (?) tam abhibhāṣya punaḥ punar avasthāṃ labhasveti | sa yady evam avasthitamatiḥ syāt | [423] tato bhinnapātram ādāya pūrṇam apāṃ sūnām apasavyaṃ kṛtvā vāmapādena pātradakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ apavarjayet | pātram apasavyaṃ kṛtvā apaviddhodakaṃ datvā laukikaṃ śaucaṃ āsīran | apapātritasya rikthapiṇḍodakāni vyāvartante | saṃbhāṣaṇasahāsanavyavahārān na tena saha kuryāt |
“abhiśastaḥ” annākṛtapātako ‘pi pātakitvena prasiddhaḥ | “satatam” pāpakarmābhisaṃdhir duṣṭātmā | “kulakṣayakaraḥ” vihitaṛtukālagamanavarjanādinā | “avasthā” śuddhyavasthā | “avasthitamatiḥ” bāndhavoktaśuddhāvasthāyām akṛtamatiḥ | “apaviddhodakam” tyāgodakam | “laukikaṃ śaucam” snānācamanādi |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [15.12–16] |
vedaviplāvakaśūdrayājakottamavarṇavargapatitāḥ teṣām | pātraniyamanaprapātra-saṃkarād akṛtsnaṃ pātram ādāya dāso ‘savarṇāputro vā bandhur asadṛśo vā guṇahīnaḥ savyena pādena pravṛttāgrān lohitān [darbhān] copastīrya pūrṇapātram asmai [ninayet |] ninetāraṃ cāsya prakīrṇakeśān jñātayo ‘nvālabheran apasarvyaṃ [kṛtvā] gṛheṣu svairam āpādyeran | ata ūrdhvaṃ tena na taṃ dharmayeyuḥ | taddharmāṇo taṃ dharmayantaḥ \
“uttamavarṇavargaḥ” brāhmaṇasamūhaḥ | “akṛtsnam” khaṇḍam | “patitam” niḥsāritaḥ | “bandhur asadṛśaḥ” asavarṇo bhrātrādiḥ | “vyaktāgrāḥ” sāgrāḥ | “lohitān” gaurikādīn | [424] “apūrṇam” riktaṃ yathā bhavati | “svairam” mandam | “āpadyeran” avasannā bhaveyuḥ | “na dharmayeyuḥ” na saṃvyavahārayeyuḥ |
manuḥ [11.182–185] |
patitasyodakaṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍair bāndhavair bahiḥ |
nindite ‘hani sāyāhne jñātyṛtvikgurusaṃnidhau ||
dāsīghaṭam apāṃ pūrṇaṃ paryasyet pretavat padā |
ahorātram upāsīran nāśaucaṃ bāndhavaiḥ saha ||
nivarteraṃś ca tasmāt tu saṃbhāṣaṇasahāsane |
dadyād yasya pradānaṃ ca pātram eva ca laukikī ||
jyeṣṭhatā ca nivarteta jyeṣṭhāvāpyaṃ ca yad dhanam |
jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ prāpnuyāc cāsya yavīyān guṇato ‘dhikaḥ ||
manur vasiṣṭhaś caturthe [Manu 11.186–187, 189] |
prāyaścitte tu carite pūrṇakumbham apāṃ navam |
tenaiva sārdhaṃ prāśyaś ca snātvā puṇye jalāśaye ||
sa tv apsu taṃ ghaṭaṃ prāsya praviśya bhavanaṃ svakam |
sarvāṇi pretakarmāṇi yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret ||
evam eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsv apatitāsv api |
vastrānnam āsāṃ deyaṃ tu vaseyuś ca gṛhāntike ||
enasvibhir anirṇikaiḥ (?) nārthaṃ kiṃcit sahācaret |
kṛtanirṇejatāṃs tv etān na jugupseta karhicit ||
[425] atrāpatitādhikāre śaṅkhalikhitau |
sa cet pretyāpannaḥ śuddhim anvicchet tam eva janam āhūya rājānam abhibhāṣya guṇānukīrtanaṃ kṛtvā, śauddhikaṃ dadhimadhughṛtajalākṣatānāṃ navaṃ pātraṃ pūrayitvā, gandhamālyopanītaṃ kṛtvā, triḥ pradakṣiṇīkuryuḥ | bāndhavāḥ yoṣitaś ca tato mahāvyāhṛtihomānte tat pātram apāṃ madhye pravāhayeran | “śāntāny aśubhāni durbhāṣitāni” iti bruvantaḥ | tataḥ snātvā śuciḥ śuklavāsāḥ punargṛhaṃ praviśed bāndhavānumataḥ | strīṇām apy evaṃ parityāgo, duṣṭānāṃ punar āvāhanaṃ ca |
“āvāhanam” bāndhavaiḥ saha prayogaḥ | “pratyāpannaḥ” punaḥ śuddhatāṃ prāptaḥ | “śuddhim anvicchet” śuddhyanurūpaḥ saṃvyavahāras tam icchate | “śauddhikaṃ” śuddhiprayojakam |
kvacit prāyaścittasya āgamanādy abhidhāya yājñavalkyaḥ [3.211] |
ghaṭe ‘pavarjite jñātimadhyastho yavasaṃ gavām |
pradadyāt prathamaṃ gobhiḥ satkṛtasya hi satkriyā ||
“satkāraḥ” satpūrvadyāsadānam (?) |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [15.17]
patitānāṃ caritavratānāṃ pratyuddhāraḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
agre ‘bhyuddharatāṃ gacchet krīḍann iva hasann iva |
paścāt pātayitāṃ (?) gacchec chocann iva rudann iva || iti |
[426]        ācāryamātṛpitṛhantāras tatpramādād apavācyād vā eṣāṃ pratyapattiḥ | pūrṇād
vratāt pavitrād vā kāñcanaṃ pātraṃ māheyenāpi pūrayitvāpohiṣṭābhir enam abhiṣiñceyuḥ | sarva evābhiṣiktasya pratyuddhāraḥ putrajanmanā vyākhyātaḥ |
“hantāraḥ” atrāpi pīḍitāraḥ | “apavācyāḥ” apagatapāpāḥ | “pratyāpattiḥ” pratyāyanam | “pravṛttam” nadījalāt “sravantībhyo vā” [GDh 20.10] iti gautamavacanāt | “pātraṃ māheyam” mṛṇmayam | “putrajanmanā vyākhyātaḥ” ity anena jātakarmādisaṃskārakarmādi kuryād ity uktam |
gautamaḥ [20.10–17] |
yaḥ prāyaścittena śudhyet tasmin śuddhe śatakumbhamayaṃ pātraṃ puṇyatamāḍ dhradāt pūrayitvā sravantībhyo vā tata enaṃ vā apa upaspṛśeyuḥ | athāsmai tat pātraṃ dadyuḥ tat pratigṛhya japet “śantā dyauḥ śāntā pṛthivī śāntaṃ śivam antarikṣaṃ ye rocanas tam iha gṛhṇāmi” iti etair yajurbhiḥ vāvamānībhiḥ taratsamandībhiḥ kūṣmāṇḍaiś cājyaṃ juhuyāt | hiraṇyaṃ vā brāhmaṇāya dadyāt | gāṃ vā | ācāryāya ca | yasya tu prāṇāntikaṃ prāyaścittaṃ sa mrtaḥ śudhyet | sarvāṇy eva tasminn udakāni pretakāryāṇi kuryuḥ | etad eva śāntyudakaṃ sarveṣu pātakeṣu japet |
[427] śāntetyādīni gṛhṇāmītyantāni | “yajūṃṣi” śrutair uktiar yajubhiḥ | “pāvamānībhiḥ” taratsamandībhiḥ | “apa upaspṛśeyur” ity atra pūrveṇānvayaḥ | brāhmaṇāya gohiraṇyayoḥ vaikalpikyaṃ dānam | ācārye ‘pi tathā |
baudhāyanaḥ [2.1.36–37] |
atha yasya jñātayaḥ pariṣady udapātraṃ ninayeyur asāv aham (?) evaṃbhūta iti | “caritvāpaḥ payo ghṛtaṃ madhulavaṇam” ity ārabdhavantam brāhmaṇā brūyuḥ “caritaṃ tvayā” iti | om ity aparaḥ pratyāha | caritanirveśaṃ savanīyaṃ kuryuḥ |
“caritanirveśam” kṛtaprāyaścittam | “savanam” prastarastatsaṃbandhijātakarmādisavanīyam |
viṣṇuḥ [54.32] |
bālaghnāṃś ca kṛtaghnāṃś ca viśuddhān api dharmataḥ |
śaraṇāgatahantṝṃś ca strīhantṝṃś ca na samviśet ||
taiḥ saṃbhūyodakapānādi na kuryāt |
hārītaḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāpi vaiśyaṃ śūdram athāpi vā |
vṛttahīnaṃ durācāraṃ dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||
svapadasthaṃ suvṛttasthaṃ svakarmaparidevinam |
śūdraṃ puṇyatamaṃ prāhur nānye vṛttacyutāṃś trayaḥ ||
[428]        vrttam eva praśaṃsanti sarvāraṃbheṣu (?) paṇḍitāḥ |
vṛttaiḥ sthitāḥ parāṃl lokān nācchantī hy amarā iva || iti |
“svapadasthaḥ” dvijaśuśrūṣakaḥ | “svavṛttasthaḥ” śrāddhādhikārī | “svakarmaparidevī” dvijaśuśrūṣāttadhanaḥ |
yamaḥ |
ya imāṃ dharmamaryādām ṛṣibhiḥ sthāpitāṃ purā |
yathā ca vindate vipraḥ sa pretyeha ca nandati ||
śiṣṭācaritamārgo ‘yam aśiṣṭānām ato ‘nyathā |
śiṣṭās tato divaṃ yānti yānty aśiṣṭās tv adhogatim ||
gautamaḥ [9.37]
manasā caiva [tatsamagram] ācāram anupālayed āpatkalpaḥ |
sāṅgam anuṣṭhānāśaktau manasā dhyānenāpi sāṅganiṣpādayed ity arthaḥ |
iti tyājyātyājyāḥ |
[429]
34
atha ṛṇāpākaraṇam
tatra baudhāyanaḥ [2.16.1–5] |
prajākāmasyopadeśaḥ | prajananimittā samākhyety aśvināv ūcatuḥ |
āyuṣā tapasā yuktaḥ svādhyāyjyāparāyaṇaḥ |
prajām utpādayed yuktaḥ sve sve varṇe jitendriyaḥ ||
brāhmaṇasyarṇasaṃyogas tribhir bhavati janmataḥ |
unmucyātmavā bhavati vimukto dharmasaṃśayāt ||
svādhyāyena ṛṣīn pūjya somena ca purandaram |
prajayā ca pitṝn pūrvān anṛṇo divi modate ||
punar baudhāyanaḥ [2.16.7–14] |
vijñāyate ca – “jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇas tribhir ṛṇair ṛṇavān jāyate, brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhyo, yajñena devebhyaḥ, prajayā pitṛbhya iti | evam ṛṇasaṃyogaṃ vedo darśayati |
bandham ṛṇaṃ tasya mokṣaḥ | prajāyattaṃ phalānukarṣam, pitṝṇāṃ cānukarṣaśabdāṃś ca, prajāvān anutsannaḥ |
[430]         “prajāvān bhavati yāvad evaṃ prajānugṛhīte tāvad akṣayaṃ lokaṃ jayati” iti |
satputram utpādyātmānaṃ tārayati |
saptāvarān sapta pūrvān ṣaḍ anyān ātmasaptamān |
satputram adhigacchaṃs tu tārayaty enaso bhayāt ||
yasmāt prajāsantānam utpādya phalam āpnoti | tasmād yatnavān prajām utpādayet | [auṣadhamantrasaṃyogena] | [tasyopadeśaḥ śrutisāmānyenopadiśyate | sarvavarnebhyaḥ phalatvād iti ] |
ātmanaḥ phalalābhāya tasmāt sa putram utpādyātmānam evotpādayatīti vijñāyate “ātmā vai putranāmāsīti” | evaṃ dvitīya ātmā jīvatād draṣṭavyo | “yaḥ putram utpādayati tasmān nātmā kvacit akṣetre samutsraṣṭavyaḥ | ātmānam any”ayo (?) hi yady ātmānam utpādayati sa tathā bhavati” | tasmād ādita evam anvicchet | savarṇe saṃkṛtam upadeśena tasmin dārasaṃyoge prajām utpādayet “auṣadhamantrasaṃyogena” tasyopadeśaśrutisāmānyenopadiśyate sarvavarṇebhyaḥ | “phalatvāt” iti prajākāmasyopadeśaḥ | kariṣyata iti śeṣaḥ | “prajananam” prajotpādanam | “samākhyā” kulānukīrtanam | “āyuṣā” āyuṣyeṇa | “dīrgha” saṃdhyopāsanavarjanādikarmaṇā | "tapasā" niyamena | "ijyā" yāgaḥ | "yuktaḥ" yatnavān | "jitendriyaḥ" paradāraniṣiddhakālamaithunavarjī | "ātmavān" praśastātmā | "dharmasaṃśrayāt" putrotpādanādirūpadharmāśrayāt | brahmacaryādīnām ṛṇāpanayanavad avaśyakartavyatvād ṛṇasyoktiḥ | “phalānukarṣam” prajādhīnasvargādiphalānusmṛtiṃ pitṝṇāṃ cānukarmarṣaḥ (?) putrāi[431]rūpeṇānuvṛttiḥ | “śabdāṃś ca” pitrādīnām anukīrtanādi prajāyām āyattam | “vedo darśayati” iti pratyekaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | “anutsannaḥ” mṛto ‘pi prajārūpeṇa vidyamāna eva bhavati | tathā ca taittirīyake [Brāhmaṇa 1.5.5.6]: “prajām anu prajāyase tad u te martyāmrtam” iti | “akṣetre” paradārādau | “savarṇe” samānajātīye | “upadeśena” garbhādhānādinā | tasya putrotpādanasya “upadeśaśrutiḥ” | “auṣadhasaṃyogena” sāmānyena sādhāraṇyena savarṇebhyaḥ śatapathabrāhmaṇa upadiśyate paṭhyata ity arthaḥ | “phalavatvādinā” iti | auṣadhamantrayor iti śeṣaḥ |
viṣṇu-vasiṣṭhau [Vi 15.45; Vas 16.2] |
ṛṇam asmin saṃnayati amṛtatvaṃ ca vindati |
pitā putrasya jātasya paśyec cej jīvato mukham ||
jātasya ṛṇāpākaraṇayogyasyeti bhartṛyajñaḥ | “saṃnayati” saṃkramayati |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
jīvatputramukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā pitṝṇām anṛṇaḥ pitā |
svargaṃ sa tena jātena tasmin saṃnyasya tatkṣaṇam ||
śaṅkhalikhita-paiṭhīnasayaḥ |
yatra kvacana jātena pitā putreṇa nandati |
tena cānṛṇatāṃ yāti pitṝṇāṃ piṇḍadena vai ||
[432] “yatra kvacana” jīvakṣetrayor anyonyasamayapūrvakaṃ parakṣetre ‘pi |
hārītaḥ |
yasya putraḥ śucir dakṣaḥ pūrve vayasi dhārmikaḥ |
saṃyattā cātmadoṣāṇāṃ sa tārayati pūrvajān ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha 120] |
puṃnāmno narakāt putraḥ pitaraṃ trāyate yataḥ |
mukhaṃ darśanenāpi tadutpattau yateta saḥ ||
etat putrotpattimātrasyaitat phalam |
hārītaḥ |
puṃnāmā nirayaḥ proktaḥ chinnatantuś ca nairakaḥ |
tatra vai trāyate yasmāt tasmāt putra iti smṛtaḥ ||
“nairakaḥ” narakagāmī |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
agnihotraṃ trayo vedā yajñāś ca śatadakṣiṇāḥ |
jyeṣṭhaputraprasūtasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
putrapautrapratiṣṭhasya bahvapatyasya jīvataḥ |
akṣīṇavedayajñasya hastaprāpyaṃ triviṣṭapam |
“jyeṣṭhaputraprasūtasya” prathamaputrajanmana ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau viṣṇu-vasiṣṭha-hārītāḥ |
putrena lokān jayati pautreṇātyantam aśnute |
atha putrasya pautreṇa bradhnasyāpnoti viṣṭapam ||
[433] baudhāyanaḥ [2.9.6] |
putreṇa lokān jayati pautreṇānantyam aśnute |
atha putrasya pautreṇa nākam evādhirohati ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
santānavardhanaṃ putraṃ yuktaṃ ca pitṛkarmaṇi |
devabrāhmaṇasampannam abhinandati pūrvajāḥ ||
“santānavardhanaḥ” putrotpādanasamarthaḥ | “devabrāhmaṇasaṃpannaḥ” devabrāhmaṇapūjanaparaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [17.2–4] |
anantāḥ putriṇo lokāḥ nāputrasya ca loko ‘stīti vijñāyate | “prajābhir agner (?) amṛtatvam atsyām” ity api nigamena ca | “aprajāḥ santv atriṇaḥ” ity abhiśāpaḥ | ādānaśīlā rākṣasāḥ |
nūnam ayaṃ mahān apakāro yena rākṣasā nākāśyata it tātparyārthaḥ |
bṛhaspatiḥ [vyavahāra 26, 88] |
aputreṇa sutaḥ kāryo yādṛk tādṛk prayatnataḥ |
piṇḍodakakriyāhetoḥ nāmasaṃkīrtanasya ca ||
kāṅkṣanti pitaraḥ putrān narakāpātabhīravaḥ |
yādṛk tādṛk mukhyaputrikāputrādiḥ |
yādṛśaṃ guṇam āpnoti kuplavaiḥ santaran jalam |
[434]        tādṛśaṃ guṇam āpnoti kuputraiḥ santaraṃs tamaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [āpaddharma, 53] |
yathā jalaṃ kuplavena taran majjati mānavaḥ |
tadvat pitā kuputraiś ca tamasy andhe nimajjati ||
asya ca putro avaśyam anuśāsanīyaḥ iti tātparyārthaḥ | tathā śatapathaśruteḥ |
putram anuśiṣṭaṃ lokam āhus tasmāt tam anuśāsatīti |
ādipurāṇe |
dauhitrasya mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā kimartham anuśocasi |
mahāsaṃsārasaṃkīrṇā nāsti vai narakād bhayam ||
manuḥ [4.257] |
maharṣipitṛdevānāṃ gatvānṛṇyaṃ yathākramam |
putre sarvaṃ samāsajya vasen mādhyasthyam āsthitaḥ ||
ekākī cintayen nityaṃ vivikte hitam ātmanaḥ |
ekākī cintayan vipraḥ paraṃ śreyo ‘dhigacchati ||
“mādhyasthyam” dhanaputrādau mamatātyāgaḥ | “ekākī” asahāyaḥ, sahādhyāyiprabhṛtibhiḥ | saṃbhāṣādivarjaṃ cintayed ātmano hitam, upaniṣaduktabrahmasvarūpaṃ dhyāyed ity arthaḥ |
yamaḥ |
tyaktamuktagṛhāvāsaṃ vimuktaṃ pūrvabāndavaiḥ |
jitendriyam alubdhaṃ ca taṃ devāḥ sthaviraṃ viduḥ |
[435] manuḥ [4.259–260] |
eṣoditā gṛhasthasya vṛttir viprasya śāśvatī |
snātakavratakalpaś ca sattvavṛddhikaraḥ śubhaḥ ||
anena vipro vṛttena vartayan vedaśāstravit |
vyapetakalmaṣo nityaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate ||
[5.169]        anena vidhinā nityaṃ pañcayajñān na hāpayet |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ gṛheṣu brāhmaṇo vaset ||
[4.1]        caturtham āyuṣo bhāgam uṣitvādyaṃ gurau dvijaḥ |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ kṛtodvāho gṛhe vaset ||
tad evamādivacanaparyālocanā | gṛhasthasyaivāpākṛtaṛṇatrayasya, kuṭumbabharaṇādi-samarthasya putrasya bhāryādiparikaraṃ samarthaṃ tyaktamamatvasyātmani samāropaṇaṃ kṛtavataḥ itthaṃ bhūtasyaiva bhāryārahitatvenaiva gatāgnihotrasya vā dravyasādhyaśrāddhādi-karmanivṛttasya saṃdhyopāsanodakatarpaṇaśaucātmacintanamātrānuṣṭhānaṃ kurvato ‘yaṃ prakāra iti medhātithiḥ |
iti śrī mahādhirājaśrīmadgovindacandradevamahāsandhivigrahika-
bhaṭṭaśrīhṛdayadharātmajabhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite
kṛtyakalpatarau gṛhasthakāṇḍam samāptam ||
